\subsection{Proverbs}

	\label{PROVERBS}

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 1} % *PROVERBS-1
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-1}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:1} % *PROVERBS-1-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-1}

			The proverbs of Solomon the son of David, king of Israel;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭שְׁלֵי שְׁלֹמֹ֣ה בֶן־דָּוִ֑ד מֶ֝֗לֶךְ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The proverbs of Solomon the son of David, king of Israel:
					\item[NIV] The proverbs of Solomon son of David, king of Israel:
					\item[NRSV] The proverbs of Solomon son of David, king of Israel:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:2} % *PROVERBS-1-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-2}

			To know wisdom and instruction; to perceive the words of understanding;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לָדַ֣עַת חָכְמָ֣ה וּמוּסָ֑ר לְ֝הָבִ֗ין אִמְרֵ֥י בִינָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To know wisdom and instruction; To discern the words of understanding;
					\item[NIV] for gaining wisdom and instruction; for understanding words of insight;
					\item[NRSV] For learning about wisdom and instruction, for understanding words of insight,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:3} % *PROVERBS-1-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-3}

			To receive the instruction of wisdom, justice, and judgment, and equity;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לָ֭קַחַת מוּסַ֣ר הַשְׂכֵּ֑ל צֶ֥דֶק וּ֝מִשְׁפָּ֗ט וּמֵישָׁרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To receive instruction in wise dealing, In righteousness and justice and equity;
					\item[NIV] for receiving instruction in prudent behavior, doing what is right and just and fair;
					\item[NRSV] for gaining instruction in wise dealing, righteousness, justice, and equity;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:4} % *PROVERBS-1-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-4}

			To give subtilty to the simple, to the young man knowledge and discretion.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לָתֵ֣ת לִפְתָאיִ֣ם עָרְמָ֑ה לְ֝נַ֗עַר דַּ֣עַת וּמְזִמָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To give prudence to the simple, To the young man knowledge and discretion:
					\item[NIV] for giving prudence to those who are simple, knowledge and discretion to the young—
					\item[NRSV] to teach shrewdness to the simple, knowledge and prudence to the young—
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:5} % *PROVERBS-1-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-5}

			A wise man will hear, and will increase learning; and a man of understanding shall attain unto wise counsels:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִשְׁמַ֣ע חָ֭כָם וְי֣וֹסֶף לֶ֑קַח וְ֝נָב֗וֹן תַּחְבֻּל֥וֹת יִקְנֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That the wise man may hear, and increase in learning; And that the man of understanding may attain unto sound counsels:
					\item[NIV] let the wise listen and add to their learning, and let the discerning get guidance—
					\item[NRSV] let the wise also hear and gain in learning, and the discerning acquire skill,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:6} % *PROVERBS-1-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-6}

			To understand a proverb, and the interpretation; the words of the wise, and their dark sayings.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְהָבִ֣ין מָ֭שָׁל וּמְלִיצָ֑ה דִּבְרֵ֥י חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים וְחִידֹתָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To understand a proverb, and a figure, The words of the wise, and their dark sayings.
					\item[NIV] for understanding proverbs and parables, the sayings and riddles of the wise.
					\item[NRSV] to understand a proverb and a figure, the words of the wise and their riddles.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:7} % *PROVERBS-1-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-7}

			The fear of the LORD is the beginning of knowledge: but fools despise wisdom and instruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה רֵאשִׁ֣ית דָּ֑עַת חָכְמָ֥ה וּ֝מוּסָ֗ר אֱוִילִ֥ים בָּֽזוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah is the beginning of knowledge; But the foolish despise wisdom and instruction.
					\item[NIV] The fear of the LORD is the beginning of knowledge, but fools despise wisdom and instruction.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD is the beginning of knowledge; fools despise wisdom and instruction.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:8} % *PROVERBS-1-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-8}

			My son, hear the instruction of thy father, and forsake not the law of thy mother:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁמַ֣ע בְּ֭נִי מוּסַ֣ר אָבִ֑יךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝טֹּ֗שׁ תּוֹרַ֥ת אִמֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, hear the instruction of thy father, And forsake not the law of thy mother:
					\item[NIV] Listen, my son, to your father’s instruction and do not forsake your mother’s teaching.
					\item[NRSV] Hear, my child, your father’s instruction, and do not reject your mother’s teaching;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:9} % *PROVERBS-1-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-9}

			For they shall be an ornament of grace unto thy head, and chains about thy neck.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י ׀ לִוְיַ֤ת חֵ֓ן הֵ֬ם לְרֹאשֶׁ֑ךָ וַ֝עֲנָקִ֗ים לְגַרְגְּרֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For they shall be a chaplet of grace unto thy head, And chains about thy neck.
					\item[NIV] They are a garland to grace your head and a chain to adorn your neck.
					\item[NRSV] for they are a fair garland for your head, and pendants for your neck.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:10} % *PROVERBS-1-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-10}

			My son, if sinners entice thee, consent thou not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּנִ֡י אִם־יְפַתּ֥וּךָ חַ֝טָּאִ֗ים אַל־תֹּבֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, if sinners entice thee, Consent thou not.
					\item[NIV] My son, if sinful men entice you, do not give in to them.
					\item[NRSV] My child, if sinners entice you, do not consent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:11} % *PROVERBS-1-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-11}

			If they say, Come with us, let us lay wait for blood, let us lurk privily for the innocent without cause:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־יֹאמְרוּ֮ לְכָ֪ה אִ֫תָּ֥נוּ נֶאֶרְבָ֥ה לְדָ֑ם נִצְפְּנָ֖ה לְנָקִ֣י חִנָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If they say, Come with us, Let us lay wait for blood; Let us lurk privily for the innocent without cause;
					\item[NIV] If they say, “Come along with us; let’s lie in wait for innocent blood, let’s ambush some harmless soul;
					\item[NRSV] If they say, “Come with us, let us lie in wait for blood; let us wantonly ambush the innocent;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:12} % *PROVERBS-1-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-12}

			Let us swallow them up alive as the grave; and whole, as those that go down into the pit:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נִ֭בְלָעֵם כִּשְׁא֣וֹל חַיִּ֑ים וּ֝תְמִימִ֗ים כְּי֣וֹרְדֵי בֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let us swallow them up alive as Sheol, And whole, as those that go down into the pit;
					\item[NIV] let’s swallow them alive, like the grave, and whole, like those who go down to the pit;
					\item[NRSV] like Sheol let us swallow them alive and whole, like those who go down to the Pit.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:13} % *PROVERBS-1-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-13}

			We shall find all precious substance, we shall fill our houses with spoil:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־ה֣וֹן יָקָ֣ר נִמְצָ֑א נְמַלֵּ֖א בָתֵּ֣ינוּ שָׁלָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] We shall find all precious substance; We shall fill our houses with spoil;
					\item[NIV] we will get all sorts of valuable things and fill our houses with plunder;
					\item[NRSV] We shall find all kinds of costly things; we shall fill our houses with booty.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:14} % *PROVERBS-1-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-14}

			Cast in thy lot among us; let us all have one purse:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גּ֭וֹרָ֣לְךָ תַּפִּ֣יל בְּתוֹכֵ֑נוּ כִּ֥יס אֶ֝חָ֗ד יִהְיֶ֥ה לְכֻלָּֽנוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou shalt cast thy lot among us; We will all have one purse:
					\item[NIV] cast lots with us; we will all share the loot"—
					\item[NRSV] Throw in your lot among us; we will all have one purse”—
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:15} % *PROVERBS-1-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-15}

			My son, walk not thou in the way with them; refrain thy foot from their path:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּנִ֗י אַל־תֵּלֵ֣ךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ אִתָּ֑ם מְנַ֥ע רַ֝גְלְךָ֗ מִנְּתִיבָתָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, walk not thou in the way with them; Refrain thy foot from their path:
					\item[NIV] my son, do not go along with them, do not set foot on their paths;
					\item[NRSV] my child, do not walk in their way, keep your foot from their paths;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:16} % *PROVERBS-1-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-16}

			For their feet run to evil, and make haste to shed blood.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י רַ֭גְלֵיהֶם לָרַ֣ע יָר֑וּצוּ וִֽ֝ימַהֲר֗וּ לִשְׁפָּךְ־דָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For their feet run to evil, And they make haste to shed blood.
					\item[NIV] for their feet rush into evil, they are swift to shed blood.
					\item[NRSV] for their feet run to evil, and they hurry to shed blood.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:17} % *PROVERBS-1-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-17}

			Surely in vain the net is spread in the sight of any bird.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־חִ֭נָּם מְזֹרָ֣ה הָרָ֑שֶׁת בְּ֝עֵינֵ֗י כָל־בַּ֥עַל כָּנָֽף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For in vain is the net spread In the sight of any bird:
					\item[NIV] How useless to spread a net where every bird can see it!
					\item[NRSV] For in vain is the net baited while the bird is looking on;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:18} % *PROVERBS-1-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-18}

			And they lay wait for their own blood; they lurk privily for their own lives.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭הֵם לְדָמָ֣ם יֶאֱרֹ֑בוּ יִ֝צְפְּנ֗וּ לְנַפְשֹׁתָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And these lay wait for their own blood; They lurk privily for their own lives.
					\item[NIV] These men lie in wait for their own blood; they ambush only themselves!
					\item[NRSV] yet they lie in wait—to kill themselves! and set an ambush—for their own lives!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:19} % *PROVERBS-1-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-19}

			So are the ways of every one that is greedy of gain; which taketh away the life of the owners thereof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֵּ֗ן אָ֭רְחוֹת כָּל־בֹּ֣צֵֽעַ בָּ֑צַע אֶת־נֶ֖פֶשׁ בְּעָלָ֣יו יִקָּֽח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So are the ways of every one that is greedy of gain; It taketh away the life of the owners thereof.
					\item[NIV] Such are the paths of all who go after ill-gotten gain; it takes away the life of those who get it.
					\item[NRSV] Such is the end of all who are greedy for gain; it takes away the life of its possessors.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:20} % *PROVERBS-1-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-20}

			Wisdom crieth without; she uttereth her voice in the streets:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָ֭כְמוֹת בַּח֣וּץ תָּרֹ֑נָּה בָּ֝רְחֹב֗וֹת תִּתֵּ֥ן קוֹלָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom crieth aloud in the street; She uttereth her voice in the broad places;
					\item[NIV] Out in the open wisdom calls aloud, she raises her voice in the public square;
					\item[NRSV] Wisdom cries out in the street; in the squares she raises her voice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:21} % *PROVERBS-1-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-21}

			She crieth in the chief place of concourse, in the openings of the gates: in the city she uttereth her words, saying,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּרֹ֥אשׁ הֹמִיּ֗וֹת תִּ֫קְרָ֥א בְּפִתְחֵ֖י שְׁעָרִ֥ים בָּעִ֗יר אֲמָרֶ֥יהָ תֹאמֵֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She crieth in the chief place of concourse; At the entrance of the gates, In the city, she uttereth her words:
					\item[NIV] on top of the wall she cries out, at the city gate she makes her speech:
					\item[NRSV] At the busiest corner she cries out; at the entrance of the city gates she speaks:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:22} % *PROVERBS-1-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-22}

			How long, ye simple ones, will ye love simplicity? and the scorners delight in their scorning, and fools hate knowledge?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַד־מָתַ֣י ׀ פְּתָיִם֮ תְּֽאֵהֲב֫וּ פֶ֥תִי וְלֵצִ֗ים לָ֭צוֹן חָמְד֣וּ לָהֶ֑ם וּ֝כְסִילִ֗ים יִשְׂנְאוּ־דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How long, ye simple ones, will ye love simplicity? And scoffers delight them in scoffing, And fools hate knowledge?
					\item[NIV] “How long will you who are simple love your simple ways? How long will mockers delight in mockery and fools hate knowledge?
					\item[NRSV] “How long, O simple ones, will you love being simple? How long will scoffers delight in their scoffing and fools hate knowledge?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:23} % *PROVERBS-1-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-23}

			Turn you at my reproof: behold, I will pour out my spirit unto you, I will make known my words unto you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תָּשׁ֗וּבוּ לְֽת֫וֹכַחְתִּ֥י הִנֵּ֤ה אַבִּ֣יעָה לָכֶ֣ם רוּחִ֑י אוֹדִ֖יעָה דְבָרַ֣י אֶתְכֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Turn you at my reproof: Behold, I will pour out my spirit upon you; I will make known my words unto you.
					\item[NIV] Repent at my rebuke! Then I will pour out my thoughts to you, I will make known to you my teachings.
					\item[NRSV] Give heed to my reproof; I will pour out my thoughts to you; I will make my words known to you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:24} % *PROVERBS-1-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-24}

			Because I have called, and ye refused; I have stretched out my hand, and no man regarded;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַ֣עַן קָ֭רָאתִי וַתְּמָאֵ֑נוּ נָטִ֥יתִי יָ֝דִ֗י וְאֵ֣ין מַקְשִֽׁיב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Because I have called, and ye have refused; I have stretched out my hand, and no man hath regarded;
					\item[NIV] But since you refuse to listen when I call and no one pays attention when I stretch out my hand,
					\item[NRSV] Because I have called and you refused, have stretched out my hand and no one heeded,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:25} % *PROVERBS-1-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-25}

			But ye have set at nought all my counsel, and would none of my reproof:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתִּפְרְע֥וּ כָל־עֲצָתִ֑י וְ֝תוֹכַחְתִּ֗י לֹ֣א אֲבִיתֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But ye have set at nought all my counsel, And would none of my reproof:
					\item[NIV] since you disregard all my advice and do not accept my rebuke,
					\item[NRSV] and because you have ignored all my counsel and would have none of my reproof,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:26} % *PROVERBS-1-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-26}

			I also will laugh at your calamity; I will mock when your fear cometh;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־אֲ֭נִי בְּאֵידְכֶ֣ם אֶשְׂחָ֑ק אֶ֝לְעַ֗ג בְּבֹ֣א פַחְדְּכֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I also will laugh in the day of your calamity; I will mock when your fear cometh;
					\item[NIV] I in turn will laugh when disaster strikes you; I will mock when calamity overtakes you—
					\item[NRSV] I also will laugh at your calamity; I will mock when panic strikes you,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:27} % *PROVERBS-1-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-27}

			When your fear cometh as desolation, and your destruction cometh as a whirlwind; when distress and anguish cometh upon you.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּבֹ֤א כְשׁוֹאָ֨ה ׀ פַּחְדְּכֶ֗ם וְֽ֭אֵידְכֶם כְּסוּפָ֣ה יֶאֱתֶ֑ה בְּבֹ֥א עֲ֝לֵיכֶ֗ם צָרָ֥ה וְצוּקָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When your fear cometh as a storm, And your calamity cometh on as a whirlwind; When distress and anguish come upon you.
					\item[NIV] when calamity overtakes you like a storm, when disaster sweeps over you like a whirlwind, when distress and trouble overwhelm you.
					\item[NRSV] when panic strikes you like a storm, and your calamity comes like a whirlwind, when distress and anguish come upon you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:28} % *PROVERBS-1-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-28}

			Then shall they call upon me, but I will not answer; they shall seek me early, but they shall not find me:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֣ז יִ֭קְרָאֻנְנִי וְלֹ֣א אֶֽעֱנֶ֑ה יְ֝שַׁחֲרֻ֗נְנִי וְלֹ֣א יִמְצָאֻֽנְנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then will they call upon me, but I will not answer; They will seek me diligently, but they shall not find me:
					\item[NIV] “Then they will call to me but I will not answer; they will look for me but will not find me,
					\item[NRSV] Then they will call upon me, but I will not answer; they will seek me diligently, but will not find me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:29} % *PROVERBS-1-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-29}

			For that they hated knowledge, and did not choose the fear of the LORD:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּ֭חַת כִּי־שָׂ֣נְאוּ דָ֑עַת וְיִרְאַ֥ת יְ֝הֹוָ֗ה לֹ֣א בָחָֽרוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For that they hated knowledge, And did not choose the fear of Jehovah:
					\item[NIV] since they hated knowledge and did not choose to fear the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] Because they hated knowledge and did not choose the fear of the LORD,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:30} % *PROVERBS-1-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-30}

			They would none of my counsel: they despised all my reproof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־אָב֥וּ לַעֲצָתִ֑י נָ֝אֲצ֗וּ כָּל־תּוֹכַחְתִּֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They would none of my counsel; They despised all my reproof.
					\item[NIV] Since they would not accept my advice and spurned my rebuke,
					\item[NRSV] would have none of my counsel, and despised all my reproof,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:31} % *PROVERBS-1-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-31}

			Therefore shall they eat of the fruit of their own way, and be filled with their own devices.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭יֹאכְלוּ מִפְּרִ֣י דַרְכָּ֑ם וּֽמִמֹּעֲצֹ֖תֵיהֶ֣ם יִשְׂבָּֽעוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Therefore shall they eat of the fruit of their own way, And be filled with their own devices.
					\item[NIV] they will eat the fruit of their ways and be filled with the fruit of their schemes.
					\item[NRSV] therefore they shall eat the fruit of their way and be sated with their own devices.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:32} % *PROVERBS-1-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-32}

			For the turning away of the simple shall slay them, and the prosperity of fools shall destroy them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י מְשׁוּבַ֣ת פְּתָיִ֣ם תַּֽהַרְגֵ֑ם וְשַׁלְוַ֖ת כְּסִילִ֣ים תְּאַבְּדֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the backsliding of the simple shall slay them, And the careless ease of fools shall destroy them.
					\item[NIV] For the waywardness of the simple will kill them, and the complacency of fools will destroy them;
					\item[NRSV] For waywardness kills the simple, and the complacency of fools destroys them;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 1:33} % *PROVERBS-1-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-1-33}

			But whoso hearkeneth unto me shall dwell safely, and shall be quiet from fear of evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְשֹׁמֵ֣עַֽ לִ֭י יִשְׁכָּן־בֶּ֑טַח וְ֝שַׁאֲנַ֗ן מִפַּ֥חַד רָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But whoso hearkeneth unto me shall dwell securely, And shall be quiet without fear of evil.
					\item[NIV] but whoever listens to me will live in safety and be at ease, without fear of harm.”
					\item[NRSV] but those who listen to me will be secure and will live at ease, without dread of disaster.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 2} % *PROVERBS-2
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-2}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:1} % *PROVERBS-2-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-1}

			My son, if thou wilt receive my words, and hide my commandments with thee;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי אִם־תִּקַּ֣ח אֲמָרָ֑י וּ֝מִצְוֺתַ֗י תִּצְפֹּ֥ן אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, if thou wilt receive my words, And lay up my commandments with thee;
					\item[NIV] My son, if you accept my words and store up my commands within you,
					\item[NRSV] My child, if you accept my words and treasure up my commandments within you,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:2} % *PROVERBS-2-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-2}

			So that thou incline thine ear unto wisdom, and apply thine heart to understanding;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְהַקְשִׁ֣יב לַֽחָכְמָ֣ה אָזְנֶ֑ךָ תַּטֶּ֥ה לִ֝בְּךָ֗ לַתְּבוּנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So as to incline thine ear unto wisdom, And apply thy heart to understanding;
					\item[NIV] turning your ear to wisdom and applying your heart to understanding—
					\item[NRSV] making your ear attentive to wisdom and inclining your heart to understanding;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:3} % *PROVERBS-2-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-3}

			Yea, if thou criest after knowledge, and liftest up thy voice for understanding;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י אִ֣ם לַבִּינָ֣ה תִקְרָ֑א לַ֝תְּבוּנָ֗ה תִּתֵּ֥ן קוֹלֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, if thou cry after discernment, And lift up thy voice for understanding;
					\item[NIV] indeed, if you call out for insight and cry aloud for understanding,
					\item[NRSV] if you indeed cry out for insight, and raise your voice for understanding;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:4} % *PROVERBS-2-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-4}

			If thou seekest her as silver, and searchest for her as for hid treasures;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־תְּבַקְשֶׁ֥נָּה כַכָּ֑סֶף וְֽכַמַּטְמוֹנִ֥ים תַּחְפְּשֶֽׂנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If thou seek her as silver, And search for her as for hid treasures:
					\item[NIV] and if you look for it as for silver and search for it as for hidden treasure,
					\item[NRSV] if you seek it like silver, and search for it as for hidden treasures—
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:5} % *PROVERBS-2-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-5}

			Then shalt thou understand the fear of the LORD, and find the knowledge of God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֗ז תָּ֭בִין יִרְאַ֣ת יְהוָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת אֱלֹהִ֣ים תִּמְצָֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then shalt thou understand the fear of Jehovah, And find the knowledge of God.
					\item[NIV] then you will understand the fear of the LORD and find the knowledge of God.
					\item[NRSV] then you will understand the fear of the LORD and find the knowledge of God.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:6} % *PROVERBS-2-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-6}

			For the LORD giveth wisdom: out of his mouth cometh knowledge and understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־יְ֭הוָה יִתֵּ֣ן חָכְמָ֑ה מִ֝פִּ֗יו דַּ֣עַת וּתְבוּנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For Jehovah giveth wisdom; Out of his mouth cometh knowledge and understanding:
					\item[NIV] For the LORD gives wisdom; from his mouth come knowledge and understanding.
					\item[NRSV] For the LORD gives wisdom; from his mouth come knowledge and understanding;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:7} % *PROVERBS-2-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-7}

			He layeth up sound wisdom for the righteous: he is a buckler to them that walk uprightly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִצְפֹּ֣ן לַ֭יְשָׁרִים תּוּשִׁיָּ֑ה מָ֝גֵ֗ן לְהֹ֣לְכֵי תֹֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He layeth up sound wisdom for the upright; He is a shield to them that walk in integrity;
					\item[NIV] He holds success in store for the upright, he is a shield to those whose walk is blameless,
					\item[NRSV] he stores up sound wisdom for the upright; he is a shield to those who walk blamelessly,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:8} % *PROVERBS-2-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-8}

			He keepeth the paths of judgment, and preserveth the way of his saints.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִ֭נְצֹר אָרְח֣וֹת מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וְדֶ֖רֶךְ חֲסִידָ֣יו יִשְׁמֹֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That he may guard the paths of justice, And preserve the way of his saints.
					\item[NIV] for he guards the course of the just and protects the way of his faithful ones.
					\item[NRSV] guarding the paths of justice and preserving the way of his faithful ones.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:9} % *PROVERBS-2-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-9}

			Then shalt thou understand righteousness, and judgment, and equity; yea, every good path.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֗ז תָּ֭בִין צֶ֣דֶק וּמִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּ֝מֵישָׁרִ֗ים כָּל־מַעְגַּל־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then shalt thou understand righteousness and justice, And equity, yea, every good path.
					\item[NIV] Then you will understand what is right and just and fair—every good path.
					\item[NRSV] Then you will understand righteousness and justice and equity, every good path;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:10} % *PROVERBS-2-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-10}

			When wisdom entereth into thine heart, and knowledge is pleasant unto thy soul;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־תָב֣וֹא חָכְמָ֣ה בְלִבֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝דַ֗עַת לְֽנַפְשְׁךָ֥ יִנְעָֽם׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For wisdom shall enter into thy heart, And knowledge shall be pleasant unto thy soul;
					\item[NIV] For wisdom will enter your heart, and knowledge will be pleasant to your soul.
					\item[NRSV] for wisdom will come into your heart, and knowledge will be pleasant to your soul;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:11} % *PROVERBS-2-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-11}

			Discretion shall preserve thee, understanding shall keep thee:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְ֭זִמָּה תִּשְׁמֹ֥ר עָלֶ֗יךָ תְּבוּנָ֥ה תִנְצְרֶֽכָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Discretion shall watch over thee; Understanding shall keep thee:
					\item[NIV] Discretion will protect you, and understanding will guard you.
					\item[NRSV] prudence will watch over you; and understanding will guard you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:12} % *PROVERBS-2-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-12}

			To deliver thee from the way of the evil man, from the man that speaketh froward things;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְ֭הַצִּ֣ילְךָ מִדֶּ֣רֶךְ רָ֑ע מֵ֝אִ֗ישׁ מְדַבֵּ֥ר תַּהְפֻּכֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To deliver thee from the way of evil, From the men that speak perverse things;
					\item[NIV] Wisdom will save you from the ways of wicked men, from men whose words are perverse,
					\item[NRSV] It will save you from the way of evil, from those who speak perversely,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:13} % *PROVERBS-2-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-13}

			Who leave the paths of uprightness, to walk in the ways of darkness;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֭עֹ֣זְבִים אָרְח֣וֹת יֹ֑שֶׁר לָ֝לֶ֗כֶת בְּדַרְכֵי־חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who forsake the paths of uprightness, To walk in the ways of darkness;
					\item[NIV] who have left the straight paths to walk in dark ways,
					\item[NRSV] who forsake the paths of uprightness to walk in the ways of darkness,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:14} % *PROVERBS-2-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-14}

			Who rejoice to do evil, and delight in the frowardness of the wicked;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֭שְּׂמֵחִים לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת רָ֑ע יָ֝גִ֗ילוּ בְּֽתַהְפֻּכ֥וֹת רָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who rejoice to do evil, And delight in the perverseness of evil;
					\item[NIV] who delight in doing wrong and rejoice in the perverseness of evil,
					\item[NRSV] who rejoice in doing evil and delight in the perverseness of evil;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:15} % *PROVERBS-2-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-15}

			Whose ways are crooked, and they froward in their paths:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁ֣ר אָרְחֹתֵיהֶ֣ם עִקְּשִׁ֑ים וּ֝נְלוֹזִ֗ים בְּמַעְגְּלוֹתָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who are crooked in their ways, And wayward in their paths:
					\item[NIV] whose paths are crooked and who are devious in their ways.
					\item[NRSV] those whose paths are crooked, and who are devious in their ways.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:16} % *PROVERBS-2-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-16}

			To deliver thee from the strange woman, even from the stranger which flattereth with her words;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְ֭הַצִּ֣ילְךָ מֵאִשָּׁ֣ה זָרָ֑ה מִ֝נָּכְרִיָּ֗ה אֲמָרֶ֥יהָ הֶחֱלִֽיקָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To deliver thee from the strange woman, Even from the foreigner that flattereth with her words;
					\item[NIV] Wisdom will save you also from the adulterous woman, from the wayward woman with her seductive words,
					\item[NRSV] You will be saved from the loose woman, from the adulteress with her smooth words,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:17} % *PROVERBS-2-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-17}

			Which forsaketh the guide of her youth, and forgetteth the covenant of her God.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֭עֹזֶבֶת אַלּ֣וּף נְעוּרֶ֑יהָ וְאֶת־בְּרִ֖ית אֱלֹהֶ֣יהָ שָׁכֵֽחָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That forsaketh the friend of her youth, And forgetteth the covenant of her God:
					\item[NIV] who has left the partner of her youth and ignored the covenant she made before God.
					\item[NRSV] who forsakes the partner of her youth and forgets her sacred covenant;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:18} % *PROVERBS-2-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-18}

			For her house inclineth unto death, and her paths unto the dead.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י שָׁ֣חָה אֶל־מָ֣וֶת בֵּיתָ֑הּ וְאֶל־רְ֝פָאִ֗ים מַעְגְּלֹתֶֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For her house inclineth unto death, And her paths unto the dead;
					\item[NIV] Surely her house leads down to death and her paths to the spirits of the dead.
					\item[NRSV] for her way leads down to death, and her paths to the shades;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:19} % *PROVERBS-2-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-19}

			None that go unto her return again, neither take they hold of the paths of life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־בָּ֭אֶיהָ לֹ֣א יְשׁוּב֑וּן וְלֹֽא־יַ֝שִּׂ֗יגוּ אָרְח֥וֹת חַיִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] None that go unto her return again, Neither do they attain unto the paths of life:
					\item[NIV] None who go to her return or attain the paths of life.
					\item[NRSV] those who go to her never come back, nor do they regain the paths of life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:20} % *PROVERBS-2-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-20}

			That thou mayest walk in the way of good men, and keep the paths of the righteous.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְמַ֗עַן תֵּ֭לֵךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ טוֹבִ֑ים וְאָרְח֖וֹת צַדִּיקִ֣ים תִּשְׁמֹֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That thou mayest walk in the way of good men, And keep the paths of the righteous.
					\item[NIV] Thus you will walk in the ways of the good and keep to the paths of the righteous.
					\item[NRSV] Therefore walk in the way of the good, and keep to the paths of the just.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:21} % *PROVERBS-2-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-21}

			For the upright shall dwell in the land, and the perfect shall remain in it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־יְשָׁרִ֥ים יִשְׁכְּנוּ אָ֑רֶץ וּ֝תְמִימִ֗ים יִוָּ֥תְרוּ בָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the upright shall dwell in the land, And the perfect shall remain in it.
					\item[NIV] For the upright will live in the land, and the blameless will remain in it;
					\item[NRSV] For the upright will abide in the land, and the innocent will remain in it;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 2:22} % *PROVERBS-2-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-2-22}

			But the wicked shall be cut off from the earth, and the transgressors shall be rooted out of it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּ֭רְשָׁעִים מֵאֶ֣רֶץ יִכָּרֵ֑תוּ וּ֝בוֹגְדִ֗ים יִסְּח֥וּ מִמֶּֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But the wicked shall be cut off from the land, And the treacherous shall be rooted out of it.
					\item[NIV] but the wicked will be cut off from the land, and the unfaithful will be torn from it.
					\item[NRSV] but the wicked will be cut off from the land, and the treacherous will be rooted out of it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 3} % *PROVERBS-3
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-3}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:1} % *PROVERBS-3-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-1}

			My son, forget not my law; but let thine heart keep my commandments:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי תּוֹרָתִ֣י אַל־תִּשְׁכָּ֑ח וּ֝מִצְוֺתַ֗י יִצֹּ֥ר לִבֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, forget not my law; But let thy heart keep my commandments:
					\item[NIV] My son, do not forget my teaching, but keep my commands in your heart,
					\item[NRSV] My child, do not forget my teaching, but let your heart keep my commandments;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:2} % *PROVERBS-3-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-2}

			For length of days, and long life, and peace, shall they add to thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י אֹ֣רֶךְ יָ֭מִים וּשְׁנ֣וֹת חַיִּ֑ים וְ֝שָׁל֗וֹם יוֹסִ֥יפוּ לָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For length of days, and years of life, And peace, will they add to thee.
					\item[NIV] for they will prolong your life many years and bring you peace and prosperity.
					\item[NRSV] for length of days and years of life and abundant welfare they will give you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:3} % *PROVERBS-3-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-3}

			Let not mercy and truth forsake thee: bind them about thy neck; write them upon the table of thine heart:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֶ֥סֶד וֶאֱמֶ֗ת אַֽל־יַעַ֫זְבֻ֥ךָ קָשְׁרֵ֥ם עַל־גַּרְגְּרוֹתֶ֑יךָ כָּ֝תְבֵ֗ם עַל־ל֥וּחַ לִבֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let not kindness and truth forsake thee: Bind them about thy neck; Write them upon the tablet of thy heart:
					\item[NIV] Let love and faithfulness never leave you; bind them around your neck, write them on the tablet of your heart.
					\item[NRSV] Do not let loyalty and faithfulness forsake you; bind them around your neck, write them on the tablet of your heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:4} % *PROVERBS-3-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-4}

			So shalt thou find favour and good understanding in the sight of God and man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּמְצָא־חֵ֖ן וְשֵֽׂכֶל־ט֑וֹב בְּעֵינֵ֖י אֱלֹהִ֣ים וְאָדָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shalt thou find favor and good understanding In the sight of God and man.
					\item[NIV] Then you will win favor and a good name in the sight of God and man.
					\item[NRSV] So you will find favor and good repute in the sight of God and of people.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:5} % *PROVERBS-3-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-5}

			Trust in the LORD with all thine heart; and lean not unto thine own understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּטַ֣ח אֶל־יְ֭הוָה בְּכָל־לִבֶּ֑ךָ וְאֶל־בִּֽ֝ינָתְךָ֗ אַל־תִּשָּׁעֵֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Trust in Jehovah with all thy heart, And lean not upon thine own understanding:
					\item[NIV] Trust in the LORD with all your heart and lean not on your own understanding;
					\item[NRSV] Trust in the LORD with all your heart, and do not rely on your own insight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:6} % *PROVERBS-3-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-6}

			In all thy ways acknowledge him, and he shall direct thy paths.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּכָל־דְּרָכֶ֥יךָ דָעֵ֑הוּ וְ֝ה֗וּא יְיַשֵּׁ֥ר אֹֽרְחֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In all thy ways acknowledge him, And he will direct thy paths.
					\item[NIV] in all your ways submit to him, and he will make your paths straight.
					\item[NRSV] In all your ways acknowledge him, and he will make straight your paths.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:7} % *PROVERBS-3-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-7}

			Be not wise in thine own eyes: fear the LORD, and depart from evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תְּהִ֣י חָכָ֣ם בְּעֵינֶ֑יךָ יְרָ֥א אֶת־יְ֝הוָ֗ה וְס֣וּר מֵרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not wise in thine own eyes; Fear Jehovah, and depart from evil:
					\item[NIV] Do not be wise in your own eyes; fear the LORD and shun evil.
					\item[NRSV] Do not be wise in your own eyes; fear the LORD, and turn away from evil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:8} % *PROVERBS-3-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-8}

			It shall be health to thy navel, and marrow to thy bones.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רִ֭פְאוּת תְּהִ֣י לְשָׁרֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝שִׁקּ֗וּי לְעַצְמוֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It will be health to thy navel, And marrow to thy bones.
					\item[NIV] This will bring health to your body and nourishment to your bones.
					\item[NRSV] It will be a healing for your flesh and a refreshment for your body.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:9} % *PROVERBS-3-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-9}

			Honour the LORD with thy substance, and with the firstfruits of all thine increase:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּבֵּ֣ד אֶת־יְ֭הוָה מֵהוֹנֶ֑ךָ וּ֝מֵרֵאשִׁ֗ית כָּל־תְּבוּאָתֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Honor Jehovah with thy substance, And with the first-fruits of all thine increase:
					\item[NIV] Honor the LORD with your wealth, with the firstfruits of all your crops;
					\item[NRSV] Honor the LORD with your substance and with the first fruits of all your produce;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:10} % *PROVERBS-3-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-10}

			So shall thy barns be filled with plenty, and thy presses shall burst out with new wine.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְיִמָּלְא֣וּ אֲסָמֶ֣יךָ שָׂבָ֑ע וְ֝תִיר֗וֹשׁ יְקָבֶ֥יךָ יִפְרֹֽצוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shall thy barns be filled with plenty, And thy vats shall overflow with new wine.
					\item[NIV] then your barns will be filled to overflowing, and your vats will brim over with new wine.
					\item[NRSV] then your barns will be filled with plenty, and your vats will be bursting with wine.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:11} % *PROVERBS-3-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-11}

			My son, despise not the chastening of the LORD; neither be weary of his correction:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מוּסַ֣ר יְ֭הוָה בְּנִ֣י אַל־תִּמְאָ֑ס וְאַל־תָּ֝קֹ֗ץ בְּתוֹכַחְתּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, despise not the chastening of Jehovah; Neither be weary of his reproof:
					\item[NIV] My son, do not despise the LORD’s discipline, and do not resent his rebuke,
					\item[NRSV] My child, do not despise the LORD’s discipline or be weary of his reproof,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:12} % *PROVERBS-3-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-12}

			For whom the LORD loveth he correcteth; even as a father the son in whom he delighteth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י אֶ֥ת אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֶאֱהַ֣ב יְהוָ֣ה יוֹכִ֑יחַ וּ֝כְאָ֗ב אֶת־בֵּ֥ן יִרְצֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For whom Jehovah loveth he reproveth; Even as a father the son in whom he delighteth.
					\item[NIV] because the LORD disciplines those he loves, as a father the son he delights in.
					\item[NRSV] for the LORD reproves the one he loves, as a father the son in whom he delights.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:13} % *PROVERBS-3-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-13}

			Happy is the man that findeth wisdom, and the man that getteth understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַשְׁרֵ֣י אָ֭דָם מָצָ֣א חָכְמָ֑ה וְ֝אָדָ֗ם יָפִ֥יק תְּבוּנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Happy is the man that findeth wisdom, And the man that getteth understanding.
					\item[NIV] Blessed are those who find wisdom, those who gain understanding,
					\item[NRSV] Happy are those who find wisdom, and those who get understanding,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:14} % *PROVERBS-3-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-14}

			For the merchandise of it is better than the merchandise of silver, and the gain thereof than fine gold.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י ט֣וֹב סַ֭חְרָהּ מִסְּחַר־כָּ֑סֶף וּ֝מֵחָר֗וּץ תְּבוּאָתָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the gaining of it is better than the gaining of silver, And the profit thereof than fine gold.
					\item[NIV] for she is more profitable than silver and yields better returns than gold.
					\item[NRSV] for her income is better than silver, and her revenue better than gold.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:15} % *PROVERBS-3-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-15}

			She is more precious than rubies: and all the things thou canst desire are not to be compared unto her.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְקָ֣רָה הִ֭יא מִפְּנִינִ֑ים וְכָל־חֲ֝פָצֶ֗יךָ לֹ֣א יִֽשְׁווּ־בָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She is more precious than rubies: And none of the things thou canst desire are to be compared unto her.
					\item[NIV] She is more precious than rubies; nothing you desire can compare with her.
					\item[NRSV] She is more precious than jewels, and nothing you desire can compare with her.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:16} % *PROVERBS-3-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-16}

			Length of days is in her right hand; and in her left hand riches and honour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֣רֶךְ יָ֭מִים בִּֽימִינָ֑הּ בִּ֝שְׂמֹאולָ֗הּ עֹ֣שֶׁר וְכָבֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Length of days is in her right hand; In her left hand are riches and honor.
					\item[NIV] Long life is in her right hand; in her left hand are riches and honor.
					\item[NRSV] Long life is in her right hand; in her left hand are riches and honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:17} % *PROVERBS-3-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-17}

			Her ways are ways of pleasantness, and all her paths are peace.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דְּרָכֶ֥יהָ דַרְכֵי־נֹ֑עַם וְֽכָל־נְתִ֖יבוֹתֶ֣יהָ שָׁלֽוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Her ways are ways of pleasantness, And all her paths are peace.
					\item[NIV] Her ways are pleasant ways, and all her paths are peace.
					\item[NRSV] Her ways are ways of pleasantness, and all her paths are peace.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:18} % *PROVERBS-3-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-18}

			She is a tree of life to them that lay hold upon her: and happy is every one that retaineth her.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵץ־חַיִּ֣ים הִ֭יא לַמַּחֲזִיקִ֣ים בָּ֑הּ וְֽתֹמְכֶ֥יהָ מְאֻשָּֽׁר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She is a tree of life to them that lay hold upon her: And happy is every one that retaineth her.
					\item[NIV] She is a tree of life to those who take hold of her; those who hold her fast will be blessed.
					\item[NRSV] She is a tree of life to those who lay hold of her; those who hold her fast are called happy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:19} % *PROVERBS-3-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-19}

			The LORD by wisdom hath founded the earth; by understanding hath he established the heavens.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְֽהוָ֗ה בְּחָכְמָ֥ה יָֽסַד־אָ֑רֶץ כּוֹנֵ֥ן שָׁ֝מַ֗יִם בִּתְבוּנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah by wisdom founded the earth; By understanding he established the heavens.
					\item[NIV] By wisdom the LORD laid the earth’s foundations, by understanding he set the heavens in place;
					\item[NRSV] The LORD by wisdom founded the earth; by understanding he established the heavens;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:20} % *PROVERBS-3-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-20}

			By his knowledge the depths are broken up, and the clouds drop down the dew.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭דַעְתּוֹ תְּהוֹמ֣וֹת נִבְקָ֑עוּ וּ֝שְׁחָקִ֗ים יִרְעֲפוּ־טָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By his knowledge the depths were broken up, And the skies drop down the dew.
					\item[NIV] by his knowledge the watery depths were divided, and the clouds let drop the dew.
					\item[NRSV] by his knowledge the deeps broke open, and the clouds drop down the dew.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:21} % *PROVERBS-3-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-21}

			My son, let not them depart from thine eyes: keep sound wisdom and discretion:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי אַל־יָלֻ֣זוּ מֵעֵינֶ֑יךָ נְצֹ֥ר תֻּ֝שִׁיָּ֗ה וּמְזִמָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, let them not depart from thine eyes; Keep sound wisdom and discretion:
					\item[NIV] My son, do not let wisdom and understanding out of your sight, preserve sound judgment and discretion;
					\item[NRSV] My child, do not let these escape from your sight: keep sound wisdom and prudence,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:22} % *PROVERBS-3-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-22}

			So shall they be life unto thy soul, and grace to thy neck.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְיִֽהְי֣וּ חַיִּ֣ים לְנַפְשֶׁ֑ךָ וְ֝חֵ֗ן לְגַרְגְּרֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shall they be life unto thy soul, And grace to thy neck.
					\item[NIV] they will be life for you, an ornament to grace your neck.
					\item[NRSV] and they will be life for your soul and adornment for your neck.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:23} % *PROVERBS-3-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-23}

			Then shalt thou walk in thy way safely, and thy foot shall not stumble.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֤ז תֵּלֵ֣ךְ לָבֶ֣טַח דַּרְכֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝רַגְלְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תִגּֽוֹף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then shalt thou walk in thy way securely, And thy foot shall not stumble.
					\item[NIV] Then you will go on your way in safety, and your foot will not stumble.
					\item[NRSV] Then you will walk on your way securely and your foot will not stumble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:24} % *PROVERBS-3-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-24}

			When thou liest down, thou shalt not be afraid: yea, thou shalt lie down, and thy sleep shall be sweet.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־תִּשְׁכַּ֥ב לֹֽא־תִפְחָ֑ד וְ֝שָׁכַבְתָּ֗ וְֽעָרְבָ֥ה שְׁנָתֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When thou liest down, thou shalt not be afraid: Yea, thou shalt lie down, and thy sleep shall be sweet.
					\item[NIV] When you lie down, you will not be afraid; when you lie down, your sleep will be sweet.
					\item[NRSV] If you sit down, you will not be afraid; when you lie down, your sleep will be sweet.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:25} % *PROVERBS-3-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-25}

			Be not afraid of sudden fear, neither of the desolation of the wicked, when it cometh.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּ֭ירָא מִפַּ֣חַד פִּתְאֹ֑ם וּמִשֹּׁאַ֥ת רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים כִּ֣י תָבֹֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not afraid of sudden fear, Neither of the desolation of the wicked, when it cometh:
					\item[NIV] Have no fear of sudden disaster or of the ruin that overtakes the wicked,
					\item[NRSV] Do not be afraid of sudden panic, or of the storm that strikes the wicked;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:26} % *PROVERBS-3-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-26}

			For the LORD shall be thy confidence, and shall keep thy foot from being taken.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־יְ֭הוָה יִהְיֶ֣ה בְכִסְלֶ֑ךָ וְשָׁמַ֖ר רַגְלְךָ֣ מִלָּֽכֶד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For Jehovah will be thy confidence, And will keep thy foot from being taken.
					\item[NIV] for the LORD will be at your side and will keep your foot from being snared.
					\item[NRSV] for the LORD will be your confidence and will keep your foot from being caught.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:27} % *PROVERBS-3-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-27}

			Withhold not good from them to whom it is due, when it is in the power of thine hand to do it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּמְנַע־ט֥וֹב מִבְּעָלָ֑יו בִּהְי֨וֹת לְאֵ֖ל יָדְךָ֣ לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Withhold not good from them to whom it is due, When it is in the power of thy hand to do it.
					\item[NIV] Do not withhold good from those to whom it is due, when it is in your power to act.
					\item[NRSV] Do not withhold good from those to whom it is due, when it is in your power to do it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:28} % *PROVERBS-3-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-28}

			Say not unto thy neighbour, Go, and come again, and to morrow I will give; when thou hast it by thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֹּ֘אמַ֤ר לְרֵֽעֲךָ֨ ׀ לֵ֣ךְ וָ֭שׁוּב וּמָחָ֥ר אֶתֵּ֗ן וְיֵ֣שׁ אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Say not unto thy neighbor, Go, and come again, And to-morrow I will give; When thou hast it by thee.
					\item[NIV] Do not say to your neighbor, “Come back tomorrow and I’ll give it to you"— when you already have it with you.
					\item[NRSV] Do not say to your neighbor, “Go, and come again, tomorrow I will give it”—when you have it with you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:29} % *PROVERBS-3-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-29}

			Devise not evil against thy neighbour, seeing he dwelleth securely by thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּחֲרֹ֣שׁ עַל־רֵעֲךָ֣ רָעָ֑ה וְהֽוּא־יוֹשֵׁ֖ב לָבֶ֣טַח אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Devise not evil against thy neighbor, Seeing he dwelleth securely by thee.
					\item[NIV] Do not plot harm against your neighbor, who lives trustfully near you.
					\item[NRSV] Do not plan harm against your neighbor who lives trustingly beside you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:30} % *PROVERBS-3-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-30}

			Strive not with a man without cause, if he have done thee no harm.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תָּרִ֣יב עִם־אָדָ֣ם חִנָּ֑ם אִם־לֹ֖א גְמָלְךָ֣ רָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Strive not with a man without cause, If he have done thee no harm.
					\item[NIV] Do not accuse anyone for no reason— when they have done you no harm.
					\item[NRSV] Do not quarrel with anyone without cause, when no harm has been done to you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:31} % *PROVERBS-3-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-31}

			Envy thou not the oppressor, and choose none of his ways.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תְּ֭קַנֵּא בְּאִ֣ישׁ חָמָ֑ס וְאַל־תִּ֝בְחַ֗ר בְּכָל־דְּרָכָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Envy thou not the man of violence, And choose none of his ways.
					\item[NIV] Do not envy the violent or choose any of their ways.
					\item[NRSV] Do not envy the violent and do not choose any of their ways;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:32} % *PROVERBS-3-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-32}

			For the froward is abomination to the LORD: but his secret is with the righteous.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י תוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהוָ֣ה נָל֑וֹז וְֽאֶת־יְשָׁרִ֥ים סוֹדֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the perverse is an abomination to Jehovah; But his friendship is with the upright.
					\item[NIV] For the LORD detests the perverse but takes the upright into his confidence.
					\item[NRSV] for the perverse are an abomination to the LORD, but the upright are in his confidence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:33} % *PROVERBS-3-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-33}

			The curse of the LORD is in the house of the wicked: but he blesseth the habitation of the just.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְאֵרַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה בְּבֵ֣ית רָשָׁ֑ע וּנְוֵ֖ה צַדִּיקִ֣ים יְבָרֵֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The curse of Jehovah is in the house of the wicked; But he blesseth the habitation of the righteous.
					\item[NIV] The LORD’s curse is on the house of the wicked, but he blesses the home of the righteous.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD’s curse is on the house of the wicked, but he blesses the abode of the righteous.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:34} % *PROVERBS-3-34
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-34}

			Surely he scorneth the scorners: but he giveth grace unto the lowly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־לַלֵּצִ֥ים הֽוּא־יָלִ֑יץ וְ֝לַעֲנָוִ֗ים יִתֶּן־חֵֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Surely he scoffeth at the scoffers; But he giveth grace unto the lowly.
					\item[NIV] He mocks proud mockers but shows favor to the humble and oppressed.
					\item[NRSV] Toward the scorners he is scornful, but to the humble he shows favor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 3:35} % *PROVERBS-3-35
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-3-35}

			The wise shall inherit glory: but shame shall be the promotion of fools.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּ֭בוֹד חֲכָמִ֣ים יִנְחָ֑לוּ וּ֝כְסִילִ֗ים מֵרִ֥ים קָלֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wise shall inherit glory; But shame shall be the promotion of fools.
					\item[NIV] The wise inherit honor, but fools get only shame.
					\item[NRSV] The wise will inherit honor, but stubborn fools, disgrace.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 4} % *PROVERBS-4
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-4}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:1} % *PROVERBS-4-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-1}

			Hear, ye children, the instruction of a father, and attend to know understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׁמְע֣וּ בָ֭נִים מ֣וּסַר אָ֑ב וְ֝הַקְשִׁ֗יבוּ לָדַ֥עַת בִּינָֽה׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear, my sons, the instruction of a father, And attend to know understanding:
					\item[NIV] Listen, my sons, to a father’s instruction; pay attention and gain understanding.
					\item[NRSV] Listen, children, to a father’s instruction, and be attentive, that you may gain insight;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:2} % *PROVERBS-4-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-2}

			For I give you good doctrine, forsake ye not my law.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י לֶ֣קַח ט֭וֹב נָתַ֣תִּי לָכֶ֑ם תּֽ֝וֹרָתִ֗י אַֽל־תַּעֲזֹֽבוּ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For I give you good doctrine; Forsake ye not my law.
					\item[NIV] I give you sound learning, so do not forsake my teaching.
					\item[NRSV] for I give you good precepts: do not forsake my teaching.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:3} % *PROVERBS-4-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-3}

			For I was my father’s son, tender and only beloved in the sight of my mother.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי־בֵ֭ן הָיִ֣יתִי לְאָבִ֑י רַ֥ךְ וְ֝יָחִ֗יד לִפְנֵ֥י אִמִּֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For I was a son unto my father, Tender and only beloved in the sight of my mother.
					\item[NIV] For I too was a son to my father, still tender, and cherished by my mother.
					\item[NRSV] When I was a son with my father, tender, and my mother’s favorite,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
