		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:4} % *PROVERBS-4-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-4}

			He taught me also, and said unto me, Let thine heart retain my words: keep my commandments, and live.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַיֹּרֵ֗נִי וַיֹּ֥אמֶר לִ֗י יִֽתְמָךְ־דְּבָרַ֥י לִבֶּ֑ךָ שְׁמֹ֖ר מִצְוֺתַ֣י וֶֽחְיֵֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And he taught me, and said unto me: Let thy heart retain my words; Keep my commandments, and live;
					\item[NIV] Then he taught me, and he said to me, “Take hold of my words with all your heart; keep my commands, and you will live.
					\item[NRSV] he taught me, and said to me, “Let your heart hold fast my words; keep my commandments, and live.

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:5} % *PROVERBS-4-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-5}

			Get wisdom, get understanding: forget it not; neither decline from the words of my mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קְנֵ֣ה חָ֭כְמָה קְנֵ֣ה בִינָ֑ה אַל־תִּשְׁכַּ֥ח וְאַל־תֵּ֝֗ט מֵֽאִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Get wisdom, get understanding; Forget not, neither decline from the words of my mouth;
					\item[NIV] Get wisdom, get understanding; do not forget my words or turn away from them.
					\item[NRSV] Get wisdom; get insight: do not forget, nor turn away from the words of my mouth.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-6}

			Forsake her not, and she shall preserve thee: love her, and she shall keep thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּעַזְבֶ֥הָ וְתִשְׁמְרֶ֑ךָּ אֱהָבֶ֥הָ וְתִצְּרֶֽךָּ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Forsake her not, and she will preserve thee; Love her, and she will keep thee.
					\item[NIV] Do not forsake wisdom, and she will protect you; love her, and she will watch over you.
					\item[NRSV] Do not forsake her, and she will keep you; love her, and she will guard you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:7} % *PROVERBS-4-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-7}

			Wisdom is the principal thing; therefore get wisdom: and with all thy getting get understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רֵאשִׁ֣ית חָ֭כְמָה קְנֵ֣ה חָכְמָ֑ה וּבְכָל־קִ֝נְיָנְךָ֗ קְנֵ֣ה בִינָֽה׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom is the principal thing; therefore get wisdom; Yea, with all thy getting get understanding.
					\item[NIV] The beginning of wisdom is this: Get wisdom. Though it cost all you have, get understanding.
					\item[NRSV] The beginning of wisdom is this: Get wisdom, and whatever else you get, get insight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:8} % *PROVERBS-4-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-8}

			Exalt her, and she shall promote thee: she shall bring thee to honour, when thou dost embrace her.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{סַלְסְלֶ֥הָ וּֽתְרוֹמְמֶ֑ךָּ תְּ֝כַבֵּ֗דְךָ כִּ֣י תְחַבְּקֶֽנָּה׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Exalt her, and she will promote thee; She will bring thee to honor, when thou dost embrace her.
					\item[NIV] Cherish her, and she will exalt you; embrace her, and she will honor you.
					\item[NRSV] Prize her highly, and she will exalt you; she will honor you if you embrace her.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:9} % *PROVERBS-4-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-9}

			She shall give to thine head an ornament of grace: a crown of glory shall she deliver to thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תִּתֵּ֣ן לְ֭רֹאשְׁךָ לִוְיַת־חֵ֑ן עֲטֶ֖רֶת תִּפְאֶ֣רֶת תְּמַגְּנֶֽךָּ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She will give to thy head a chaplet of grace; A crown of beauty will she deliver to thee.
					\item[NIV] She will give you a garland to grace your head and present you with a glorious crown.”
					\item[NRSV] She will place on your head a fair garland; she will bestow on you a beautiful crown.”

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-10}

			Hear, O my son, and receive my sayings; and the years of thy life shall be many.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁמַ֣ע בְּ֭נִי וְקַ֣ח אֲמָרָ֑י וְיִרְבּ֥וּ לְ֝ךָ֗ שְׁנ֣וֹת חַיִּֽים׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear, O my son, and receive my sayings; And the years of thy life shall be many.
					\item[NIV] Listen, my son, accept what I say, and the years of your life will be many.
					\item[NRSV] Hear, my child, and accept my words, that the years of your life may be many.

		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:11} % *PROVERBS-4-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-11}

			I have taught thee in the way of wisdom; I have led thee in right paths.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ חָ֭כְמָה הֹרֵתִ֑יךָ הִ֝דְרַכְתִּ֗יךָ בְּמַעְגְּלֵי־יֹֽשֶׁר׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I have taught thee in the way of wisdom; I have led thee in paths of uprightness.
					\item[NIV] I instruct you in the way of wisdom and lead you along straight paths.
					\item[NRSV] I have taught you the way of wisdom; I have led you in the paths of uprightness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:12} % *PROVERBS-4-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-12}

			When thou goest, thy steps shall not be straitened; and when thou runnest, thou shalt not stumble.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּֽ֭לֶכְתְּךָ לֹא־יֵצַ֣ר צַעֲדֶ֑ךָ וְאִם־תָּ֝ר֗וּץ לֹ֣א תִכָּשֵֽׁל׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When thou goest, thy steps shall not be straitened; And if thou runnest, thou shalt not stumble.
					\item[NIV] When you walk, your steps will not be hampered; when you run, you will not stumble.
					\item[NRSV] When you walk, your step will not be hampered; and if you run, you will not stumble.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:13} % *PROVERBS-4-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-13}

			Take fast hold of instruction; let her not go: keep her; for she is thy life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַחֲזֵ֣ק בַּמּוּסָ֣ר אַל־תֶּ֑רֶף נִ֝צְּרֶ֗הָ כִּי־הִ֥יא חַיֶּֽיךָ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Take fast hold of instruction; let her not go: Keep her; for she is thy life.
					\item[NIV] Hold on to instruction, do not let it go; guard it well, for it is your life.
					\item[NRSV] Keep hold of instruction; do not let go; guard her, for she is your life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:14} % *PROVERBS-4-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-14}

			Enter not into the path of the wicked, and go not in the way of evil men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֹ֣רַח רְ֭שָׁעִים אַל־תָּבֹ֑א וְאַל־תְּ֝אַשֵּׁ֗ר בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ רָעִֽים׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Enter not into the path of the wicked, And walk not in the way of evil men.
					\item[NIV] Do not set foot on the path of the wicked or walk in the way of evildoers.
					\item[NRSV] Do not enter the path of the wicked, and do not walk in the way of evildoers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:15} % *PROVERBS-4-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-15}

			Avoid it, pass not by it, turn from it, and pass away.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פְּרָעֵ֥הוּ אַל־תַּעֲבָר־בּ֑וֹ שְׂטֵ֖ה מֵעָלָ֣יו וַעֲבֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Avoid it, pass not by it; Turn from it, and pass on.
					\item[NIV] Avoid it, do not travel on it; turn from it and go on your way.
					\item[NRSV] Avoid it; do not go on it; turn away from it and pass on.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:16} % *PROVERBS-4-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-16}

			For they sleep not, except they have done mischief; and their sleep is taken away, unless they cause some to fall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י לֹ֣א יִֽ֭שְׁנוּ אִם־לֹ֣א יָרֵ֑עוּ וְֽנִגְזְלָ֥ה שְׁ֝נָתָ֗ם אִם־לֹ֥א יַכְשִֽׁילוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For they sleep not, except they do evil; And their sleep is taken away, unless they cause some to fall.
					\item[NIV] For they cannot rest until they do evil; they are robbed of sleep till they make someone stumble.
					\item[NRSV] For they cannot sleep unless they have done wrong; they are robbed of sleep unless they have made someone stumble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:17} % *PROVERBS-4-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-17}

			For they eat the bread of wickedness, and drink the wine of violence.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י לָ֭חֲמוּ לֶ֣חֶם רֶ֑שַׁע וְיֵ֖ין חֲמָסִ֣ים יִשְׁתּֽוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For they eat the bread of wickedness, And drink the wine of violence.
					\item[NIV] They eat the bread of wickedness and drink the wine of violence.
					\item[NRSV] For they eat the bread of wickedness and drink the wine of violence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:18} % *PROVERBS-4-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-18}

			But the path of the just is as the shining light, that shineth more and more unto the perfect day.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְאֹ֣רַח צַ֭דִּיקִים כְּא֣וֹר נֹ֑גַהּ הוֹלֵ֥ךְ וָ֝א֗וֹר עַד־נְכ֥וֹן הַיּֽוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But the path of the righteous is as the dawning light, That shineth more and more unto the perfect day.
					\item[NIV] The path of the righteous is like the morning sun, shining ever brighter till the full light of day.
					\item[NRSV] But the path of the righteous is like the light of dawn, which shines brighter and brighter until full day.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:19} % *PROVERBS-4-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-19}

			The way of the wicked is as darkness: they know not at what they stumble.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דֶּ֣רֶךְ רְ֭שָׁעִים כָּֽאֲפֵלָ֑ה לֹ֥א יָ֝דְע֗וּ בַּמֶּ֥ה יִכָּשֵֽׁלוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The way of the wicked is as darkness: They know not at what they stumble.
					\item[NIV] But the way of the wicked is like deep darkness; they do not know what makes them stumble.
					\item[NRSV] The way of the wicked is like deep darkness; they do not know what they stumble over.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:20} % *PROVERBS-4-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-20}

			My son, attend to my words; incline thine ear unto my sayings.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי לִדְבָרַ֣י הַקְשִׁ֑יבָה לַ֝אֲמָרַ֗י הַט־אָזְנֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, attend to my words; Incline thine ear unto my sayings.
					\item[NIV] My son, pay attention to what I say; turn your ear to my words.
					\item[NRSV] My child, be attentive to my words; incline your ear to my sayings.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:21} % *PROVERBS-4-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-21}

			Let them not depart from thine eyes; keep them in the midst of thine heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־יַלִּ֥יזוּ מֵעֵינֶ֑יךָ שָׁ֝מְרֵ֗ם בְּת֣וֹךְ לְבָבֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let them not depart from thine eyes; Keep them in the midst of thy heart.
					\item[NIV] Do not let them out of your sight, keep them within your heart;
					\item[NRSV] Do not let them escape from your sight; keep them within your heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:22} % *PROVERBS-4-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-22}

			For they are life unto those that find them, and health to all their flesh.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־חַיִּ֣ים הֵ֭ם לְמֹצְאֵיהֶ֑ם וּֽלְכָל־בְּשָׂר֥וֹ מַרְפֵּֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For they are life unto those that find them, And health to all their flesh.
					\item[NIV] for they are life to those who find them and health to one’s whole body.
					\item[NRSV] For they are life to those who find them, and healing to all their flesh.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:23} % *PROVERBS-4-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-23}

			Keep thy heart with all diligence; for out of it are the issues of life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִֽכָּל־מִ֭שְׁמָר נְצֹ֣ר לִבֶּ֑ךָ כִּֽי־מִ֝מֶּ֗נּוּ תּוֹצְא֥וֹת חַיִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Keep thy heart with all diligence; For out of it are the issues of life.
					\item[NIV] Above all else, guard your heart, for everything you do flows from it.
					\item[NRSV] Keep your heart with all vigilance, for from it flow the springs of life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:24} % *PROVERBS-4-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-24}

			Put away from thee a froward mouth, and perverse lips put far from thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָסֵ֣ר מִ֭מְּךָ עִקְּשׁ֣וּת פֶּ֑ה וּלְז֥וּת שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם הַרְחֵ֥ק מִמֶּֽךָּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Put away from thee a wayward mouth, And perverse lips put far from thee.
					\item[NIV] Keep your mouth free of perversity; keep corrupt talk far from your lips.
					\item[NRSV] Put away from you crooked speech, and put devious talk far from you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:25} % *PROVERBS-4-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-25}

			Let thine eyes look right on, and let thine eyelids look straight before thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֭ינֶיךָ לְנֹ֣כַח יַבִּ֑יטוּ וְ֝עַפְעַפֶּ֗יךָ יַיְשִׁ֥רוּ נֶגְדֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thine eyes look right on, And let thine eyelids look straight before thee.
					\item[NIV] Let your eyes look straight ahead; fix your gaze directly before you.
					\item[NRSV] Let your eyes look directly forward, and your gaze be straight before you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:26} % *PROVERBS-4-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-26}

			Ponder the path of thy feet, and let all thy ways be established.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פַּ֭לֵּס מַעְגַּ֣ל רַגְלֶ֑ךָ וְֽכָל־דְּרָכֶ֥יךָ יִכֹּֽנוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Make level the path of thy feet, And let all thy ways be established.
					\item[NIV] Give careful thought to the paths for your feet and be steadfast in all your ways.
					\item[NRSV] Keep straight the path of your feet, and all your ways will be sure.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 4:27} % *PROVERBS-4-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-4-27}

			Turn not to the right hand nor to the left: remove thy foot from evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַֽל־תֵּט־יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹ֑אול הָסֵ֖ר רַגְלְךָ֣ מֵרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Turn not to the right hand nor to the left: Remove thy foot from evil.
					\item[NIV] Do not turn to the right or the left; keep your foot from evil.
					\item[NRSV] Do not swerve to the right or to the left; turn your foot away from evil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 5} % *PROVERBS-5
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-5}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:1} % *PROVERBS-5-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-1}

			My son, attend unto my wisdom, and bow thine ear to my understanding:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי לְחָכְמָתִ֣י הַקְשִׁ֑יבָה לִ֝תְבוּנָתִ֗י הַט־אָזְנֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, attend unto my wisdom; Incline thine ear to my understanding:
					\item[NIV] My son, pay attention to my wisdom, turn your ear to my words of insight,
					\item[NRSV] My child, be attentive to my wisdom; incline your ear to my understanding,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:2} % *PROVERBS-5-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-2}

			That thou mayest regard discretion, and that thy lips may keep knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִשְׁמֹ֥ר מְזִמּ֑וֹת וְ֝דַ֗עַת שְׂפָתֶ֥יךָ יִנְצֹֽרוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That thou mayest preserve discretion, And that thy lips may keep knowledge.
					\item[NIV] that you may maintain discretion and your lips may preserve knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] so that you may hold on to prudence, and your lips may guard knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:3} % *PROVERBS-5-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-3}

			For the lips of a strange woman drop as an honeycomb, and her mouth is smoother than oil:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י נֹ֣פֶת תִּ֭טֹּפְנָה שִׂפְתֵ֣י זָרָ֑ה וְחָלָ֖ק מִשֶּׁ֣מֶן חִכָּֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the lips of a strange woman drop honey, And her mouth is smoother than oil:
					\item[NIV] For the lips of the adulterous woman drip honey, and her speech is smoother than oil;
					\item[NRSV] For the lips of a loose woman drip honey, and her speech is smoother than oil;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:4} % *PROVERBS-5-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-4}

			But her end is bitter as wormwood, sharp as a two-edged sword.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭אַחֲרִיתָהּ מָרָ֣ה כַֽלַּעֲנָ֑ה חַ֝דָּ֗ה כְּחֶ֣רֶב פִּיּֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But in the end she is bitter as wormwood, Sharp as a two-edged sword.
					\item[NIV] but in the end she is bitter as gall, sharp as a double-edged sword.
					\item[NRSV] but in the end she is bitter as wormwood, sharp as a two-edged sword.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:5} % *PROVERBS-5-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-5}

			Her feet go down to death; her steps take hold on hell.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַ֭גְלֶיהָ יֹרְד֣וֹת מָ֑וֶת שְׁ֝א֗וֹל צְעָדֶ֥יהָ יִתְמֹֽכוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Her feet go down to death; Her steps take hold on Sheol;
					\item[NIV] Her feet go down to death; her steps lead straight to the grave.
					\item[NRSV] Her feet go down to death; her steps follow the path to Sheol.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:6} % *PROVERBS-5-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-6}

			Lest thou shouldest ponder the path of life, her ways are moveable, that thou canst not know them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֣רַח חַ֭יִּים פֶּן־תְּפַלֵּ֑ס נָע֥וּ מַ֝עְגְּלֹתֶ֗יהָ לֹ֣א תֵדָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So that she findeth not the level path of life: Her ways are unstable, and she knoweth it not.
					\item[NIV] She gives no thought to the way of life; her paths wander aimlessly, but she does not know it.
					\item[NRSV] She does not keep straight to the path of life; her ways wander, and she does not know it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:7} % *PROVERBS-5-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-7}

			Hear me now therefore, O ye children, and depart not from the words of my mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֣ה בָ֭נִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִ֑י וְאַל־תָּ֝ס֗וּרוּ מֵאִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now therefore, my sons, hearken unto me, And depart not from the words of my mouth.
					\item[NIV] Now then, my sons, listen to me; do not turn aside from what I say.
					\item[NRSV] And now, my child, listen to me, and do not depart from the words of my mouth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:8} % *PROVERBS-5-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-8}

			Remove thy way far from her, and come not nigh the door of her house:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַרְחֵ֣ק מֵעָלֶ֣יהָ דַרְכֶּ֑ךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝קְרַ֗ב אֶל־פֶּ֥תַח בֵּיתָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Remove thy way far from her, And come not nigh the door of her house;
					\item[NIV] Keep to a path far from her, do not go near the door of her house,
					\item[NRSV] Keep your way far from her, and do not go near the door of her house;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:9} % *PROVERBS-5-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-9}

			Lest thou give thine honour unto others, and thy years unto the cruel:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּן־תִּתֵּ֣ן לַאֲחֵרִ֣ים הוֹדֶ֑ךָ וּ֝שְׁנֹתֶ֗יךָ לְאַכְזָרִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lest thou give thine honor unto others, And thy years unto the cruel;
					\item[NIV] lest you lose your honor to others and your dignity to one who is cruel,
					\item[NRSV] or you will give your honor to others, and your years to the merciless,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:10} % *PROVERBS-5-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-10}

			Lest strangers be filled with thy wealth; and thy labours be in the house of a stranger;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּֽן־יִשְׂבְּע֣וּ זָרִ֣ים כֹּחֶ֑ךָ וַ֝עֲצָבֶ֗יךָ בְּבֵ֣ית נָכְרִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lest strangers be filled with thy strength, And thy labors be in the house of an alien,
					\item[NIV] lest strangers feast on your wealth and your toil enrich the house of another.
					\item[NRSV] and strangers will take their fill of your wealth, and your labors will go to the house of an alien;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:11} % *PROVERBS-5-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-11}

			And thou mourn at the last, when thy flesh and thy body are consumed,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְנָהַמְתָּ֥ בְאַחֲרִיתֶ֑ךָ בִּכְל֥וֹת בְּ֝שָׂרְךָ֗ וּשְׁאֵרֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And thou mourn at thy latter end, When thy flesh and thy body are consumed,
					\item[NIV] At the end of your life you will groan, when your flesh and body are spent.
					\item[NRSV] and at the end of your life you will groan, when your flesh and body are consumed,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:12} % *PROVERBS-5-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-12}

			And say, How have I hated instruction, and my heart despised reproof;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽאָמַרְתָּ֗ אֵ֭יךְ שָׂנֵ֣אתִי מוּסָ֑ר וְ֝תוֹכַ֗חַת נָאַ֥ץ לִבִּֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And say, How have I hated instruction, And my heart despised reproof;
					\item[NIV] You will say, “How I hated discipline! How my heart spurned correction!
					\item[NRSV] and you say, “Oh, how I hated discipline, and my heart despised reproof!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:13} % *PROVERBS-5-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-13}

			And have not obeyed the voice of my teachers, nor inclined mine ear to them that instructed me!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽלֹא־שָׁ֭מַעְתִּי בְּק֣וֹל מוֹרָ֑י וְ֝לִֽמְלַמְּדַ֗י לֹא־הִטִּ֥יתִי אָזְנִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Neither have I obeyed the voice of my teachers, Nor inclined mine ear to them that instructed me!
					\item[NIV] I would not obey my teachers or turn my ear to my instructors.
					\item[NRSV] I did not listen to the voice of my teachers or incline my ear to my instructors.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:14} % *PROVERBS-5-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-14}

			I was almost in all evil in the midst of the congregation and assembly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֭מְעַט הָיִ֣יתִי בְכָל־רָ֑ע בְּת֖וֹךְ קָהָ֣ל וְעֵדָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I was well-nigh in all evil In the midst of the assembly and congregation.
					\item[NIV] And I was soon in serious trouble in the assembly of God’s people.”
					\item[NRSV] Now I am at the point of utter ruin in the public assembly.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:15} % *PROVERBS-5-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-15}

			Drink waters out of thine own cistern, and running waters out of thine own well.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁתֵה־מַ֥יִם מִבּוֹרֶ֑ךָ וְ֝נֹזְלִ֗ים מִתּ֥וֹךְ בְּאֵרֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Drink waters out of thine own cistern, And running waters out of thine own well.
					\item[NIV] Drink water from your own cistern, running water from your own well.
					\item[NRSV] Drink water from your own cistern, flowing water from your own well.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:16} % *PROVERBS-5-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-16}

			Let thy fountains be dispersed abroad, and rivers of waters in the streets.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָפ֣וּצוּ מַעְיְנֹתֶ֣יךָ ח֑וּצָה בָּ֝רְחֹב֗וֹת פַּלְגֵי־מָֽיִם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Should thy springs be dispersed abroad, And streams of water in the streets?
					\item[NIV] Should your springs overflow in the streets, your streams of water in the public squares?
					\item[NRSV] Should your springs be scattered abroad, streams of water in the streets?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:17} % *PROVERBS-5-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-17}

			Let them be only thine own, and not strangers’ with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִֽהְיוּ־לְךָ֥ לְבַדֶּ֑ךָ וְאֵ֖ין לְזָרִ֣ים אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let them be for thyself alone, And not for strangers with thee.
					\item[NIV] Let them be yours alone, never to be shared with strangers.
					\item[NRSV] Let them be for yourself alone, and not for sharing with strangers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:18} % *PROVERBS-5-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-18}

			Let thy fountain be blessed: and rejoice with the wife of thy youth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְהִֽי־מְקוֹרְךָ֥ בָר֑וּךְ וּ֝שְׂמַ֗ח מֵאֵ֥שֶׁת נְעוּרֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thy fountain be blessed; And rejoice in the wife of thy youth.
					\item[NIV] May your fountain be blessed, and may you rejoice in the wife of your youth.
					\item[NRSV] Let your fountain be blessed, and rejoice in the wife of your youth,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:19} % *PROVERBS-5-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-19}

			Let her be as the loving hind and pleasant roe; let her breasts satisfy thee at all times; and be thou ravished always with her love.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַיֶּ֥לֶת אֲהָבִ֗ים וְֽיַעֲלַ֫ת־חֵ֥ן דַּ֭דֶּיהָ יְרַוֻּ֣ךָ בְכָל־עֵ֑ת בְּ֝אַהֲבָתָ֗הּ תִּשְׁגֶּ֥ה תָמִֽיד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a loving hind and a pleasant doe, Let her breasts satisfy thee at all times; And be thou ravished always with her love.
					\item[NIV] A loving doe, a graceful deer— may her breasts satisfy you always, may you ever be intoxicated with her love.
					\item[NRSV] a lovely deer, a graceful doe. May her breasts satisfy you at all times; may you be intoxicated always by her love.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:20} % *PROVERBS-5-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-20}

			And why wilt thou, my son, be ravished with a strange woman, and embrace the bosom of a stranger?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְלָ֤מָּה תִשְׁגֶּ֣ה בְנִ֣י בְזָרָ֑ה וּ֝תְחַבֵּ֗ק חֵ֣ק נָכְרִיָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For why shouldest thou, my son, be ravished with a strange woman, And embrace the bosom of a foreigner?
					\item[NIV] Why, my son, be intoxicated with another man’s wife? Why embrace the bosom of a wayward woman?
					\item[NRSV] Why should you be intoxicated, my son, by another woman and embrace the bosom of an adulteress?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:21} % *PROVERBS-5-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-21}

			For the ways of man are before the eyes of the LORD, and he pondereth all his goings.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י נֹ֨כַח ׀ עֵינֵ֣י יְ֭הוָה דַּרְכֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְֽכָל־מַעְגְּלֹתָ֥יו מְפַלֵּֽס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the ways of man are before the eyes of Jehovah; And he maketh level all his paths.
					\item[NIV] For your ways are in full view of the LORD, and he examines all your paths.
					\item[NRSV] For human ways are under the eyes of the LORD, and he examines all their paths.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:22} % *PROVERBS-5-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-22}

			His own iniquities shall take the wicked himself, and he shall be holden with the cords of his sins.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַֽווֹנוֹתָ֗יו יִלְכְּדֻנ֥וֹ אֶת־הָרָשָׁ֑ע וּבְחַבְלֵ֥י חַ֝טָּאת֗וֹ יִתָּמֵֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] His own iniquities shall take the wicked, And he shall be holden with the cords of his sin.
					\item[NIV] The evil deeds of the wicked ensnare them; the cords of their sins hold them fast.
					\item[NRSV] The iniquities of the wicked ensnare them, and they are caught in the toils of their sin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 5:23} % *PROVERBS-5-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-5-23}

			He shall die without instruction; and in the greatness of his folly he shall go astray.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֗וּא יָ֭מוּת בְּאֵ֣ין מוּסָ֑ר וּבְרֹ֖ב אִוַּלְתּ֣וֹ יִשְׁגֶּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He shall die for lack of instruction; And in the greatness of his folly he shall go astray.
					\item[NIV] For lack of discipline they will die, led astray by their own great folly.
					\item[NRSV] They die for lack of discipline, and because of their great folly they are lost.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 6} % *PROVERBS-6
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-6}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:1} % *PROVERBS-6-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-1}

			My son, if thou be surety for thy friend, if thou hast stricken thy hand with a stranger,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי אִם־עָרַ֣בְתָּ לְרֵעֶ֑ךָ תָּקַ֖עְתָּ לַזָּ֣ר כַּפֶּֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, if thou art become surety for thy neighbor, If thou hast stricken thy hands for a stranger;
					\item[NIV] My son, if you have put up security for your neighbor, if you have shaken hands in pledge for a stranger,
					\item[NRSV] My child, if you have given your pledge to your neighbor, if you have bound yourself to another,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:2} % *PROVERBS-6-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-2}

			Thou art snared with the words of thy mouth, thou art taken with the words of thy mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נוֹקַ֥שְׁתָּ בְאִמְרֵי־פִ֑יךָ נִ֝לְכַּ֗דְתָּ בְּאִמְרֵי־פִֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou art snared with the words of thy mouth, Thou art taken with the words of thy mouth.
					\item[NIV] you have been trapped by what you said, ensnared by the words of your mouth.
					\item[NRSV] you are snared by the utterance of your lips, caught by the words of your mouth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:3} % *PROVERBS-6-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-3}

			Do this now, my son, and deliver thyself, when thou art come into the hand of thy friend; go, humble thyself, and make sure thy friend.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲשֵׂ֨ה זֹ֥את אֵפ֪וֹא ׀ בְּנִ֡י וְֽהִנָּצֵ֗ל כִּ֘י בָ֤אתָ בְכַף־רֵעֶ֑ךָ לֵ֥ךְ הִ֝תְרַפֵּ֗ס וּרְהַ֥ב רֵעֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Do this now, my son, and deliver thyself, Seeing thou art come into the hand of thy neighbor: Go, humble thyself, and importune thy neighbor;
					\item[NIV] So do this, my son, to free yourself, since you have fallen into your neighbor’s hands: Go—to the point of exhaustion— and give your neighbor no rest!
					\item[NRSV] So do this, my child, and save yourself, for you have come into your neighbor’s power: go, hurry, and plead with your neighbor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:4} % *PROVERBS-6-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-4}

			Give not sleep to thine eyes, nor slumber to thine eyelids.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּתֵּ֣ן שֵׁנָ֣ה לְעֵינֶ֑יךָ וּ֝תְנוּמָ֗ה לְעַפְעַפֶּֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Give not sleep to thine eyes, Nor slumber to thine eyelids;
					\item[NIV] Allow no sleep to your eyes, no slumber to your eyelids.
					\item[NRSV] Give your eyes no sleep and your eyelids no slumber;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:5} % *PROVERBS-6-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-5}

			Deliver thyself as a roe from the hand of the hunter, and as a bird from the hand of the fowler.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הִ֭נָּצֵל כִּצְבִ֣י מִיָּ֑ד וּ֝כְצִפּ֗וֹר מִיַּ֥ד יָקֽוּשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Deliver thyself as a roe from the hand of the hunter, And as a bird from the hand of the fowler.
					\item[NIV] Free yourself, like a gazelle from the hand of the hunter, like a bird from the snare of the fowler.
					\item[NRSV] save yourself like a gazelle from the hunter, like a bird from the hand of the fowler.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:6} % *PROVERBS-6-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-6}

			Go to the ant, thou sluggard; consider her ways, and be wise:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵֽךְ־אֶל־נְמָלָ֥ה עָצֵ֑ל רְאֵ֖ה דְרָכֶ֣יהָ וַחֲכָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Go to the ant, thou sluggard; Consider her ways, and be wise:
					\item[NIV] Go to the ant, you sluggard; consider its ways and be wise!
					\item[NRSV] Go to the ant, you lazybones; consider its ways, and be wise.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:7} % *PROVERBS-6-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-7}

			Which having no guide, overseer, or ruler,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲשֶׁ֖ר אֵֽין־לָ֥הּ קָצִ֗ין שֹׁטֵ֥ר וּמֹשֵֽׁל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Which having no chief, Overseer, or ruler,
					\item[NIV] It has no commander, no overseer or ruler,
					\item[NRSV] Without having any chief or officer or ruler,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:8} % *PROVERBS-6-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-8}

			Provideth her meat in the summer, and gathereth her food in the harvest.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תָּכִ֣ין בַּקַּ֣יִץ לַחְמָ֑הּ אָגְרָ֥ה בַ֝קָּצִ֗יר מַאֲכָלָֽהּ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Provideth her bread in the summer, And gathereth her food in the harvest.
					\item[NIV] yet it stores its provisions in summer and gathers its food at harvest.
					\item[NRSV] it prepares its food in summer, and gathers its sustenance in harvest.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-9}

			How long wilt thou sleep, O sluggard? when wilt thou arise out of thy sleep?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַד־מָתַ֖י עָצֵ֥ל ׀ תִּשְׁכָּ֑ב מָ֝תַ֗י תָּק֥וּם מִשְּׁנָתֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How long wilt thou sleep, O sluggard? When wilt thou arise out of thy sleep?
					\item[NIV] How long will you lie there, you sluggard? When will you get up from your sleep?
					\item[NRSV] How long will you lie there, O lazybones? When will you rise from your sleep?

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-10}

			Yet a little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to sleep:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְעַ֣ט שֵׁ֭נוֹת מְעַ֣ט תְּנוּמ֑וֹת מְעַ֓ט ׀ חִבֻּ֖ק יָדַ֣יִם לִשְׁכָּֽב׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to rest—
					\item[NRSV] A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to rest,

		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:11} % *PROVERBS-6-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-11}

			So shall thy poverty come as one that travelleth, and thy want as an armed man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבָֽא־כִמְהַלֵּ֥ךְ רֵאשֶׁ֑ךָ וּ֝מַחְסֹֽרְךָ֗ כְּאִ֣ישׁ מָגֵֽן׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shall thy poverty come as a robber, And thy want as an armed man.
					\item[NIV] and poverty will come on you like a thief and scarcity like an armed man.
					\item[NRSV] and poverty will come upon you like a robber, and want, like an armed warrior.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:12} % *PROVERBS-6-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-12}

			A naughty person, a wicked man, walketh with a froward mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָדָ֣ם בְּ֭לִיַּעַל אִ֣ישׁ אָ֑וֶן ה֝וֹלֵ֗ךְ עִקְּשׁ֥וּת פֶּֽה׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A worthless person, a man of iniquity, Is he that walketh with a perverse mouth;
					\item[NIV] A troublemaker and a villain, who goes about with a corrupt mouth,
					\item[NRSV] A scoundrel and a villain goes around with crooked speech,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-13}

			He winketh with his eyes, he speaketh with his feet, he teacheth with his fingers;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קֹרֵ֣ץ בְּ֭עֵינָו מֹלֵ֣ל בְּרַגְלָ֑ו מֹ֝רֶ֗ה בְּאֶצְבְּעֹתָֽיו׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] who winks maliciously with his eye, signals with his feet and motions with his fingers,
					\item[NRSV] winking the eyes, shuffling the feet, pointing the fingers,

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-14}

			Frowardness is in his heart, he deviseth mischief continually; he soweth discord.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּֽהְפֻּכ֨וֹת ׀ בְּלִבּ֗וֹ חֹרֵ֣שׁ רָ֣ע בְּכָל־עֵ֑ת מִדְיָנִ֥ים יְשַׁלֵּֽחַ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] who plots evil with deceit in his heart— he always stirs up conflict.
					\item[NRSV] with perverted mind devising evil, continually sowing discord;

					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]

					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-15}

			Therefore shall his calamity come suddenly; suddenly shall he be broken without remedy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַל־כֵּ֗ן פִּ֭תְאֹם יָב֣וֹא אֵיד֑וֹ פֶּ֥תַע יִ֝שָּׁבֵ֗ר וְאֵ֣ין מַרְפֵּֽא׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Therefore shall his calamity come suddenly; On a sudden shall he be broken, and that without remedy.
					\item[NIV] Therefore disaster will overtake him in an instant; he will suddenly be destroyed—without remedy.
					\item[NRSV] on such a one calamity will descend suddenly; in a moment, damage beyond repair.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-16}

			These six things doth the LORD hate: yea, seven are an abomination unto him:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֶׁשׁ־הֵ֭נָּה שָׂנֵ֣א יְהוָ֑ה וְ֝שֶׁ֗בַע תּוֹעֲבַ֥ת נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] There are six things the LORD hates, seven that are detestable to him:
					\item[NRSV] There are six things that the LORD hates, seven that are an abomination to him:

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:17} % *PROVERBS-6-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-17}

			A proud look, a lying tongue, and hands that shed innocent blood,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵינַ֣יִם רָ֭מוֹת לְשׁ֣וֹן שָׁ֑קֶר וְ֝יָדַ֗יִם שֹׁפְכ֥וֹת דָּם־נָקִֽי׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Haughty eyes, a lying tongue, And hands that shed innocent blood;
					\item[NIV] haughty eyes, a lying tongue, hands that shed innocent blood,
					\item[NRSV] haughty eyes, a lying tongue, and hands that shed innocent blood,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:18} % *PROVERBS-6-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-18}

			An heart that deviseth wicked imaginations, feet that be swift in running to mischief,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֗ב חֹ֭רֵשׁ מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת אָ֑וֶן רַגְלַ֥יִם מְ֝מַהֲר֗וֹת לָר֥וּץ לָֽרָעָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A heart that deviseth wicked purposes, Feet that are swift in running to mischief,
					\item[NIV] a heart that devises wicked schemes, feet that are quick to rush into evil,
					\item[NRSV] a heart that devises wicked plans, feet that hurry to run to evil,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:19} % *PROVERBS-6-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-19}

			A false witness that speaketh lies, and he that soweth discord among brethren.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָפִ֣יחַ כְּ֭זָבִים עֵ֣ד שָׁ֑קֶר וּמְשַׁלֵּ֥חַ מְ֝דָנִ֗ים בֵּ֣ין אַחִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A false witness that uttereth lies, And he that soweth discord among brethren.
					\item[NIV] a false witness who pours out lies and a person who stirs up conflict in the community.
					\item[NRSV] a lying witness who testifies falsely, and one who sows discord in a family.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:20} % *PROVERBS-6-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-20}

			My son, keep thy father’s commandment, and forsake not the law of thy mother:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נְצֹ֣ר בְּ֭נִי מִצְוַ֣ת אָבִ֑יךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝טֹּ֗שׁ תּוֹרַ֥ת אִמֶּֽךָ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, keep the commandment of thy father, And forsake not the law of thy mother:
					\item[NIV] My son, keep your father’s command and do not forsake your mother’s teaching.
					\item[NRSV] My child, keep your father’s commandment, and do not forsake your mother’s teaching.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:21} % *PROVERBS-6-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-21}

			Bind them continually upon thine heart, and tie them about thy neck.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קָשְׁרֵ֣ם עַל־לִבְּךָ֣ תָמִ֑יד עָ֝נְדֵ֗ם עַל־גַּרְגְּרֹתֶֽךָ׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Bind them continually upon thy heart; Tie them about thy neck.
					\item[NIV] Bind them always on your heart; fasten them around your neck.
					\item[NRSV] Bind them upon your heart always; tie them around your neck.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:22} % *PROVERBS-6-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-22}

			When thou goest, it shall lead thee; when thou sleepest, it shall keep thee; and when thou awakest, it shall talk with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּהִתְהַלֶּכְךָ֨ ׀ תַּנְחֶ֬ה אֹתָ֗ךְ בְּֽ֭שָׁכְבְּךָ תִּשְׁמֹ֣ר עָלֶ֑יךָ וַ֝הֲקִיצ֗וֹתָ הִ֣יא תְשִׂיחֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When thou walkest, it shall lead thee; When thou sleepest, it shall watch over thee; And when thou awakest, it shall talk with thee.
					\item[NIV] When you walk, they will guide you; when you sleep, they will watch over you; when you awake, they will speak to you.
					\item[NRSV] When you walk, they will lead you; when you lie down, they will watch over you; and when you awake, they will talk with you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:23} % *PROVERBS-6-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-23}

			For the commandment is a lamp; and the law is light; and reproofs of instruction are the way of life:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י נֵ֣ר מִ֭צְוָה וְת֣וֹרָה א֑וֹר וְדֶ֥רֶךְ חַ֝יִּ֗ים תּוֹכְח֥וֹת מוּסָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the commandment is a lamp; and the law is light; And reproofs of instruction are the way of life:
					\item[NIV] For this command is a lamp, this teaching is a light, and correction and instruction are the way to life,
					\item[NRSV] For the commandment is a lamp and the teaching a light, and the reproofs of discipline are the way of life,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:24} % *PROVERBS-6-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-24}

			To keep thee from the evil woman, from the flattery of the tongue of a strange woman.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִ֭שְׁמָרְךָ מֵאֵ֣שֶׁת רָ֑ע מֵֽ֝חֶלְקַ֗ת לָשׁ֥וֹן נָכְרִיָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To keep thee from the evil woman, From the flattery of the foreigner's tongue.
					\item[NIV] keeping you from your neighbor’s wife, from the smooth talk of a wayward woman.
					\item[NRSV] to preserve you from the wife of another, from the smooth tongue of the adulteress.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:25} % *PROVERBS-6-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-25}

			Lust not after her beauty in thine heart; neither let her take thee with her eyelids.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּחְמֹ֣ד יָ֭פְיָהּ בִּלְבָבֶ֑ךָ וְאַל־תִּ֝קָּֽחֲךָ֗ בְּעַפְעַפֶּֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lust not after her beauty in thy heart; Neither let her take thee with her eyelids.
					\item[NIV] Do not lust in your heart after her beauty or let her captivate you with her eyes.
					\item[NRSV] Do not desire her beauty in your heart, and do not let her capture you with her eyelashes;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:26} % *PROVERBS-6-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-26}

			For by means of a whorish woman a man is brought to a piece of bread: and the adulteress will hunt for the precious life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י בְעַד־אִשָּׁ֥ה זוֹנָ֗ה עַֽד־כִּכַּ֫ר לָ֥חֶם וְאֵ֥שֶׁת אִ֑ישׁ נֶ֖פֶשׁ יְקָרָ֣ה תָצֽוּד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For on account of a harlot a man is brought to a piece of bread; And the adulteress hunteth for the precious life.
					\item[NIV] For a prostitute can be had for a loaf of bread, but another man’s wife preys on your very life.
					\item[NRSV] for a prostitute’s fee is only a loaf of bread, but the wife of another stalks a man’s very life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:27} % *PROVERBS-6-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-27}

			Can a man take fire in his bosom, and his clothes not be burned?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲיַחְתֶּ֤ה אִ֓ישׁ אֵ֬שׁ בְּחֵיק֑וֹ וּ֝בְגָדָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִשָּׂרַֽפְנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Can a man take fire in his bosom, And his clothes not be burned?
					\item[NIV] Can a man scoop fire into his lap without his clothes being burned?
					\item[NRSV] Can fire be carried in the bosom without burning one’s clothes?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:28} % *PROVERBS-6-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-28}

			Can one go upon hot coals, and his feet not be burned?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־יְהַלֵּ֣ךְ אִ֭ישׁ עַל־הַגֶּחָלִ֑ים וְ֝רַגְלָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִכָּוֶֽינָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Or can one walk upon hot coals, And his feet not be scorched?
					\item[NIV] Can a man walk on hot coals without his feet being scorched?
					\item[NRSV] Or can one walk on hot coals without scorching the feet?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:29} % *PROVERBS-6-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-29}

			So he that goeth in to his neighbour’s wife; whosoever toucheth her shall not be innocent.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֵּ֗ן הַ֭בָּא אֶל־אֵ֣שֶׁת רֵעֵ֑הוּ לֹ֥א יִ֝נָּקֶ֗ה כָּֽל־הַנֹּגֵ֥עַ בָּֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So he that goeth in to his neighbor's wife; Whosoever toucheth her shall not be unpunished.
					\item[NIV] So is he who sleeps with another man’s wife; no one who touches her will go unpunished.
					\item[NRSV] So is he who sleeps with his neighbor’s wife; no one who touches her will go unpunished.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:30} % *PROVERBS-6-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-30}

			Men do not despise a thief, if he steal to satisfy his soul when he is hungry;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יָב֣וּזוּ לַ֭גַּנָּב כִּ֣י יִגְנ֑וֹב לְמַלֵּ֥א נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ כִּ֣י יִרְעָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Men do not despise a thief, if he steal To satisfy himself when he is hungry:
					\item[NIV] People do not despise a thief if he steals to satisfy his hunger when he is starving.
					\item[NRSV] Thieves are not despised who steal only to satisfy their appetite when they are hungry.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:31} % *PROVERBS-6-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-31}

			But if he be found, he shall restore sevenfold; he shall give all the substance of his house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭נִמְצָא יְשַׁלֵּ֣ם שִׁבְעָתָ֑יִם אֶת־כָּל־ה֖וֹן בֵּית֣וֹ יִתֵּֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But if he be found, he shall restore sevenfold; He shall give all the substance of his house.
					\item[NIV] Yet if he is caught, he must pay sevenfold, though it costs him all the wealth of his house.
					\item[NRSV] Yet if they are caught, they will pay sevenfold; they will forfeit all the goods of their house.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:32} % *PROVERBS-6-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-32}

			But whoso committeth adultery with a woman lacketh understanding: he that doeth it destroyeth his own soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֹאֵ֣ף אִשָּׁ֣ה חֲסַר־לֵ֑ב מַֽשְׁחִ֥ית נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ ה֣וּא יַעֲשֶֽׂנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that committeth adultery with a woman is void of understanding: He doeth it who would destroy his own soul.
					\item[NIV] But a man who commits adultery has no sense; whoever does so destroys himself.
					\item[NRSV] But he who commits adultery has no sense; he who does it destroys himself.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:33} % *PROVERBS-6-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-33}

			A wound and dishonour shall he get; and his reproach shall not be wiped away.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶֽגַע־וְקָל֥וֹן יִמְצָ֑א וְ֝חֶרְפָּת֗וֹ לֹ֣א תִמָּחֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wounds and dishonor shall he get; And his reproach shall not be wiped away.
					\item[NIV] Blows and disgrace are his lot, and his shame will never be wiped away.
					\item[NRSV] He will get wounds and dishonor, and his disgrace will not be wiped away.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:34} % *PROVERBS-6-34
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-34}

			For jealousy is the rage of a man: therefore he will not spare in the day of vengeance.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־קִנְאָ֥ה חֲמַת־גָּ֑בֶר וְלֹֽא־יַ֝חְמ֗וֹל בְּי֣וֹם נָקָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For jealousy is the rage of a man; And he will not spare in the day of vengeance.
					\item[NIV] For jealousy arouses a husband’s fury, and he will show no mercy when he takes revenge.
					\item[NRSV] For jealousy arouses a husband’s fury, and he shows no restraint when he takes revenge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 6:35} % *PROVERBS-6-35
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-6-35}

			He will not regard any ransom; neither will he rest content, though thou givest many gifts.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יִ֭שָּׂא פְּנֵ֣י כָל־כֹּ֑פֶר וְלֹֽא־יֹ֝אבֶ֗ה כִּ֣י תַרְבֶּה־שֹֽׁחַד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He will not regard any ransom; Neither will he rest content, though thou givest many gifts.
					\item[NIV] He will not accept any compensation; he will refuse a bribe, however great it is.
					\item[NRSV] He will accept no compensation, and refuses a bribe no matter how great.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 7} % *PROVERBS-7
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-7}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:1} % *PROVERBS-7-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-1}

			My son, keep my words, and lay up my commandments with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי שְׁמֹ֣ר אֲמָרָ֑י וּ֝מִצְוֺתַ֗י תִּצְפֹּ֥ן אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, keep my words, And lay up my commandments with thee.
					\item[NIV] My son, keep my words and store up my commands within you.
					\item[NRSV] My child, keep my words and store up my commandments with you;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:2} % *PROVERBS-7-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-2}

			Keep my commandments, and live; and my law as the apple of thine eye.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁמֹ֣ר מִצְוֺתַ֣י וֶחְיֵ֑ה וְ֝תוֹרָתִ֗י כְּאִישׁ֥וֹן עֵינֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Keep my commandments and live; And my law as the apple of thine eye.
					\item[NIV] Keep my commands and you will live; guard my teachings as the apple of your eye.
					\item[NRSV] keep my commandments and live, keep my teachings as the apple of your eye;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:3} % *PROVERBS-7-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-3}

			Bind them upon thy fingers, write them upon the table of thine heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קָשְׁרֵ֥ם עַל־אֶצְבְּעֹתֶ֑יךָ כָּ֝תְבֵ֗ם עַל־ל֥וּחַ לִבֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Bind them upon thy fingers; Write them upon the tablet of thy heart.
					\item[NIV] Bind them on your fingers; write them on the tablet of your heart.
					\item[NRSV] bind them on your fingers, write them on the tablet of your heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:4} % *PROVERBS-7-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-4}

			Say unto wisdom, Thou art my sister; and call understanding thy kinswoman:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱמֹ֣ר לַֽ֭חָכְמָה אֲחֹ֣תִי אָ֑תְּ וּ֝מֹדָ֗ע לַבִּינָ֥ה תִקְרָֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Say unto wisdom, Thou art my sister; And call understanding thy kinswoman:
					\item[NIV] Say to wisdom, “You are my sister,” and to insight, “You are my relative.”
					\item[NRSV] Say to wisdom, “You are my sister,” and call insight your intimate friend,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:5} % *PROVERBS-7-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-5}

			That they may keep thee from the strange woman, from the stranger which flattereth with her words.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִ֭שְׁמָרְךָ מֵאִשָּׁ֣ה זָרָ֑ה מִ֝נָּכְרִיָּ֗ה אֲמָרֶ֥יהָ הֶחֱלִֽיקָה׃}

					\item[NIV] They will keep you from the adulterous woman, from the wayward woman with her seductive words.
					\item[NRSV] that they may keep you from the loose woman, from the adulteress with her smooth words.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-19}

			For the goodman is not at home, he is gone a long journey:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י אֵ֣ין הָאִ֣ישׁ בְּבֵית֑וֹ הָ֝לַ֗ךְ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ מֵרָחֽוֹק׃}

		% % ---

					\item[NIV] He took his purse filled with money and will not be home till full moon.”
					\item[NRSV] He took a bag of money with him; he will not come home until full moon.”

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:21} % *PROVERBS-7-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-21}

			With her much fair speech she caused him to yield, with the flattering of her lips she forced him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הִ֭טַּתּוּ בְּרֹ֣ב לִקְחָ֑הּ בְּחֵ֥לֶק שְׂ֝פָתֶ֗יהָ תַּדִּיחֶֽנּוּ׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NRSV] With much seductive speech she persuades him; with her smooth talk she compels him.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-22}

			He goeth after her straightway, as an ox goeth to the slaughter, or as a fool to the correction of the stocks;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֤וֹלֵ֥ךְ אַחֲרֶ֗יהָ פִּ֫תְאֹ֥ם כְּ֭שׁוֹר אֶל־טָ֣בַח יָב֑וֹא וּ֝כְעֶ֗כֶס אֶל־מוּסַ֥ר אֱוִֽיל׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] All at once he followed her like an ox going to the slaughter, like a deer stepping into a noose
					\item[NRSV] Right away he follows her, and goes like an ox to the slaughter, or bounds like a stag toward the trap
