		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:23} % *PROVERBS-7-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-23}

			Till a dart strike through his liver; as a bird hasteth to the snare, and knoweth not that it is for his life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַ֤ד יְפַלַּ֪ח חֵ֡ץ כְּֽבֵד֗וֹ כְּמַהֵ֣ר צִפּ֣וֹר אֶל־פָּ֑ח וְלֹֽא־יָ֝דַ֗ע כִּֽי־בְנַפְשׁ֥וֹ הֽוּא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Till an arrow strike through his liver; As a bird hasteth to the snare, And knoweth not that it is for his life.
					\item[NIV] till an arrow pierces his liver, like a bird darting into a snare, little knowing it will cost him his life.
					\item[NRSV] until an arrow pierces its entrails. He is like a bird rushing into a snare, not knowing that it will cost him his life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:24} % *PROVERBS-7-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-24}

			Hearken unto me now therefore, O ye children, and attend to the words of my mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֣ה בָ֭נִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִ֑י וְ֝הַקְשִׁ֗יבוּ לְאִמְרֵי־פִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now therefore, my sons, hearken unto me, And attend to the words of my mouth.
					\item[NIV] Now then, my sons, listen to me; pay attention to what I say.
					\item[NRSV] And now, my children, listen to me, and be attentive to the words of my mouth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:25} % *PROVERBS-7-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-25}

			Let not thine heart decline to her ways, go not astray in her paths.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־יֵ֣שְׂטְ אֶל־דְּרָכֶ֣יהָ לִבֶּ֑ךָ אַל־תֵּ֝תַע בִּנְתִיבוֹתֶֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let not thy heart decline to her ways; Go not astray in her paths.
					\item[NIV] Do not let your heart turn to her ways or stray into her paths.
					\item[NRSV] Do not let your hearts turn aside to her ways; do not stray into her paths.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:26} % *PROVERBS-7-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-26}

			For she hath cast down many wounded: yea, many strong men have been slain by her.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־רַבִּ֣ים חֲלָלִ֣ים הִפִּ֑ילָה וַ֝עֲצֻמִ֗ים כָּל־הֲרֻגֶֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For she hath cast down many wounded: Yea, all her slain are a mighty host.
					\item[NIV] Many are the victims she has brought down; her slain are a mighty throng.
					\item[NRSV] For many are those she has laid low, and numerous are her victims.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 7:27} % *PROVERBS-7-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-7-27}

			Her house is the way to hell, going down to the chambers of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דַּרְכֵ֣י שְׁא֣וֹל בֵּיתָ֑הּ יֹ֝רְד֗וֹת אֶל־חַדְרֵי־מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Her house is the way to Sheol, Going down to the chambers of death.
					\item[NIV] Her house is a highway to the grave, leading down to the chambers of death.
					\item[NRSV] Her house is the way to Sheol, going down to the chambers of death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 8} % *PROVERBS-8
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-8}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:1} % *PROVERBS-8-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-1}

			Doth not wisdom cry? and understanding put forth her voice?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲלֹֽא־חָכְמָ֥ה תִקְרָ֑א וּ֝תְבוּנָ֗ה תִּתֵּ֥ן קוֹלָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Doth not wisdom cry, And understanding put forth her voice?
					\item[NIV] Does not wisdom call out? Does not understanding raise her voice?
					\item[NRSV] Does not wisdom call, and does not understanding raise her voice?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:2} % *PROVERBS-8-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-2}

			She standeth in the top of high places, by the way in the places of the paths.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּרֹאשׁ־מְרוֹמִ֥ים עֲלֵי־דָ֑רֶךְ בֵּ֖ית נְתִיב֣וֹת נִצָּֽבָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] On the top of high places by the way, Where the paths meet, she standeth;
					\item[NIV] At the highest point along the way, where the paths meet, she takes her stand;
					\item[NRSV] On the heights, beside the way, at the crossroads she takes her stand;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:3} % *PROVERBS-8-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-3}

			She crieth at the gates, at the entry of the city, at the coming in at the doors.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְיַד־שְׁעָרִ֥ים לְפִי־קָ֑רֶת מְב֖וֹא פְתָחִ֣ים תָּרֹֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Beside the gates, at the entry of the city, At the coming in at the doors, she crieth aloud:
					\item[NIV] beside the gate leading into the city, at the entrance, she cries aloud:
					\item[NRSV] beside the gates in front of the town, at the entrance of the portals she cries out:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:4} % *PROVERBS-8-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-4}

			Unto you, O men, I call; and my voice is to the sons of man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲלֵיכֶ֣ם אִישִׁ֣ים אֶקְרָ֑א וְ֝קוֹלִ֗י אֶל־בְּנֵ֥י אָדָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Unto you, O men, I call; And my voice is to the sons of men.
					\item[NIV] “To you, O people, I call out; I raise my voice to all mankind.
					\item[NRSV] “To you, O people, I call, and my cry is to all that live.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:5} % *PROVERBS-8-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-5}

			O ye simple, understand wisdom: and, ye fools, be ye of an understanding heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָבִ֣ינוּ פְתָאיִ֣ם עָרְמָ֑ה וּ֝כְסִילִ֗ים הָבִ֥ינוּ לֵֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] O ye simple, understand prudence; And, ye fools, be of an understanding heart.
					\item[NIV] You who are simple, gain prudence; you who are foolish, set your hearts on it.
					\item[NRSV] O simple ones, learn prudence; acquire intelligence, you who lack it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:6} % *PROVERBS-8-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-6}

			Hear; for I will speak of excellent things; and the opening of my lips shall be right things.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׁ֭מְעוּ כִּֽי־נְגִידִ֣ים אֲדַבֵּ֑ר וּמִפְתַּ֥ח שְׂ֝פָתַ֗י מֵישָׁרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear, for I will speak excellent things; And the opening of my lips shall be right things.
					\item[NIV] Listen, for I have trustworthy things to say; I open my lips to speak what is right.
					\item[NRSV] Hear, for I will speak noble things, and from my lips will come what is right;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:7} % *PROVERBS-8-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-7}

			For my mouth shall speak truth; and wickedness is an abomination to my lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־אֱ֭מֶת יֶהְגֶּ֣ה חִכִּ֑י וְתוֹעֲבַ֖ת שְׂפָתַ֣י רֶֽשַׁע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For my mouth shall utter truth; And wickedness is an abomination to my lips.
					\item[NIV] My mouth speaks what is true, for my lips detest wickedness.
					\item[NRSV] for my mouth will utter truth; wickedness is an abomination to my lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:8} % *PROVERBS-8-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-8}

			All the words of my mouth are in righteousness; there is nothing froward or perverse in them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּצֶ֥דֶק כָּל־אִמְרֵי־פִ֑י אֵ֥ין בָּ֝הֶ֗ם נִפְתָּ֥ל וְעִקֵּֽשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All the words of my mouth are in righteousness; There is nothing crooked or perverse in them.
					\item[NIV] All the words of my mouth are just; none of them is crooked or perverse.
					\item[NRSV] All the words of my mouth are righteous; there is nothing twisted or crooked in them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:9} % *PROVERBS-8-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-9}

			They are all plain to him that understandeth, and right to them that find knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֻּלָּ֣ם נְ֭כֹחִים לַמֵּבִ֑ין וִֽ֝ישָׁרִ֗ים לְמֹ֣צְאֵי דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They are all plain to him that understandeth, And right to them that find knowledge.
					\item[NIV] To the discerning all of them are right; they are upright to those who have found knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] They are all straight to one who understands and right to those who find knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:10} % *PROVERBS-8-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-10}

			Receive my instruction, and not silver; and knowledge rather than choice gold.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קְחֽוּ־מוּסָרִ֥י וְאַל־כָּ֑סֶף וְ֝דַ֗עַת מֵחָר֥וּץ נִבְחָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Receive my instruction, and not silver; And knowledge rather than choice gold.
					\item[NIV] Choose my instruction instead of silver, knowledge rather than choice gold,
					\item[NRSV] Take my instruction instead of silver, and knowledge rather than choice gold;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:11} % *PROVERBS-8-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-11}

			For wisdom is better than rubies; and all the things that may be desired are not to be compared to it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־טוֹבָ֣ה חָ֭כְמָה מִפְּנִינִ֑ים וְכָל־חֲ֝פָצִ֗ים לֹ֣א יִֽשְׁווּ־בָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For wisdom is better than rubies; And all the things that may be desired are not to be compared unto it.
					\item[NIV] for wisdom is more precious than rubies, and nothing you desire can compare with her.
					\item[NRSV] for wisdom is better than jewels, and all that you may desire cannot compare with her.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:12} % *PROVERBS-8-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-12}

			I wisdom dwell with prudence, and find out knowledge of witty inventions.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲ‍ֽנִי־חָ֭כְמָה שָׁכַ֣נְתִּי עָרְמָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת מְזִמּ֣וֹת אֶמְצָֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I wisdom have made prudence my dwelling, And find out knowledge and discretion.
					\item[NIV] “I, wisdom, dwell together with prudence; I possess knowledge and discretion.
					\item[NRSV] I, wisdom, live with prudence, and I attain knowledge and discretion.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:13} % *PROVERBS-8-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-13}

			The fear of the LORD is to hate evil: pride, and arrogancy, and the evil way, and the froward mouth, do I hate.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִֽרְאַ֣ת יְהוָה֮ שְֽׂנֹ֫את רָ֥ע גֵּ֘אָ֤ה וְגָא֨וֹן ׀ וְדֶ֣רֶךְ רָ֭ע וּפִ֨י תַהְפֻּכ֬וֹת שָׂנֵֽאתִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah is to hate evil: Pride, and arrogancy, and the evil way, And the perverse mouth, do I hate.
					\item[NIV] To fear the LORD is to hate evil; I hate pride and arrogance, evil behavior and perverse speech.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD is hatred of evil. Pride and arrogance and the way of evil and perverted speech I hate.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:14} % *PROVERBS-8-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-14}

			Counsel is mine, and sound wisdom: I am understanding; I have strength.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִֽי־עֵ֭צָה וְתוּשִׁיָּ֑ה אֲנִ֥י בִ֝ינָ֗ה לִ֣י גְבוּרָֽה׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Counsel is mine, and sound knowledge: I am understanding; I have might.
					\item[NIV] Counsel and sound judgment are mine; I have insight, I have power.
					\item[NRSV] I have good advice and sound wisdom; I have insight, I have strength.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:15} % *PROVERBS-8-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-15}

			By me kings reign, and princes decree justice.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּ֭י מְלָכִ֣ים יִמְלֹ֑כוּ וְ֝רוֹזְנִ֗ים יְחֹ֣קְקוּ צֶֽדֶק׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By me kings reign, And princes decree justice.
					\item[NIV] By me kings reign and rulers issue decrees that are just;
					\item[NRSV] By me kings reign, and rulers decree what is just;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-16}

			By me princes rule, and nobles, even all the judges of the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּ֭י שָׂרִ֣ים יָשֹׂ֑רוּ וּ֝נְדִיבִ֗ים כָּל־שֹׁ֥פְטֵי צֶֽדֶק׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] by me princes govern, and nobles—all who rule on earth.
					\item[NRSV] by me rulers rule, and nobles, all who govern rightly.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-17}

			I love them that love me; and those that seek me early shall find me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲ֭נִי אֹהֲבַ֣י אֵהָ֑ב וּ֝מְשַׁחֲרַ֗י יִמְצָאֻֽנְנִי׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I love them that love me; And those that seek me diligently shall find me.
					\item[NIV] I love those who love me, and those who seek me find me.
					\item[NRSV] I love those who love me, and those who seek me diligently find me.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-18}

			Riches and honour are with me; yea, durable riches and righteousness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹֽשֶׁר־וְכָב֥וֹד אִתִּ֑י ה֥וֹן עָ֝תֵ֗ק וּצְדָקָֽה׃}

					\item[NIV] With me are riches and honor, enduring wealth and prosperity.
					\item[NRSV] Riches and honor are with me, enduring wealth and prosperity.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-19}

			My fruit is better than gold, yea, than fine gold; and my revenue than choice silver.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֣וֹב פִּ֭רְיִי מֵחָר֣וּץ וּמִפָּ֑ז וּ֝תְבוּאָתִ֗י מִכֶּ֥סֶף נִבְחָֽר׃}

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-20}

			I lead in the way of righteousness, in the midst of the paths of judgment:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֹֽרַח־צְדָקָ֥ה אֲהַלֵּ֑ך בְּ֝ת֗וֹךְ נְתִיב֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] I walk in the way of righteousness, along the paths of justice,

		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:21} % *PROVERBS-8-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-21}

			That I may cause those that love me to inherit substance; and I will fill their treasures.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְהַנְחִ֖יל אֹהֲבַ֥י ׀ יֵ֑שׁ וְאֹצְרֹ֖תֵיהֶ֣ם אֲמַלֵּֽא׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] bestowing a rich inheritance on those who love me and making their treasuries full.
					\item[NRSV] endowing with wealth those who love me, and filling their treasuries.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:22} % *PROVERBS-8-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-22}

			The LORD possessed me in the beginning of his way, before his works of old.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְֽהוָ֗ה קָ֭נָנִי רֵאשִׁ֣ית דַּרְכּ֑וֹ קֶ֖דֶם מִפְעָלָ֣יו מֵאָֽז׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah possessed me in the beginning of his way, Before his works of old.
					\item[NIV] “The LORD brought me forth as the first of his works, before his deeds of old;
					\item[NRSV] The LORD created me at the beginning of his work, the first of his acts of long ago.

		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:23} % *PROVERBS-8-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-23}

			I was set up from everlasting, from the beginning, or ever the earth was.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵ֭עוֹלָם נִסַּ֥כְתִּי מֵרֹ֗אשׁ מִקַּדְמֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] I was formed long ages ago, at the very beginning, when the world came to be.
					\item[NRSV] Ages ago I was set up, at the first, before the beginning of the earth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-24}

			When there were no depths, I was brought forth; when there were no fountains abounding with water.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֵין־תְּהֹמ֥וֹת חוֹלָ֑לְתִּי בְּאֵ֥ין מַ֝עְיָנ֗וֹת נִכְבַּדֵּי־מָֽיִם׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] When there were no watery depths, I was given birth, when there were no springs overflowing with water;
					\item[NRSV] When there were no depths I was brought forth, when there were no springs abounding with water.

		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:25} % *PROVERBS-8-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-25}

			Before the mountains were settled, before the hills was I brought forth:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּטֶ֣רֶם הָרִ֣ים הָטְבָּ֑עוּ לִפְנֵ֖י גְבָע֣וֹת חוֹלָֽלְתִּי׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Before the mountains were settled, Before the hills was I brought forth;
					\item[NIV] before the mountains were settled in place, before the hills, I was given birth,
					\item[NRSV] Before the mountains had been shaped, before the hills, I was brought forth—
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:26} % *PROVERBS-8-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-26}

			While as yet he had not made the earth, nor the fields, nor the highest part of the dust of the world.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַד־לֹ֣א עָ֭שָׂה אֶ֣רֶץ וְחוּצ֑וֹת וְ֝רֹ֗אשׁ עָפְר֥וֹת תֵּבֵֽל׃}

					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] While as yet he had not made the earth, nor the fields, Nor the beginning of the dust of the world.
					\item[NIV] before he made the world or its fields or any of the dust of the earth.
					\item[NRSV] when he had not yet made earth and fields, or the world’s first bits of soil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:27} % *PROVERBS-8-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-27}

			When he prepared the heavens, I was there: when he set a compass upon the face of the depth:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּהֲכִינ֣וֹ שָׁ֭מַיִם שָׁ֣ם אָ֑נִי בְּח֥וּקוֹ ח֝֗וּג עַל־פְּנֵ֥י תְהֽוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When he established the heavens, I was there: When he set a circle upon the face of the deep,
					\item[NIV] I was there when he set the heavens in place, when he marked out the horizon on the face of the deep,
					\item[NRSV] When he established the heavens, I was there, when he drew a circle on the face of the deep,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:28} % *PROVERBS-8-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-28}

			When he established the clouds above: when he strengthened the fountains of the deep:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאַמְּצ֣וֹ שְׁחָקִ֣ים מִמָּ֑עַל בַּ֝עֲז֗וֹז עִינ֥וֹת תְּהוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When he made firm the skies above, When the fountains of the deep became strong,
					\item[NIV] when he established the clouds above and fixed securely the fountains of the deep,
					\item[NRSV] when he made firm the skies above, when he established the fountains of the deep,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:29} % *PROVERBS-8-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-29}

			When he gave to the sea his decree, that the waters should not pass his commandment: when he appointed the foundations of the earth:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּשׂ֘וּמ֤וֹ לַיָּ֨ם ׀ חֻקּ֗וֹ וּ֭מַיִם לֹ֣א יַֽעַבְרוּ־פִ֑יו בְּ֝חוּק֗וֹ מ֣וֹסְדֵי אָֽרֶץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When he gave to the sea its bound, That the waters should not transgress his commandment, When he marked out the foundations of the earth;
					\item[NIV] when he gave the sea its boundary so the waters would not overstep his command, and when he marked out the foundations of the earth.
					\item[NRSV] when he assigned to the sea its limit, so that the waters might not transgress his command, when he marked out the foundations of the earth,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:30} % *PROVERBS-8-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-30}

			Then I was by him, as one brought up with him: and I was daily his delight, rejoicing always before him;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וָֽאֶהְיֶ֥ה אֶצְל֗וֹ אָ֫מ֥וֹן וָֽאֶהְיֶ֣ה שַׁ֭עֲשֻׁעִים י֤וֹם ׀ י֑וֹם מְשַׂחֶ֖קֶת לְפָנָ֣יו בְּכָל־עֵֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then I was by him, as a master workman; And I was daily his delight, Rejoicing always before him,
					\item[NIV] Then I was constantly at his side. I was filled with delight day after day, rejoicing always in his presence,
					\item[NRSV] then I was beside him, like a master worker; and I was daily his delight, rejoicing before him always,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:31} % *PROVERBS-8-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-31}

			Rejoicing in the habitable part of his earth; and my delights were with the sons of men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְ֭שַׂחֶקֶת בְּתֵבֵ֣ל אַרְצ֑וֹ וְ֝שַׁעֲשֻׁעַ֗י אֶת־בְּנֵ֥י אָדָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Rejoicing in his habitable earth; And my delight was with the sons of men.
					\item[NIV] rejoicing in his whole world and delighting in mankind.
					\item[NRSV] rejoicing in his inhabited world and delighting in the human race.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:32} % *PROVERBS-8-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-32}

			Now therefore hearken unto me, O ye children: for blessed are they that keep my ways.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְעַתָּ֣ה בָ֭נִים שִׁמְעוּ־לִ֑י וְ֝אַשְׁרֵ֗י דְּרָכַ֥י יִשְׁמֹֽרוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Now therefore, my sons, hearken unto me; For blessed are they that keep my ways.
					\item[NIV] “Now then, my children, listen to me; blessed are those who keep my ways.
					\item[NRSV] “And now, my children, listen to me: happy are those who keep my ways.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:33} % *PROVERBS-8-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-33}

			Hear instruction, and be wise, and refuse it not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׁמְע֖וּ מוּסָ֥ר וַחֲכָ֗מוּ וְאַל־תִּפְרָֽעוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear instruction, and be wise, And refuse it not.
					\item[NIV] Listen to my instruction and be wise; do not disregard it.
					\item[NRSV] Hear instruction and be wise, and do not neglect it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:34} % *PROVERBS-8-34
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-34}

			Blessed is the man that heareth me, watching daily at my gates, waiting at the posts of my doors.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַ֥שְֽׁרֵי אָדָם֮ שֹׁמֵ֪עַֽ֫ לִ֥י לִשְׁקֹ֣ד עַל־דַּ֭לְתֹתַי י֤וֹם ׀ י֑וֹם לִ֝שְׁמֹ֗ר מְזוּזֹ֥ת פְּתָחָֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Blessed is the man that heareth me, Watching daily at my gates, Waiting at the posts of my doors.
					\item[NIV] Blessed are those who listen to me, watching daily at my doors, waiting at my doorway.
					\item[NRSV] Happy is the one who listens to me, watching daily at my gates, waiting beside my doors.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:35} % *PROVERBS-8-35
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-35}

			For whoso findeth me findeth life, and shall obtain favour of the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י מֹ֭צְאִי מָצָ֣א חַיִּ֑ים וַיָּ֥פֶק רָ֝צ֗וֹן מֵיְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For whoso findeth me findeth life, And shall obtain favor of Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] For those who find me find life and receive favor from the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] For whoever finds me finds life and obtains favor from the LORD;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 8:36} % *PROVERBS-8-36
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-8-36}

			But he that sinneth against me wrongeth his own soul: all they that hate me love death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭חֹטְאִי חֹמֵ֣ס נַפְשׁ֑וֹ כָּל־מְ֝שַׂנְאַ֗י אָ֣הֲבוּ מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But he that sinneth against me wrongeth his own soul: All they that hate me love death.
					\item[NIV] But those who fail to find me harm themselves; all who hate me love death.”
					\item[NRSV] but those who miss me injure themselves; all who hate me love death.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 9} % *PROVERBS-9
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-9}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:1} % *PROVERBS-9-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-1}

			Wisdom hath builded her house, she hath hewn out her seven pillars:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָ֭כְמוֹת בָּנְתָ֣ה בֵיתָ֑הּ חָצְבָ֖ה עַמּוּדֶ֣יהָ שִׁבְעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom hath builded her house; She hath hewn out her seven pillars:
					\item[NIV] Wisdom has built her house; she has set up its seven pillars.
					\item[NRSV] Wisdom has built her house, she has hewn her seven pillars.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:2} % *PROVERBS-9-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-2}

			She hath killed her beasts; she hath mingled her wine; she hath also furnished her table.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טָבְחָ֣ה טִ֭בְחָהּ מָסְכָ֣ה יֵינָ֑הּ אַ֝֗ף עָֽרְכָ֥ה שֻׁלְחָנָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She hath killed her beasts; she hath mingled her wine; She hath also furnished her table:
					\item[NIV] She has prepared her meat and mixed her wine; she has also set her table.
					\item[NRSV] She has slaughtered her animals, she has mixed her wine, she has also set her table.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:3} % *PROVERBS-9-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-3}

			She hath sent forth her maidens: she crieth upon the highest places of the city,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שָֽׁלְחָ֣ה נַעֲרֹתֶ֣יהָ תִקְרָ֑א עַל־גַּ֝פֵּ֗י מְרֹ֣מֵי קָֽרֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She hath sent forth her maidens; She crieth upon the highest places of the city:
					\item[NIV] She has sent out her servants, and she calls from the highest point of the city,
					\item[NRSV] She has sent out her servant-girls, she calls from the highest places in the town,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:4} % *PROVERBS-9-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-4}

			Whoso is simple, let him turn in hither: as for him that wanteth understanding, she saith to him,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִי־פֶ֭תִי יָסֻ֣ר הֵ֑נָּה חֲסַר־לֵ֝֗ב אָ֣מְרָה לּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso is simple, let him turn in hither: As for him that is void of understanding, she saith to him,
					\item[NIV] “Let all who are simple come to my house!” To those who have no sense she says,
					\item[NRSV] “You that are simple, turn in here!” To those without sense she says,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:5} % *PROVERBS-9-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-5}

			Come, eat of my bread, and drink of the wine which I have mingled.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְ֭כוּ לַחֲמ֣וּ בְֽלַחֲמִ֑י וּ֝שְׁת֗וּ בְּיַ֣יִן מָסָֽכְתִּי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Come, eat ye of my bread, And drink of the wine which I have mingled.
					\item[NIV] “Come, eat my food and drink the wine I have mixed.
					\item[NRSV] “Come, eat of my bread and drink of the wine I have mixed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:6} % *PROVERBS-9-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-6}

			Forsake the foolish, and live; and go in the way of understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִזְב֣וּ פְתָאיִ֣ם וִֽחְי֑וּ וְ֝אִשְׁר֗וּ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ בִּינָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Leave off, ye simple ones, and live; And walk in the way of understanding.
					\item[NIV] Leave your simple ways and you will live; walk in the way of insight.”
					\item[NRSV] Lay aside immaturity, and live, and walk in the way of insight.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:7} % *PROVERBS-9-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-7}

			He that reproveth a scorner getteth to himself shame: and he that rebuketh a wicked man getteth himself a blot.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֹ֤סֵ֨ר ׀ לֵ֗ץ לֹקֵ֣חַֽ ל֣וֹ קָל֑וֹן וּמוֹכִ֖יחַ לְרָשָׁ֣ע מוּמֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that correcteth a scoffer getteth to himself reviling; And he that reproveth a wicked man getteth himself a blot.
					\item[NIV] Whoever corrects a mocker invites insults; whoever rebukes the wicked incurs abuse.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever corrects a scoffer wins abuse; whoever rebukes the wicked gets hurt.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:8} % *PROVERBS-9-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-8}

			Reprove not a scorner, lest he hate thee: rebuke a wise man, and he will love thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תּ֣וֹכַח לֵ֭ץ פֶּן־יִשְׂנָאֶ֑ךָּ הוֹכַ֥ח לְ֝חָכָ֗ם וְיֶאֱהָבֶֽךָּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Reprove not a scoffer, lest he hate thee: Reprove a wise man, and he will love thee.
					\item[NIV] Do not rebuke mockers or they will hate you; rebuke the wise and they will love you.
					\item[NRSV] A scoffer who is rebuked will only hate you; the wise, when rebuked, will love you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:9} % *PROVERBS-9-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-9}

			Give instruction to a wise man, and he will be yet wiser: teach a just man, and he will increase in learning.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תֵּ֣ן לְ֭חָכָם וְיֶחְכַּם־ע֑וֹד הוֹדַ֥ע לְ֝צַדִּ֗יק וְי֣וֹסֶף לֶֽקַח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Give instruction to a wise man, and he will be yet wiser: Teach a righteous man, and he will increase in learning.
					\item[NIV] Instruct the wise and they will be wiser still; teach the righteous and they will add to their learning.
					\item[NRSV] Give instruction to the wise, and they will become wiser still; teach the righteous and they will gain in learning.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:10} % *PROVERBS-9-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-10}

			The fear of the LORD is the beginning of wisdom: and the knowledge of the holy is understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תְּחִלַּ֣ת חָ֭כְמָה יִרְאַ֣ת יְהוָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת קְדֹשִׁ֣ים בִּינָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah is the beginning of wisdom; And the knowledge of the Holy One is understanding.
					\item[NIV] The fear of the LORD is the beginning of wisdom, and knowledge of the Holy One is understanding.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD is the beginning of wisdom, and the knowledge of the Holy One is insight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:11} % *PROVERBS-9-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-11}

			For by me thy days shall be multiplied, and the years of thy life shall be increased.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי־בִ֭י יִרְבּ֣וּ יָמֶ֑יךָ וְיוֹסִ֥יפוּ לְּ֝ךָ֗ שְׁנ֣וֹת חַיִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For by me thy days shall be multiplied, And the years of thy life shall be increased.
					\item[NIV] For through wisdom your days will be many, and years will be added to your life.
					\item[NRSV] For by me your days will be multiplied, and years will be added to your life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:12} % *PROVERBS-9-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-12}

			If thou be wise, thou shalt be wise for thyself: but if thou scornest, thou alone shalt bear it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־חָ֭כַמְתָּ חָכַ֣מְתָּ לָּ֑ךְ וְ֝לַ֗צְתָּ לְֽבַדְּךָ֥ תִשָּֽׂא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If thou art wise, thou art wise for thyself; And if thou scoffest, thou alone shalt bear it.
					\item[NIV] If you are wise, your wisdom will reward you; if you are a mocker, you alone will suffer.
					\item[NRSV] If you are wise, you are wise for yourself; if you scoff, you alone will bear it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:13} % *PROVERBS-9-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-13}

			A foolish woman is clamorous: she is simple, and knoweth nothing.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵ֣שֶׁת כְּ֭סִילוּת הֹֽמִיָּ֑ה פְּ֝תַיּ֗וּת וּבַל־יָ֥דְעָה מָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The foolish woman is clamorous; She is simple, and knoweth nothing.
					\item[NIV] Folly is an unruly woman; she is simple and knows nothing.
					\item[NRSV] The foolish woman is loud; she is ignorant and knows nothing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:14} % *PROVERBS-9-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-14}

			For she sitteth at the door of her house, on a seat in the high places of the city,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽ֭יָשְׁבָה לְפֶ֣תַח בֵּיתָ֑הּ עַל־כִּ֝סֵּ֗א מְרֹ֣מֵי קָֽרֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And she sitteth at the door of her house, On a seat in the high places of the city,
					\item[NIV] She sits at the door of her house, on a seat at the highest point of the city,
					\item[NRSV] She sits at the door of her house, on a seat at the high places of the town,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:15} % *PROVERBS-9-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-15}

			To call passengers who go right on their ways:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִקְרֹ֥א לְעֹֽבְרֵי־דָ֑רֶךְ הַֽ֝מְיַשְּׁרִ֗ים אֹֽרְחוֹתָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To call to them that pass by, Who go right on their ways:
					\item[NIV] calling out to those who pass by, who go straight on their way,
					\item[NRSV] calling to those who pass by, who are going straight on their way,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:16} % *PROVERBS-9-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-16}

			Whoso is simple, let him turn in hither: and as for him that wanteth understanding, she saith to him,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִי־פֶ֭תִי יָסֻ֣ר הֵ֑נָּה וַחֲסַר־לֵ֝֗ב וְאָ֣מְרָה לּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso is simple, let him turn in hither; And as for him that is void of understanding, she saith to him,
					\item[NIV] “Let all who are simple come to my house!” To those who have no sense she says,
					\item[NRSV] “You who are simple, turn in here!” And to those without sense she says,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:17} % *PROVERBS-9-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-17}

			Stolen waters are sweet, and bread eaten in secret is pleasant.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַֽיִם־גְּנוּבִ֥ים יִמְתָּ֑קוּ וְלֶ֖חֶם סְתָרִ֣ים יִנְעָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Stolen waters are sweet, And bread eaten in secret is pleasant.
					\item[NIV] “Stolen water is sweet; food eaten in secret is delicious!”
					\item[NRSV] “Stolen water is sweet, and bread eaten in secret is pleasant.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 9:18} % *PROVERBS-9-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-9-18}

			But he knoweth not that the dead are there; and that her guests are in the depths of hell.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְֽלֹא־יָ֭דַע כִּֽי־רְפָאִ֣ים שָׁ֑ם בְּעִמְקֵ֖י שְׁא֣וֹל קְרֻאֶֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But he knoweth not that the dead are there; That her guests are in the depths of Sheol.
					\item[NIV] But little do they know that the dead are there, that her guests are deep in the realm of the dead.
					\item[NRSV] But they do not know that the dead are there, that her guests are in the depths of Sheol.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 10} % *PROVERBS-10
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-10}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:1} % *PROVERBS-10-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-1}

			The proverbs of Solomon. A wise son maketh a glad father: but a foolish son is the heaviness of his mother.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִשְׁלֵ֗י שְׁלֹ֫מֹ֥ה בֵּ֣ן חָ֭כָם יְשַׂמַּח־אָ֑ב וּבֵ֥ן כְּ֝סִ֗יל תּוּגַ֥ת אִמּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The proverbs of Solomon. A wise son maketh a glad father; But a foolish son is the heaviness of his mother.
					\item[NIV] The proverbs of Solomon: A wise son brings joy to his father, but a foolish son brings grief to his mother.
					\item[NRSV] The proverbs of Solomon. A wise child makes a glad father, but a foolish child is a mother’s grief.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:2} % *PROVERBS-10-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-2}

			Treasures of wickedness profit nothing: but righteousness delivereth from death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־י֭וֹעִילוּ אוֹצְר֣וֹת רֶ֑שַׁע וּ֝צְדָקָ֗ה תַּצִּ֥יל מִמָּֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Treasures of wickedness profit nothing; But righteousness delivereth from death.
					\item[NIV] Ill-gotten treasures have no lasting value, but righteousness delivers from death.
					\item[NRSV] Treasures gained by wickedness do not profit, but righteousness delivers from death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:3} % *PROVERBS-10-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-3}

			The LORD will not suffer the soul of the righteous to famish: but he casteth away the substance of the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־יַרְעִ֣יב יְ֭הוָה נֶ֣פֶשׁ צַדִּ֑יק וְהַוַּ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים יֶהְדֹּֽף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah will not suffer the soul of the righteous to famish; But he thrusteth away the desire of the wicked.
					\item[NIV] The LORD does not let the righteous go hungry, but he thwarts the craving of the wicked.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD does not let the righteous go hungry, but he thwarts the craving of the wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:4} % *PROVERBS-10-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-4}

			He becometh poor that dealeth with a slack hand: but the hand of the diligent maketh rich.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָ֗אשׁ עֹשֶׂ֥ה כַף־רְמִיָּ֑ה וְיַ֖ד חָרוּצִ֣ים תַּעֲשִֽׁיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He becometh poor that worketh with a slack hand; But the hand of the diligent maketh rich.
					\item[NIV] Lazy hands make for poverty, but diligent hands bring wealth.
					\item[NRSV] A slack hand causes poverty, but the hand of the diligent makes rich.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:5} % *PROVERBS-10-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-5}

			He that gathereth in summer is a wise son: but he that sleepeth in harvest is a son that causeth shame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹגֵ֣ר בַּ֭קַּיִץ בֵּ֣ן מַשְׂכִּ֑יל נִרְדָּ֥ם בַּ֝קָּצִ֗יר בֵּ֣ן מֵבִֽישׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that gathereth in summer is a wise son; But he that sleepeth in harvest is a son that causeth shame.
					\item[NIV] He who gathers crops in summer is a prudent son, but he who sleeps during harvest is a disgraceful son.
					\item[NRSV] A child who gathers in summer is prudent, but a child who sleeps in harvest brings shame.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:6} % *PROVERBS-10-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-6}

			Blessings are upon the head of the just: but violence covereth the mouth of the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭רָכוֹת לְרֹ֣אשׁ צַדִּ֑יק וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְכַסֶּ֥ה חָמָֽס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Blessings are upon the head of the righteous; But violence covereth the mouth of the wicked.
					\item[NIV] Blessings crown the head of the righteous, but violence overwhelms the mouth of the wicked.
					\item[NRSV] Blessings are on the head of the righteous, but the mouth of the wicked conceals violence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:7} % *PROVERBS-10-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-7}

			The memory of the just is blessed: but the name of the wicked shall rot.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זֵ֣כֶר צַ֭דִּיק לִבְרָכָ֑ה וְשֵׁ֖ם רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִרְקָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The memory of the righteous is blessed; But the name of the wicked shall rot.
					\item[NIV] The name of the righteous is used in blessings, but the name of the wicked will rot.
					\item[NRSV] The memory of the righteous is a blessing, but the name of the wicked will rot.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:8} % *PROVERBS-10-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-8}

			The wise in heart will receive commandments: but a prating fool shall fall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֲכַם־לֵ֭ב יִקַּ֣ח מִצְוֺ֑ת וֶאֱוִ֥יל שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם יִלָּבֵֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wise in heart will receive commandments; But a prating fool shall fall.
					\item[NIV] The wise in heart accept commands, but a chattering fool comes to ruin.
					\item[NRSV] The wise of heart will heed commandments, but a babbling fool will come to ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:9} % *PROVERBS-10-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-9}

			He that walketh uprightly walketh surely: but he that perverteth his ways shall be known.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בַּ֭תֹּם יֵ֣לֶךְ בֶּ֑טַח וּמְעַקֵּ֥שׁ דְּ֝רָכָ֗יו יִוָּדֵֽעַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that walketh uprightly walketh surely; But he that perverteth his ways shall be known.
					\item[NIV] Whoever walks in integrity walks securely, but whoever takes crooked paths will be found out.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever walks in integrity walks securely, but whoever follows perverse ways will be found out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:10} % *PROVERBS-10-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-10}

			He that winketh with the eye causeth sorrow: but a prating fool shall fall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קֹ֣רֵֽץ עַ֭יִן יִתֵּ֣ן עַצָּ֑בֶת וֶאֱוִ֥יל שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם יִלָּבֵֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that winketh with the eye causeth sorrow; But a prating fool shall fall.
					\item[NIV] Whoever winks maliciously causes grief, and a chattering fool comes to ruin.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever winks the eye causes trouble, but the one who rebukes boldly makes peace.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:11} % *PROVERBS-10-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-11}

			The mouth of a righteous man is a well of life: but violence covereth the mouth of the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְק֣וֹר חַ֭יִּים פִּ֣י צַדִּ֑יק וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְכַסֶּ֥ה חָמָֽס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The mouth of the righteous is a fountain of life; But violence covereth the mouth of the wicked.
					\item[NIV] The mouth of the righteous is a fountain of life, but the mouth of the wicked conceals violence.
					\item[NRSV] The mouth of the righteous is a fountain of life, but the mouth of the wicked conceals violence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:12} % *PROVERBS-10-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-12}

			Hatred stirreth up strifes: but love covereth all sins.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂ֭נְאָה תְּעוֹרֵ֣ר מְדָנִ֑ים וְעַ֥ל כָּל־פְּ֝שָׁעִ֗ים תְּכַסֶּ֥ה אַהֲבָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hatred stirreth up strifes; But love covereth all transgressions.
					\item[NIV] Hatred stirs up conflict, but love covers over all wrongs.
					\item[NRSV] Hatred stirs up strife, but love covers all offenses.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:13} % *PROVERBS-10-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-13}

			In the lips of him that hath understanding wisdom is found: but a rod is for the back of him that is void of understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּשִׂפְתֵ֣י נָ֭בוֹן תִּמָּצֵ֣א חָכְמָ֑ה וְ֝שֵׁ֗בֶט לְגֵ֣ו חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the lips of him that hath discernment wisdom is found; But a rod is for the back of him that is void of understanding.
					\item[NIV] Wisdom is found on the lips of the discerning, but a rod is for the back of one who has no sense.
					\item[NRSV] On the lips of one who has understanding wisdom is found, but a rod is for the back of one who lacks sense.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:14} % *PROVERBS-10-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-14}

			Wise men lay up knowledge: but the mouth of the foolish is near destruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֲכָמִ֥ים יִצְפְּנוּ־דָ֑עַת וּפִֽי־אֱ֝וִיל מְחִתָּ֥ה קְרֹבָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wise men lay up knowledge; But the mouth of the foolish is a present destruction.
					\item[NIV] The wise store up knowledge, but the mouth of a fool invites ruin.
					\item[NRSV] The wise lay up knowledge, but the babbling of a fool brings ruin near.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:15} % *PROVERBS-10-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-15}

			The rich man’s wealth is his strong city: the destruction of the poor is their poverty.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֣וֹן עָ֭שִׁיר קִרְיַ֣ת עֻזּ֑וֹ מְחִתַּ֖ת דַּלִּ֣ים רֵישָֽׁם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The rich man's wealth is his strong city: The destruction of the poor is their poverty.
					\item[NIV] The wealth of the rich is their fortified city, but poverty is the ruin of the poor.
					\item[NRSV] The wealth of the rich is their fortress; the poverty of the poor is their ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:16} % *PROVERBS-10-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-16}

			The labour of the righteous tendeth to life: the fruit of the wicked to sin.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פְּעֻלַּ֣ת צַדִּ֣יק לְחַיִּ֑ים תְּבוּאַ֖ת רָשָׁ֣ע לְחַטָּֽאת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The labor of the righteous tendeth to life; The increase of the wicked, to sin.
					\item[NIV] The wages of the righteous is life, but the earnings of the wicked are sin and death.
					\item[NRSV] The wage of the righteous leads to life, the gain of the wicked to sin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:17} % *PROVERBS-10-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-17}

			He is in the way of life that keepeth instruction: but he that refuseth reproof erreth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֣רַח לְ֭חַיִּים שׁוֹמֵ֣ר מוּסָ֑ר וְעוֹזֵ֖ב תּוֹכַ֣חַת מַתְעֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He is in the way of life that heedeth correction; But he that forsaketh reproof erreth.
					\item[NIV] Whoever heeds discipline shows the way to life, but whoever ignores correction leads others astray.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever heeds instruction is on the path to life, but one who rejects a rebuke goes astray.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:18} % *PROVERBS-10-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-18}

			He that hideth hatred with lying lips, and he that uttereth a slander, is a fool.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְכַסֶּ֣ה שִׂ֭נְאָה שִׂפְתֵי־שָׁ֑קֶר וּמוֹצִ֥א דִ֝בָּ֗ה ה֣וּא כְסִֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that hideth hatred is of lying lips; And he that uttereth a slander is a fool.
					\item[NIV] Whoever conceals hatred with lying lips and spreads slander is a fool.
					\item[NRSV] Lying lips conceal hatred, and whoever utters slander is a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:19} % *PROVERBS-10-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-19}

			In the multitude of words there wanteth not sin: but he that refraineth his lips is wise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּרֹ֣ב דְּ֭בָרִים לֹ֣א יֶחְדַּל־פָּ֑שַׁע וְחֹשֵׂ֖ךְ שְׂפָתָ֣יו מַשְׂכִּֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the multitude of words there wanteth not transgression; But he that refraineth his lips doeth wisely.
					\item[NIV] Sin is not ended by multiplying words, but the prudent hold their tongues.
					\item[NRSV] When words are many, transgression is not lacking, but the prudent are restrained in speech.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:20} % *PROVERBS-10-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-20}

			The tongue of the just is as choice silver: the heart of the wicked is little worth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֶּ֣סֶף נִ֭בְחָר לְשׁ֣וֹן צַדִּ֑יק לֵ֖ב רְשָׁעִ֣ים כִּמְעָֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The tongue of the righteous is as choice silver: The heart of the wicked is little worth.
					\item[NIV] The tongue of the righteous is choice silver, but the heart of the wicked is of little value.
					\item[NRSV] The tongue of the righteous is choice silver; the mind of the wicked is of little worth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:21} % *PROVERBS-10-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-21}

			The lips of the righteous feed many: but fools die for want of wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂפְתֵ֣י צַ֭דִּיק יִרְע֣וּ רַבִּ֑ים וֶֽ֝אֱוִילִ֗ים בַּחֲסַר־לֵ֥ב יָמֽוּתוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lips of the righteous feed many; But the foolish die for lack of understanding.
					\item[NIV] The lips of the righteous nourish many, but fools die for lack of sense.
					\item[NRSV] The lips of the righteous feed many, but fools die for lack of sense.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:22} % *PROVERBS-10-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-22}

			The blessing of the LORD, it maketh rich, and he addeth no sorrow with it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּרְכַּ֣ת יְ֭הוָה הִ֣יא תַעֲשִׁ֑יר וְלֹֽא־יוֹסִ֖ף עֶ֣צֶב עִמָּֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The blessing of Jehovah, it maketh rich; And he addeth no sorrow therewith.
					\item[NIV] The blessing of the LORD brings wealth, without painful toil for it.
					\item[NRSV] The blessing of the LORD makes rich, and he adds no sorrow with it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:23} % *PROVERBS-10-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-23}

			It is as sport to a fool to do mischief: but a man of understanding hath wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּשְׂח֣וֹק לִ֭כְסִיל עֲשׂ֣וֹת זִמָּ֑ה וְ֝חָכְמָ֗ה לְאִ֣ישׁ תְּבוּנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is as sport to a fool to do wickedness; And so is wisdom to a man of understanding.
					\item[NIV] A fool finds pleasure in wicked schemes, but a person of understanding delights in wisdom.
					\item[NRSV] Doing wrong is like sport to a fool, but wise conduct is pleasure to a person of understanding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:24} % *PROVERBS-10-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-24}

			The fear of the wicked, it shall come upon him: but the desire of the righteous shall be granted.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְגוֹרַ֣ת רָ֭שָׁע הִ֣יא תְבוֹאֶ֑נּוּ וְתַאֲוַ֖ת צַדִּיקִ֣ים יִתֵּֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of the wicked, it shall come upon him; And the desire of the righteous shall be granted.
					\item[NIV] What the wicked dread will overtake them; what the righteous desire will be granted.
					\item[NRSV] What the wicked dread will come upon them, but the desire of the righteous will be granted.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:25} % *PROVERBS-10-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-25}

			As the whirlwind passeth, so is the wicked no more: but the righteous is an everlasting foundation.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּעֲב֣וֹר ס֭וּפָה וְאֵ֣ין רָשָׁ֑ע וְ֝צַדִּ֗יק יְס֣וֹד עוֹלָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the whirlwind passeth, the wicked is no more; But the righteous is an everlasting foundation.
					\item[NIV] When the storm has swept by, the wicked are gone, but the righteous stand firm forever.
					\item[NRSV] When the tempest passes, the wicked are no more, but the righteous are established forever.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:26} % *PROVERBS-10-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-26}

			As vinegar to the teeth, and as smoke to the eyes, so is the sluggard to them that send him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּחֹ֤מֶץ ׀ לַשִּׁנַּ֗יִם וְכֶעָשָׁ֥ן לָעֵינָ֑יִם כֵּ֥ן הֶ֝עָצֵ֗ל לְשֹׁלְחָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As vinegar to the teeth, and as smoke to the eyes, So is the sluggard to them that send him.
					\item[NIV] As vinegar to the teeth and smoke to the eyes, so are sluggards to those who send them.
					\item[NRSV] Like vinegar to the teeth, and smoke to the eyes, so are the lazy to their employers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:27} % *PROVERBS-10-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-27}

			The fear of the LORD prolongeth days: but the years of the wicked shall be shortened.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה תּוֹסִ֣יף יָמִ֑ים וּשְׁנ֖וֹת רְשָׁעִ֣ים תִּקְצֹֽרְנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah prolongeth days; But the years of the wicked shall be shortened.
					\item[NIV] The fear of the LORD adds length to life, but the years of the wicked are cut short.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD prolongs life, but the years of the wicked will be short.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:28} % *PROVERBS-10-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-28}

			The hope of the righteous shall be gladness: but the expectation of the wicked shall perish.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹחֶ֣לֶת צַדִּיקִ֣ים שִׂמְחָ֑ה וְתִקְוַ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים תֹּאבֵֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The hope of the righteous shall be gladness; But the expectation of the wicked shall perish.
					\item[NIV] The prospect of the righteous is joy, but the hopes of the wicked come to nothing.
					\item[NRSV] The hope of the righteous ends in gladness, but the expectation of the wicked comes to nothing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:29} % *PROVERBS-10-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-29}

			The way of the LORD is strength to the upright: but destruction shall be to the workers of iniquity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מָע֣וֹז לַ֭תֹּם דֶּ֣רֶךְ יְהוָ֑ה וּ֝מְחִתָּ֗ה לְפֹ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The way of Jehovah is a stronghold to the upright; But it is a destruction to the workers of iniquity.
					\item[NIV] The way of the LORD is a refuge for the blameless, but it is the ruin of those who do evil.
					\item[NRSV] The way of the LORD is a stronghold for the upright, but destruction for evildoers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:30} % *PROVERBS-10-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-30}

			The righteous shall never be removed: but the wicked shall not inhabit the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צַדִּ֣יק לְעוֹלָ֣ם בַּל־יִמּ֑וֹט וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים לֹ֣א יִשְׁכְּנוּ־אָֽרֶץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteous shall never be removed; But the wicked shall not dwell in the land.
					\item[NIV] The righteous will never be uprooted, but the wicked will not remain in the land.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous will never be removed, but the wicked will not remain in the land.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:31} % *PROVERBS-10-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-31}

			The mouth of the just bringeth forth wisdom: but the froward tongue shall be cut out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פִּֽי־צַ֭דִּיק יָנ֣וּב חָכְמָ֑ה וּלְשׁ֥וֹן תַּ֝הְפֻּכ֗וֹת תִּכָּרֵֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The mouth of the righteous bringeth forth wisdom; But the perverse tongue shall be cut off.
					\item[NIV] From the mouth of the righteous comes the fruit of wisdom, but a perverse tongue will be silenced.
					\item[NRSV] The mouth of the righteous brings forth wisdom, but the perverse tongue will be cut off.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 10:32} % *PROVERBS-10-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-10-32}

			The lips of the righteous know what is acceptable: but the mouth of the wicked speaketh frowardness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂפְתֵ֣י צַ֭דִּיק יֵדְע֣וּן רָצ֑וֹן וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים תַּהְפֻּכֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lips of the righteous know what is acceptable; But the mouth of the wicked speaketh perverseness.
					\item[NIV] The lips of the righteous know what finds favor, but the mouth of the wicked only what is perverse.
					\item[NRSV] The lips of the righteous know what is acceptable, but the mouth of the wicked what is perverse.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 11} % *PROVERBS-11
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-11}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:1} % *PROVERBS-11-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-1}

			A false balance is abomination to the LORD: but a just weight is his delight.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֹאזְנֵ֣י מִ֭רְמָה תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהוָ֑ה וְאֶ֖בֶן שְׁלֵמָ֣ה רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A false balance is an abomination to Jehovah; But a just weight is his delight.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests dishonest scales, but accurate weights find favor with him.
					\item[NRSV] A false balance is an abomination to the LORD, but an accurate weight is his delight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:2} % *PROVERBS-11-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-2}

			When pride cometh, then cometh shame: but with the lowly is wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בָּֽא־זָ֭דוֹן וַיָּבֹ֣א קָל֑וֹן וְֽאֶת־צְנוּעִ֥ים חָכְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When pride cometh, then cometh shame; But with the lowly is wisdom.
					\item[NIV] When pride comes, then comes disgrace, but with humility comes wisdom.
					\item[NRSV] When pride comes, then comes disgrace; but wisdom is with the humble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:3} % *PROVERBS-11-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-3}

			The integrity of the upright shall guide them: but the perverseness of transgressors shall destroy them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תֻּמַּ֣ת יְשָׁרִ֣ים תַּנְחֵ֑ם וְסֶ֖לֶף בּוֹגְדִ֣ים יְשָׁדֵּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The integrity of the upright shall guide them; But the perverseness of the treacherous shall destroy them.
					\item[NIV] The integrity of the upright guides them, but the unfaithful are destroyed by their duplicity.
					\item[NRSV] The integrity of the upright guides them, but the crookedness of the treacherous destroys them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:4} % *PROVERBS-11-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-4}

			Riches profit not in the day of wrath: but righteousness delivereth from death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יוֹעִ֣יל ה֭וֹן בְּי֣וֹם עֶבְרָ֑ה וּ֝צְדָקָ֗ה תַּצִּ֥יל מִמָּֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Riches profit not in the day of wrath; But righteousness delivereth from death.
					\item[NIV] Wealth is worthless in the day of wrath, but righteousness delivers from death.
					\item[NRSV] Riches do not profit in the day of wrath, but righteousness delivers from death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:5} % *PROVERBS-11-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-5}

			The righteousness of the perfect shall direct his way: but the wicked shall fall by his own wickedness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צִדְקַ֣ת תָּ֭מִים תְּיַשֵּׁ֣ר דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וּ֝בְרִשְׁעָת֗וֹ יִפֹּ֥ל רָשָֽׁע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
