					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteousness of the perfect shall direct his way; But the wicked shall fall by his own wickedness.
					\item[NIV] The righteousness of the blameless makes their paths straight, but the wicked are brought down by their own wickedness.
					\item[NRSV] The righteousness of the blameless keeps their ways straight, but the wicked fall by their own wickedness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:6} % *PROVERBS-11-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-6}

			The righteousness of the upright shall deliver them: but transgressors shall be taken in their own naughtiness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צִדְקַ֣ת יְ֭שָׁרִים תַּצִּילֵ֑ם וּ֝בְהַוַּ֗ת בֹּגְדִ֥ים יִלָּכֵֽדוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteousness of the upright shall deliver them; But the treacherous shall be taken in their own iniquity.
					\item[NIV] The righteousness of the upright delivers them, but the unfaithful are trapped by evil desires.
					\item[NRSV] The righteousness of the upright saves them, but the treacherous are taken captive by their schemes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:7} % *PROVERBS-11-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-7}

			When a wicked man dieth, his expectation shall perish: and the hope of unjust men perisheth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּמ֤וֹת אָדָ֣ם רָ֭שָׁע תֹּאבַ֣ד תִּקְוָ֑ה וְתוֹחֶ֖לֶת אוֹנִ֣ים אָבָֽדָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When a wicked man dieth, his expectation shall perish; And the hope of iniquity perisheth.
					\item[NIV] Hopes placed in mortals die with them; all the promise of their power comes to nothing.
					\item[NRSV] When the wicked die, their hope perishes, and the expectation of the godless comes to nothing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:8} % *PROVERBS-11-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-8}

			The righteous is delivered out of trouble, and the wicked cometh in his stead.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צַ֭דִּיק מִצָּרָ֣ה נֶחֱלָ֑ץ וַיָּבֹ֖א רָשָׁ֣ע תַּחְתָּֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteous is delivered out of trouble, And the wicked cometh in his stead.
					\item[NIV] The righteous person is rescued from trouble, and it falls on the wicked instead.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous are delivered from trouble, and the wicked get into it instead.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:9} % *PROVERBS-11-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-9}

			An hypocrite with his mouth destroyeth his neighbour: but through knowledge shall the just be delivered.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּפֶ֗ה חָ֭נֵף יַשְׁחִ֣ת רֵעֵ֑הוּ וּ֝בְדַ֗עַת צַדִּיקִ֥ים יֵחָלֵֽצוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] With his mouth the godless man destroyeth his neighbor; But through knowledge shall the righteous be delivered.
					\item[NIV] With their mouths the godless destroy their neighbors, but through knowledge the righteous escape.
					\item[NRSV] With their mouths the godless would destroy their neighbors, but by knowledge the righteous are delivered.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:10} % *PROVERBS-11-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-10}

			When it goeth well with the righteous, the city rejoiceth: and when the wicked perish, there is shouting.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּט֣וּב צַ֭דִּיקִים תַּעֲלֹ֣ץ קִרְיָ֑ה וּבַאֲבֹ֖ד רְשָׁעִ֣ים רִנָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When it goeth well with the righteous, the city rejoiceth; And when the wicked perish, there is shouting.
					\item[NIV] When the righteous prosper, the city rejoices; when the wicked perish, there are shouts of joy.
					\item[NRSV] When it goes well with the righteous, the city rejoices; and when the wicked perish, there is jubilation.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:11} % *PROVERBS-11-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-11}

			By the blessing of the upright the city is exalted: but it is overthrown by the mouth of the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּבִרְכַּ֣ת יְ֭שָׁרִים תָּר֣וּם קָ֑רֶת וּבְפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים תֵּהָרֵֽס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By the blessing of the upright the city is exalted; But it is overthrown by the mouth of the wicked.
					\item[NIV] Through the blessing of the upright a city is exalted, but by the mouth of the wicked it is destroyed.
					\item[NRSV] By the blessing of the upright a city is exalted, but it is overthrown by the mouth of the wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:12} % *PROVERBS-11-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-12}

			He that is void of wisdom despiseth his neighbour: but a man of understanding holdeth his peace.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בָּז־לְרֵעֵ֥הוּ חֲסַר־לֵ֑ב וְאִ֖ישׁ תְּבוּנ֣וֹת יַחֲרִֽישׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that despiseth his neighbor is void of wisdom; But a man of understanding holdeth his peace.
					\item[NIV] Whoever derides their neighbor has no sense, but the one who has understanding holds their tongue.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever belittles another lacks sense, but an intelligent person remains silent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:13} % *PROVERBS-11-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-13}

			A talebearer revealeth secrets: but he that is of a faithful spirit concealeth the matter.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הוֹלֵ֣ךְ רָ֭כִיל מְגַלֶּה־סּ֑וֹד וְנֶאֱמַן־ר֝֗וּחַ מְכַסֶּ֥ה דָבָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that goeth about as a tale-bearer revealeth secrets; But he that is of a faithful spirit concealeth a matter.
					\item[NIV] A gossip betrays a confidence, but a trustworthy person keeps a secret.
					\item[NRSV] A gossip goes about telling secrets, but one who is trustworthy in spirit keeps a confidence.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:14} % *PROVERBS-11-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-14}

			Where no counsel is, the people fall: but in the multitude of counsellors there is safety.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֵ֣ין תַּ֭חְבֻּלוֹת יִפָּל־עָ֑ם וּ֝תְשׁוּעָ֗ה בְּרֹ֣ב יוֹעֵֽץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Where no wise guidance is, the people falleth; But in the multitude of counsellors there is safety.
					\item[NIV] For lack of guidance a nation falls, but victory is won through many advisers.
					\item[NRSV] Where there is no guidance, a nation falls, but in an abundance of counselors there is safety.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:15} % *PROVERBS-11-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-15}

			He that is surety for a stranger shall smart for it: and he that hateth suretiship is sure.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַע־יֵ֭רוֹעַ כִּי־עָ֣רַב זָ֑ר וְשֹׂנֵ֖א תֹקְעִ֣ים בּוֹטֵֽחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is surety for a stranger shall smart for it; But he that hateth suretyship is secure.
					\item[NIV] Whoever puts up security for a stranger will surely suffer, but whoever refuses to shake hands in pledge is safe.
					\item[NRSV] To guarantee loans for a stranger brings trouble, but there is safety in refusing to do so.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:16} % *PROVERBS-11-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-16}

			A gracious woman retaineth honour: and strong men retain riches.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵֽשֶׁת־חֵ֭ן תִּתְמֹ֣ךְ כָּב֑וֹד וְ֝עָרִיצִ֗ים יִתְמְכוּ־עֹֽשֶׁר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A gracious woman obtaineth honor; And violent men obtain riches.
					\item[NIV] A kindhearted woman gains honor, but ruthless men gain only wealth.
					\item[NRSV] A gracious woman gets honor, but she who hates virtue is covered with shame. The timid become destitute, but the aggressive gain riches.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:17} % *PROVERBS-11-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-17}

			The merciful man doeth good to his own soul: but he that is cruel troubleth his own flesh.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גֹּמֵ֣ל נַ֭פְשׁוֹ אִ֣ישׁ חָ֑סֶד וְעֹכֵ֥ר שְׁ֝אֵר֗וֹ אַכְזָרִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The merciful man doeth good to his own soul; But he that is cruel troubleth his own flesh.
					\item[NIV] Those who are kind benefit themselves, but the cruel bring ruin on themselves.
					\item[NRSV] Those who are kind reward themselves, but the cruel do themselves harm.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:18} % *PROVERBS-11-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-18}

			The wicked worketh a deceitful work: but to him that soweth righteousness shall be a sure reward.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָשָׁ֗ע עֹשֶׂ֥ה פְעֻלַּת־שָׁ֑קֶר וְזֹרֵ֥עַ צְ֝דָקָ֗ה שֶׂ֣כֶר אֱמֶֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wicked earneth deceitful wages; But he that soweth righteousness hath a sure reward.
					\item[NIV] A wicked person earns deceptive wages, but the one who sows righteousness reaps a sure reward.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked earn no real gain, but those who sow righteousness get a true reward.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:19} % *PROVERBS-11-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-19}

			As righteousness tendeth to life: so he that pursueth evil pursueth it to his own death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֵּן־צְדָקָ֥ה לְחַיִּ֑ים וּמְרַדֵּ֖ף רָעָ֣ה לְמוֹתֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is stedfast in righteousness shall attain unto life; And he that pursueth evil doeth it to his own death.
					\item[NIV] Truly the righteous attain life, but whoever pursues evil finds death.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever is steadfast in righteousness will live, but whoever pursues evil will die.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:20} % *PROVERBS-11-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-20}

			They that are of a froward heart are abomination to the LORD: but such as are upright in their way are his delight.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה עִקְּשֵׁי־לֵ֑ב וּ֝רְצוֹנ֗וֹ תְּמִ֣ימֵי דָֽרֶךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They that are perverse in heart are an abomination to Jehovah; But such as are perfect in their way are his delight.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests those whose hearts are perverse, but he delights in those whose ways are blameless.
					\item[NRSV] Crooked minds are an abomination to the LORD, but those of blameless ways are his delight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:21} % *PROVERBS-11-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-21}

			Though hand join in hand, the wicked shall not be unpunished: but the seed of the righteous shall be delivered.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָ֣ד לְ֭יָד לֹא־יִנָּ֣קֶה רָּ֑ע וְזֶ֖רַע צַדִּיקִ֣ים נִמְלָֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Though hand join in hand, the evil man shall not be unpunished; But the seed of the righteous shall be delivered.
					\item[NIV] Be sure of this: The wicked will not go unpunished, but those who are righteous will go free.
					\item[NRSV] Be assured, the wicked will not go unpunished, but those who are righteous will escape.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:22} % *PROVERBS-11-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-22}

			As a jewel of gold in a swine’s snout, so is a fair woman which is without discretion.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶ֣זֶם זָ֭הָב בְּאַ֣ף חֲזִ֑יר אִשָּׁ֥ה יָ֝פָ֗ה וְסָ֣רַת טָֽעַם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a ring of gold in a swine's snout, So is a fair woman that is without discretion.
					\item[NIV] Like a gold ring in a pig’s snout is a beautiful woman who shows no discretion.
					\item[NRSV] Like a gold ring in a pig’s snout is a beautiful woman without good sense.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:23} % *PROVERBS-11-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-23}

			The desire of the righteous is only good: but the expectation of the wicked is wrath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּאֲוַ֣ת צַדִּיקִ֣ים אַךְ־ט֑וֹב תִּקְוַ֖ת רְשָׁעִ֣ים עֶבְרָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The desire of the righteous is only good; But the expectation of the wicked is wrath.
					\item[NIV] The desire of the righteous ends only in good, but the hope of the wicked only in wrath.
					\item[NRSV] The desire of the righteous ends only in good; the expectation of the wicked in wrath.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:24} % *PROVERBS-11-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-24}

			There is that scattereth, and yet increaseth; and there is that withholdeth more than is meet, but it tendeth to poverty.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֣שׁ מְ֭פַזֵּר וְנוֹסָ֥ף ע֑וֹד וְחוֹשֵׂ֥ךְ מִ֝יֹּ֗שֶׁר אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is that scattereth, and increaseth yet more; And there is that withholdeth more than is meet, but it tendeth only to want.
					\item[NIV] One person gives freely, yet gains even more; another withholds unduly, but comes to poverty.
					\item[NRSV] Some give freely, yet grow all the richer; others withhold what is due, and only suffer want.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:25} % *PROVERBS-11-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-25}

			The liberal soul shall be made fat: and he that watereth shall be watered also himself.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶֽפֶשׁ־בְּרָכָ֥ה תְדֻשָּׁ֑ן וּ֝מַרְוֶ֗ה גַּם־ה֥וּא יוֹרֶֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The liberal soul shall be made fat; And he that watereth shall be watered also himself.
					\item[NIV] A generous person will prosper; whoever refreshes others will be refreshed.
					\item[NRSV] A generous person will be enriched, and one who gives water will get water.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:26} % *PROVERBS-11-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-26}

			He that withholdeth corn, the people shall curse him: but blessing shall be upon the head of him that selleth it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֹ֣נֵֽעַ בָּ֭ר יִקְּבֻ֣הוּ לְא֑וֹם וּ֝בְרָכָ֗ה לְרֹ֣אשׁ מַשְׁבִּֽיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that withholdeth grain, the people shall curse him; But blessing shall be upon the head of him that selleth it.
					\item[NIV] People curse the one who hoards grain, but they pray God’s blessing on the one who is willing to sell.
					\item[NRSV] The people curse those who hold back grain, but a blessing is on the head of those who sell it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:27} % *PROVERBS-11-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-27}

			He that diligently seeketh good procureth favour: but he that seeketh mischief, it shall come unto him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֹׁ֣חֵֽר ט֭וֹב יְבַקֵּ֣שׁ רָצ֑וֹן וְדֹרֵ֖שׁ רָעָ֣ה תְבוֹאֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that diligently seeketh good seeketh favor; But he that searcheth after evil, it shall come unto him.
					\item[NIV] Whoever seeks good finds favor, but evil comes to one who searches for it.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever diligently seeks good seeks favor, but evil comes to the one who searches for it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:28} % *PROVERBS-11-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-28}

			He that trusteth in his riches shall fall; but the righteous shall flourish as a branch.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בּוֹטֵ֣חַ בְּ֭עָשְׁרוֹ ה֣וּא יִפֹּ֑ל וְ֝כֶעָלֶ֗ה צַדִּיקִ֥ים יִפְרָֽחוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that trusteth in his riches shall fall; But the righteous shall flourish as the green leaf.
					\item[NIV] Those who trust in their riches will fall, but the righteous will thrive like a green leaf.
					\item[NRSV] Those who trust in their riches will wither, but the righteous will flourish like green leaves.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:29} % *PROVERBS-11-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-29}

			He that troubleth his own house shall inherit the wind: and the fool shall be servant to the wise of heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עוֹכֵ֣ר בֵּ֭יתוֹ יִנְחַל־ר֑וּחַ וְעֶ֥בֶד אֱ֝וִ֗יל לַחֲכַם־לֵֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that troubleth his own house shall inherit the wind; And the foolish shall be servant to the wise of heart.
					\item[NIV] Whoever brings ruin on their family will inherit only wind, and the fool will be servant to the wise.
					\item[NRSV] Those who trouble their households will inherit wind, and the fool will be servant to the wise.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:30} % *PROVERBS-11-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-30}

			The fruit of the righteous is a tree of life; and he that winneth souls is wise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פְּֽרִי־צַ֭דִּיק עֵ֣ץ חַיִּ֑ים וְלֹקֵ֖חַ נְפָשׂ֣וֹת חָכָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fruit of the righteous is a tree of life; And he that is wise winneth souls.
					\item[NIV] The fruit of the righteous is a tree of life, and the one who is wise saves lives.
					\item[NRSV] The fruit of the righteous is a tree of life, but violence takes lives away.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 11:31} % *PROVERBS-11-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-11-31}

			Behold, the righteous shall be recompensed in the earth: much more the wicked and the sinner.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵ֣ן צַ֭דִּיק בָּאָ֣רֶץ יְשֻׁלָּ֑ם אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־רָשָׁ֥ע וְחוֹטֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Behold, the righteous shall be recompensed in the earth: How much more the wicked and the sinner!
					\item[NIV] If the righteous receive their due on earth, how much more the ungodly and the sinner!
					\item[NRSV] If the righteous are repaid on earth, how much more the wicked and the sinner!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 12} % *PROVERBS-12
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-12}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:1} % *PROVERBS-12-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-1}

			Whoso loveth instruction loveth knowledge: but he that hateth reproof is brutish.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹהֵ֣ב מ֭וּסָר אֹ֣הֵֽב דָּ֑עַת וְשֹׂנֵ֖א תוֹכַ֣חַת בָּֽעַר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso loveth correction loveth knowledge; But he that hateth reproof is brutish.
					\item[NIV] Whoever loves discipline loves knowledge, but whoever hates correction is stupid.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever loves discipline loves knowledge, but those who hate to be rebuked are stupid.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:2} % *PROVERBS-12-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-2}

			A good man obtaineth favour of the LORD: but a man of wicked devices will he condemn.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֗וֹב יָפִ֣יק רָ֭צוֹן מֵיְהוָ֑ה וְאִ֖ישׁ מְזִמּ֣וֹת יַרְשִֽׁיעַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A good man shall obtain favor of Jehovah; But a man of wicked devices will he condemn.
					\item[NIV] Good people obtain favor from the LORD, but he condemns those who devise wicked schemes.
					\item[NRSV] The good obtain favor from the LORD, but those who devise evil he condemns.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:3} % *PROVERBS-12-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-3}

			A man shall not be established by wickedness: but the root of the righteous shall not be moved.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יִכּ֣וֹן אָדָ֣ם בְּרֶ֑שַׁע וְשֹׁ֥רֶשׁ צַ֝דִּיקִ֗ים בַּל־יִמּֽוֹט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man shall not be established by wickedness; But the root of the righteous shall not be moved.
					\item[NIV] No one can be established through wickedness, but the righteous cannot be uprooted.
					\item[NRSV] No one finds security by wickedness, but the root of the righteous will never be moved.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:4} % *PROVERBS-12-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-4}

			A virtuous woman is a crown to her husband: but she that maketh ashamed is as rottenness in his bones.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵֽשֶׁת־חַ֭יִל עֲטֶ֣רֶת בַּעְלָ֑הּ וּכְרָקָ֖ב בְּעַצְמוֹתָ֣יו מְבִישָֽׁה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A worthy woman is the crown of her husband; But she that maketh ashamed is as rottenness in his bones.
					\item[NIV] A wife of noble character is her husband’s crown, but a disgraceful wife is like decay in his bones.
					\item[NRSV] A good wife is the crown of her husband, but she who brings shame is like rottenness in his bones.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:5} % *PROVERBS-12-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-5}

			The thoughts of the righteous are right: but the counsels of the wicked are deceit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת צַדִּיקִ֣ים מִשְׁפָּ֑ט תַּחְבֻּל֖וֹת רְשָׁעִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The thoughts of the righteous are just; But the counsels of the wicked are deceit.
					\item[NIV] The plans of the righteous are just, but the advice of the wicked is deceitful.
					\item[NRSV] The thoughts of the righteous are just; the advice of the wicked is treacherous.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:6} % *PROVERBS-12-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-6}

			The words of the wicked are to lie in wait for blood: but the mouth of the upright shall deliver them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּבְרֵ֣י רְשָׁעִ֣ים אֱרָב־דָּ֑ם וּפִ֥י יְ֝שָׁרִ֗ים יַצִּילֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of the wicked are of lying in wait for blood; But the mouth of the upright shall deliver them.
					\item[NIV] The words of the wicked lie in wait for blood, but the speech of the upright rescues them.
					\item[NRSV] The words of the wicked are a deadly ambush, but the speech of the upright delivers them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:7} % *PROVERBS-12-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-7}

			The wicked are overthrown, and are not: but the house of the righteous shall stand.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָפ֣וֹךְ רְשָׁעִ֣ים וְאֵינָ֑ם וּבֵ֖ית צַדִּיקִ֣ים יַעֲמֹֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wicked are overthrown, and are not; But the house of the righteous shall stand.
					\item[NIV] The wicked are overthrown and are no more, but the house of the righteous stands firm.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked are overthrown and are no more, but the house of the righteous will stand.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:8} % *PROVERBS-12-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-8}

			A man shall be commended according to his wisdom: but he that is of a perverse heart shall be despised.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְֽפִי־שִׂ֭כְלוֹ יְהֻלַּל־אִ֑ישׁ וְנַעֲוֵה־לֵ֝֗ב יִהְיֶ֥ה לָבֽוּז׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man shall be commended according to his wisdom; But he that is of a perverse heart shall be despised.
					\item[NIV] A person is praised according to their prudence, and one with a warped mind is despised.
					\item[NRSV] One is commended for good sense, but a perverse mind is despised.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:9} % *PROVERBS-12-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-9}

			He that is despised, and hath a servant, is better than he that honoureth himself, and lacketh bread.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֣וֹב נִ֭קְלֶה וְעֶ֣בֶד ל֑וֹ מִ֝מְּתַכַּבֵּ֗ד וַחֲסַר־לָֽחֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is he that is lightly esteemed, and hath a servant, Than he that honoreth himself, and lacketh bread.
					\item[NIV] Better to be a nobody and yet have a servant than pretend to be somebody and have no food.
					\item[NRSV] Better to be despised and have a servant, than to be self-important and lack food.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:10} % *PROVERBS-12-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-10}

			A righteous man regardeth the life of his beast: but the tender mercies of the wicked are cruel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יוֹדֵ֣עַ צַ֭דִּיק נֶ֣פֶשׁ בְּהֶמְתּ֑וֹ וְֽרַחֲמֵ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים אַכְזָרִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A righteous man regardeth the life of his beast; But the tender mercies of the wicked are cruel.
					\item[NIV] The righteous care for the needs of their animals, but the kindest acts of the wicked are cruel.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous know the needs of their animals, but the mercy of the wicked is cruel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:11} % *PROVERBS-12-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-11}

			He that tilleth his land shall be satisfied with bread: but he that followeth vain persons is void of understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹבֵ֣ד אַ֭דְמָתוֹ יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־לָ֑חֶם וּמְרַדֵּ֖ף רֵיקִ֣ים חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that tilleth his land shall have plenty of bread; But he that followeth after vain persons is void of understanding.
					\item[NIV] Those who work their land will have abundant food, but those who chase fantasies have no sense.
					\item[NRSV] Those who till their land will have plenty of food, but those who follow worthless pursuits have no sense.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:12} % *PROVERBS-12-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-12}

			The wicked desireth the net of evil men: but the root of the righteous yieldeth fruit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָמַ֣ד רָ֭שָׁע מְצ֣וֹד רָעִ֑ים וְשֹׁ֖רֶשׁ צַדִּיקִ֣ים יִתֵּֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wicked desireth the net of evil men; But the root of the righteous yieldeth fruit.
					\item[NIV] The wicked desire the stronghold of evildoers, but the root of the righteous endures.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked covet the proceeds of wickedness, but the root of the righteous bears fruit.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:13} % *PROVERBS-12-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-13}

			The wicked is snared by the transgression of his lips: but the just shall come out of trouble.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּפֶ֣שַׁע שְׂ֭פָתַיִם מוֹקֵ֣שׁ רָ֑ע וַיֵּצֵ֖א מִצָּרָ֣ה צַדִּֽיק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the transgression of the lips is a snare to the evil man; But the righteous shall come out of trouble.
					\item[NIV] Evildoers are trapped by their sinful talk, and so the innocent escape trouble.
					\item[NRSV] The evil are ensnared by the transgression of their lips, but the righteous escape from trouble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:14} % *PROVERBS-12-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-14}

			A man shall be satisfied with good by the fruit of his mouth: and the recompence of a man’s hands shall be rendered unto him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִפְּרִ֣י פִי־אִ֭ישׁ יִשְׂבַּע־ט֑וֹב וּגְמ֥וּל יְדֵי־אָ֝דָ֗ם יָשִׁ֥יב לֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man shall be satisfied with good by the fruit of his mouth; And the doings of a man's hands shall be rendered unto him.
					\item[NIV] From the fruit of their lips people are filled with good things, and the work of their hands brings them reward.
					\item[NRSV] From the fruit of the mouth one is filled with good things, and manual labor has its reward.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:15} % *PROVERBS-12-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-15}

			The way of a fool is right in his own eyes: but he that hearkeneth unto counsel is wise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דֶּ֣רֶךְ אֱ֭וִיל יָשָׁ֣ר בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְשֹׁמֵ֖עַ לְעֵצָ֣ה חָכָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The way of a fool is right in his own eyes; But he that is wise hearkeneth unto counsel.
					\item[NIV] The way of fools seems right to them, but the wise listen to advice.
					\item[NRSV] Fools think their own way is right, but the wise listen to advice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:16} % *PROVERBS-12-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-16}

			A fool’s wrath is presently known: but a prudent man covereth shame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱוִ֗יל בַּ֭יּוֹם יִוָּדַ֣ע כַּעְס֑וֹ וְכֹסֶ֖ה קָל֣וֹן עָרֽוּם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool's vexation is presently known; But a prudent man concealeth shame.
					\item[NIV] Fools show their annoyance at once, but the prudent overlook an insult.
					\item[NRSV] Fools show their anger at once, but the prudent ignore an insult.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:17} % *PROVERBS-12-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-17}

			He that speaketh truth sheweth forth righteousness: but a false witness deceit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָפִ֣יחַ אֱ֭מוּנָה יַגִּ֣יד צֶ֑דֶק וְעֵ֖ד שְׁקָרִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that uttereth truth showeth forth righteousness; But a false witness, deceit.
					\item[NIV] An honest witness tells the truth, but a false witness tells lies.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever speaks the truth gives honest evidence, but a false witness speaks deceitfully.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:18} % *PROVERBS-12-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-18}

			There is that speaketh like the piercings of a sword: but the tongue of the wise is health.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֣שׁ בּ֭וֹטֶה כְּמַדְקְר֣וֹת חָ֑רֶב וּלְשׁ֖וֹן חֲכָמִ֣ים מַרְפֵּֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is that speaketh rashly like the piercings of a sword; But the tongue of the wise is health.
					\item[NIV] The words of the reckless pierce like swords, but the tongue of the wise brings healing.
					\item[NRSV] Rash words are like sword thrusts, but the tongue of the wise brings healing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:19} % *PROVERBS-12-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-19}

			The lip of truth shall be established for ever: but a lying tongue is but for a moment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְֽׂפַת־אֱ֭מֶת תִּכּ֣וֹן לָעַ֑ד וְעַד־אַ֝רְגִּ֗יעָה לְשׁ֣וֹן שָֽׁקֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lip of truth shall be established for ever; But a lying tongue is but for a moment.
					\item[NIV] Truthful lips endure forever, but a lying tongue lasts only a moment.
					\item[NRSV] Truthful lips endure forever, but a lying tongue lasts only a moment.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:20} % *PROVERBS-12-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-20}

			Deceit is in the heart of them that imagine evil: but to the counsellors of peace is joy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭רְמָה בְּלֶב־חֹ֣רְשֵׁי רָ֑ע וּֽלְיֹעֲצֵ֖י שָׁל֣וֹם שִׂמְחָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Deceit is in the heart of them that devise evil; But to the counsellors of peace is joy.
					\item[NIV] Deceit is in the hearts of those who plot evil, but those who promote peace have joy.
					\item[NRSV] Deceit is in the mind of those who plan evil, but those who counsel peace have joy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:21} % *PROVERBS-12-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-21}

			There shall no evil happen to the just: but the wicked shall be filled with mischief.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יְאֻנֶּ֣ה לַצַּדִּ֣יק כָּל־אָ֑וֶן וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים מָ֣לְאוּ רָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There shall no mischief happen to the righteous; But the wicked shall be filled with evil.
					\item[NIV] No harm overtakes the righteous, but the wicked have their fill of trouble.
					\item[NRSV] No harm happens to the righteous, but the wicked are filled with trouble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:22} % *PROVERBS-12-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-22}

			Lying lips are abomination to the LORD: but they that deal truly are his delight.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה שִׂפְתֵי־שָׁ֑קֶר וְעֹשֵׂ֖י אֱמוּנָ֣ה רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lying lips are an abomination to Jehovah; But they that deal truly are his delight.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests lying lips, but he delights in people who are trustworthy.
					\item[NRSV] Lying lips are an abomination to the LORD, but those who act faithfully are his delight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:23} % *PROVERBS-12-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-23}

			A prudent man concealeth knowledge: but the heart of fools proclaimeth foolishness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָדָ֣ם עָ֭רוּם כֹּ֣סֶה דָּ֑עַת וְלֵ֥ב כְּ֝סִילִ֗ים יִקְרָ֥א אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A prudent man concealeth knowledge; But the heart of fools proclaimeth foolishness.
					\item[NIV] The prudent keep their knowledge to themselves, but a fool’s heart blurts out folly.
					\item[NRSV] One who is clever conceals knowledge, but the mind of a fool broadcasts folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:24} % *PROVERBS-12-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-24}

			The hand of the diligent shall bear rule: but the slothful shall be under tribute.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַד־חָרוּצִ֥ים תִּמְשׁ֑וֹל ו֝רְמִיָּ֗ה תִּהְיֶ֥ה לָמַֽס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The hand of the diligent shall bear rule; But the slothful shall be put under taskwork.
					\item[NIV] Diligent hands will rule, but laziness ends in forced labor.
					\item[NRSV] The hand of the diligent will rule, while the lazy will be put to forced labor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:25} % *PROVERBS-12-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-25}

			Heaviness in the heart of man maketh it stoop: but a good word maketh it glad.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דְּאָגָ֣ה בְלֶב־אִ֣ישׁ יַשְׁחֶ֑נָּה וְדָבָ֖ר ט֣וֹב יְשַׂמְּחֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Heaviness in the heart of a man maketh it stoop; But a good word maketh it glad.
					\item[NIV] Anxiety weighs down the heart, but a kind word cheers it up.
					\item[NRSV] Anxiety weighs down the human heart, but a good word cheers it up.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:26} % *PROVERBS-12-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-26}

			The righteous is more excellent than his neighbour: but the way of the wicked seduceth them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָתֵ֣ר מֵרֵעֵ֣הוּ צַדִּ֑יק וְדֶ֖רֶךְ רְשָׁעִ֣ים תַּתְעֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteous is a guide to his neighbor; But the way of the wicked causeth them to err.
					\item[NIV] The righteous choose their friends carefully, but the way of the wicked leads them astray.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous gives good advice to friends, but the way of the wicked leads astray.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:27} % *PROVERBS-12-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-27}

			The slothful man roasteth not that which he took in hunting: but the substance of a diligent man is precious.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־יַחֲרֹ֣ךְ רְמִיָּ֣ה צֵיד֑וֹ וְהוֹן־אָדָ֖ם יָקָ֣ר חָרֽוּץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The slothful man roasteth not that which he took in hunting; But the precious substance of men is to the diligent.
					\item[NIV] The lazy do not roast any game, but the diligent feed on the riches of the hunt.
					\item[NRSV] The lazy do not roast their game, but the diligent obtain precious wealth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 12:28} % *PROVERBS-12-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-12-28}

			In the way of righteousness is life: and in the pathway thereof there is no death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֹֽרַח־צְדָקָ֥ה חַיִּ֑ים וְדֶ֖רֶךְ נְתִיבָ֣ה אַל־מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the way of righteousness is life; And in the pathway thereof there is no death.
					\item[NIV] In the way of righteousness there is life; along that path is immortality.
					\item[NRSV] In the path of righteousness there is life, in walking its path there is no death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 13} % *PROVERBS-13
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-13}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:1} % *PROVERBS-13-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-1}

			A wise son heareth his father’s instruction: but a scorner heareth not rebuke.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּ֣ן חָ֭כָם מ֣וּסַר אָ֑ב וְ֝לֵ֗ץ לֹא־שָׁמַ֥ע גְּעָרָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise son heareth his father's instruction; But a scoffer heareth not rebuke.
					\item[NIV] A wise son heeds his father’s instruction, but a mocker does not respond to rebukes.
					\item[NRSV] A wise child loves discipline, but a scoffer does not listen to rebuke.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:2} % *PROVERBS-13-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-2}

			A man shall eat good by the fruit of his mouth: but the soul of the transgressors shall eat violence.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִפְּרִ֣י פִי־אִ֭ישׁ יֹ֣אכַל ט֑וֹב וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ בֹּגְדִ֣ים חָמָֽס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man shall eat good by the fruit of his mouth; But the soul of the treacherous shall eat violence.
					\item[NIV] From the fruit of their lips people enjoy good things, but the unfaithful have an appetite for violence.
					\item[NRSV] From the fruit of their words good persons eat good things, but the desire of the treacherous is for wrongdoing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:3} % *PROVERBS-13-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-3}

			He that keepeth his mouth keepeth his life: but he that openeth wide his lips shall have destruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֹצֵ֣ר פִּ֭יו שֹׁמֵ֣ר נַפְשׁ֑וֹ פֹּשֵׂ֥ק שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו מְחִתָּה־לֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that guardeth his mouth keepeth his life; But he that openeth wide his lips shall have destruction.
					\item[NIV] Those who guard their lips preserve their lives, but those who speak rashly will come to ruin.
					\item[NRSV] Those who guard their mouths preserve their lives; those who open wide their lips come to ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:4} % *PROVERBS-13-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-4}

			The soul of the sluggard desireth, and hath nothing: but the soul of the diligent shall be made fat.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִתְאַוָּ֣ה וָ֭אַיִן נַפְשׁ֣וֹ עָצֵ֑ל וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ חָרֻצִ֣ים תְּדֻשָּֽׁן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The soul of the sluggard desireth, and hath nothing; But the soul of the diligent shall be made fat.
					\item[NIV] A sluggard’s appetite is never filled, but the desires of the diligent are fully satisfied.
					\item[NRSV] The appetite of the lazy craves, and gets nothing, while the appetite of the diligent is richly supplied.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:5} % *PROVERBS-13-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-5}

			A righteous man hateth lying: but a wicked man is loathsome, and cometh to shame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דְּבַר־שֶׁ֭קֶר יִשְׂנָ֣א צַדִּ֑יק וְ֝רָשָׁ֗ע יַבְאִ֥ישׁ וְיַחְפִּֽיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A righteous man hateth lying; But a wicked man is loathsome, and cometh to shame.
					\item[NIV] The righteous hate what is false, but the wicked make themselves a stench and bring shame on themselves.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous hate falsehood, but the wicked act shamefully and disgracefully.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:6} % *PROVERBS-13-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-6}

			Righteousness keepeth him that is upright in the way: but wickedness overthroweth the sinner.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צְ֭דָקָה תִּצֹּ֣ר תָּם־דָּ֑רֶךְ וְ֝רִשְׁעָ֗ה תְּסַלֵּ֥ף חַטָּֽאת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Righteousness guardeth him that is upright in the way; But wickedness overthroweth the sinner.
					\item[NIV] Righteousness guards the person of integrity, but wickedness overthrows the sinner.
					\item[NRSV] Righteousness guards one whose way is upright, but sin overthrows the wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:7} % *PROVERBS-13-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-7}

			There is that maketh himself rich, yet hath nothing: there is that maketh himself poor, yet hath great riches.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֣שׁ מִ֭תְעַשֵּׁר וְאֵ֣ין כֹּ֑ל מִ֝תְרוֹשֵׁ֗שׁ וְה֣וֹן רָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is that maketh himself rich, yet hath nothing: There is that maketh himself poor, yet hath great wealth.
					\item[NIV] One person pretends to be rich, yet has nothing; another pretends to be poor, yet has great wealth.
					\item[NRSV] Some pretend to be rich, yet have nothing; others pretend to be poor, yet have great wealth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:8} % *PROVERBS-13-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-8}

			The ransom of a man’s life are his riches: but the poor heareth not rebuke.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֹּ֣פֶר נֶֽפֶשׁ־אִ֣ישׁ עָשְׁר֑וֹ וְ֝רָ֗שׁ לֹא־שָׁמַ֥ע גְּעָרָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The ransom of a man's life is his riches; But the poor heareth no threatening.
					\item[NIV] A person’s riches may ransom their life, but the poor cannot respond to threatening rebukes.
					\item[NRSV] Wealth is a ransom for a person’s life, but the poor get no threats.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:9} % *PROVERBS-13-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-9}

			The light of the righteous rejoiceth: but the lamp of the wicked shall be put out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אוֹר־צַדִּיקִ֥ים יִשְׂמָ֑ח וְנֵ֖ר רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִדְעָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The light of the righteous rejoiceth; But the lamp of the wicked shall be put out.
					\item[NIV] The light of the righteous shines brightly, but the lamp of the wicked is snuffed out.
					\item[NRSV] The light of the righteous rejoices, but the lamp of the wicked goes out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:10} % *PROVERBS-13-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-10}

			Only by pride cometh contention: but with the well advised is wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַק־בְּ֭זָדוֹן יִתֵּ֣ן מַצָּ֑ה וְאֶת־נ֖וֹעָצִ֣ים חָכְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By pride cometh only contention; But with the well-advised is wisdom.
					\item[NIV] Where there is strife, there is pride, but wisdom is found in those who take advice.
					\item[NRSV] By insolence the heedless make strife, but wisdom is with those who take advice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:11} % *PROVERBS-13-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-11}

			Wealth gotten by vanity shall be diminished: but he that gathereth by labour shall increase.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֭וֹן מֵהֶ֣בֶל יִמְעָ֑ט וְקֹבֵ֖ץ עַל־יָ֣ד יַרְבֶּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wealth gotten by vanity shall be diminished; But he that gathereth by labor shall have increase.
					\item[NIV] Dishonest money dwindles away, but whoever gathers money little by little makes it grow.
					\item[NRSV] Wealth hastily gotten will dwindle, but those who gather little by little will increase it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:12} % *PROVERBS-13-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-12}

			Hope deferred maketh the heart sick: but when the desire cometh, it is a tree of life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹחֶ֣לֶת מְ֭מֻשָּׁכָה מַחֲלָה־לֵ֑ב וְעֵ֥ץ חַ֝יִּ֗ים תַּאֲוָ֥ה בָאָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hope deferred maketh the heart sick; But when the desire cometh, it is a tree of life.
					\item[NIV] Hope deferred makes the heart sick, but a longing fulfilled is a tree of life.
					\item[NRSV] Hope deferred makes the heart sick, but a desire fulfilled is a tree of life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:13} % *PROVERBS-13-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-13}

			Whoso despiseth the word shall be destroyed: but he that feareth the commandment shall be rewarded.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בָּ֣ז לְ֭דָבָר יֵחָ֣בֶל ל֑וֹ וִירֵ֥א מִ֝צְוָ֗ה ה֣וּא יְשֻׁלָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso despiseth the word bringeth destruction on himself; But he that feareth the commandment shall be rewarded.
					\item[NIV] Whoever scorns instruction will pay for it, but whoever respects a command is rewarded.
					\item[NRSV] Those who despise the word bring destruction on themselves, but those who respect the commandment will be rewarded.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:14} % *PROVERBS-13-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-14}

			The law of the wise is a fountain of life, to depart from the snares of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹרַ֣ת חָ֭כָם מְק֣וֹר חַיִּ֑ים לָ֝ס֗וּר מִמֹּ֥קְשֵׁי מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The law of the wise is a fountain of life, That one may depart from the snares of death.
					\item[NIV] The teaching of the wise is a fountain of life, turning a person from the snares of death.
					\item[NRSV] The teaching of the wise is a fountain of life, so that one may avoid the snares of death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:15} % *PROVERBS-13-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-15}

			Good understanding giveth favour: but the way of transgressors is hard.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֵֽׂכֶל־ט֭וֹב יִתֶּן־חֵ֑ן וְדֶ֖רֶךְ בֹּגְדִ֣ים אֵיתָֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Good understanding giveth favor; But the way of the transgressor is hard.
					\item[NIV] Good judgment wins favor, but the way of the unfaithful leads to their destruction.
					\item[NRSV] Good sense wins favor, but the way of the faithless is their ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:16} % *PROVERBS-13-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-16}

			Every prudent man dealeth with knowledge: but a fool layeth open his folly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־עָ֭רוּם יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה בְדָ֑עַת וּ֝כְסִ֗יל יִפְרֹ֥שׂ אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Every prudent man worketh with knowledge; But a fool flaunteth his folly.
					\item[NIV] All who are prudent act with knowledge, but fools expose their folly.
					\item[NRSV] The clever do all things intelligently, but the fool displays folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:17} % *PROVERBS-13-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-17}

			A wicked messenger falleth into mischief: but a faithful ambassador is health.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַלְאָ֣ךְ רָ֭שָׁע יִפֹּ֣ל בְּרָ֑ע וְצִ֖יר אֱמוּנִ֣ים מַרְפֵּֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wicked messenger falleth into evil; But a faithful ambassador is health.
					\item[NIV] A wicked messenger falls into trouble, but a trustworthy envoy brings healing.
					\item[NRSV] A bad messenger brings trouble, but a faithful envoy, healing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:18} % *PROVERBS-13-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-18}

			Poverty and shame shall be to him that refuseth instruction: but he that regardeth reproof shall be honoured.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רֵ֣ישׁ וְ֭קָלוֹן פּוֹרֵ֣עַ מוּסָ֑ר וְשׁוֹמֵ֖ר תּוֹכַ֣חַת יְכֻבָּֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Poverty and shame shall be to him that refuseth correction; But he that regardeth reproof shall be honored.
					\item[NIV] Whoever disregards discipline comes to poverty and shame, but whoever heeds correction is honored.
					\item[NRSV] Poverty and disgrace are for the one who ignores instruction, but one who heeds reproof is honored.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:19} % *PROVERBS-13-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-19}

			The desire accomplished is sweet to the soul: but it is abomination to fools to depart from evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּאֲוָ֣ה נִ֭הְיָה תֶּעֱרַ֣ב לְנָ֑פֶשׁ וְתוֹעֲבַ֥ת כְּ֝סִילִ֗ים ס֣וּר מֵרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The desire accomplished is sweet to the soul; But it is an abomination to fools to depart from evil.
					\item[NIV] A longing fulfilled is sweet to the soul, but fools detest turning from evil.
					\item[NRSV] A desire realized is sweet to the soul, but to turn away from evil is an abomination to fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:20} % *PROVERBS-13-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-20}

			He that walketh with wise men shall be wise: but a companion of fools shall be destroyed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הוֹלֵ֣ךְ אֶת־חֲכָמִ֣ים יֶחְכָּ֑ם וְרֹעֶ֖ה כְסִילִ֣ים יֵרֽוֹעַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Walk with wise men, and thou shalt be wise; But the companion of fools shall smart for it.
					\item[NIV] Walk with the wise and become wise, for a companion of fools suffers harm.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever walks with the wise becomes wise, but the companion of fools suffers harm.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:21} % *PROVERBS-13-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-21}

			Evil pursueth sinners: but to the righteous good shall be repayed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַ֭טָּאִים תְּרַדֵּ֣ף רָעָ֑ה וְאֶת־צַ֝דִּיקִ֗ים יְשַׁלֶּם־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Evil pursueth sinners; But the righteous shall be recompensed with good.
					\item[NIV] Trouble pursues the sinner, but the righteous are rewarded with good things.
					\item[NRSV] Misfortune pursues sinners, but prosperity rewards the righteous.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:22} % *PROVERBS-13-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-22}

			A good man leaveth an inheritance to his children’s children: and the wealth of the sinner is laid up for the just.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֗וֹב יַנְחִ֥יל בְּנֵֽי־בָנִ֑ים וְצָפ֥וּן לַ֝צַּדִּ֗יק חֵ֣יל חוֹטֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A good man leaveth an inheritance to his children's children; And the wealth of the sinner is laid up for the righteous.
					\item[NIV] A good person leaves an inheritance for their children’s children, but a sinner’s wealth is stored up for the righteous.
					\item[NRSV] The good leave an inheritance to their children’s children, but the sinner’s wealth is laid up for the righteous.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:23} % *PROVERBS-13-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-23}

			Much food is in the tillage of the poor: but there is that is destroyed for want of judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָב־אֹ֭כֶל נִ֣יר רָאשִׁ֑ים וְיֵ֥שׁ נִ֝סְפֶּ֗ה בְּלֹ֣א מִשְׁפָּֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Much food is in the tillage of the poor; But there is that is destroyed by reason of injustice.
					\item[NIV] An unplowed field produces food for the poor, but injustice sweeps it away.
					\item[NRSV] The field of the poor may yield much food, but it is swept away through injustice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:24} % *PROVERBS-13-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-24}

			He that spareth his rod hateth his son: but he that loveth him chasteneth him betimes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חוֹשֵׂ֣ךְ שִׁ֭בְטוֹ שׂוֹנֵ֣א בְנ֑וֹ וְ֝אֹהֲב֗וֹ שִֽׁחֲר֥וֹ מוּסָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that spareth his rod hateth his son; But he that loveth him chasteneth him betimes.
					\item[NIV] Whoever spares the rod hates their children, but the one who loves their children is careful to discipline them.
					\item[NRSV] Those who spare the rod hate their children, but those who love them are diligent to discipline them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 13:25} % *PROVERBS-13-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-13-25}

			The righteous eateth to the satisfying of his soul: but the belly of the wicked shall want.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צַדִּ֗יק אֹ֭כֵל לְשֹׂ֣בַע נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וּבֶ֖טֶן רְשָׁעִ֣ים תֶּחְסָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteous eateth to the satisfying of his soul; But the belly of the wicked shall want.
					\item[NIV] The righteous eat to their hearts’ content, but the stomach of the wicked goes hungry.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous have enough to satisfy their appetite, but the belly of the wicked is empty.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 14} % *PROVERBS-14
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-14}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:1} % *PROVERBS-14-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-1}

			Every wise woman buildeth her house: but the foolish plucketh it down with her hands.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַכְמ֣וֹת נָ֭שִׁים בָּנְתָ֣ה בֵיתָ֑הּ וְ֝אִוֶּ֗לֶת בְּיָדֶ֥יהָ תֶהֶרְסֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Every wise woman buildeth her house; But the foolish plucketh it down with her own hands.
					\item[NIV] The wise woman builds her house, but with her own hands the foolish one tears hers down.
					\item[NRSV] The wise woman builds her house, but the foolish tears it down with her own hands.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:2} % *PROVERBS-14-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-2}

			He that walketh in his uprightness feareth the LORD: but he that is perverse in his ways despiseth him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בְּ֭יָשְׁרוֹ יְרֵ֣א יְהוָ֑ה וּנְל֖וֹז דְּרָכָ֣יו בּוֹזֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that walketh in his uprightness feareth Jehovah; But he that is perverse in his ways despiseth him.
					\item[NIV] Whoever fears the LORD walks uprightly, but those who despise him are devious in their ways.
					\item[NRSV] Those who walk uprightly fear the LORD, but one who is devious in conduct despises him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:3} % *PROVERBS-14-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-3}

			In the mouth of the foolish is a rod of pride: but the lips of the wise shall preserve them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּֽפִי־אֱ֭וִיל חֹ֣טֶר גַּאֲוָ֑ה וְשִׂפְתֵ֥י חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים תִּשְׁמוּרֵֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the mouth of the foolish is a rod for his pride; But the lips of the wise shall preserve them.
					\item[NIV] A fool’s mouth lashes out with pride, but the lips of the wise protect them.
					\item[NRSV] The talk of fools is a rod for their backs, but the lips of the wise preserve them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:4} % *PROVERBS-14-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-4}

			Where no oxen are, the crib is clean: but much increase is by the strength of the ox.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֵ֣ין אֲ֭לָפִים אֵב֣וּס בָּ֑ר וְרָב־תְּ֝בוּא֗וֹת בְּכֹ֣חַ שֽׁוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Where no oxen are, the crib is clean; But much increase is by the strength of the ox.
					\item[NIV] Where there are no oxen, the manger is empty, but from the strength of an ox come abundant harvests.
					\item[NRSV] Where there are no oxen, there is no grain; abundant crops come by the strength of the ox.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:5} % *PROVERBS-14-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-5}

			A faithful witness will not lie: but a false witness will utter lies.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֣ד אֱ֭מוּנִים לֹ֣א יְכַזֵּ֑ב וְיָפִ֥יחַ כְּ֝זָבִ֗ים עֵ֣ד שָֽׁקֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A faithful witness will not lie; But a false witness uttereth lies.
					\item[NIV] An honest witness does not deceive, but a false witness pours out lies.
					\item[NRSV] A faithful witness does not lie, but a false witness breathes out lies.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:6} % *PROVERBS-14-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-6}

			A scorner seeketh wisdom, and findeth it not: but knowledge is easy unto him that understandeth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּקֶּשׁ־לֵ֣ץ חָכְמָ֣ה וָאָ֑יִן וְדַ֖עַת לְנָב֣וֹן נָקָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A scoffer seeketh wisdom, and findeth it not; But knowledge is easy unto him that hath understanding.
					\item[NIV] The mocker seeks wisdom and finds none, but knowledge comes easily to the discerning.
					\item[NRSV] A scoffer seeks wisdom in vain, but knowledge is easy for one who understands.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:7} % *PROVERBS-14-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-7}

			Go from the presence of a foolish man, when thou perceivest not in him the lips of knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ךְ מִ֭נֶּגֶד לְאִ֣ישׁ כְּסִ֑יל וּבַל־יָ֝דַ֗עְתָּ שִׂפְתֵי־דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Go into the presence of a foolish man, And thou shalt not perceive in him the lips of knowledge.
					\item[NIV] Stay away from a fool, for you will not find knowledge on their lips.
					\item[NRSV] Leave the presence of a fool, for there you do not find words of knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:8} % *PROVERBS-14-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-8}

			The wisdom of the prudent is to understand his way: but the folly of fools is deceit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָכְמַ֣ת עָ֭רוּם הָבִ֣ין דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וְאִוֶּ֖לֶת כְּסִילִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wisdom of the prudent is to understand his way; But the folly of fools is deceit.
					\item[NIV] The wisdom of the prudent is to give thought to their ways, but the folly of fools is deception.
					\item[NRSV] It is the wisdom of the clever to understand where they go, but the folly of fools misleads.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:9} % *PROVERBS-14-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-9}

			Fools make a mock at sin: but among the righteous there is favour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱ֭וִלִים יָלִ֣יץ אָשָׁ֑ם וּבֵ֖ין יְשָׁרִ֣ים רָצֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A trespass-offering mocketh fools; But among the upright there is good will.
					\item[NIV] Fools mock at making amends for sin, but goodwill is found among the upright.
