					\item[NRSV] Fools mock at the guilt offering, but the upright enjoy God’s favor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:10} % *PROVERBS-14-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-10}

			The heart knoweth his own bitterness; and a stranger doth not intermeddle with his joy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֗ב י֭וֹדֵעַ מָרַּ֣ת נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וּ֝בְשִׂמְחָת֗וֹ לֹא־יִתְעָ֥רַב זָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The heart knoweth its own bitterness; And a stranger doth not inter-meddle with its joy.
					\item[NIV] Each heart knows its own bitterness, and no one else can share its joy.
					\item[NRSV] The heart knows its own bitterness, and no stranger shares its joy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:11} % *PROVERBS-14-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-11}

			The house of the wicked shall be overthrown: but the tabernacle of the upright shall flourish.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּ֣ית רְ֭שָׁעִים יִשָּׁמֵ֑ד וְאֹ֖הֶל יְשָׁרִ֣ים יַפְרִֽיחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The house of the wicked shall be overthrown; But the tent of the upright shall flourish.
					\item[NIV] The house of the wicked will be destroyed, but the tent of the upright will flourish.
					\item[NRSV] The house of the wicked is destroyed, but the tent of the upright flourishes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:12} % *PROVERBS-14-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-12}

			There is a way which seemeth right unto a man, but the end thereof are the ways of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֤שׁ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יָ֭שָׁר לִפְנֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְ֝אַחֲרִיתָ֗הּ דַּרְכֵי־מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is a way which seemeth right unto a man; But the end thereof are the ways of death.
					\item[NIV] There is a way that appears to be right, but in the end it leads to death.
					\item[NRSV] There is a way that seems right to a person, but its end is the way to death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:13} % *PROVERBS-14-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-13}

			Even in laughter the heart is sorrowful; and the end of that mirth is heaviness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־בִּשְׂח֥וֹק יִכְאַב־לֵ֑ב וְאַחֲרִיתָ֖הּ שִׂמְחָ֣ה תוּגָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Even in laughter the heart is sorrowful; And the end of mirth is heaviness.
					\item[NIV] Even in laughter the heart may ache, and rejoicing may end in grief.
					\item[NRSV] Even in laughter the heart is sad, and the end of joy is grief.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:14} % *PROVERBS-14-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-14}

			The backslider in heart shall be filled with his own ways: and a good man shall be satisfied from himself.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִדְּרָכָ֣יו יִ֭שְׂבַּע ס֣וּג לֵ֑ב וּ֝מֵעָלָ֗יו אִ֣ישׁ טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The backslider in heart shall be filled with his own ways; And a good man shall be satisfied from himself.
					\item[NIV] The faithless will be fully repaid for their ways, and the good rewarded for theirs.
					\item[NRSV] The perverse get what their ways deserve, and the good, what their deeds deserve.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:15} % *PROVERBS-14-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-15}

			The simple believeth every word: but the prudent man looketh well to his going.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּ֭תִי יַאֲמִ֣ין לְכָל־דָּבָ֑ר וְ֝עָר֗וּם יָבִ֥ין לַאֲשֻׁרֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The simple believeth every word; But the prudent man looketh well to his going.
					\item[NIV] The simple believe anything, but the prudent give thought to their steps.
					\item[NRSV] The simple believe everything, but the clever consider their steps.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:16} % *PROVERBS-14-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-16}

			A wise man feareth, and departeth from evil: but the fool rageth, and is confident.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָכָ֣ם יָ֭רֵא וְסָ֣ר מֵרָ֑ע וּ֝כְסִ֗יל מִתְעַבֵּ֥ר וּבוֹטֵֽחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise man feareth, and departeth from evil; But the fool beareth himself insolently, and is confident.
					\item[NIV] The wise fear the LORD and shun evil, but a fool is hotheaded and yet feels secure.
					\item[NRSV] The wise are cautious and turn away from evil, but the fool throws off restraint and is careless.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:17} % *PROVERBS-14-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-17}

			He that is soon angry dealeth foolishly: and a man of wicked devices is hated.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קְֽצַר־אַ֭פַּיִם יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה אִוֶּ֑לֶת וְאִ֥ישׁ מְ֝זִמּ֗וֹת יִשָּׂנֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is soon angry will deal foolishly; And a man of wicked devices is hated.
					\item[NIV] A quick-tempered person does foolish things, and the one who devises evil schemes is hated.
					\item[NRSV] One who is quick-tempered acts foolishly, and the schemer is hated.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:18} % *PROVERBS-14-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-18}

			The simple inherit folly: but the prudent are crowned with knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נָחֲל֣וּ פְתָאיִ֣ם אִוֶּ֑לֶת וַֽ֝עֲרוּמִ֗ים יַכְתִּ֥רוּ דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The simple inherit folly; But the prudent are crowned with knowledge.
					\item[NIV] The simple inherit folly, but the prudent are crowned with knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] The simple are adorned with folly, but the clever are crowned with knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:19} % *PROVERBS-14-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-19}

			The evil bow before the good; and the wicked at the gates of the righteous.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שַׁח֣וּ רָ֭עִים לִפְנֵ֣י טוֹבִ֑ים וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים עַֽל־שַׁעֲרֵ֥י צַדִּֽיק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The evil bow down before the good; And the wicked, at the gates of the righteous.
					\item[NIV] Evildoers will bow down in the presence of the good, and the wicked at the gates of the righteous.
					\item[NRSV] The evil bow down before the good, the wicked at the gates of the righteous.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:20} % *PROVERBS-14-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-20}

			The poor is hated even of his own neighbour: but the rich hath many friends.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־לְ֭רֵעֵהוּ יִשָּׂ֣נֵא רָ֑שׁ וְאֹהֲבֵ֖י עָשִׁ֣יר רַבִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The poor is hated even of his own neighbor; But the rich hath many friends.
					\item[NIV] The poor are shunned even by their neighbors, but the rich have many friends.
					\item[NRSV] The poor are disliked even by their neighbors, but the rich have many friends.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:21} % *PROVERBS-14-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-21}

			He that despiseth his neighbour sinneth: but he that hath mercy on the poor, happy is he.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בָּז־לְרֵעֵ֥הוּ חוֹטֵ֑א וּמְחוֹנֵ֖ן עֲנָוִ֣ים אַשְׁרָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that despiseth his neighbor sinneth; But he that hath pity on the poor, happy is he.
					\item[NIV] It is a sin to despise one’s neighbor, but blessed is the one who is kind to the needy.
					\item[NRSV] Those who despise their neighbors are sinners, but happy are those who are kind to the poor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:22} % *PROVERBS-14-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-22}

			Do they not err that devise evil? but mercy and truth shall be to them that devise good.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲ‍ֽלוֹא־יִ֭תְעוּ חֹ֣רְשֵׁי רָ֑ע וְחֶ֥סֶד וֶ֝אֱמֶ֗ת חֹ֣רְשֵׁי טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Do they not err that devise evil? But mercy and truth shall be to them that devise good.
					\item[NIV] Do not those who plot evil go astray? But those who plan what is good find love and faithfulness.
					\item[NRSV] Do they not err that plan evil? Those who plan good find loyalty and faithfulness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:23} % *PROVERBS-14-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-23}

			In all labour there is profit: but the talk of the lips tendeth only to penury.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּכָל־עֶ֭צֶב יִהְיֶ֣ה מוֹתָ֑ר וּדְבַר־שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In all labor there is profit; But the talk of the lips tendeth only to penury.
					\item[NIV] All hard work brings a profit, but mere talk leads only to poverty.
					\item[NRSV] In all toil there is profit, but mere talk leads only to poverty.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:24} % *PROVERBS-14-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-24}

			The crown of the wise is their riches: but the foolishness of fools is folly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲטֶ֣רֶת חֲכָמִ֣ים עָשְׁרָ֑ם אִוֶּ֖לֶת כְּסִילִ֣ים אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The crown of the wise is their riches; But the folly of fools is only folly.
					\item[NIV] The wealth of the wise is their crown, but the folly of fools yields folly.
					\item[NRSV] The crown of the wise is their wisdom, but folly is the garland of fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:25} % *PROVERBS-14-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-25}

			A true witness delivereth souls: but a deceitful witness speaketh lies.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַצִּ֣יל נְ֭פָשׁוֹת עֵ֣ד אֱמֶ֑ת וְיָפִ֖חַ כְּזָבִ֣ים מִרְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A true witness delivereth souls; But he that uttereth lies causeth deceit.
					\item[NIV] A truthful witness saves lives, but a false witness is deceitful.
					\item[NRSV] A truthful witness saves lives, but one who utters lies is a betrayer.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:26} % *PROVERBS-14-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-26}

			In the fear of the LORD is strong confidence: and his children shall have a place of refuge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּיִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה מִבְטַח־עֹ֑ז וּ֝לְבָנָ֗יו יִהְיֶ֥ה מַחְסֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the fear of Jehovah is strong confidence; And his children shall have a place of refuge.
					\item[NIV] Whoever fears the LORD has a secure fortress, and for their children it will be a refuge.
					\item[NRSV] In the fear of the LORD one has strong confidence, and one’s children will have a refuge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:27} % *PROVERBS-14-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-27}

			The fear of the LORD is a fountain of life, to depart from the snares of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה מְק֣וֹר חַיִּ֑ים לָ֝ס֗וּר מִמֹּ֥קְשֵׁי מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah is a fountain of life, That one may depart from the snares of death.
					\item[NIV] The fear of the LORD is a fountain of life, turning a person from the snares of death.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD is a fountain of life, so that one may avoid the snares of death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:28} % *PROVERBS-14-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-28}

			In the multitude of people is the king’s honour: but in the want of people is the destruction of the prince.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּרָב־עָ֥ם הַדְרַת־מֶ֑לֶךְ וּבְאֶ֥פֶס לְ֝אֹ֗ם מְחִתַּ֥ת רָזֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the multitude of people is the king's glory; But in the want of people is the destruction of the prince.
					\item[NIV] A large population is a king’s glory, but without subjects a prince is ruined.
					\item[NRSV] The glory of a king is a multitude of people; without people a prince is ruined.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:29} % *PROVERBS-14-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-29}

			He that is slow to wrath is of great understanding: but he that is hasty of spirit exalteth folly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶ֣רֶךְ אַ֭פַּיִם רַב־תְּבוּנָ֑ה וּקְצַר־ר֝֗וּחַ מֵרִ֥ים אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is slow to anger is of great understanding; But he that is hasty of spirit exalteth folly.
					\item[NIV] Whoever is patient has great understanding, but one who is quick-tempered displays folly.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever is slow to anger has great understanding, but one who has a hasty temper exalts folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:30} % *PROVERBS-14-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-30}

			A sound heart is the life of the flesh: but envy the rottenness of the bones.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַיֵּ֣י בְ֭שָׂרִים לֵ֣ב מַרְפֵּ֑א וּרְקַ֖ב עֲצָמ֣וֹת קִנְאָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A tranquil heart is the life of the flesh; But envy is the rottenness of the bones.
					\item[NIV] A heart at peace gives life to the body, but envy rots the bones.
					\item[NRSV] A tranquil mind gives life to the flesh, but passion makes the bones rot.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:31} % *PROVERBS-14-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-31}

			He that oppresseth the poor reproacheth his Maker: but he that honoureth him hath mercy on the poor.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹ֣שֵֽׁק־דָּ֭ל חֵרֵ֣ף עֹשֵׂ֑הוּ וּ֝מְכַבְּד֗וֹ חֹנֵ֥ן אֶבְיֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that oppresseth the poor reproacheth his Maker; But he that hath mercy on the needy honoreth him.
					\item[NIV] Whoever oppresses the poor shows contempt for their Maker, but whoever is kind to the needy honors God.
					\item[NRSV] Those who oppress the poor insult their Maker, but those who are kind to the needy honor him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:32} % *PROVERBS-14-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-32}

			The wicked is driven away in his wickedness: but the righteous hath hope in his death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּֽ֭רָעָתוֹ יִדָּחֶ֣ה רָשָׁ֑ע וְחֹסֶ֖ה בְמוֹת֣וֹ צַדִּֽיק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wicked is thrust down in his evil-doing; But the righteous hath a refuge in his death.
					\item[NIV] When calamity comes, the wicked are brought down, but even in death the righteous seek refuge in God.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked are overthrown by their evildoing, but the righteous find a refuge in their integrity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:33} % *PROVERBS-14-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-33}

			Wisdom resteth in the heart of him that hath understanding: but that which is in the midst of fools is made known.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּלֵ֣ב נָ֭בוֹן תָּנ֣וּחַ חָכְמָ֑ה וּבְקֶ֥רֶב כְּ֝סִילִ֗ים תִּוָּדֵֽעַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom resteth in the heart of him that hath understanding; But that which is in the inward part of fools is made known.
					\item[NIV] Wisdom reposes in the heart of the discerning and even among fools she lets herself be known.
					\item[NRSV] Wisdom is at home in the mind of one who has understanding, but it is not known in the heart of fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:34} % *PROVERBS-14-34
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-34}

			Righteousness exalteth a nation: but sin is a reproach to any people.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צְדָקָ֥ה תְרֽוֹמֵֽם־גּ֑וֹי וְחֶ֖סֶד לְאֻמִּ֣ים חַטָּֽאת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Righteousness exalteth a nation; But sin is a reproach to any people.
					\item[NIV] Righteousness exalts a nation, but sin condemns any people.
					\item[NRSV] Righteousness exalts a nation, but sin is a reproach to any people.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 14:35} % *PROVERBS-14-35
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-14-35}

			The king’s favour is toward a wise servant: but his wrath is against him that causeth shame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רְֽצוֹן־מֶ֭לֶךְ לְעֶ֣בֶד מַשְׂכִּ֑יל וְ֝עֶבְרָת֗וֹ תִּהְיֶ֥ה מֵבִֽישׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The king's favor is toward a servant that dealeth wisely; But his wrath will be against him that causeth shame.
					\item[NIV] A king delights in a wise servant, but a shameful servant arouses his fury.
					\item[NRSV] A servant who deals wisely has the king’s favor, but his wrath falls on one who acts shamefully.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 15} % *PROVERBS-15
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-15}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:1} % *PROVERBS-15-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-1}

			A soft answer turneth away wrath: but grievous words stir up anger.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַֽעֲנֶה־רַּ֭ךְ יָשִׁ֣יב חֵמָ֑ה וּדְבַר־עֶ֝֗צֶב יַעֲלֶה־אָֽף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A soft answer turneth away wrath; But a grievous word stirreth up anger.
					\item[NIV] A gentle answer turns away wrath, but a harsh word stirs up anger.
					\item[NRSV] A soft answer turns away wrath, but a harsh word stirs up anger.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:2} % *PROVERBS-15-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-2}

			The tongue of the wise useth knowledge aright: but the mouth of fools poureth out foolishness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְשׁ֣וֹן חֲ֭כָמִים תֵּיטִ֣יב דָּ֑עַת וּפִ֥י כְ֝סִילִ֗ים יַבִּ֥יעַ אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The tongue of the wise uttereth knowledge aright; But the mouth of fools poureth out folly.
					\item[NIV] The tongue of the wise adorns knowledge, but the mouth of the fool gushes folly.
					\item[NRSV] The tongue of the wise dispenses knowledge, but the mouths of fools pour out folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:3} % *PROVERBS-15-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-3}

			The eyes of the LORD are in every place, beholding the evil and the good.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּֽכָל־מָ֭קוֹם עֵינֵ֣י יְהוָ֑ה צֹ֝פ֗וֹת רָעִ֥ים וטוֹבִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The eyes of Jehovah are in every place, Keeping watch upon the evil and the good.
					\item[NIV] The eyes of the LORD are everywhere, keeping watch on the wicked and the good.
					\item[NRSV] The eyes of the LORD are in every place, keeping watch on the evil and the good.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:4} % *PROVERBS-15-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-4}

			A wholesome tongue is a tree of life: but perverseness therein is a breach in the spirit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַרְפֵּ֣א לָ֭שׁוֹן עֵ֣ץ חַיִּ֑ים וְסֶ֥לֶף בָּ֝֗הּ שֶׁ֣בֶר בְּרֽוּחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A gentle tongue is a tree of life; But perverseness therein is a breaking of the spirit.
					\item[NIV] The soothing tongue is a tree of life, but a perverse tongue crushes the spirit.
					\item[NRSV] A gentle tongue is a tree of life, but perverseness in it breaks the spirit.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:5} % *PROVERBS-15-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-5}

			A fool despiseth his father’s instruction: but he that regardeth reproof is prudent.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱוִ֗יל יִ֭נְאַץ מוּסַ֣ר אָבִ֑יו וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר תּוֹכַ֣חַת יַעְרִֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool despiseth his father's correction; But he that regardeth reproof getteth prudence.
					\item[NIV] A fool spurns a parent’s discipline, but whoever heeds correction shows prudence.
					\item[NRSV] A fool despises a parent’s instruction, but the one who heeds admonition is prudent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:6} % *PROVERBS-15-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-6}

			In the house of the righteous is much treasure: but in the revenues of the wicked is trouble.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּ֣ית צַ֭דִּיק חֹ֣סֶן רָ֑ב וּבִתְבוּאַ֖ת רָשָׁ֣ע נֶעְכָּֽרֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the house of the righteous is much treasure; But in the revenues of the wicked is trouble.
					\item[NIV] The house of the righteous contains great treasure, but the income of the wicked brings ruin.
					\item[NRSV] In the house of the righteous there is much treasure, but trouble befalls the income of the wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:7} % *PROVERBS-15-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-7}

			The lips of the wise disperse knowledge: but the heart of the foolish doeth not so.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂפְתֵ֣י חֲ֭כָמִים יְזָ֣רוּ דָ֑עַת וְלֵ֖ב כְּסִילִ֣ים לֹא־כֵֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lips of the wise disperse knowledge; But the heart of the foolish doeth not so.
					\item[NIV] The lips of the wise spread knowledge, but the hearts of fools are not upright.
					\item[NRSV] The lips of the wise spread knowledge; not so the minds of fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:8} % *PROVERBS-15-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-8}

			The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination to the LORD: but the prayer of the upright is his delight.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זֶ֣בַח רְ֭שָׁעִים תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְהוָ֑ה וּתְפִלַּ֖ת יְשָׁרִ֣ים רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination to Jehovah; But the prayer of the upright is his delight.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests the sacrifice of the wicked, but the prayer of the upright pleases him.
					\item[NRSV] The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination to the LORD, but the prayer of the upright is his delight.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:9} % *PROVERBS-15-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-9}

			The way of the wicked is an abomination unto the LORD: but he loveth him that followeth after righteousness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה דֶּ֣רֶךְ רָשָׁ֑ע וּמְרַדֵּ֖ף צְדָקָ֣ה יֶאֱהָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The way of the wicked is an abomination to Jehovah; But he loveth him that followeth after righteousness.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests the way of the wicked, but he loves those who pursue righteousness.
					\item[NRSV] The way of the wicked is an abomination to the LORD, but he loves the one who pursues righteousness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:10} % *PROVERBS-15-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-10}

			Correction is grievous unto him that forsaketh the way: and he that hateth reproof shall die.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מוּסָ֣ר רָ֭ע לְעֹזֵ֣ב אֹ֑רַח שׂוֹנֵ֖א תוֹכַ֣חַת יָמֽוּת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is grievous correction for him that forsaketh the way; And he that hateth reproof shall die.
					\item[NIV] Stern discipline awaits anyone who leaves the path; the one who hates correction will die.
					\item[NRSV] There is severe discipline for one who forsakes the way, but one who hates a rebuke will die.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:11} % *PROVERBS-15-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-11}

			Hell and destruction are before the LORD: how much more then the hearts of the children of men?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁא֣וֹל וַ֭אֲבַדּוֹן נֶ֣גֶד יְהוָ֑ה אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־לִבּ֥וֹת בְּֽנֵי־אָדָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Sheol and Abaddon are before Jehovah: How much more then the hearts of the children of men!
					\item[NIV] Death and Destruction lie open before the LORD— how much more do human hearts!
					\item[NRSV] Sheol and Abaddon lie open before the LORD, how much more human hearts!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:12} % *PROVERBS-15-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-12}

			A scorner loveth not one that reproveth him: neither will he go unto the wise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹ֣א יֶאֱהַב־לֵ֭ץ הוֹכֵ֣חַֽ ל֑וֹ אֶל־חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים לֹ֣א יֵלֵֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A scoffer loveth not to be reproved; He will not go unto the wise.
					\item[NIV] Mockers resent correction, so they avoid the wise.
					\item[NRSV] Scoffers do not like to be rebuked; they will not go to the wise.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:13} % *PROVERBS-15-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-13}

			A merry heart maketh a cheerful countenance: but by sorrow of the heart the spirit is broken.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב שָׂ֭מֵחַ יֵיטִ֣ב פָּנִ֑ים וּבְעַצְּבַת־לֵ֝ב ר֣וּחַ נְכֵאָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A glad heart maketh a cheerful countenance; But by sorrow of heart the spirit is broken.
					\item[NIV] A happy heart makes the face cheerful, but heartache crushes the spirit.
					\item[NRSV] A glad heart makes a cheerful countenance, but by sorrow of heart the spirit is broken.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:14} % *PROVERBS-15-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-14}

			The heart of him that hath understanding seeketh knowledge: but the mouth of fools feedeth on foolishness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב נָ֭בוֹן יְבַקֶּשׁ־דָּ֑עַת וּפִ֥י כְ֝סִילִ֗ים יִרְעֶ֥ה אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The heart of him that hath understanding seeketh knowledge; But the mouth of fools feedeth on folly.
					\item[NIV] The discerning heart seeks knowledge, but the mouth of a fool feeds on folly.
					\item[NRSV] The mind of one who has understanding seeks knowledge, but the mouths of fools feed on folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:15} % *PROVERBS-15-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-15}

			All the days of the afflicted are evil: but he that is of a merry heart hath a continual feast.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־יְמֵ֣י עָנִ֣י רָעִ֑ים וְטֽוֹב־לֵ֝֗ב מִשְׁתֶּ֥ה תָמִֽיד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All the days of the afflicted are evil; But he that is of a cheerful heart hath a continual feast.
					\item[NIV] All the days of the oppressed are wretched, but the cheerful heart has a continual feast.
					\item[NRSV] All the days of the poor are hard, but a cheerful heart has a continual feast.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:16} % *PROVERBS-15-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-16}

			Better is little with the fear of the LORD than great treasure and trouble therewith.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טוֹב־מְ֭עַט בְּיִרְאַ֣ת יְהוָ֑ה מֵאוֹצָ֥ר רָ֝֗ב וּמְה֥וּמָה בֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is little, with the fear of Jehovah, Than great treasure and trouble therewith.
					\item[NIV] Better a little with the fear of the LORD than great wealth with turmoil.
					\item[NRSV] Better is a little with the fear of the LORD than great treasure and trouble with it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:17} % *PROVERBS-15-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-17}

			Better is a dinner of herbs where love is, than a stalled ox and hatred therewith.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֤וֹב אֲרֻחַ֣ת יָ֭רָק וְאַהֲבָה־שָׁ֑ם מִשּׁ֥וֹר אָ֝ב֗וּס וְשִׂנְאָה־בֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is a dinner of herbs, where love is, Than a stalled ox and hatred therewith.
					\item[NIV] Better a small serving of vegetables with love than a fattened calf with hatred.
					\item[NRSV] Better is a dinner of vegetables where love is than a fatted ox and hatred with it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:18} % *PROVERBS-15-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-18}

			A wrathful man stirreth up strife: but he that is slow to anger appeaseth strife.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ חֵ֭מָה יְגָרֶ֣ה מָד֑וֹן וְאֶ֥רֶך אַ֝פַּ֗יִם יַשְׁקִ֥יט רִֽיב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wrathful man stirreth up contention; But he that is slow to anger appeaseth strife.
					\item[NIV] A hot-tempered person stirs up conflict, but the one who is patient calms a quarrel.
					\item[NRSV] Those who are hot-tempered stir up strife, but those who are slow to anger calm contention.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:19} % *PROVERBS-15-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-19}

			The way of the slothful man is as an hedge of thorns: but the way of the righteous is made plain.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דֶּ֣רֶךְ עָ֭צֵל כִּמְשֻׂ֣כַת חָ֑דֶק וְאֹ֖רַח יְשָׁרִ֣ים סְלֻלָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The way of the sluggard is as a hedge of thorns; But the path of the upright is made a highway.
					\item[NIV] The way of the sluggard is blocked with thorns, but the path of the upright is a highway.
					\item[NRSV] The way of the lazy is overgrown with thorns, but the path of the upright is a level highway.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:20} % *PROVERBS-15-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-20}

			A wise son maketh a glad father: but a foolish man despiseth his mother.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּ֣ן חָ֭כָם יְשַׂמַּח־אָ֑ב וּכְסִ֥יל אָ֝דָ֗ם בּוֹזֶ֥ה אִמּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise son maketh a glad father; But a foolish man despiseth his mother.
					\item[NIV] A wise son brings joy to his father, but a foolish man despises his mother.
					\item[NRSV] A wise child makes a glad father, but the foolish despise their mothers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:21} % *PROVERBS-15-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-21}

			Folly is joy to him that is destitute of wisdom: but a man of understanding walketh uprightly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֭וֶּלֶת שִׂמְחָ֣ה לַחֲסַר־לֵ֑ב וְאִ֥ישׁ תְּ֝בוּנָ֗ה יְיַשֶׁר־לָֽכֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Folly is joy to him that is void of wisdom; But a man of understanding maketh straight his going.
					\item[NIV] Folly brings joy to one who has no sense, but whoever has understanding keeps a straight course.
					\item[NRSV] Folly is a joy to one who has no sense, but a person of understanding walks straight ahead.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:22} % *PROVERBS-15-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-22}

			Without counsel purposes are disappointed: but in the multitude of counsellors they are established.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָפֵ֣ר מַ֭חֲשָׁבוֹת בְּאֵ֣ין ס֑וֹד וּבְרֹ֖ב יוֹעֲצִ֣ים תָּקֽוּם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Where there is no counsel, purposes are disappointed; But in the multitude of counsellors they are established.
					\item[NIV] Plans fail for lack of counsel, but with many advisers they succeed.
					\item[NRSV] Without counsel, plans go wrong, but with many advisers they succeed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:23} % *PROVERBS-15-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-23}

			A man hath joy by the answer of his mouth: and a word spoken in due season, how good is it!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂמְחָ֣ה לָ֭אִישׁ בְּמַעֲנֵה־פִ֑יו וְדָבָ֖ר בְּעִתּ֣וֹ מַה־טּֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man hath joy in the answer of his mouth; And a word in due season, how good is it!
					\item[NIV] A person finds joy in giving an apt reply— and how good is a timely word!
					\item[NRSV] To make an apt answer is a joy to anyone, and a word in season, how good it is!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:24} % *PROVERBS-15-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-24}

			The way of life is above to the wise, that he may depart from hell beneath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֣רַח חַ֭יִּים לְמַ֣עְלָה לְמַשְׂכִּ֑יל לְמַ֥עַן ס֝֗וּר מִשְּׁא֥וֹל מָֽטָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To the wise the way of life goeth upward, That he may depart from Sheol beneath.
					\item[NIV] The path of life leads upward for the prudent to keep them from going down to the realm of the dead.
					\item[NRSV] For the wise the path of life leads upward, in order to avoid Sheol below.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:25} % *PROVERBS-15-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-25}

			The LORD will destroy the house of the proud: but he will establish the border of the widow.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בֵּ֣ית גֵּ֭אִים יִסַּ֥ח ׀ יְהוָ֑ה וְ֝יַצֵּ֗ב גְּב֣וּל אַלְמָנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah will root up the house of the proud; But he will establish the border of the widow.
					\item[NIV] The LORD tears down the house of the proud, but he sets the widow’s boundary stones in place.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD tears down the house of the proud, but maintains the widow’s boundaries.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:26} % *PROVERBS-15-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-26}

			The thoughts of the wicked are an abomination to the LORD: but the words of the pure are pleasant words.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת רָ֑ע וּ֝טְהֹרִ֗ים אִמְרֵי־נֹֽעַם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Evil devices are an abomination to Jehovah; But pleasant words are pure.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests the thoughts of the wicked, but gracious words are pure in his sight.
					\item[NRSV] Evil plans are an abomination to the LORD, but gracious words are pure.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:27} % *PROVERBS-15-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-27}

			He that is greedy of gain troubleth his own house; but he that hateth gifts shall live.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹכֵ֣ר בֵּ֭יתוֹ בּוֹצֵ֣עַ בָּ֑צַע וְשׂוֹנֵ֖א מַתָּנֹ֣ת יִחְיֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is greedy of gain troubleth his own house; But he that hateth bribes shall live.
					\item[NIV] The greedy bring ruin to their households, but the one who hates bribes will live.
					\item[NRSV] Those who are greedy for unjust gain make trouble for their households, but those who hate bribes will live.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:28} % *PROVERBS-15-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-28}

			The heart of the righteous studieth to answer: but the mouth of the wicked poureth out evil things.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב צַ֭דִּיק יֶהְגֶּ֣ה לַעֲנ֑וֹת וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יַבִּ֥יעַ רָעֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The heart of the righteous studieth to answer; But the mouth of the wicked poureth out evil things.
					\item[NIV] The heart of the righteous weighs its answers, but the mouth of the wicked gushes evil.
					\item[NRSV] The mind of the righteous ponders how to answer, but the mouth of the wicked pours out evil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:29} % *PROVERBS-15-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-29}

			The LORD is far from the wicked: but he heareth the prayer of the righteous.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָח֣וֹק יְ֭הוָה מֵרְשָׁעִ֑ים וּתְפִלַּ֖ת צַדִּיקִ֣ים יִשְׁמָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah is far from the wicked; But he heareth the prayer of the righteous.
					\item[NIV] The LORD is far from the wicked, but he hears the prayer of the righteous.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD is far from the wicked, but he hears the prayer of the righteous.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:30} % *PROVERBS-15-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-30}

			The light of the eyes rejoiceth the heart: and a good report maketh the bones fat.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְֽאוֹר־עֵ֭ינַיִם יְשַׂמַּֽח־לֵ֑ב שְׁמוּעָ֥ה ט֝וֹבָ֗ה תְּדַשֶּׁן־עָֽצֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The light of the eyes rejoiceth the heart; And good tidings make the bones fat.
					\item[NIV] Light in a messenger’s eyes brings joy to the heart, and good news gives health to the bones.
					\item[NRSV] The light of the eyes rejoices the heart, and good news refreshes the body.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:31} % *PROVERBS-15-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-31}

			The ear that heareth the reproof of life abideth among the wise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֗זֶן שֹׁ֭מַעַת תּוֹכַ֣חַת חַיִּ֑ים בְּקֶ֖רֶב חֲכָמִ֣ים תָּלִֽין׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The ear that hearkeneth to the reproof of life Shall abide among the wise.
					\item[NIV] Whoever heeds life-giving correction will be at home among the wise.
					\item[NRSV] The ear that heeds wholesome admonition will lodge among the wise.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:32} % *PROVERBS-15-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-32}

			He that refuseth instruction despiseth his own soul: but he that heareth reproof getteth understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פּוֹרֵ֣עַ מ֭וּסָר מוֹאֵ֣ס נַפְשׁ֑וֹ וְשׁוֹמֵ֥עַ תּ֝וֹכַ֗חַת ק֣וֹנֶה לֵּֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that refuseth correction despiseth his own soul; But he that hearkeneth to reproof getteth understanding.
					\item[NIV] Those who disregard discipline despise themselves, but the one who heeds correction gains understanding.
					\item[NRSV] Those who ignore instruction despise themselves, but those who heed admonition gain understanding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 15:33} % *PROVERBS-15-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-15-33}

			The fear of the LORD is the instruction of wisdom; and before honour is humility.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִרְאַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה מוּסַ֣ר חָכְמָ֑ה וְלִפְנֵ֖י כָב֣וֹד עֲנָוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah is the instruction of wisdom; And before honor goeth humility.
					\item[NIV] Wisdom’s instruction is to fear the LORD, and humility comes before honor.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD is instruction in wisdom, and humility goes before honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 16} % *PROVERBS-16
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-16}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:1} % *PROVERBS-16-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-1}

			The preparations of the heart in man, and the answer of the tongue, is from the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְאָדָ֥ם מַֽעַרְכֵי־לֵ֑ב וּ֝מֵיְהוָ֗ה מַעֲנֵ֥ה לָשֽׁוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The plans of the heart belong to man; But the answer of the tongue is from Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] To humans belong the plans of the heart, but from the LORD comes the proper answer of the tongue.
					\item[NRSV] The plans of the mind belong to mortals, but the answer of the tongue is from the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:2} % *PROVERBS-16-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-2}

			All the ways of a man are clean in his own eyes; but the LORD weigheth the spirits.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּֽל־דַּרְכֵי־אִ֭ישׁ זַ֣ךְ בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְתֹכֵ֖ן רוּח֣וֹת יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All the ways of a man are clean in his own eyes; But Jehovah weigheth the spirits.
					\item[NIV] All a person’s ways seem pure to them, but motives are weighed by the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] All one’s ways may be pure in one’s own eyes, but the LORD weighs the spirit.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:3} % *PROVERBS-16-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-3}

			Commit thy works unto the LORD, and thy thoughts shall be established.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גֹּ֣ל אֶל־יְהוָ֣ה מַעֲשֶׂ֑יךָ וְ֝יִכֹּ֗נוּ מַחְשְׁבֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Commit thy works unto Jehovah, And thy purposes shall be established.
					\item[NIV] Commit to the LORD whatever you do, and he will establish your plans.
					\item[NRSV] Commit your work to the LORD, and your plans will be established.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:4} % *PROVERBS-16-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-4}

			The LORD hath made all things for himself: yea, even the wicked for the day of evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֹּ֤ל פָּעַ֣ל יְ֭הוָה לַֽמַּעֲנֵ֑הוּ וְגַם־רָ֝שָׁ֗ע לְי֣וֹם רָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Jehovah hath made everything for its own end; Yea, even the wicked for the day of evil.
					\item[NIV] The LORD works out everything to its proper end— even the wicked for a day of disaster.
					\item[NRSV] The LORD has made everything for its purpose, even the wicked for the day of trouble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:5} % *PROVERBS-16-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-5}

			Every one that is proud in heart is an abomination to the LORD: though hand join in hand, he shall not be unpunished.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה כָּל־גְּבַהּ־לֵ֑ב יָ֥ד לְ֝יָ֗ד לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Every one that is proud in heart is an abomination to Jehovah: Though hand join in hand, he shall not be unpunished.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests all the proud of heart. Be sure of this: They will not go unpunished.
					\item[NRSV] All those who are arrogant are an abomination to the LORD; be assured, they will not go unpunished.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:6} % *PROVERBS-16-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-6}

			By mercy and truth iniquity is purged: and by the fear of the LORD men depart from evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּחֶ֣סֶד וֶ֭אֱמֶת יְכֻפַּ֣ר עָוֺ֑ן וּבְיִרְאַ֥ת יְ֝הוָ֗ה ס֣וּר מֵרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By mercy and truth iniquity is atoned for; And by the fear of Jehovah men depart from evil.
					\item[NIV] Through love and faithfulness sin is atoned for; through the fear of the LORD evil is avoided.
					\item[NRSV] By loyalty and faithfulness iniquity is atoned for, and by the fear of the LORD one avoids evil.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:7} % *PROVERBS-16-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-7}

			When a man’s ways please the LORD, he maketh even his enemies to be at peace with him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּרְצ֣וֹת יְ֭הוָה דַּרְכֵי־אִ֑ישׁ גַּם־א֝וֹיְבָ֗יו יַשְׁלִ֥ם אִתּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When a man's ways please Jehovah, He maketh even his enemies to be at peace with him.
					\item[NIV] When the LORD takes pleasure in anyone’s way, he causes their enemies to make peace with them.
					\item[NRSV] When the ways of people please the LORD, he causes even their enemies to be at peace with them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:8} % *PROVERBS-16-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-8}

			Better is a little with righteousness than great revenues without right.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טוֹב־מְ֭עַט בִּצְדָקָ֑ה מֵרֹ֥ב תְּ֝בוּא֗וֹת בְּלֹ֣א מִשְׁפָּֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is a little, with righteousness, Than great revenues with injustice.
					\item[NIV] Better a little with righteousness than much gain with injustice.
					\item[NRSV] Better is a little with righteousness than large income with injustice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:9} % *PROVERBS-16-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-9}

			A man’s heart deviseth his way: but the LORD directeth his steps.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב אָ֭דָם יְחַשֵּׁ֣ב דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וַֽ֝יהוָ֗ה יָכִ֥ין צַעֲדֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man's heart deviseth his way; But Jehovah directeth his steps.
					\item[NIV] In their hearts humans plan their course, but the LORD establishes their steps.
					\item[NRSV] The human mind plans the way, but the LORD directs the steps.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:10} % *PROVERBS-16-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-10}

			A divine sentence is in the lips of the king: his mouth transgresseth not in judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קֶ֤סֶם ׀ עַֽל־שִׂפְתֵי־מֶ֑לֶךְ בְּ֝מִשְׁפָּ֗ט לֹ֣א יִמְעַל־פִּֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A divine sentence is in the lips of the king; His mouth shall not transgress in judgment.
					\item[NIV] The lips of a king speak as an oracle, and his mouth does not betray justice.
					\item[NRSV] Inspired decisions are on the lips of a king; his mouth does not sin in judgment.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:11} % *PROVERBS-16-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-11}

			A just weight and balance are the LORD’s: all the weights of the bag are his work.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּ֤לֶס ׀ וּמֹאזְנֵ֣י מִ֭שְׁפָּט לַֽיהוָ֑ה מַ֝עֲשֵׂ֗הוּ כָּל־אַבְנֵי־כִֽיס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A just balance and scales are Jehovah's; All the weights of the bag are his work.
					\item[NIV] Honest scales and balances belong to the LORD; all the weights in the bag are of his making.
					\item[NRSV] Honest balances and scales are the LORD’s; all the weights in the bag are his work.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:12} % *PROVERBS-16-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-12}

			It is an abomination to kings to commit wickedness: for the throne is established by righteousness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת מְ֭לָכִים עֲשׂ֣וֹת רֶ֑שַׁע כִּ֥י בִ֝צְדָקָ֗ה יִכּ֥וֹן כִּסֵּֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is an abomination to kings to commit wickedness; For the throne is established by righteousness.
					\item[NIV] Kings detest wrongdoing, for a throne is established through righteousness.
					\item[NRSV] It is an abomination to kings to do evil, for the throne is established by righteousness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:13} % *PROVERBS-16-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-13}

			Righteous lips are the delight of kings; and they love him that speaketh right.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רְצ֣וֹן מְ֭לָכִים שִׂפְתֵי־צֶ֑דֶק וְדֹבֵ֖ר יְשָׁרִ֣ים יֶאֱהָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Righteous lips are the delight of kings; And they love him that speaketh right.
					\item[NIV] Kings take pleasure in honest lips; they value the one who speaks what is right.
					\item[NRSV] Righteous lips are the delight of a king, and he loves those who speak what is right.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:14} % *PROVERBS-16-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-14}

			The wrath of a king is as messengers of death: but a wise man will pacify it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֲמַת־מֶ֥לֶךְ מַלְאֲכֵי־מָ֑וֶת וְאִ֖ישׁ חָכָ֣ם יְכַפְּרֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wrath of a king is as messengers of death; But a wise man will pacify it.
					\item[NIV] A king’s wrath is a messenger of death, but the wise will appease it.
					\item[NRSV] A king’s wrath is a messenger of death, and whoever is wise will appease it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:15} % *PROVERBS-16-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-15}

			In the light of the king’s countenance is life; and his favour is as a cloud of the latter rain.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאוֹר־פְּנֵי־מֶ֥לֶךְ חַיִּ֑ים וּ֝רְצוֹנ֗וֹ כְּעָ֣ב מַלְקֽוֹשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the light of the king's countenance is life; And his favor is as a cloud of the latter rain.
					\item[NIV] When a king’s face brightens, it means life; his favor is like a rain cloud in spring.
					\item[NRSV] In the light of a king’s face there is life, and his favor is like the clouds that bring the spring rain.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:16} % *PROVERBS-16-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-16}

			How much better is it to get wisdom than gold! and to get understanding rather to be chosen than silver!

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קְֽנֹה־חָכְמָ֗ה מַה־טּ֥וֹב מֵחָר֑וּץ וּקְנ֥וֹת בִּ֝ינָ֗ה נִבְחָ֥ר מִכָּֽסֶף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] How much better is it to get wisdom than gold! Yea, to get understanding is rather to be chosen than silver.
					\item[NIV] How much better to get wisdom than gold, to get insight rather than silver!
					\item[NRSV] How much better to get wisdom than gold! To get understanding is to be chosen rather than silver.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:17} % *PROVERBS-16-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-17}

			The highway of the upright is to depart from evil: he that keepeth his way preserveth his soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְסִלַּ֣ת יְ֭שָׁרִים ס֣וּר מֵרָ֑ע שֹׁמֵ֥ר נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ נֹצֵ֥ר דַּרְכּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The highway of the upright is to depart from evil: He that keepeth his way preserveth his soul.
					\item[NIV] The highway of the upright avoids evil; those who guard their ways preserve their lives.
					\item[NRSV] The highway of the upright avoids evil; those who guard their way preserve their lives.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:18} % *PROVERBS-16-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-18}

			Pride goeth before destruction, and an haughty spirit before a fall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִפְנֵי־שֶׁ֥בֶר גָּא֑וֹן וְלִפְנֵ֥י כִ֝שָּׁל֗וֹן גֹּ֣בַהּ רֽוּחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Pride goeth before destruction, And a haughty spirit before a fall.
					\item[NIV] Pride goes before destruction, a haughty spirit before a fall.
					\item[NRSV] Pride goes before destruction, and a haughty spirit before a fall.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:19} % *PROVERBS-16-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-19}

			Better it is to be of an humble spirit with the lowly, than to divide the spoil with the proud.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֣וֹב שְׁפַל־ר֭וּחַ עֲנָוִ֑ים מֵֽחַלֵּ֥ק שָׁ֝לָ֗ל אֶת־גֵּאִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better it is to be of a lowly spirit with the poor, Than to divide the spoil with the proud.
					\item[NIV] Better to be lowly in spirit along with the oppressed than to share plunder with the proud.
					\item[NRSV] It is better to be of a lowly spirit among the poor than to divide the spoil with the proud.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:20} % *PROVERBS-16-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-20}

			He that handleth a matter wisely shall find good: and whoso trusteth in the LORD, happy is he.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַשְׂכִּ֣יל עַל־דָּ֭בָר יִמְצָא־ט֑וֹב וּבוֹטֵ֖חַ בַּיהוָ֣ה אַשְׁרָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that giveth heed unto the word shall find good; And whoso trusteth in Jehovah, happy is he.
					\item[NIV] Whoever gives heed to instruction prospers, and blessed is the one who trusts in the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] Those who are attentive to a matter will prosper, and happy are those who trust in the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:21} % *PROVERBS-16-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-21}

			The wise in heart shall be called prudent: and the sweetness of the lips increaseth learning.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לַחֲכַם־לֵ֭ב יִקָּרֵ֣א נָב֑וֹן וּמֶ֥תֶק שְׂ֝פָתַ֗יִם יֹסִ֥יף לֶֽקַח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wise in heart shall be called prudent; And the sweetness of the lips increaseth learning.
					\item[NIV] The wise in heart are called discerning, and gracious words promote instruction.
					\item[NRSV] The wise of heart is called perceptive, and pleasant speech increases persuasiveness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:22} % *PROVERBS-16-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-22}

			Understanding is a wellspring of life unto him that hath it: but the instruction of fools is folly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְק֣וֹר חַ֭יִּים שֵׂ֣כֶל בְּעָלָ֑יו וּמוּסַ֖ר אֱוִלִ֣ים אִוֶּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Understanding is a well-spring of life unto him that hath it; But the correction of fools is their folly.
					\item[NIV] Prudence is a fountain of life to the prudent, but folly brings punishment to fools.
					\item[NRSV] Wisdom is a fountain of life to one who has it, but folly is the punishment of fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:23} % *PROVERBS-16-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-23}

			The heart of the wise teacheth his mouth, and addeth learning to his lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב חָ֭כָם יַשְׂכִּ֣יל פִּ֑יהוּ וְעַל־שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו יֹסִ֥יף לֶֽקַח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The heart of the wise instructeth his mouth, And addeth learning to his lips.
					\item[NIV] The hearts of the wise make their mouths prudent, and their lips promote instruction.
					\item[NRSV] The mind of the wise makes their speech judicious, and adds persuasiveness to their lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:24} % *PROVERBS-16-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-24}

			Pleasant words are as an honeycomb, sweet to the soul, and health to the bones.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צוּף־דְּ֭בַשׁ אִמְרֵי־נֹ֑עַם מָת֥וֹק לַ֝נֶּפֶשׁ וּמַרְפֵּ֥א לָעָֽצֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Pleasant words are as a honeycomb, Sweet to the soul, and health to the bones.
					\item[NIV] Gracious words are a honeycomb, sweet to the soul and healing to the bones.
					\item[NRSV] Pleasant words are like a honeycomb, sweetness to the soul and health to the body.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:25} % *PROVERBS-16-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-25}

			There is a way that seemeth right unto a man, but the end thereof are the ways of death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֤שׁ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יָ֭שָׁר לִפְנֵי־אִ֑ישׁ וְ֝אַחֲרִיתָ֗הּ דַּרְכֵי־מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is a way which seemeth right unto a man, But the end thereof are the ways of death.
					\item[NIV] There is a way that appears to be right, but in the end it leads to death.
					\item[NRSV] Sometimes there is a way that seems to be right, but in the end it is the way to death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:26} % *PROVERBS-16-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-26}

			He that laboureth laboureth for himself; for his mouth craveth it of him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶ֣פֶשׁ עָ֭מֵל עָ֣מְלָה לּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־אָכַ֖ף עָלָ֣יו פִּֽיהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The appetite of the laboring man laboreth for him; For his mouth urgeth him thereto.
					\item[NIV] The appetite of laborers works for them; their hunger drives them on.
					\item[NRSV] The appetite of workers works for them; their hunger urges them on.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:27} % *PROVERBS-16-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-27}

			An ungodly man diggeth up evil: and in his lips there is as a burning fire.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ בְּ֭לִיַּעַל כֹּרֶ֣ה רָעָ֑ה שְׂ֝פָת֗וֹ כְּאֵ֣שׁ צָרָֽבֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A worthless man deviseth mischief; And in his lips there is as a scorching fire.
					\item[NIV] A scoundrel plots evil, and on their lips it is like a scorching fire.
					\item[NRSV] Scoundrels concoct evil, and their speech is like a scorching fire.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:28} % *PROVERBS-16-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-28}

			A froward man soweth strife: and a whisperer separateth chief friends.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ תַּ֭הְפֻּכוֹת יְשַׁלַּ֣ח מָד֑וֹן וְ֝נִרְגָּ֗ן מַפְרִ֥יד אַלּֽוּף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A perverse man scattereth abroad strife; And a whisperer separateth chief friends.
					\item[NIV] A perverse person stirs up conflict, and a gossip separates close friends.
					\item[NRSV] A perverse person spreads strife, and a whisperer separates close friends.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:29} % *PROVERBS-16-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-29}

			A violent man enticeth his neighbour, and leadeth him into the way that is not good.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ חָ֭מָס יְפַתֶּ֣ה רֵעֵ֑הוּ וְ֝הוֹלִיכ֗וֹ בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ לֹא־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man of violence enticeth his neighbor, And leadeth him in a way that is not good.
					\item[NIV] A violent person entices their neighbor and leads them down a path that is not good.
					\item[NRSV] The violent entice their neighbors, and lead them in a way that is not good.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:30} % *PROVERBS-16-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-30}

			He shutteth his eyes to devise froward things: moving his lips he bringeth evil to pass.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹצֶ֣ה עֵ֭ינָיו לַחְשֹׁ֣ב תַּהְפֻּכ֑וֹת קֹרֵ֥ץ שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו כִּלָּ֥ה רָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that shutteth his eyes, it is to devise perverse things: He that compresseth his lips bringeth evil to pass.
					\item[NIV] Whoever winks with their eye is plotting perversity; whoever purses their lips is bent on evil.
					\item[NRSV] One who winks the eyes plans perverse things; one who compresses the lips brings evil to pass.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:31} % *PROVERBS-16-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-31}

			The hoary head is a crown of glory, if it be found in the way of righteousness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲטֶ֣רֶת תִּפְאֶ֣רֶת שֵׂיבָ֑ה בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ צְ֝דָקָ֗ה תִּמָּצֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The hoary head is a crown of glory; It shall be found in the way of righteousness.
					\item[NIV] Gray hair is a crown of splendor; it is attained in the way of righteousness.
					\item[NRSV] Gray hair is a crown of glory; it is gained in a righteous life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
