		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:32} % *PROVERBS-16-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-32}

			He that is slow to anger is better than the mighty; and he that ruleth his spirit than he that taketh a city.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֤וֹב אֶ֣רֶךְ אַ֭פַּיִם מִגִּבּ֑וֹר וּמֹשֵׁ֥ל בְּ֝רוּח֗וֹ מִלֹּכֵ֥ד עִֽיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is slow to anger is better than the mighty; And he that ruleth his spirit, than he that taketh a city.
					\item[NIV] Better a patient person than a warrior, one with self-control than one who takes a city.
					\item[NRSV] One who is slow to anger is better than the mighty, and one whose temper is controlled than one who captures a city.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 16:33} % *PROVERBS-16-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-16-33}

			The lot is cast into the lap; but the whole disposing thereof is of the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּ֭חֵיק יוּטַ֣ל אֶת־הַגּוֹרָ֑ל וּ֝מֵיְהוָ֗ה כָּל־מִשְׁפָּטֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lot is cast into the lap; But the whole disposing thereof is of Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] The lot is cast into the lap, but its every decision is from the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] The lot is cast into the lap, but the decision is the LORD’s alone.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 17} % *PROVERBS-17
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-17}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:1} % *PROVERBS-17-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-1}

			Better is a dry morsel, and quietness therewith, than an house full of sacrifices with strife.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֤וֹב פַּ֣ת חֲ֭רֵבָה וְשַׁלְוָה־בָ֑הּ מִ֝בַּ֗יִת מָלֵ֥א זִבְחֵי־רִֽיב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is a dry morsel, and quietness therewith, Than a house full of feasting with strife.
					\item[NIV] Better a dry crust with peace and quiet than a house full of feasting, with strife.
					\item[NRSV] Better is a dry morsel with quiet than a house full of feasting with strife.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:2} % *PROVERBS-17-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-2}

			A wise servant shall have rule over a son that causeth shame, and shall have part of the inheritance among the brethren.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֶֽבֶד־מַשְׂכִּ֗יל יִ֭מְשֹׁל בְּבֵ֣ן מֵבִ֑ישׁ וּבְת֥וֹךְ אַ֝חִ֗ים יַחֲלֹ֥ק נַחֲלָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A servant that dealeth wisely shall have rule over a son that causeth shame, And shall have part in the inheritance among the brethren.
					\item[NIV] A prudent servant will rule over a disgraceful son and will share the inheritance as one of the family.
					\item[NRSV] A slave who deals wisely will rule over a child who acts shamefully, and will share the inheritance as one of the family.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:3} % *PROVERBS-17-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-3}

			The fining pot is for silver, and the furnace for gold: but the LORD trieth the hearts.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַצְרֵ֣ף לַ֭כֶּסֶף וְכ֣וּר לַזָּהָ֑ב וּבֹחֵ֖ן לִבּ֣וֹת יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The refining pot is for silver, and the furnace for gold; But Jehovah trieth the hearts.
					\item[NIV] The crucible for silver and the furnace for gold, but the LORD tests the heart.
					\item[NRSV] The crucible is for silver, and the furnace is for gold, but the LORD tests the heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:4} % *PROVERBS-17-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-4}

			A wicked doer giveth heed to false lips; and a liar giveth ear to a naughty tongue.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵ֭רַע מַקְשִׁ֣יב עַל־שְׂפַת־אָ֑וֶן שֶׁ֥קֶר מֵ֝זִין עַל־לְשׁ֥וֹן הַוֺּֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] An evil-doer giveth heed to wicked lips; And a liar giveth ear to a mischievous tongue.
					\item[NIV] A wicked person listens to deceitful lips; a liar pays attention to a destructive tongue.
					\item[NRSV] An evildoer listens to wicked lips; and a liar gives heed to a mischievous tongue.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:5} % *PROVERBS-17-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-5}

			Whoso mocketh the poor reproacheth his Maker: and he that is glad at calamities shall not be unpunished.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹעֵ֣ג לָ֭רָשׁ חֵרֵ֣ף עֹשֵׂ֑הוּ שָׂמֵ֥חַ לְ֝אֵ֗יד לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso mocketh the poor reproacheth his Maker; And he that is glad at calamity shall not be unpunished.
					\item[NIV] Whoever mocks the poor shows contempt for their Maker; whoever gloats over disaster will not go unpunished.
					\item[NRSV] Those who mock the poor insult their Maker; those who are glad at calamity will not go unpunished.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:6} % *PROVERBS-17-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-6}

			Children’s children are the crown of old men; and the glory of children are their fathers.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲטֶ֣רֶת זְ֭קֵנִים בְּנֵ֣י בָנִ֑ים וְתִפְאֶ֖רֶת בָּנִ֣ים אֲבוֹתָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Children's children are the crown of old men; And the glory of children are their fathers.
					\item[NIV] Children’s children are a crown to the aged, and parents are the pride of their children.
					\item[NRSV] Grandchildren are the crown of the aged, and the glory of children is their parents.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:7} % *PROVERBS-17-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-7}

			Excellent speech becometh not a fool: much less do lying lips a prince.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־נָאוָ֣ה לְנָבָ֣ל שְׂפַת־יֶ֑תֶר אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־לְנָדִ֥יב שְׂפַת־שָֽׁקֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Excellent speech becometh not a fool; Much less do lying lips a prince.
					\item[NIV] Eloquent lips are unsuited to a godless fool— how much worse lying lips to a ruler!
					\item[NRSV] Fine speech is not becoming to a fool; still less is false speech to a ruler.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:8} % *PROVERBS-17-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-8}

			A gift is as a precious stone in the eyes of him that hath it: whithersoever it turneth, it prospereth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶֽבֶן־חֵ֣ן הַ֭שֹּׁחַד בְּעֵינֵ֣י בְעָלָ֑יו אֶֽל־כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֖ר יִפְנֶ֣ה יַשְׂכִּֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A bribe is as a precious stone in the eyes of him that hath it; Whithersoever it turneth, it prospereth.
					\item[NIV] A bribe is seen as a charm by the one who gives it; they think success will come at every turn.
					\item[NRSV] A bribe is like a magic stone in the eyes of those who give it; wherever they turn they prosper.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:9} % *PROVERBS-17-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-9}

			He that covereth a transgression seeketh love; but he that repeateth a matter separateth very friends.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְֽכַסֶּה־פֶּ֭שַׁע מְבַקֵּ֣שׁ אַהֲבָ֑ה וְשֹׁנֶ֥ה בְ֝דָבָ֗ר מַפְרִ֥יד אַלּֽוּף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that covereth a transgression seeketh love; But he that harpeth on a matter separateth chief friends.
					\item[NIV] Whoever would foster love covers over an offense, but whoever repeats the matter separates close friends.
					\item[NRSV] One who forgives an affront fosters friendship, but one who dwells on disputes will alienate a friend.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:10} % *PROVERBS-17-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-10}

			A reproof entereth more into a wise man than an hundred stripes into a fool.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תֵּ֣חַת גְּעָרָ֣ה בְמֵבִ֑ין מֵהַכּ֖וֹת כְּסִ֣יל מֵאָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A rebuke entereth deeper into one that hath understanding Than a hundred stripes into a fool.
					\item[NIV] A rebuke impresses a discerning person more than a hundred lashes a fool.
					\item[NRSV] A rebuke strikes deeper into a discerning person than a hundred blows into a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:11} % *PROVERBS-17-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-11}

			An evil man seeketh only rebellion: therefore a cruel messenger shall be sent against him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַךְ־מְרִ֥י יְבַקֶּשׁ־רָ֑ע וּמַלְאָ֥ךְ אַ֝כְזָרִ֗י יְשֻׁלַּח־בּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] An evil man seeketh only rebellion; Therefore a cruel messenger shall be sent against him.
					\item[NIV] Evildoers foster rebellion against God; the messenger of death will be sent against them.
					\item[NRSV] Evil people seek only rebellion, but a cruel messenger will be sent against them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:12} % *PROVERBS-17-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-12}

			Let a bear robbed of her whelps meet a man, rather than a fool in his folly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פָּג֬וֹשׁ דֹּ֣ב שַׁכּ֣וּל בְּאִ֑ישׁ וְאַל־כְּ֝סִ֗יל בְּאִוַּלְתּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let a bear robbed of her whelps meet a man, Rather than a fool in his folly.
					\item[NIV] Better to meet a bear robbed of her cubs than a fool bent on folly.
					\item[NRSV] Better to meet a she-bear robbed of its cubs than to confront a fool immersed in folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:13} % *PROVERBS-17-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-13}

			Whoso rewardeth evil for good, evil shall not depart from his house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵשִׁ֣יב רָ֭עָה תַּ֣חַת טוֹבָ֑ה תָמ֥וּשׁ רָ֝עָ֗ה מִבֵּיתֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso rewardeth evil for good, Evil shall not depart from his house.
					\item[NIV] Evil will never leave the house of one who pays back evil for good.
					\item[NRSV] Evil will not depart from the house of one who returns evil for good.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:14} % *PROVERBS-17-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-14}

			The beginning of strife is as when one letteth out water: therefore leave off contention, before it be meddled with.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פּ֣וֹטֵֽר מַ֭יִם רֵאשִׁ֣ית מָד֑וֹן וְלִפְנֵ֥י הִ֝תְגַּלַּ֗ע הָרִ֥יב נְטֽוֹשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The beginning of strife is as when one letteth out water: Therefore leave off contention, before there is quarrelling.
					\item[NIV] Starting a quarrel is like breaching a dam; so drop the matter before a dispute breaks out.
					\item[NRSV] The beginning of strife is like letting out water; so stop before the quarrel breaks out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:15} % *PROVERBS-17-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-15}

			He that justifieth the wicked, and he that condemneth the just, even they both are abomination to the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַצְדִּ֣יק רָ֭שָׁע וּמַרְשִׁ֣יעַ צַדִּ֑יק תּוֹעֲבַ֥ת יְ֝הוָ֗ה גַּם־שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that justifieth the wicked, and he that condemneth the righteous, Both of them alike are an abomination to Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] Acquitting the guilty and condemning the innocent— the LORD detests them both.
					\item[NRSV] One who justifies the wicked and one who condemns the righteous are both alike an abomination to the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:16} % *PROVERBS-17-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-16}

			Wherefore is there a price in the hand of a fool to get wisdom, seeing he hath no heart to it?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לָמָּה־זֶּ֣ה מְחִ֣יר בְּיַד־כְּסִ֑יל לִקְנ֖וֹת חָכְמָ֣ה וְלֶב־אָֽיִן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wherefore is there a price in the hand of a fool to buy wisdom, Seeing he hath no understanding?
					\item[NIV] Why should fools have money in hand to buy wisdom, when they are not able to understand it?
					\item[NRSV] Why should fools have a price in hand to buy wisdom, when they have no mind to learn?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:17} % *PROVERBS-17-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-17}

			A friend loveth at all times, and a brother is born for adversity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּכָל־עֵ֭ת אֹהֵ֣ב הָרֵ֑עַ וְאָ֥ח לְ֝צָרָ֗ה יִוָּלֵֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A friend loveth at all times; And a brother is born for adversity.
					\item[NIV] A friend loves at all times, and a brother is born for a time of adversity.
					\item[NRSV] A friend loves at all times, and kinsfolk are born to share adversity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:18} % *PROVERBS-17-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-18}

			A man void of understanding striketh hands, and becometh surety in the presence of his friend.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָדָ֣ם חֲסַר־לֵ֭ב תּוֹקֵ֣עַ כָּ֑ף עֹרֵ֥ב עֲ֝רֻבָּ֗ה לִפְנֵ֥י רֵעֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man void of understanding striketh hands, And becometh surety in the presence of his neighbor.
					\item[NIV] One who has no sense shakes hands in pledge and puts up security for a neighbor.
					\item[NRSV] It is senseless to give a pledge, to become surety for a neighbor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:19} % *PROVERBS-17-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-19}

			He loveth transgression that loveth strife: and he that exalteth his gate seeketh destruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֣הֵֽב פֶּ֭שַׁע אֹהֵ֣ב מַצָּ֑ה מַגְבִּ֥יהַּ פִּ֝תְח֗וֹ מְבַקֶּשׁ־שָֽׁבֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He loveth transgression that loveth strife: He that raiseth high his gate seeketh destruction.
					\item[NIV] Whoever loves a quarrel loves sin; whoever builds a high gate invites destruction.
					\item[NRSV] One who loves transgression loves strife; one who builds a high threshold invites broken bones.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:20} % *PROVERBS-17-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-20}

			He that hath a froward heart findeth no good: and he that hath a perverse tongue falleth into mischief.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִקֶּשׁ־לֵ֭ב לֹ֣א יִמְצָא־ט֑וֹב וְנֶהְפָּ֥ךְ בִּ֝לְשׁוֹנ֗וֹ יִפּ֥וֹל בְּרָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that hath a wayward heart findeth no good; And he that hath a perverse tongue falleth into mischief.
					\item[NIV] One whose heart is corrupt does not prosper; one whose tongue is perverse falls into trouble.
					\item[NRSV] The crooked of mind do not prosper, and the perverse of tongue fall into calamity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:21} % *PROVERBS-17-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-21}

			He that begetteth a fool doeth it to his sorrow: and the father of a fool hath no joy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֹלֵ֣ד כְּ֭סִיל לְת֣וּגָה ל֑וֹ וְלֹֽא־יִ֝שְׂמַ֗ח אֲבִ֣י נָבָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that begetteth a fool doeth it to his sorrow; And the father of a fool hath no joy.
					\item[NIV] To have a fool for a child brings grief; there is no joy for the parent of a godless fool.
					\item[NRSV] The one who begets a fool gets trouble; the parent of a fool has no joy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:22} % *PROVERBS-17-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-22}

			A merry heart doeth good like a medicine: but a broken spirit drieth the bones.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב שָׂ֭מֵחַ יֵיטִ֣ב גֵּהָ֑ה וְר֥וּחַ נְ֝כֵאָ֗ה תְּיַבֶּשׁ־גָּֽרֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A cheerful heart is a good medicine; But a broken spirit drieth up the bones.
					\item[NIV] A cheerful heart is good medicine, but a crushed spirit dries up the bones.
					\item[NRSV] A cheerful heart is a good medicine, but a downcast spirit dries up the bones.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:23} % *PROVERBS-17-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-23}

			A wicked man taketh a gift out of the bosom to pervert the ways of judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֹׁ֣חַד מֵ֭חֵיק רָשָׁ֣ע יִקָּ֑ח לְ֝הַטּ֗וֹת אָרְח֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wicked man receiveth a bribe out of the bosom, To pervert the ways of justice.
					\item[NIV] The wicked accept bribes in secret to pervert the course of justice.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked accept a concealed bribe to pervert the ways of justice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:24} % *PROVERBS-17-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-24}

			Wisdom is before him that hath understanding; but the eyes of a fool are in the ends of the earth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶת־פְּנֵ֣י מֵבִ֣ין חָכְמָ֑ה וְעֵינֵ֥י כְ֝סִ֗יל בִּקְצֵה־אָֽרֶץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom is before the face of him that hath understanding; But the eyes of a fool are in the ends of the earth.
					\item[NIV] A discerning person keeps wisdom in view, but a fool’s eyes wander to the ends of the earth.
					\item[NRSV] The discerning person looks to wisdom, but the eyes of a fool to the ends of the earth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:25} % *PROVERBS-17-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-25}

			A foolish son is a grief to his father, and bitterness to her that bare him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּ֣עַס לְ֭אָבִיו בֵּ֣ן כְּסִ֑יל וּ֝מֶ֗מֶר לְיוֹלַדְתּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A foolish son is a grief to his father, And bitterness to her that bare him.
					\item[NIV] A foolish son brings grief to his father and bitterness to the mother who bore him.
					\item[NRSV] Foolish children are a grief to their father and bitterness to her who bore them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:26} % *PROVERBS-17-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-26}

			Also to punish the just is not good, nor to strike princes for equity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֤ם עֲנ֣וֹשׁ לַצַּדִּ֣יק לֹא־ט֑וֹב לְהַכּ֖וֹת נְדִיבִ֣ים עַל־יֹֽשֶׁר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Also to punish the righteous is not good, Nor to smite the noble for their uprightness.
					\item[NIV] If imposing a fine on the innocent is not good, surely to flog honest officials is not right.
					\item[NRSV] To impose a fine on the innocent is not right, or to flog the noble for their integrity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:27} % *PROVERBS-17-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-27}

			He that hath knowledge spareth his words: and a man of understanding is of an excellent spirit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חוֹשֵׂ֣ךְ אֲ֭מָרָיו יוֹדֵ֣עַ דָּ֑עַת וקר־[יְקַר־] ר֝֗וּחַ אִ֣ישׁ תְּבוּנָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that spareth his words hath knowledge; And he that is of a cool spirit is a man of understanding.
					\item[NIV] The one who has knowledge uses words with restraint, and whoever has understanding is even-tempered.
					\item[NRSV] One who spares words is knowledgeable; one who is cool in spirit has understanding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 17:28} % *PROVERBS-17-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-17-28}

			Even a fool, when he holdeth his peace, is counted wise: and he that shutteth his lips is esteemed a man of understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֤ם אֱוִ֣יל מַ֭חֲרִישׁ חָכָ֣ם יֵחָשֵׁ֑ב אֹטֵ֖ם שְׂפָתָ֣יו נָבֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Even a fool, when he holdeth his peace, is counted wise; When he shutteth his lips, he is esteemed as prudent.
					\item[NIV] Even fools are thought wise if they keep silent, and discerning if they hold their tongues.
					\item[NRSV] Even fools who keep silent are considered wise; when they close their lips, they are deemed intelligent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 18} % *PROVERBS-18
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-18}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:1} % *PROVERBS-18-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-1}

			Through desire a man, having separated himself, seeketh and intermeddleth with all wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְֽ֭תַאֲוָה יְבַקֵּ֣שׁ נִפְרָ֑ד בְּכָל־תּ֝וּשִׁיָּ֗ה יִתְגַּלָּֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that separateth himself seeketh his own desire, And rageth against all sound wisdom.
					\item[NIV] An unfriendly person pursues selfish ends and against all sound judgment starts quarrels.
					\item[NRSV] The one who lives alone is self-indulgent, showing contempt for all who have sound judgment.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:2} % *PROVERBS-18-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-2}

			A fool hath no delight in understanding, but that his heart may discover itself.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־יַחְפֹּ֣ץ כְּ֭סִיל בִּתְבוּנָ֑ה כִּ֝֗י אִֽם־בְּהִתְגַּלּ֥וֹת לִבּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool hath no delight in understanding, But only that his heart may reveal itself.
					\item[NIV] Fools find no pleasure in understanding but delight in airing their own opinions.
					\item[NRSV] A fool takes no pleasure in understanding, but only in expressing personal opinion.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:3} % *PROVERBS-18-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-3}

			When the wicked cometh, then cometh also contempt, and with ignominy reproach.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּֽבוֹא־רָ֭שָׁע בָּ֣א גַם־בּ֑וּז וְֽעִם־קָל֥וֹן חֶרְפָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the wicked cometh, there cometh also contempt, And with ignominy cometh reproach.
					\item[NIV] When wickedness comes, so does contempt, and with shame comes reproach.
					\item[NRSV] When wickedness comes, contempt comes also; and with dishonor comes disgrace.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:4} % *PROVERBS-18-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-4}

			The words of a man’s mouth are as deep waters, and the wellspring of wisdom as a flowing brook.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַ֣יִם עֲ֭מֻקִּים דִּבְרֵ֣י פִי־אִ֑ישׁ נַ֥חַל נֹ֝בֵ֗עַ מְק֣וֹר חָכְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of a man's mouth are as deep waters; The wellspring of wisdom is as a flowing brook.
					\item[NIV] The words of the mouth are deep waters, but the fountain of wisdom is a rushing stream.
					\item[NRSV] The words of the mouth are deep waters; the fountain of wisdom is a gushing stream.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:5} % *PROVERBS-18-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-5}

			It is not good to accept the person of the wicked, to overthrow the righteous in judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׂאֵ֣ת פְּנֵי־רָשָׁ֣ע לֹא־ט֑וֹב לְהַטּ֥וֹת צַ֝דִּ֗יק בַּמִּשְׁפָּֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To respect the person of the wicked is not good, Nor to turn aside the righteous in judgment.
					\item[NIV] It is not good to be partial to the wicked and so deprive the innocent of justice.
					\item[NRSV] It is not right to be partial to the guilty, or to subvert the innocent in judgment.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:6} % *PROVERBS-18-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-6}

			A fool’s lips enter into contention, and his mouth calleth for strokes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂפְתֵ֣י כְ֭סִיל יָבֹ֣אֽוּ בְרִ֑יב וּ֝פִ֗יו לְֽמַהֲלֻמ֥וֹת יִקְרָֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool's lips enter into contention, And his mouth calleth for stripes.
					\item[NIV] The lips of fools bring them strife, and their mouths invite a beating.
					\item[NRSV] A fool’s lips bring strife, and a fool’s mouth invites a flogging.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:7} % *PROVERBS-18-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-7}

			A fool’s mouth is his destruction, and his lips are the snare of his soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פִּֽי־כְ֭סִיל מְחִתָּה־ל֑וֹ וּ֝שְׂפָתָ֗יו מוֹקֵ֥שׁ נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool's mouth is his destruction, And his lips are the snare of his soul.
					\item[NIV] The mouths of fools are their undoing, and their lips are a snare to their very lives.
					\item[NRSV] The mouths of fools are their ruin, and their lips a snare to themselves.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:8} % *PROVERBS-18-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-8}

			The words of a talebearer are as wounds, and they go down into the innermost parts of the belly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּבְרֵ֣י נִ֭רְגָּן כְּמִֽתְלַהֲמִ֑ים וְ֝הֵ֗ם יָרְד֥וּ חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of a whisperer are as dainty morsels, And they go down into the innermost parts.
					\item[NIV] The words of a gossip are like choice morsels; they go down to the inmost parts.
					\item[NRSV] The words of a whisperer are like delicious morsels; they go down into the inner parts of the body.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:9} % *PROVERBS-18-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-9}

			He also that is slothful in his work is brother to him that is a great waster.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֭ם מִתְרַפֶּ֣ה בִמְלַאכְתּ֑וֹ אָ֥ח ה֝֗וּא לְבַ֣עַל מַשְׁחִֽית׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He also that is slack in his work Is brother to him that is a destroyer.
					\item[NIV] One who is slack in his work is brother to one who destroys.
					\item[NRSV] One who is slack in work is close kin to a vandal.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:10} % *PROVERBS-18-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-10}

			The name of the LORD is a strong tower: the righteous runneth into it, and is safe.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִגְדַּל־עֹ֭ז שֵׁ֣ם יְהוָ֑ה בּֽוֹ־יָר֖וּץ צַדִּ֣יק וְנִשְׂגָּֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The name of Jehovah is a strong tower; The righteous runneth into it, and is safe.
					\item[NIV] The name of the LORD is a fortified tower; the righteous run to it and are safe.
					\item[NRSV] The name of the LORD is a strong tower; the righteous run into it and are safe.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:11} % *PROVERBS-18-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-11}

			The rich man’s wealth is his strong city, and as an high wall in his own conceit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֣וֹן עָ֭שִׁיר קִרְיַ֣ת עֻזּ֑וֹ וּכְחוֹמָ֥ה נִ֝שְׂגָּבָ֗ה בְּמַשְׂכִּיתֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The rich man's wealth is his strong city, And as a high wall in his own imagination.
					\item[NIV] The wealth of the rich is their fortified city; they imagine it a wall too high to scale.
					\item[NRSV] The wealth of the rich is their strong city; in their imagination it is like a high wall.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:12} % *PROVERBS-18-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-12}

			Before destruction the heart of man is haughty, and before honour is humility.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִפְנֵי־שֶׁ֭בֶר יִגְבַּ֣הּ לֵב־אִ֑ישׁ וְלִפְנֵ֖י כָב֣וֹד עֲנָוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Before destruction the heart of man is haughty; And before honor goeth humility.
					\item[NIV] Before a downfall the heart is haughty, but humility comes before honor.
					\item[NRSV] Before destruction one’s heart is haughty, but humility goes before honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:13} % *PROVERBS-18-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-13}

			He that answereth a matter before he heareth it, it is folly and shame unto him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵשִׁ֣יב דָּ֭בָר בְּטֶ֣רֶם יִשְׁמָ֑ע אִוֶּ֥לֶת הִיא־ל֝֗וֹ וּכְלִמָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that giveth answer before he heareth, It is folly and shame unto him.
					\item[NIV] To answer before listening— that is folly and shame.
					\item[NRSV] If one gives answer before hearing, it is folly and shame.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:14} % *PROVERBS-18-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-14}

			The spirit of a man will sustain his infirmity; but a wounded spirit who can bear?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רֽוּחַ־אִ֭ישׁ יְכַלְכֵּ֣ל מַחֲלֵ֑הוּ וְר֥וּחַ נְ֝כֵאָ֗ה מִ֣י יִשָּׂאֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The spirit of a man will sustain his infirmity; But a broken spirit who can bear?
					\item[NIV] The human spirit can endure in sickness, but a crushed spirit who can bear?
					\item[NRSV] The human spirit will endure sickness; but a broken spirit—who can bear?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:15} % *PROVERBS-18-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-15}

			The heart of the prudent getteth knowledge; and the ear of the wise seeketh knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ב נָ֭בוֹן יִקְנֶה־דָּ֑עַת וְאֹ֥זֶן חֲ֝כָמִ֗ים תְּבַקֶּשׁ־דָּֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The heart of the prudent getteth knowledge; And the ear of the wise seeketh knowledge.
					\item[NIV] The heart of the discerning acquires knowledge, for the ears of the wise seek it out.
					\item[NRSV] An intelligent mind acquires knowledge, and the ear of the wise seeks knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:16} % *PROVERBS-18-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-16}

			A man’s gift maketh room for him, and bringeth him before great men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַתָּ֣ן אָ֭דָם יַרְחִ֣יב ל֑וֹ וְלִפְנֵ֖י גְדֹלִ֣ים יַנְחֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man's gift maketh room for him, And bringeth him before great men.
					\item[NIV] A gift opens the way and ushers the giver into the presence of the great.
					\item[NRSV] A gift opens doors; it gives access to the great.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:17} % *PROVERBS-18-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-17}

			He that is first in his own cause seemeth just; but his neighbour cometh and searcheth him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צַדִּ֣יק הָרִאשׁ֣וֹן בְּרִיב֑וֹ יבא־[וּבָֽא־] רֵ֝עֵ֗הוּ וַחֲקָרֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that pleadeth his cause first seemeth just; But his neighbor cometh and searcheth him out.
					\item[NIV] In a lawsuit the first to speak seems right, until someone comes forward and cross-examines.
					\item[NRSV] The one who first states a case seems right, until the other comes and cross-examines.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:18} % *PROVERBS-18-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-18}

			The lot causeth contentions to cease, and parteth between the mighty.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֭דְיָנִים יַשְׁבִּ֣ית הַגּוֹרָ֑ל וּבֵ֖ין עֲצוּמִ֣ים יַפְרִֽיד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lot causeth contentions to cease, And parteth between the mighty.
					\item[NIV] Casting the lot settles disputes and keeps strong opponents apart.
					\item[NRSV] Casting the lot puts an end to disputes and decides between powerful contenders.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:19} % *PROVERBS-18-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-19}

			A brother offended is harder to be won than a strong city: and their contentions are like the bars of a castle.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֗ח נִפְשָׁ֥ע מִקִּרְיַת־עֹ֑ז וּ֝מִדְיָנִ֗ים כִּבְרִ֥יחַ אַרְמֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A brother offended is harder to be won than a strong city; And such contentions are like the bars of a castle.
					\item[NIV] A brother wronged is more unyielding than a fortified city; disputes are like the barred gates of a citadel.
					\item[NRSV] An ally offended is stronger than a city; such quarreling is like the bars of a castle.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:20} % *PROVERBS-18-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-20}

			A man’s belly shall be satisfied with the fruit of his mouth; and with the increase of his lips shall he be filled.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִפְּרִ֣י פִי־אִ֭ישׁ תִּשְׂבַּ֣ע בִּטְנ֑וֹ תְּבוּאַ֖ת שְׂפָתָ֣יו יִשְׂבָּֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man's belly shall be filled with the fruit of his mouth; With the increase of his lips shall he be satisfied.
					\item[NIV] From the fruit of their mouth a person’s stomach is filled; with the harvest of their lips they are satisfied.
					\item[NRSV] From the fruit of the mouth one’s stomach is satisfied; the yield of the lips brings satisfaction.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:21} % *PROVERBS-18-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-21}

			Death and life are in the power of the tongue: and they that love it shall eat the fruit thereof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מָ֣וֶת וְ֭חַיִּים בְּיַד־לָשׁ֑וֹן וְ֝אֹהֲבֶ֗יהָ יֹאכַ֥ל פִּרְיָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Death and life are in the power of the tongue; And they that love it shall eat the fruit thereof.
					\item[NIV] The tongue has the power of life and death, and those who love it will eat its fruit.
					\item[NRSV] Death and life are in the power of the tongue, and those who love it will eat its fruits.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:22} % *PROVERBS-18-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-22}

			Whoso findeth a wife findeth a good thing, and obtaineth favour of the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מָצָ֣א אִ֭שָּׁה מָ֣צָא ט֑וֹב וַיָּ֥פֶק רָ֝צ֗וֹן מֵיְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso findeth a wife findeth a good thing, And obtaineth favor of Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] He who finds a wife finds what is good and receives favor from the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] He who finds a wife finds a good thing, and obtains favor from the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:23} % *PROVERBS-18-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-23}

			The poor useth intreaties; but the rich answereth roughly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּחֲנוּנִ֥ים יְדַבֶּר־רָ֑שׁ וְ֝עָשִׁ֗יר יַעֲנֶ֥ה עַזּֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The poor useth entreaties; But the rich answereth roughly.
					\item[NIV] The poor plead for mercy, but the rich answer harshly.
					\item[NRSV] The poor use entreaties, but the rich answer roughly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 18:24} % *PROVERBS-18-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-18-24}

			A man that hath friends must shew himself friendly: and there is a friend that sticketh closer than a brother.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ רֵ֭עִים לְהִתְרֹעֵ֑עַ וְיֵ֥שׁ אֹ֝הֵ֗ב דָּבֵ֥ק מֵאָֽח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that maketh many friends doeth it to his own destruction; But there is a friend that sticketh closer than a brother.
					\item[NIV] One who has unreliable friends soon comes to ruin, but there is a friend who sticks closer than a brother.
					\item[NRSV] Some friends play at friendship but a true friend sticks closer than one’s nearest kin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 19} % *PROVERBS-19
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-19}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:1} % *PROVERBS-19-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-1}

			Better is the poor that walketh in his integrity, than he that is perverse in his lips, and is a fool.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טֽוֹב־רָ֭שׁ הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בְּתֻמּ֑וֹ מֵעִקֵּ֥שׁ שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו וְה֣וּא כְסִֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is the poor that walketh in his integrity Than he that is perverse in his lips and is a fool.
					\item[NIV] Better the poor whose walk is blameless than a fool whose lips are perverse.
					\item[NRSV] Better the poor walking in integrity than one perverse of speech who is a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:2} % *PROVERBS-19-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-2}

			Also, that the soul be without knowledge, it is not good; and he that hasteth with his feet sinneth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֤ם בְּלֹא־דַ֣עַת נֶ֣פֶשׁ לֹא־ט֑וֹב וְאָ֖ץ בְּרַגְלַ֣יִם חוֹטֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Also, that the soul be without knowledge is not good; And he that hasteth with his feet sinneth.
					\item[NIV] Desire without knowledge is not good— how much more will hasty feet miss the way!
					\item[NRSV] Desire without knowledge is not good, and one who moves too hurriedly misses the way.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:3} % *PROVERBS-19-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-3}

			The foolishness of man perverteth his way: and his heart fretteth against the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִוֶּ֣לֶת אָ֭דָם תְּסַלֵּ֣ף דַּרְכּ֑וֹ וְעַל־יְ֝הוָ֗ה יִזְעַ֥ף לִבּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The foolishness of man subverteth his way; And his heart fretteth against Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] A person’s own folly leads to their ruin, yet their heart rages against the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] One’s own folly leads to ruin, yet the heart rages against the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:4} % *PROVERBS-19-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-4}

			Wealth maketh many friends; but the poor is separated from his neighbour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ה֗וֹן יֹ֭סִיף רֵעִ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים וְ֝דָ֗ל מֵרֵ֥עהוּ יִפָּרֵֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wealth addeth many friends; But the poor is separated from his friend.
					\item[NIV] Wealth attracts many friends, but even the closest friend of the poor person deserts them.
					\item[NRSV] Wealth brings many friends, but the poor are left friendless.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:5} % *PROVERBS-19-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-5}

			A false witness shall not be unpunished, and he that speaketh lies shall not escape.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֣ד שְׁ֭קָרִים לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶ֑ה וְיָפִ֥יחַ כְּ֝זָבִ֗ים לֹ֣א יִמָּלֵֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A false witness shall not be unpunished; And he that uttereth lies shall not escape.
					\item[NIV] A false witness will not go unpunished, and whoever pours out lies will not go free.
					\item[NRSV] A false witness will not go unpunished, and a liar will not escape.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:6} % *PROVERBS-19-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-6}

			Many will intreat the favour of the prince: and every man is a friend to him that giveth gifts.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַ֭בִּים יְחַלּ֣וּ פְנֵֽי־נָדִ֑יב וְכָל־הָ֝רֵ֗עַ לְאִ֣ישׁ מַתָּֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Many will entreat the favor of the liberal man; And every man is a friend to him that giveth gifts.
					\item[NIV] Many curry favor with a ruler, and everyone is the friend of one who gives gifts.
					\item[NRSV] Many seek the favor of the generous, and everyone is a friend to a giver of gifts.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:7} % *PROVERBS-19-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-7}

			All the brethren of the poor do hate him: how much more do his friends go far from him? he pursueth them with words, yet they are wanting to him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּ֥ל אֲחֵי־רָ֨שׁ ׀ שְֽׂנֵאֻ֗הוּ אַ֤ף כִּ֣י מְ֭רֵעֵהוּ רָחֲק֣וּ מִמֶּ֑נּוּ מְרַדֵּ֖ף אֲמָרִ֣ים לא־[לוֹ־] הֵֽמָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] All the brethren of the poor do hate him: How much more do his friends go far from him! He pursueth them with words, but they are gone.
					\item[NIV] The poor are shunned by all their relatives— how much more do their friends avoid them! Though the poor pursue them with pleading, they are nowhere to be found.
					\item[NRSV] If the poor are hated even by their kin, how much more are they shunned by their friends! When they call after them, they are not there.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:8} % *PROVERBS-19-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-8}

			He that getteth wisdom loveth his own soul: he that keepeth understanding shall find good.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קֹֽנֶה־לֵּ֭ב אֹהֵ֣ב נַפְשׁ֑וֹ שֹׁמֵ֥ר תְּ֝בוּנָ֗ה לִמְצֹא־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that getteth wisdom loveth his own soul: He that keepeth understanding shall find good.
					\item[NIV] The one who gets wisdom loves life; the one who cherishes understanding will soon prosper.
					\item[NRSV] To get wisdom is to love oneself; to keep understanding is to prosper.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:9} % *PROVERBS-19-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-9}

			A false witness shall not be unpunished, and he that speaketh lies shall perish.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֣ד שְׁ֭קָרִים לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶ֑ה וְיָפִ֖יחַ כְּזָבִ֣ים יֹאבֵֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A false witness shall not be unpunished; And he that uttereth lies shall perish.
					\item[NIV] A false witness will not go unpunished, and whoever pours out lies will perish.
					\item[NRSV] A false witness will not go unpunished, and the liar will perish.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:10} % *PROVERBS-19-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-10}

			Delight is not seemly for a fool; much less for a servant to have rule over princes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹֽא־נָאוֶ֣ה לִכְסִ֣יל תַּעֲנ֑וּג אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־לְעֶ֤בֶד ׀ מְשֹׁ֬ל בְּשָׂרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Delicate living is not seemly for a fool; Much less for a servant to have rule over princes.
					\item[NIV] It is not fitting for a fool to live in luxury— how much worse for a slave to rule over princes!
					\item[NRSV] It is not fitting for a fool to live in luxury, much less for a slave to rule over princes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:11} % *PROVERBS-19-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-11}

			The discretion of a man deferreth his anger; and it is his glory to pass over a transgression.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֵׂ֣כֶל אָ֭דָם הֶאֱרִ֣יךְ אַפּ֑וֹ וְ֝תִפאַרְתּ֗וֹ עֲבֹ֣ר עַל־פָּֽשַׁע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The discretion of a man maketh him slow to anger; And it is his glory to pass over a transgression.
					\item[NIV] A person’s wisdom yields patience; it is to one’s glory to overlook an offense.
					\item[NRSV] Those with good sense are slow to anger, and it is their glory to overlook an offense.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:12} % *PROVERBS-19-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-12}

			The king’s wrath is as the roaring of a lion; but his favour is as dew upon the grass.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נַ֣הַם כַּ֭כְּפִיר זַ֣עַף מֶ֑לֶךְ וּכְטַ֖ל עַל־עֵ֣שֶׂב רְצוֹנֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The king's wrath is as the roaring of a lion; But his favor is as dew upon the grass.
					\item[NIV] A king’s rage is like the roar of a lion, but his favor is like dew on the grass.
					\item[NRSV] A king’s anger is like the growling of a lion, but his favor is like dew on the grass.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:13} % *PROVERBS-19-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-13}

			A foolish son is the calamity of his father: and the contentions of a wife are a continual dropping.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַוֺּ֣ת לְ֭אָבִיו בֵּ֣ן כְּסִ֑יל וְדֶ֥לֶף טֹ֝רֵ֗ד מִדְיְנֵ֥י אִשָּֽׁה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A foolish son is the calamity of his father; And the contentions of a wife are a continual dropping.
					\item[NIV] A foolish child is a father’s ruin, and a quarrelsome wife is like the constant dripping of a leaky roof.
					\item[NRSV] A stupid child is ruin to a father, and a wife’s quarreling is a continual dripping of rain.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:14} % *PROVERBS-19-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-14}

			House and riches are the inheritance of fathers: and a prudent wife is from the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּ֣יִת וָ֭הוֹן נַחֲלַ֣ת אָב֑וֹת וּ֝מֵיְהוָ֗ה אִשָּׁ֥ה מַשְׂכָּֽלֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] House and riches are an inheritance from fathers; But a prudent wife is from Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] Houses and wealth are inherited from parents, but a prudent wife is from the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] House and wealth are inherited from parents, but a prudent wife is from the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:15} % *PROVERBS-19-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-15}

			Slothfulness casteth into a deep sleep; and an idle soul shall suffer hunger.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַ֭צְלָה תַּפִּ֣יל תַּרְדֵּמָ֑ה וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ רְמִיָּ֣ה תִרְעָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Slothfulness casteth into a deep sleep; And the idle soul shall suffer hunger.
					\item[NIV] Laziness brings on deep sleep, and the shiftless go hungry.
					\item[NRSV] Laziness brings on deep sleep; an idle person will suffer hunger.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:16} % *PROVERBS-19-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-16}

			He that keepeth the commandment keepeth his own soul; but he that despiseth his ways shall die.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֹׁמֵ֣ר מִ֭צְוָה שֹׁמֵ֣ר נַפְשׁ֑וֹ בּוֹזֵ֖ה דְרָכָ֣יו יָמֽוּת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that keepeth the commandment keepeth his soul; But he that is careless of his ways shall die.
					\item[NIV] Whoever keeps commandments keeps their life, but whoever shows contempt for their ways will die.
					\item[NRSV] Those who keep the commandment will live; those who are heedless of their ways will die.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:17} % *PROVERBS-19-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-17}

			He that hath pity upon the poor lendeth unto the LORD; and that which he hath given will he pay him again.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַלְוֵ֣ה יְ֭הוָה ח֣וֹנֵֽן דָּ֑ל וּ֝גְמֻל֗וֹ יְשַׁלֶּם־לֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that hath pity upon the poor lendeth unto Jehovah, And his good deed will he pay him again.
					\item[NIV] Whoever is kind to the poor lends to the LORD, and he will reward them for what they have done.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever is kind to the poor lends to the LORD, and will be repaid in full.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:18} % *PROVERBS-19-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-18}

			Chasten thy son while there is hope, and let not thy soul spare for his crying.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַסֵּ֣ר בִּ֭נְךָ כִּי־יֵ֣שׁ תִּקְוָ֑ה וְאֶל־הֲ֝מִית֗וֹ אַל־תִּשָּׂ֥א נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Chasten thy son, seeing there is hope; And set not thy heart on his destruction.
					\item[NIV] Discipline your children, for in that there is hope; do not be a willing party to their death.
					\item[NRSV] Discipline your children while there is hope; do not set your heart on their destruction.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:19} % *PROVERBS-19-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-19}

			A man of great wrath shall suffer punishment: for if thou deliver him, yet thou must do it again.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גרל־[גְּֽדָל־] חֵ֭מָה נֹ֣שֵׂא עֹ֑נֶשׁ כִּ֥י אִם־תַּ֝צִּ֗יל וְע֣וֹד תּוֹסִֽף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man of great wrath shall bear the penalty; For if thou deliver him, thou must do it yet again.
					\item[NIV] A hot-tempered person must pay the penalty; rescue them, and you will have to do it again.
					\item[NRSV] A violent tempered person will pay the penalty; if you effect a rescue, you will only have to do it again.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:20} % *PROVERBS-19-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-20}

			Hear counsel, and receive instruction, that thou mayest be wise in thy latter end.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁמַ֣ע עֵ֭צָה וְקַבֵּ֣ל מוּסָ֑ר לְ֝מַ֗עַן תֶּחְכַּ֥ם בְּאַחֲרִיתֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear counsel, and receive instruction, That thou mayest be wise in thy latter end.
					\item[NIV] Listen to advice and accept discipline, and at the end you will be counted among the wise.
					\item[NRSV] Listen to advice and accept instruction, that you may gain wisdom for the future.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:21} % *PROVERBS-19-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-21}

			There are many devices in a man’s heart; nevertheless the counsel of the LORD, that shall stand.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַבּ֣וֹת מַחֲשָׁב֣וֹת בְּלֶב־אִ֑ישׁ וַעֲצַ֥ת יְ֝הוָ֗ה הִ֣יא תָקֽוּם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There are many devices in a man's heart; But the counsel of Jehovah, that shall stand.
					\item[NIV] Many are the plans in a person’s heart, but it is the LORD’s purpose that prevails.
					\item[NRSV] The human mind may devise many plans, but it is the purpose of the LORD that will be established.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:22} % *PROVERBS-19-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-22}

			The desire of a man is his kindness: and a poor man is better than a liar.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּאֲוַ֣ת אָדָ֣ם חַסְדּ֑וֹ וְטֽוֹב־רָ֝שׁ מֵאִ֥ישׁ כָּזָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That which maketh a man to be desired is his kindness; And a poor man is better than a liar.
					\item[NIV] What a person desires is unfailing love; better to be poor than a liar.
					\item[NRSV] What is desirable in a person is loyalty, and it is better to be poor than a liar.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:23} % *PROVERBS-19-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-23}

			The fear of the LORD tendeth to life: and he that hath it shall abide satisfied; he shall not be visited with evil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִרְאַ֣ת יְהוָ֣ה לְחַיִּ֑ים וְשָׂבֵ֥עַ יָ֝לִ֗ין בַּל־יִפָּ֥קֶד רָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of Jehovah tendeth to life; And he that hath it shall abide satisfied; He shall not be visited with evil.
					\item[NIV] The fear of the LORD leads to life; then one rests content, untouched by trouble.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of the LORD is life indeed; filled with it one rests secure and suffers no harm.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:24} % *PROVERBS-19-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-24}

			A slothful man hideth his hand in his bosom, and will not so much as bring it to his mouth again.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טָ֘מַ֤ן עָצֵ֣ל יָ֭דוֹ בַּצַּלָּ֑חַת גַּם־אֶל־פִּ֝֗יהוּ לֹ֣א יְשִׁיבֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sluggard burieth his hand in the dish, And will not so much as bring it to his mouth again.
					\item[NIV] A sluggard buries his hand in the dish; he will not even bring it back to his mouth!
					\item[NRSV] The lazy person buries a hand in the dish, and will not even bring it back to the mouth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:25} % *PROVERBS-19-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-25}

			Smite a scorner, and the simple will beware: and reprove one that hath understanding, and he will understand knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ץ תַּ֭כֶּה וּפֶ֣תִי יַעְרִ֑ם וְהוֹכִ֥יחַ לְ֝נָב֗וֹן יָבִ֥ין דָּֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Smite a scoffer, and the simple will learn prudence; And reprove one that hath understanding, and he will understand knowledge.
					\item[NIV] Flog a mocker, and the simple will learn prudence; rebuke the discerning, and they will gain knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] Strike a scoffer, and the simple will learn prudence; reprove the intelligent, and they will gain knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:26} % *PROVERBS-19-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-26}

			He that wasteth his father, and chaseth away his mother, is a son that causeth shame, and bringeth reproach.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְֽשַׁדֶּד־אָ֭ב יַבְרִ֣יחַ אֵ֑ם בֵּ֝֗ן מֵבִ֥ישׁ וּמַחְפִּֽיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that doeth violence to his father, and chaseth away his mother, Is a son that causeth shame and bringeth reproach.
					\item[NIV] Whoever robs their father and drives out their mother is a child who brings shame and disgrace.
					\item[NRSV] Those who do violence to their father and chase away their mother are children who cause shame and bring reproach.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:27} % *PROVERBS-19-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-27}

			Cease, my son, to hear the instruction that causeth to err from the words of knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַֽדַל־בְּ֭נִי לִשְׁמֹ֣עַ מוּסָ֑ר לִ֝שְׁג֗וֹת מֵֽאִמְרֵי־דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Cease, my son, to hear instruction Only to err from the words of knowledge.
					\item[NIV] Stop listening to instruction, my son, and you will stray from the words of knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] Cease straying, my child, from the words of knowledge, in order that you may hear instruction.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:28} % *PROVERBS-19-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-28}

			An ungodly witness scorneth judgment: and the mouth of the wicked devoureth iniquity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֣ד בְּ֭לִיַּעַל יָלִ֣יץ מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּפִ֥י רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְבַלַּע־אָֽוֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A worthless witness mocketh at justice; And the mouth of the wicked swalloweth iniquity.
					\item[NIV] A corrupt witness mocks at justice, and the mouth of the wicked gulps down evil.
					\item[NRSV] A worthless witness mocks at justice, and the mouth of the wicked devours iniquity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 19:29} % *PROVERBS-19-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-19-29}

			Judgments are prepared for scorners, and stripes for the back of fools.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נָכ֣וֹנוּ לַלֵּצִ֣ים שְׁפָטִ֑ים וּ֝מַהֲלֻמ֗וֹת לְגֵ֣ו כְּסִילִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Judgments are prepared for scoffers, And stripes for the back of fools.
					\item[NIV] Penalties are prepared for mockers, and beatings for the backs of fools.
					\item[NRSV] Condemnation is ready for scoffers, and flogging for the backs of fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 20} % *PROVERBS-20
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-20}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:1} % *PROVERBS-20-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-1}

			Wine is a mocker, strong drink is raging: and whosoever is deceived thereby is not wise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֵ֣ץ הַ֭יַּין הֹמֶ֣ה שֵׁכָ֑ר וְכָל־שֹׁ֥גֶה בּ֝֗וֹ לֹ֣א יֶחְכָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wine is a mocker, strong drink a brawler; And whosoever erreth thereby is not wise.
					\item[NIV] Wine is a mocker and beer a brawler; whoever is led astray by them is not wise.
					\item[NRSV] Wine is a mocker, strong drink a brawler, and whoever is led astray by it is not wise.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:2} % *PROVERBS-20-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-2}

			The fear of a king is as the roaring of a lion: whoso provoketh him to anger sinneth against his own soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נַ֣הַם כַּ֭כְּפִיר אֵ֣ימַת מֶ֑לֶךְ מִ֝תְעַבְּר֗וֹ חוֹטֵ֥א נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The terror of a king is as the roaring of a lion: He that provoketh him to anger sinneth against his own life.
					\item[NIV] A king’s wrath strikes terror like the roar of a lion; those who anger him forfeit their lives.
					\item[NRSV] The dread anger of a king is like the growling of a lion; anyone who provokes him to anger forfeits life itself.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:3} % *PROVERBS-20-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-3}

			It is an honour for a man to cease from strife: but every fool will be meddling.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּב֣וֹד לָ֭אִישׁ שֶׁ֣בֶת מֵרִ֑יב וְכָל־אֱ֝וִ֗יל יִתְגַּלָּֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is an honor for a man to keep aloof from strife; But every fool will be quarrelling.
					\item[NIV] It is to one’s honor to avoid strife, but every fool is quick to quarrel.
					\item[NRSV] It is honorable to refrain from strife, but every fool is quick to quarrel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:4} % *PROVERBS-20-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-4}

			The sluggard will not plow by reason of the cold; therefore shall he beg in harvest, and have nothing.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵ֭חֹרֶף עָצֵ֣ל לֹא־יַחֲרֹ֑שׁ וְשָׁאַ֖ל בַּקָּצִ֣יר וָאָֽיִן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sluggard will not plow by reason of the winter; Therefore he shall beg in harvest, and have nothing.
					\item[NIV] Sluggards do not plow in season; so at harvest time they look but find nothing.
					\item[NRSV] The lazy person does not plow in season; harvest comes, and there is nothing to be found.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:5} % *PROVERBS-20-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-5}

			Counsel in the heart of man is like deep water; but a man of understanding will draw it out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַ֣יִם עֲ֭מֻקִּים עֵצָ֣ה בְלֶב־אִ֑ישׁ וְאִ֖ישׁ תְּבוּנָ֣ה יִדְלֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Counsel in the heart of man is like deep water; But a man of understanding will draw it out.
					\item[NIV] The purposes of a person’s heart are deep waters, but one who has insight draws them out.
					\item[NRSV] The purposes in the human mind are like deep water, but the intelligent will draw them out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:6} % *PROVERBS-20-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-6}

			Most men will proclaim every one his own goodness: but a faithful man who can find?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָב־אָדָ֗ם יִ֭קְרָא אִ֣ישׁ חַסְדּ֑וֹ וְאִ֥ישׁ אֱ֝מוּנִ֗ים מִ֣י יִמְצָֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Most men will proclaim every one his own kindness; But a faithful man who can find?
