					\item[NIV] Many claim to have unfailing love, but a faithful person who can find?
					\item[NRSV] Many proclaim themselves loyal, but who can find one worthy of trust?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:7} % *PROVERBS-20-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-7}

			The just man walketh in his integrity: his children are blessed after him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִתְהַלֵּ֣ךְ בְּתֻמּ֣וֹ צַדִּ֑יק אַשְׁרֵ֖י בָנָ֣יו אַחֲרָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A righteous man that walketh in his integrity, Blessed are his children after him.
					\item[NIV] The righteous lead blameless lives; blessed are their children after them.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous walk in integrity— happy are the children who follow them!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:8} % *PROVERBS-20-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-8}

			A king that sitteth in the throne of judgment scattereth away all evil with his eyes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֶ֗לֶךְ יוֹשֵׁ֥ב עַל־כִּסֵּא־דִ֑ין מְזָרֶ֖ה בְעֵינָ֣יו כָּל־רָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A king that sitteth on the throne of judgment Scattereth away all evil with his eyes.
					\item[NIV] When a king sits on his throne to judge, he winnows out all evil with his eyes.
					\item[NRSV] A king who sits on the throne of judgment winnows all evil with his eyes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:9} % *PROVERBS-20-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-9}

			Who can say, I have made my heart clean, I am pure from my sin?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִֽי־יֹ֭אמַר זִכִּ֣יתִי לִבִּ֑י טָ֝הַ֗רְתִּי מֵחַטָּאתִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who can say, I have made my heart clean, I am pure from my sin?
					\item[NIV] Who can say, “I have kept my heart pure; I am clean and without sin"?
					\item[NRSV] Who can say, “I have made my heart clean; I am pure from my sin”?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:10} % *PROVERBS-20-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-10}

			Divers weights, and divers measures, both of them are alike abomination to the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֶ֣בֶן וָ֭אֶבֶן אֵיפָ֣ה וְאֵיפָ֑ה תּוֹעֲבַ֥ת יְ֝הוָ֗ה גַּם־שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Diverse weights, and diverse measures, Both of them alike are an abomination to Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] Differing weights and differing measures— the LORD detests them both.
					\item[NRSV] Diverse weights and diverse measures are both alike an abomination to the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:11} % *PROVERBS-20-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-11}

			Even a child is known by his doings, whether his work be pure, and whether it be right.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּ֣ם בְּ֭מַעֲלָלָיו יִתְנַכֶּר־נָ֑עַר אִם־זַ֖ךְ וְאִם־יָשָׁ֣ר פָּעֳלֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Even a child maketh himself known by his doings, Whether his work be pure, and whether it be right.
					\item[NIV] Even small children are known by their actions, so is their conduct really pure and upright?
					\item[NRSV] Even children make themselves known by their acts, by whether what they do is pure and right.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:12} % *PROVERBS-20-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-12}

			The hearing ear, and the seeing eye, the LORD hath made even both of them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֣זֶן שֹׁ֭מַעַת וְעַ֣יִן רֹאָ֑ה יְ֝הוָ֗ה עָשָׂ֥ה גַם־שְׁנֵיהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The hearing ear, and the seeing eye, Jehovah hath made even both of them.
					\item[NIV] Ears that hear and eyes that see— the LORD has made them both.
					\item[NRSV] The hearing ear and the seeing eye— the LORD has made them both.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:13} % *PROVERBS-20-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-13}

			Love not sleep, lest thou come to poverty; open thine eyes, and thou shalt be satisfied with bread.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֶּֽאֱהַ֣ב שֵׁ֭נָה פֶּן־תִּוָּרֵ֑שׁ פְּקַ֖ח עֵינֶ֣יךָ שְֽׂבַֽע־לָֽחֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Love not sleep, let thou come to poverty; Open thine eyes, and thou shalt be satisfied with bread.
					\item[NIV] Do not love sleep or you will grow poor; stay awake and you will have food to spare.
					\item[NRSV] Do not love sleep, or else you will come to poverty; open your eyes, and you will have plenty of bread.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:14} % *PROVERBS-20-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-14}

			It is naught, it is naught, saith the buyer: but when he is gone his way, then he boasteth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַ֣ע רַ֭ע יֹאמַ֣ר הַקּוֹנֶ֑ה וְאֹזֵ֥ל ל֝֗וֹ אָ֣ז יִתְהַלָּֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is bad, it is bad, saith the buyer; But when he is gone his way, then he boasteth.
					\item[NIV] “It’s no good, it’s no good!” says the buyer— then goes off and boasts about the purchase.
					\item[NRSV] “Bad, bad,” says the buyer, then goes away and boasts.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:15} % *PROVERBS-20-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-15}

			There is gold, and a multitude of rubies: but the lips of knowledge are a precious jewel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֵ֣שׁ זָ֭הָב וְרָב־פְּנִינִ֑ים וּכְלִ֥י יְ֝קָ֗ר שִׂפְתֵי־דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is gold, and abundance of rubies; But the lips of knowledge are a precious jewel.
					\item[NIV] Gold there is, and rubies in abundance, but lips that speak knowledge are a rare jewel.
					\item[NRSV] There is gold, and abundance of costly stones; but the lips informed by knowledge are a precious jewel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:16} % *PROVERBS-20-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-16}

			Take his garment that is surety for a stranger: and take a pledge of him for a strange woman.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְֽקַח־בִּ֭גְדוֹ כִּי־עָ֣רַב זָ֑ר וּבְעַ֖ד נָכְרִיָּ֣ה חַבְלֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Take his garment that is surety for a stranger; And hold him in pledge that is surety for foreigners.
					\item[NIV] Take the garment of one who puts up security for a stranger; hold it in pledge if it is done for an outsider.
					\item[NRSV] Take the garment of one who has given surety for a stranger; seize the pledge given as surety for foreigners.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:17} % *PROVERBS-20-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-17}

			Bread of deceit is sweet to a man; but afterwards his mouth shall be filled with gravel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָרֵ֣ב לָ֭אִישׁ לֶ֣חֶם שָׁ֑קֶר וְ֝אַחַ֗ר יִמָּֽלֵא־פִ֥יהוּ חָצָֽץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Bread of falsehood is sweet to a man; But afterwards his mouth shall be filled with gravel.
					\item[NIV] Food gained by fraud tastes sweet, but one ends up with a mouth full of gravel.
					\item[NRSV] Bread gained by deceit is sweet, but afterward the mouth will be full of gravel.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:18} % *PROVERBS-20-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-18}

			Every purpose is established by counsel: and with good advice make war.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַ֭חֲשָׁבוֹת בְּעֵצָ֣ה תִכּ֑וֹן וּ֝בְתַחְבֻּל֗וֹת עֲשֵׂ֣ה מִלְחָמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Every purpose is established by counsel; And by wise guidance make thou war.
					\item[NIV] Plans are established by seeking advice; so if you wage war, obtain guidance.
					\item[NRSV] Plans are established by taking advice; wage war by following wise guidance.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:19} % *PROVERBS-20-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-19}

			He that goeth about as a talebearer revealeth secrets: therefore meddle not with him that flattereth with his lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גּֽוֹלֶה־סּ֭וֹד הוֹלֵ֣ךְ רָכִ֑יל וּלְפֹתֶ֥ה שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו לֹ֣א תִתְעָרָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that goeth about as a tale-bearer revealeth secrets; Therefore company not with him that openeth wide his lips.
					\item[NIV] A gossip betrays a confidence; so avoid anyone who talks too much.
					\item[NRSV] A gossip reveals secrets; therefore do not associate with a babbler.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:20} % *PROVERBS-20-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-20}

			Whoso curseth his father or his mother, his lamp shall be put out in obscure darkness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְ֭קַלֵּל אָבִ֣יו וְאִמּ֑וֹ יִֽדְעַ֥ךְ נֵ֝ר֗וֹ בֶּאֱשׁ֥וּן חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso curseth his father or his mother, His lamp shall be put out in blackness of darkness.
					\item[NIV] If someone curses their father or mother, their lamp will be snuffed out in pitch darkness.
					\item[NRSV] If you curse father or mother, your lamp will go out in utter darkness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:21} % *PROVERBS-20-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-21}

			An inheritance may be gotten hastily at the beginning; but the end thereof shall not be blessed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נַ֭חֲלָה מְבֹהֶ֣לֶת בָּרִאשֹׁנָ֑ה וְ֝אַחֲרִיתָ֗הּ לֹ֣א תְבֹרָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] An inheritance may be gotten hastily at the beginning; But the end thereof shall not be blessed.
					\item[NIV] An inheritance claimed too soon will not be blessed at the end.
					\item[NRSV] An estate quickly acquired in the beginning will not be blessed in the end.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:22} % *PROVERBS-20-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-22}

			Say not thou, I will recompense evil; but wait on the LORD, and he shall save thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֹּאמַ֥ר אֲשַׁלְּמָה־רָ֑ע קַוֵּ֥ה לַֽ֝יהוָ֗ה וְיֹ֣שַֽׁע לָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Say not thou, I will recompense evil: Wait for Jehovah, and he will save thee.
					\item[NIV] Do not say, “I’ll pay you back for this wrong!” Wait for the LORD, and he will avenge you.
					\item[NRSV] Do not say, “I will repay evil”; wait for the LORD, and he will help you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:23} % *PROVERBS-20-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-23}

			Divers weights are an abomination unto the LORD; and a false balance is not good.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת יְ֭הוָה אֶ֣בֶן וָאָ֑בֶן וּמֹאזְנֵ֖י מִרְמָ֣ה לֹא־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Diverse weights are an abomination to Jehovah; And a false balance is not good.
					\item[NIV] The LORD detests differing weights, and dishonest scales do not please him.
					\item[NRSV] Differing weights are an abomination to the LORD, and false scales are not good.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:24} % *PROVERBS-20-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-24}

			Man’s goings are of the LORD; how can a man then understand his own way?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵיהוָ֥ה מִצְעֲדֵי־גָ֑בֶר וְ֝אָדָ֗ם מַה־יָּבִ֥ין דַּרְכּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man's goings are of Jehovah; How then can man understand his way?
					\item[NIV] A person’s steps are directed by the LORD. How then can anyone understand their own way?
					\item[NRSV] All our steps are ordered by the LORD; how then can we understand our own ways?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:25} % *PROVERBS-20-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-25}

			It is a snare to the man who devoureth that which is holy, and after vows to make enquiry.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מוֹקֵ֣שׁ אָ֭דָם יָ֣לַע קֹ֑דֶשׁ וְאַחַ֖ר נְדָרִ֣ים לְבַקֵּֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is a snare to a man rashly to say, It is holy, And after vows to make inquiry.
					\item[NIV] It is a trap to dedicate something rashly and only later to consider one’s vows.
					\item[NRSV] It is a snare for one to say rashly, “It is holy,” and begin to reflect only after making a vow.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:26} % *PROVERBS-20-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-26}

			A wise king scattereth the wicked, and bringeth the wheel over them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְזָרֶ֣ה רְ֭שָׁעִים מֶ֣לֶךְ חָכָ֑ם וַיָּ֖שֶׁב עֲלֵיהֶ֣ם אוֹפָֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise king winnoweth the wicked, And bringeth the threshing-wheel over them.
					\item[NIV] A wise king winnows out the wicked; he drives the threshing wheel over them.
					\item[NRSV] A wise king winnows the wicked, and drives the wheel over them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:27} % *PROVERBS-20-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-27}

			The spirit of man is the candle of the LORD, searching all the inward parts of the belly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֵ֣ר יְ֭הוָה נִשְׁמַ֣ת אָדָ֑ם חֹ֝פֵ֗שׂ כָּל־חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The spirit of man is the lamp of Jehovah, Searching all his innermost parts.
					\item[NIV] The human spirit is the lamp of the LORD that sheds light on one’s inmost being.
					\item[NRSV] The human spirit is the lamp of the LORD, searching every inmost part.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:28} % *PROVERBS-20-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-28}

			Mercy and truth preserve the king: and his throne is upholden by mercy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֶ֣סֶד וֶ֭אֱמֶת יִצְּרוּ־מֶ֑לֶךְ וְסָעַ֖ד בַּחֶ֣סֶד כִּסְאֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Kindness and truth preserve the king; And his throne is upholden by kindness.
					\item[NIV] Love and faithfulness keep a king safe; through love his throne is made secure.
					\item[NRSV] Loyalty and faithfulness preserve the king, and his throne is upheld by righteousness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:29} % *PROVERBS-20-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-29}

			The glory of young men is their strength: and the beauty of old men is the grey head.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תִּפְאֶ֣רֶת בַּחוּרִ֣ים כֹּחָ֑ם וַהֲדַ֖ר זְקֵנִ֣ים שֵׂיבָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The glory of young men is their strength; And the beauty of old men is the hoary head.
					\item[NIV] The glory of young men is their strength, gray hair the splendor of the old.
					\item[NRSV] The glory of youths is their strength, but the beauty of the aged is their gray hair.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 20:30} % *PROVERBS-20-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-20-30}

			The blueness of a wound cleanseth away evil: so do stripes the inward parts of the belly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חַבֻּר֣וֹת פֶּ֭צַע תַּמְר֣וּק בְּרָ֑ע וּ֝מַכּ֗וֹת חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Stripes that wound cleanse away evil; And strokes reach the innermost parts.
					\item[NIV] Blows and wounds scrub away evil, and beatings purge the inmost being.
					\item[NRSV] Blows that wound cleanse away evil; beatings make clean the innermost parts.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 21} % *PROVERBS-21
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-21}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:1} % *PROVERBS-21-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-1}

			The king’s heart is in the hand of the LORD, as the rivers of water: he turneth it whithersoever he will.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פַּלְגֵי־מַ֣יִם לֶב־מֶ֭לֶךְ בְּיַד־יְהוָ֑ה עַֽל־כָּל־אֲשֶׁ֖ר יַחְפֹּ֣ץ יַטֶּֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The king's heart is in the hand of Jehovah as the watercourses: He turneth it whithersoever he will.
					\item[NIV] In the LORD’s hand the king’s heart is a stream of water that he channels toward all who please him.
					\item[NRSV] The king’s heart is a stream of water in the hand of the LORD; he turns it wherever he will.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:2} % *PROVERBS-21-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-2}

			Every way of a man is right in his own eyes: but the LORD pondereth the hearts.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּֽל־דֶּרֶךְ־אִ֭ישׁ יָשָׁ֣ר בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְתֹכֵ֖ן לִבּ֣וֹת יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Every way of a man is right in his own eyes; But Jehovah weighteth the hearts.
					\item[NIV] A person may think their own ways are right, but the LORD weighs the heart.
					\item[NRSV] All deeds are right in the sight of the doer, but the LORD weighs the heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:3} % *PROVERBS-21-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-3}

			To do justice and judgment is more acceptable to the LORD than sacrifice.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲ֭שֹׂה צְדָקָ֣ה וּמִשְׁפָּ֑ט נִבְחָ֖ר לַיהוָ֣ה מִזָּֽבַח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To do righteousness and justice Is more acceptable to Jehovah than sacrifice.
					\item[NIV] To do what is right and just is more acceptable to the LORD than sacrifice.
					\item[NRSV] To do righteousness and justice is more acceptable to the LORD than sacrifice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:4} % *PROVERBS-21-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-4}

			An high look, and a proud heart, and the plowing of the wicked, is sin.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רוּם־עֵ֭ינַיִם וּרְחַב־לֵ֑ב נִ֖ר רְשָׁעִ֣ים חַטָּֽאת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A high look, and a proud heart, Even the lamp of the wicked, is sin.
					\item[NIV] Haughty eyes and a proud heart— the unplowed field of the wicked—produce sin.
					\item[NRSV] Haughty eyes and a proud heart— the lamp of the wicked—are sin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:5} % *PROVERBS-21-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-5}

			The thoughts of the diligent tend only to plenteousness; but of every one that is hasty only to want.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת חָ֭רוּץ אַךְ־לְמוֹתָ֑ר וְכָל־אָ֝֗ץ אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The thoughts of the diligent tend only to plenteousness; But every one that is hasty hasteth only to want.
					\item[NIV] The plans of the diligent lead to profit as surely as haste leads to poverty.
					\item[NRSV] The plans of the diligent lead surely to abundance, but everyone who is hasty comes only to want.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:6} % *PROVERBS-21-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-6}

			The getting of treasures by a lying tongue is a vanity tossed to and fro of them that seek death.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֹּ֣עַל א֭וֹצָרוֹת בִּלְשׁ֣וֹן שָׁ֑קֶר הֶ֥בֶל נִ֝דָּ֗ף מְבַקְשֵׁי־מָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The getting of treasures by a lying tongue Is a vapor driven to and fro by them that seek death.
					\item[NIV] A fortune made by a lying tongue is a fleeting vapor and a deadly snare.
					\item[NRSV] The getting of treasures by a lying tongue is a fleeting vapor and a snare of death.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:7} % *PROVERBS-21-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-7}

			The robbery of the wicked shall destroy them; because they refuse to do judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֹׁד־רְשָׁעִ֥ים יְגוֹרֵ֑ם כִּ֥י מֵ֝אֲנ֗וּ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת מִשְׁפָּֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The violence of the wicked shall sweep them away, Because they refuse to do justice.
					\item[NIV] The violence of the wicked will drag them away, for they refuse to do what is right.
					\item[NRSV] The violence of the wicked will sweep them away, because they refuse to do what is just.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:8} % *PROVERBS-21-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-8}

			The way of man is froward and strange: but as for the pure, his work is right.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲפַכְפַּ֬ךְ דֶּ֣רֶךְ אִ֣ישׁ וָזָ֑ר וְ֝זַ֗ךְ יָשָׁ֥ר פָּעֳלֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The way of him that is laden with guilt is exceeding crooked; But as for the pure, his work is right.
					\item[NIV] The way of the guilty is devious, but the conduct of the innocent is upright.
					\item[NRSV] The way of the guilty is crooked, but the conduct of the pure is right.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:9} % *PROVERBS-21-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-9}

			It is better to dwell in a corner of the housetop, than with a brawling woman in a wide house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֗וֹב לָשֶׁ֥בֶת עַל־פִּנַּת־גָּ֑ג מֵאֵ֥שֶׁת מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים וּבֵ֥ית חָֽבֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is better to dwell in the corner of the housetop, Than with a contentious woman in a wide house.
					\item[NIV] Better to live on a corner of the roof than share a house with a quarrelsome wife.
					\item[NRSV] It is better to live in a corner of the housetop than in a house shared with a contentious wife.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:10} % *PROVERBS-21-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-10}

			The soul of the wicked desireth evil: his neighbour findeth no favour in his eyes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶ֣פֶשׁ רָ֭שָׁע אִוְּתָה־רָ֑ע לֹא־יֻחַ֖ן בְּעֵינָ֣יו רֵעֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The soul of the wicked desireth evil: His neighbor findeth no favor in his eyes.
					\item[NIV] The wicked crave evil; their neighbors get no mercy from them.
					\item[NRSV] The souls of the wicked desire evil; their neighbors find no mercy in their eyes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:11} % *PROVERBS-21-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-11}

			When the scorner is punished, the simple is made wise: and when the wise is instructed, he receiveth knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּעְנָשׁ־לֵ֭ץ יֶחְכַּם־פֶּ֑תִי וּבְהַשְׂכִּ֥יל לְ֝חָכָ֗ם יִקַּח־דָּֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the scoffer is punished, the simple is made wise; And when the wise is instructed, he receiveth knowledge.
					\item[NIV] When a mocker is punished, the simple gain wisdom; by paying attention to the wise they get knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] When a scoffer is punished, the simple become wiser; when the wise are instructed, they increase in knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:12} % *PROVERBS-21-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-12}

			The righteous man wisely considereth the house of the wicked: but God overthroweth the wicked for their wickedness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַשְׂכִּ֣יל צַ֭דִּיק לְבֵ֣ית רָשָׁ֑ע מְסַלֵּ֖ף רְשָׁעִ֣ים לָרָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteous man considereth the house of the wicked, How the wicked are overthrown to their ruin.
					\item[NIV] The Righteous One takes note of the house of the wicked and brings the wicked to ruin.
					\item[NRSV] The Righteous One observes the house of the wicked; he casts the wicked down to ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:13} % *PROVERBS-21-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-13}

			Whoso stoppeth his ears at the cry of the poor, he also shall cry himself, but shall not be heard.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹטֵ֣ם אָ֭זְנוֹ מִזַּעֲקַת־דָּ֑ל גַּֽם־ה֥וּא יִ֝קְרָ֗א וְלֹ֣א יֵעָנֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso stoppeth his ears at the cry of the poor, He also shall cry, but shall not be heard.
					\item[NIV] Whoever shuts their ears to the cry of the poor will also cry out and not be answered.
					\item[NRSV] If you close your ear to the cry of the poor, you will cry out and not be heard.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:14} % *PROVERBS-21-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-14}

			A gift in secret pacifieth anger: and a reward in the bosom strong wrath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַתָּ֣ן בַּ֭סֵּתֶר יִכְפֶּה־אָ֑ף וְשֹׁ֥חַד בַּ֝חֵ֗ק חֵמָ֥ה עַזָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A gift in secret pacifieth anger; And a present in the bosom, strong wrath.
					\item[NIV] A gift given in secret soothes anger, and a bribe concealed in the cloak pacifies great wrath.
					\item[NRSV] A gift in secret averts anger; and a concealed bribe in the bosom, strong wrath.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:15} % *PROVERBS-21-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-15}

			It is joy to the just to do judgment: but destruction shall be to the workers of iniquity.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שִׂמְחָ֣ה לַ֭צַּדִּיק עֲשׂ֣וֹת מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּ֝מְחִתָּ֗ה לְפֹ֣עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is joy to the righteous to do justice; But it is a destruction to the workers of iniquity.
					\item[NIV] When justice is done, it brings joy to the righteous but terror to evildoers.
					\item[NRSV] When justice is done, it is a joy to the righteous, but dismay to evildoers.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:16} % *PROVERBS-21-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-16}

			The man that wandereth out of the way of understanding shall remain in the congregation of the dead.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָדָ֗ם תּ֭וֹעֶה מִדֶּ֣רֶךְ הַשְׂכֵּ֑ל בִּקְהַ֖ל רְפָאִ֣ים יָנֽוּחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The man that wandereth out of the way of understanding Shall rest in the assembly of the dead.
					\item[NIV] Whoever strays from the path of prudence comes to rest in the company of the dead.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever wanders from the way of understanding will rest in the assembly of the dead.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:17} % *PROVERBS-21-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-17}

			He that loveth pleasure shall be a poor man: he that loveth wine and oil shall not be rich.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ מַ֭חְסוֹר אֹהֵ֣ב שִׂמְחָ֑ה אֹהֵ֥ב יַֽיִן־וָ֝שֶׁ֗מֶן לֹ֣א יַעֲשִֽׁיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that loveth pleasure shall be a poor man: He that loveth wine and oil shall not be rich.
					\item[NIV] Whoever loves pleasure will become poor; whoever loves wine and olive oil will never be rich.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever loves pleasure will suffer want; whoever loves wine and oil will not be rich.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:18} % *PROVERBS-21-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-18}

			The wicked shall be a ransom for the righteous, and the transgressor for the upright.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֹּ֣פֶר לַצַּדִּ֣יק רָשָׁ֑ע וְתַ֖חַת יְשָׁרִ֣ים בּוֹגֵֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wicked is a ransom for the righteous; And the treacherous cometh in the stead of the upright.
					\item[NIV] The wicked become a ransom for the righteous, and the unfaithful for the upright.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked is a ransom for the righteous, and the faithless for the upright.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:19} % *PROVERBS-21-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-19}

			It is better to dwell in the wilderness, than with a contentious and an angry woman.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֗וֹב שֶׁ֥בֶת בְּאֶֽרֶץ־מִדְבָּ֑ר מֵאֵ֖שֶׁת מִדְיָנִ֣ים וָכָֽעַס׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is better to dwell in a desert land, Than with a contentious and fretful woman.
					\item[NIV] Better to live in a desert than with a quarrelsome and nagging wife.
					\item[NRSV] It is better to live in a desert land than with a contentious and fretful wife.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:20} % *PROVERBS-21-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-20}

			There is treasure to be desired and oil in the dwelling of the wise; but a foolish man spendeth it up.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אוֹצָ֤ר ׀ נֶחְמָ֣ד וָ֭שֶׁמֶן בִּנְוֵ֣ה חָכָ֑ם וּכְסִ֖יל אָדָ֣ם יְבַלְּעֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is precious treasure and oil in the dwelling of the wise; But a foolish man swalloweth it up.
					\item[NIV] The wise store up choice food and olive oil, but fools gulp theirs down.
					\item[NRSV] Precious treasure remains in the house of the wise, but the fool devours it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:21} % *PROVERBS-21-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-21}

			He that followeth after righteousness and mercy findeth life, righteousness, and honour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רֹ֭דֵף צְדָקָ֣ה וָחָ֑סֶד יִמְצָ֥א חַ֝יִּ֗ים צְדָקָ֥ה וְכָבֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that followeth after righteousness and kindness Findeth life, righteousness, and honor.
					\item[NIV] Whoever pursues righteousness and love finds life, prosperity and honor.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever pursues righteousness and kindness will find life and honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:22} % *PROVERBS-21-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-22}

			A wise man scaleth the city of the mighty, and casteth down the strength of the confidence thereof.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִ֣יר גִּ֭בֹּרִים עָלָ֣ה חָכָ֑ם וַ֝יֹּ֗רֶד עֹ֣ז מִבְטֶחָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise man scaleth the city of the mighty, And bringeth down the strength of the confidence thereof.
					\item[NIV] One who is wise can go up against the city of the mighty and pull down the stronghold in which they trust.
					\item[NRSV] One wise person went up against a city of warriors and brought down the stronghold in which they trusted.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:23} % *PROVERBS-21-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-23}

			Whoso keepeth his mouth and his tongue keepeth his soul from troubles.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֹׁמֵ֣ר פִּ֭יו וּלְשׁוֹנ֑וֹ שֹׁמֵ֖ר מִצָּר֣וֹת נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso keepeth his mouth and his tongue Keepeth his soul from troubles.
					\item[NIV] Those who guard their mouths and their tongues keep themselves from calamity.
					\item[NRSV] To watch over mouth and tongue is to keep out of trouble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:24} % *PROVERBS-21-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-24}

			Proud and haughty scorner is his name, who dealeth in proud wrath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זֵ֣ד יָ֭הִיר לֵ֣ץ שְׁמ֑וֹ ע֝וֹשֶׂ֗ה בְּעֶבְרַ֥ת זָדֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The proud and haughty man, scoffer is his name; He worketh in the arrogance of pride.
					\item[NIV] The proud and arrogant person—"Mocker” is his name— behaves with insolent fury.
					\item[NRSV] The proud, haughty person, named “Scoffer,” acts with arrogant pride.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:25} % *PROVERBS-21-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-25}

			The desire of the slothful killeth him; for his hands refuse to labour.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּאֲוַ֣ת עָצֵ֣ל תְּמִיתֶ֑נּוּ כִּֽי־מֵאֲנ֖וּ יָדָ֣יו לַעֲשֽׂוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The desire of the sluggard killeth him; For his hands refuse to labor.
					\item[NIV] The craving of a sluggard will be the death of him, because his hands refuse to work.
					\item[NRSV] The craving of the lazy person is fatal, for lazy hands refuse to labor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:26} % *PROVERBS-21-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-26}

			He coveteth greedily all the day long: but the righteous giveth and spareth not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־הַ֭יּוֹם הִתְאַוָּ֣ה תַאֲוָ֑ה וְצַדִּ֥יק יִ֝תֵּ֗ן וְלֹ֣א יַחְשֹֽׂךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is that coveteth greedily all the day long; But the righteous giveth and withholdeth not.
					\item[NIV] All day long he craves for more, but the righteous give without sparing.
					\item[NRSV] All day long the wicked covet, but the righteous give and do not hold back.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:27} % *PROVERBS-21-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-27}

			The sacrifice of the wicked is abomination: how much more, when he bringeth it with a wicked mind?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זֶ֣בַח רְ֭שָׁעִים תּוֹעֵבָ֑ה אַ֝֗ף כִּֽי־בְזִמָּ֥ה יְבִיאֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination: How much more, when he bringeth it with a wicked mind!
					\item[NIV] The sacrifice of the wicked is detestable— how much more so when brought with evil intent!
					\item[NRSV] The sacrifice of the wicked is an abomination; how much more when brought with evil intent.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:28} % *PROVERBS-21-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-28}

			A false witness shall perish: but the man that heareth speaketh constantly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵד־כְּזָבִ֥ים יֹאבֵ֑ד וְאִ֥ישׁ שׁ֝וֹמֵ֗עַ לָנֶ֥צַח יְדַבֵּֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A false witness shall perish; But the man that heareth shall speak so as to endure.
					\item[NIV] A false witness will perish, but a careful listener will testify successfully.
					\item[NRSV] A false witness will perish, but a good listener will testify successfully.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:29} % *PROVERBS-21-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-29}

			A wicked man hardeneth his face: but as for the upright, he directeth his way.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֵעֵ֬ז אִ֣ישׁ רָשָׁ֣ע בְּפָנָ֑יו וְ֝יָשָׁ֗ר ה֤וּא ׀ יָבִ֬ין דַּרְכּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wicked man hardeneth his face; But as for the upright, he establisheth his ways.
					\item[NIV] The wicked put up a bold front, but the upright give thought to their ways.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked put on a bold face, but the upright give thought to their ways.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:30} % *PROVERBS-21-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-30}

			There is no wisdom nor understanding nor counsel against the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵ֣ין חָ֭כְמָה וְאֵ֣ין תְּבוּנָ֑ה וְאֵ֥ין עֵ֝צָ֗ה לְנֶ֣גֶד יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is no wisdom nor understanding Nor counsel against Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] There is no wisdom, no insight, no plan that can succeed against the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] No wisdom, no understanding, no counsel, can avail against the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 21:31} % *PROVERBS-21-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-21-31}

			The horse is prepared against the day of battle: but safety is of the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ס֗וּס מ֭וּכָן לְי֣וֹם מִלְחָמָ֑ה וְ֝לַֽיהוָ֗ה הַתְּשׁוּעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The horse is prepared against the day of battle; But victory is of Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] The horse is made ready for the day of battle, but victory rests with the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] The horse is made ready for the day of battle, but the victory belongs to the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 22} % *PROVERBS-22
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-22}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:1} % *PROVERBS-22-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-1}

			A GOOD name is rather to be chosen than great riches, and loving favour rather than silver and gold.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נִבְחָ֣ר שֵׁ֭ם מֵעֹ֣שֶׁר רָ֑ב מִכֶּ֥סֶף וּ֝מִזָּהָ֗ב חֵ֣ן טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A good name is rather to be chosen than great riches, And loving favor rather than silver and gold.
					\item[NIV] A good name is more desirable than great riches; to be esteemed is better than silver or gold.
					\item[NRSV] A good name is to be chosen rather than great riches, and favor is better than silver or gold.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:2} % *PROVERBS-22-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-2}

			The rich and poor meet together: the LORD is the maker of them all.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָשִׁ֣יר וָרָ֣שׁ נִפְגָּ֑שׁוּ עֹשֵׂ֖ה כֻלָּ֣ם יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The rich and the poor meet together: Jehovah is the maker of them all.
					\item[NIV] Rich and poor have this in common: The LORD is the Maker of them all.
					\item[NRSV] The rich and the poor have this in common: the LORD is the maker of them all.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:3} % *PROVERBS-22-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-3}

			A prudent man foreseeth the evil, and hideth himself: but the simple pass on, and are punished.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָר֤וּם ׀ רָאָ֣ה רָעָ֣ה וְנִסְתָּ֑ר וּ֝פְתָיִ֗ים עָבְר֥וּ וְֽנֶעֱנָֽשׁוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A prudent man seeth the evil, and hideth himself; But the simple pass on, and suffer for it.
					\item[NIV] The prudent see danger and take refuge, but the simple keep going and pay the penalty.
					\item[NRSV] The clever see danger and hide; but the simple go on, and suffer for it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:4} % *PROVERBS-22-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-4}

			By humility and the fear of the LORD are riches, and honour, and life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֣קֶב עֲ֭נָוָה יִרְאַ֣ת יְהוָ֑ה עֹ֖שֶׁר וְכָב֣וֹד וְחַיִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The reward of humility and the fear of Jehovah Is riches, and honor, and life.
					\item[NIV] Humility is the fear of the LORD; its wages are riches and honor and life.
					\item[NRSV] The reward for humility and fear of the LORD is riches and honor and life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:5} % *PROVERBS-22-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-5}

			Thorns and snares are in the way of the froward: he that doth keep his soul shall be far from them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צִנִּ֣ים פַּ֭חִים בְּדֶ֣רֶךְ עִקֵּ֑שׁ שׁוֹמֵ֥ר נַ֝פְשׁ֗וֹ יִרְחַ֥ק מֵהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thorns and snares are in the way of the perverse: He that keepeth his soul shall be far from them.
					\item[NIV] In the paths of the wicked are snares and pitfalls, but those who would preserve their life stay far from them.
					\item[NRSV] Thorns and snares are in the way of the perverse; the cautious will keep far from them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:6} % *PROVERBS-22-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-6}

			Train up a child in the way he should go: and when he is old, he will not depart from it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֲנֹ֣ךְ לַ֭נַּעַר עַל־פִּ֣י דַרְכּ֑וֹ גַּ֥ם כִּֽי־יַ֝זְקִ֗ין לֹֽא־יָס֥וּר מִמֶּֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Train up a child in the way he should go, And even when he is old he will not depart from it.
					\item[NIV] Start children off on the way they should go, and even when they are old they will not turn from it.
					\item[NRSV] Train children in the right way, and when old, they will not stray.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:7} % *PROVERBS-22-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-7}

			The rich ruleth over the poor, and the borrower is servant to the lender.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָ֭שִׁיר בְּרָשִׁ֣ים יִמְשׁ֑וֹל וְעֶ֥בֶד לֹ֝וֶ֗ה לְאִ֣ישׁ מַלְוֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The rich ruleth over the poor; And the borrower is servant to the lender.
					\item[NIV] The rich rule over the poor, and the borrower is slave to the lender.
					\item[NRSV] The rich rule over the poor, and the borrower is the slave of the lender.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:8} % *PROVERBS-22-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-8}

			He that soweth iniquity shall reap vanity: and the rod of his anger shall fail.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זוֹרֵ֣עַ עַ֭וְלָה יקצור־[יִקְצָר־] אָ֑וֶן וְשֵׁ֖בֶט עֶבְרָת֣וֹ יִכְלֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that soweth iniquity shall reap calamity; And the rod of his wrath shall fail.
					\item[NIV] Whoever sows injustice reaps calamity, and the rod they wield in fury will be broken.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever sows injustice will reap calamity, and the rod of anger will fail.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:9} % *PROVERBS-22-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-9}

			He that hath a bountiful eye shall be blessed; for he giveth of his bread to the poor.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טֽוֹב־עַ֭יִן ה֣וּא יְבֹרָ֑ךְ כִּֽי־נָתַ֖ן מִלַּחְמ֣וֹ לַדָּֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that hath a bountiful eye shall be blessed; For he giveth of his bread to the poor.
					\item[NIV] The generous will themselves be blessed, for they share their food with the poor.
					\item[NRSV] Those who are generous are blessed, for they share their bread with the poor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:10} % *PROVERBS-22-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-10}

			Cast out the scorner, and contention shall go out; yea, strife and reproach shall cease.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גָּ֣רֵֽשׁ לֵ֭ץ וְיֵצֵ֣א מָד֑וֹן וְ֝יִשְׁבֹּ֗ת דִּ֣ין וְקָלֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Cast out the scoffer, and contention will go out; Yea, strife and ignominy will cease.
					\item[NIV] Drive out the mocker, and out goes strife; quarrels and insults are ended.
					\item[NRSV] Drive out a scoffer, and strife goes out; quarreling and abuse will cease.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:11} % *PROVERBS-22-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-11}

			He that loveth pureness of heart, for the grace of his lips the king shall be his friend.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹהֵ֥ב טהור־[טְהָר־] לֵ֑ב חֵ֥ן שְׂ֝פָתָ֗יו רֵעֵ֥הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that loveth pureness of heart, For the grace of his lips the king will be his friend.
					\item[NIV] One who loves a pure heart and who speaks with grace will have the king for a friend.
					\item[NRSV] Those who love a pure heart and are gracious in speech will have the king as a friend.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:12} % *PROVERBS-22-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-12}

			The eyes of the LORD preserve knowledge, and he overthroweth the words of the transgressor.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵינֵ֣י יְ֭הוָה נָ֣צְרוּ דָ֑עַת וַ֝יְסַלֵּ֗ף דִּבְרֵ֥י בֹגֵֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The eyes of Jehovah preserve him that hath knowledge; But he overthroweth the words of the treacherous man.
					\item[NIV] The eyes of the LORD keep watch over knowledge, but he frustrates the words of the unfaithful.
					\item[NRSV] The eyes of the LORD keep watch over knowledge, but he overthrows the words of the faithless.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:13} % *PROVERBS-22-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-13}

			The slothful man saith, There is a lion without, I shall be slain in the streets.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָמַ֣ר עָ֭צֵל אֲרִ֣י בַח֑וּץ בְּת֥וֹךְ רְ֝חֹב֗וֹת אֵֽרָצֵֽחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sluggard saith, There is a lion without; I shall be slain in the streets.
					\item[NIV] The sluggard says, “There’s a lion outside! I’ll be killed in the public square!”
					\item[NRSV] The lazy person says, “There is a lion outside! I shall be killed in the streets!”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:14} % *PROVERBS-22-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-14}

			The mouth of strange women is a deep pit: he that is abhorred of the LORD shall fall therein.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שׁוּחָ֣ה עֲ֭מֻקָּה פִּ֣י זָר֑וֹת זְע֥וּם יְ֝הוָ֗ה יפול־[יִפָּל־] שָֽׁם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The mouth of strange women is a deep pit; He that is abhorred of Jehovah shall fall therein.
					\item[NIV] The mouth of an adulterous woman is a deep pit; a man who is under the LORD’s wrath falls into it.
					\item[NRSV] The mouth of a loose woman is a deep pit; he with whom the LORD is angry falls into it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:15} % *PROVERBS-22-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-15}

			Foolishness is bound in the heart of a child; but the rod of correction shall drive it far from him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֭וֶּלֶת קְשׁוּרָ֣ה בְלֶב־נָ֑עַר שֵׁ֥בֶט מ֝וּסָ֗ר יַרְחִיקֶ֥נָּה מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Foolishness is bound up in the heart of a child; But the rod of correction shall drive it far from him.
					\item[NIV] Folly is bound up in the heart of a child, but the rod of discipline will drive it far away.
					\item[NRSV] Folly is bound up in the heart of a boy, but the rod of discipline drives it far away.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:16} % *PROVERBS-22-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-16}

			He that oppresseth the poor to increase his riches, and he that giveth to the rich, shall surely come to want.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹ֣שֵֽׁק דָּ֭ל לְהַרְבּ֣וֹת ל֑וֹ נֹתֵ֥ן לְ֝עָשִׁ֗יר אַךְ־לְמַחְסֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that oppresseth the poor to increase his gain, And he that giveth to the rich, shall come only to want.
					\item[NIV] One who oppresses the poor to increase his wealth and one who gives gifts to the rich—both come to poverty.
					\item[NRSV] Oppressing the poor in order to enrich oneself, and giving to the rich, will lead only to loss.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:17} % *PROVERBS-22-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-17}

			Bow down thine ear, and hear the words of the wise, and apply thine heart unto my knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֥ט אָזְנְךָ֗ וּ֭שְׁמַע דִּבְרֵ֣י חֲכָמִ֑ים וְ֝לִבְּךָ֗ תָּשִׁ֥ית לְדַעְתִּֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Incline thine ear, and hear the words of the wise, And apply thy heart unto my knowledge.
					\item[NIV] Pay attention and turn your ear to the sayings of the wise; apply your heart to what I teach,
					\item[NRSV] The words of the wise: Incline your ear and hear my words, and apply your mind to my teaching;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:18} % *PROVERBS-22-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-18}

			For it is a pleasant thing if thou keep them within thee; they shall withal be fitted in thy lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־נָ֭עִים כִּֽי־תִשְׁמְרֵ֣ם בְּבִטְנֶ֑ךָ יִכֹּ֥נוּ יַ֝חְדָּ֗ו עַל־שְׂפָתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For it is a pleasant thing if thou keep them within thee, If they be established together upon thy lips.
					\item[NIV] for it is pleasing when you keep them in your heart and have all of them ready on your lips.
					\item[NRSV] for it will be pleasant if you keep them within you, if all of them are ready on your lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:19} % *PROVERBS-22-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-19}

			That thy trust may be in the LORD, I have made known to thee this day, even to thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לִהְי֣וֹת בַּ֭יהוָה מִבְטַחֶ֑ךָ הוֹדַעְתִּ֖יךָ הַיּ֣וֹם אַף־אָֽתָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] That thy trust may be in Jehovah, I have made them known to thee this day, even to thee.
					\item[NIV] So that your trust may be in the LORD, I teach you today, even you.
					\item[NRSV] So that your trust may be in the LORD, I have made them known to you today—yes, to you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:20} % *PROVERBS-22-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-20}

			Have not I written to thee excellent things in counsels and knowledge,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲלֹ֤א כָתַ֣בְתִּי לְ֭ךָ שָׁלִישִׁ֑ים בְּמ֖וֹעֵצֹ֣ת וָדָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Have not I written unto thee excellent things Of counsels and knowledge,
					\item[NIV] Have I not written thirty sayings for you, sayings of counsel and knowledge,
					\item[NRSV] Have I not written for you thirty sayings of admonition and knowledge,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:21} % *PROVERBS-22-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-21}

			That I might make thee know the certainty of the words of truth; that thou mightest answer the words of truth to them that send unto thee?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְהוֹדִֽיעֲךָ֗ קֹ֭שְׁטְ אִמְרֵ֣י אֱמֶ֑ת לְהָשִׁ֥יב אֲמָרִ֥ים אֱ֝מֶ֗ת לְשֹׁלְחֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To make thee know the certainty of the words of truth, That thou mayest carry back words of truth to them that send thee?
					\item[NIV] teaching you to be honest and to speak the truth, so that you bring back truthful reports to those you serve?
					\item[NRSV] to show you what is right and true, so that you may give a true answer to those who sent you?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:22} % *PROVERBS-22-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-22}

			Rob not the poor, because he is poor: neither oppress the afflicted in the gate:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַֽל־תִּגְזָל־דָּ֭ל כִּ֣י דַל־ה֑וּא וְאַל־תְּדַכֵּ֖א עָנִ֣י בַשָּֽׁעַר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Rob not the poor, because he is poor; Neither oppress the afflicted in the gate:
					\item[NIV] Do not exploit the poor because they are poor and do not crush the needy in court,
					\item[NRSV] Do not rob the poor because they are poor, or crush the afflicted at the gate;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:23} % *PROVERBS-22-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-23}

			For the LORD will plead their cause, and spoil the soul of those that spoiled them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־יְ֭הוָה יָרִ֣יב רִיבָ֑ם וְקָבַ֖ע אֶת־קֹבְעֵיהֶ֣ם נָֽפֶשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For Jehovah will plead their cause, And despoil of life those that despoil them.
					\item[NIV] for the LORD will take up their case and will exact life for life.
					\item[NRSV] for the LORD pleads their cause and despoils of life those who despoil them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:24} % *PROVERBS-22-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-24}

			Make no friendship with an angry man; and with a furious man thou shalt not go:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּ֭תְרַע אֶת־בַּ֣עַל אָ֑ף וְאֶת־אִ֥ישׁ חֵ֝מוֹת לֹ֣א תָבֽוֹא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Make no friendship with a man that is given to anger; And with a wrathful man thou shalt not go:
					\item[NIV] Do not make friends with a hot-tempered person, do not associate with one easily angered,
					\item[NRSV] Make no friends with those given to anger, and do not associate with hotheads,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:25} % *PROVERBS-22-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-25}

			Lest thou learn his ways, and get a snare to thy soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּן־תֶּאֱלַ֥ף אֹֽרְחֹתָ֑יו וְלָקַחְתָּ֖ מוֹקֵ֣שׁ לְנַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lest thou learn this ways, And get a snare to thy soul.
					\item[NIV] or you may learn their ways and get yourself ensnared.
					\item[NRSV] or you may learn their ways and entangle yourself in a snare.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:26} % *PROVERBS-22-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-26}

			Be not thou one of them that strike hands, or of them that are sureties for debts.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תְּהִ֥י בְתֹֽקְעֵי־כָ֑ף בַּ֝עֹרְבִ֗ים מַשָּׁאֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be thou not one of them that strike hands, Or of them that are sureties for debts.
					\item[NIV] Do not be one who shakes hands in pledge or puts up security for debts;
					\item[NRSV] Do not be one of those who give pledges, who become surety for debts.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:27} % *PROVERBS-22-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-27}

			If thou hast nothing to pay, why should he take away thy bed from under thee?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־אֵֽין־לְךָ֥ לְשַׁלֵּ֑ם לָ֥מָּה יִקַּ֥ח מִ֝שְׁכָּבְךָ֗ מִתַּחְתֶּֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If thou hast not wherewith to pay, Why should he take away thy bed from under thee?
					\item[NIV] if you lack the means to pay, your very bed will be snatched from under you.
					\item[NRSV] If you have nothing with which to pay, why should your bed be taken from under you?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:28} % *PROVERBS-22-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-28}

			Remove not the ancient landmark, which thy fathers have set.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּ֭סֵּג גְּב֣וּל עוֹלָ֑ם אֲשֶׁ֖ר עָשׂ֣וּ אֲבוֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Remove not the ancient landmark, Which thy fathers have set.
					\item[NIV] Do not move an ancient boundary stone set up by your ancestors.
					\item[NRSV] Do not remove the ancient landmark that your ancestors set up.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 22:29} % *PROVERBS-22-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-22-29}

			Seest thou a man diligent in his business? he shall stand before kings; he shall not stand before mean men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָזִ֡יתָ אִ֤ישׁ ׀ מָ֘הִ֤יר בִּמְלַאכְתּ֗וֹ לִֽפְנֵֽי־מְלָכִ֥ים יִתְיַצָּ֑ב בַּל־יִ֝תְיַצֵּב לִפְנֵ֥י חֲשֻׁכִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Seest thou a man diligent in his business? he shall stand before kings; He shall not stand before mean men.
					\item[NIV] Do you see someone skilled in their work? They will serve before kings; they will not serve before officials of low rank.
					\item[NRSV] Do you see those who are skillful in their work? They will serve kings; they will not serve common people.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 23} % *PROVERBS-23
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-23}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:1} % *PROVERBS-23-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-1}

			When thou sittest to eat with a ruler, consider diligently what is before thee:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־תֵ֭שֵׁב לִלְח֣וֹם אֶת־מוֹשֵׁ֑ל בִּ֥ין תָּ֝בִ֗ין אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֥ר לְפָנֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When thou sittest to eat with a ruler, Consider diligently him that is before thee;
					\item[NIV] When you sit to dine with a ruler, note well what is before you,
					\item[NRSV] When you sit down to eat with a ruler, observe carefully what is before you,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:2} % *PROVERBS-23-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-2}

			And put a knife to thy throat, if thou be a man given to appetite.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְשַׂמְתָּ֣ שַׂכִּ֣ין בְּלֹעֶ֑ךָ אִם־בַּ֖עַל נֶ֣פֶשׁ אָֽתָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And put a knife to thy throat, If thou be a man given to appetite.
					\item[NIV] and put a knife to your throat if you are given to gluttony.
					\item[NRSV] and put a knife to your throat if you have a big appetite.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:3} % *PROVERBS-23-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-3}

			Be not desirous of his dainties: for they are deceitful meat.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּ֭תְאָו לְמַטְעַמּוֹתָ֑יו וְ֝ה֗וּא לֶ֣חֶם כְּזָבִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not desirous of his dainties; Seeing they are deceitful food.
					\item[NIV] Do not crave his delicacies, for that food is deceptive.
					\item[NRSV] Do not desire the ruler’s delicacies, for they are deceptive food.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:4} % *PROVERBS-23-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-4}

			Labour not to be rich: cease from thine own wisdom.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּיגַ֥ע לְֽהַעֲשִׁ֑יר מִֽבִּינָתְךָ֥ חֲדָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Weary not thyself to be rich; Cease from thine own wisdom.
					\item[NIV] Do not wear yourself out to get rich; do not trust your own cleverness.
					\item[NRSV] Do not wear yourself out to get rich; be wise enough to desist.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:5} % *PROVERBS-23-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-5}

			Wilt thou set thine eyes upon that which is not? for riches certainly make themselves wings; they fly away as an eagle toward heaven.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֲתָ֤עִיף עֵינֶ֥יךָ בּ֗וֹ וְֽאֵ֫ינֶ֥נּוּ כִּ֤י עָשֹׂ֣ה יַעֲשֶׂה־לּ֣וֹ כְנָפַ֑יִם כְּ֝נֶ֗שֶׁר יָע֥וּף הַשָּׁמָֽיִם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wilt thou set thine eyes upon that which is not? For riches certainly make themselves wings, Like an eagle that flieth toward heaven.
					\item[NIV] Cast but a glance at riches, and they are gone, for they will surely sprout wings and fly off to the sky like an eagle.
					\item[NRSV] When your eyes light upon it, it is gone; for suddenly it takes wings to itself, flying like an eagle toward heaven.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:6} % *PROVERBS-23-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-6}

			Eat thou not the bread of him that hath an evil eye, neither desire thou his dainty meats:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּלְחַ֗ם אֶת־לֶ֭חֶם רַ֣ע עָ֑יִן תִּ֝תְאָ֗יו לְמַטְעַמֹּתָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Eat thou not the bread of him that hath an evil eye, Neither desire thou his dainties:
					\item[NIV] Do not eat the food of a begrudging host, do not crave his delicacies;
					\item[NRSV] Do not eat the bread of the stingy; do not desire their delicacies;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:7} % *PROVERBS-23-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-7}
