			For as he thinketh in his heart, so is he: Eat and drink, saith he to thee; but his heart is not with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י ׀ כְּמוֹ־שָׁעַ֥ר בְּנַפְשׁ֗וֹ כֶּ֫ן־ה֥וּא אֱכֹ֣ל וּ֭שְׁתֵה יֹ֣אמַר לָ֑ךְ וְ֝לִבּ֗וֹ בַּל־עִמָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For as he thinketh within himself, so is he: Eat and drink, saith he to thee; But his heart is not with thee.
					\item[NIV] for he is the kind of person who is always thinking about the cost. “Eat and drink,” he says to you, but his heart is not with you.
					\item[NRSV] for like a hair in the throat, so are they. “Eat and drink!” they say to you; but they do not mean it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:8} % *PROVERBS-23-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-8}

			The morsel which thou hast eaten shalt thou vomit up, and lose thy sweet words.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פִּֽתְּךָ־אָכַ֥לְתָּ תְקִיאֶ֑נָּה וְ֝שִׁחַ֗תָּ דְּבָרֶ֥יךָ הַנְּעִימִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The morsel which thou hast eaten shalt thou vomit up, And lose thy sweet words.
					\item[NIV] You will vomit up the little you have eaten and will have wasted your compliments.
					\item[NRSV] You will vomit up the little you have eaten, and you will waste your pleasant words.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:9} % *PROVERBS-23-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-9}

			Speak not in the ears of a fool: for he will despise the wisdom of thy words.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאָזְנֵ֣י כְ֭סִיל אַל־תְּדַבֵּ֑ר כִּֽי־יָ֝ב֗וּז לְשֵׂ֣כֶל מִלֶּֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Speak not in the hearing of a fool; For he will despise the wisdom of thy words.
					\item[NIV] Do not speak to fools, for they will scorn your prudent words.
					\item[NRSV] Do not speak in the hearing of a fool, who will only despise the wisdom of your words.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:10} % *PROVERBS-23-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-10}

			Remove not the old landmark; and enter not into the fields of the fatherless:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּ֭סֵּג גְּב֣וּל עוֹלָ֑ם וּבִשְׂדֵ֥י יְ֝תוֹמִ֗ים אַל־תָּבֹֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Remove not the ancient landmark; And enter not into the fields of the fatherless:
					\item[NIV] Do not move an ancient boundary stone or encroach on the fields of the fatherless,
					\item[NRSV] Do not remove an ancient landmark or encroach on the fields of orphans,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:11} % *PROVERBS-23-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-11}

			For their redeemer is mighty; he shall plead their cause with thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־גֹאֲלָ֥ם חָזָ֑ק הֽוּא־יָרִ֖יב אֶת־רִיבָ֣ם אִתָּֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For their Redeemer is strong; He will plead their cause against thee.
					\item[NIV] for their Defender is strong; he will take up their case against you.
					\item[NRSV] for their redeemer is strong; he will plead their cause against you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:12} % *PROVERBS-23-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-12}

			Apply thine heart unto instruction, and thine ears to the words of knowledge.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָבִ֣יאָה לַמּוּסָ֣ר לִבֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝אָזְנֶ֗ךָ לְאִמְרֵי־דָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Apply thy heart unto instruction, And thine ears to the words of knowledge.
					\item[NIV] Apply your heart to instruction and your ears to words of knowledge.
					\item[NRSV] Apply your mind to instruction and your ear to words of knowledge.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:13} % *PROVERBS-23-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-13}

			Withhold not correction from the child: for if thou beatest him with the rod, he shall not die.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּמְנַ֣ע מִנַּ֣עַר מוּסָ֑ר כִּֽי־תַכֶּ֥נּוּ בַ֝שֵּׁ֗בֶט לֹ֣א יָמֽוּת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Withhold not correction from the child; For if thou beat him with the rod, he will not die.
					\item[NIV] Do not withhold discipline from a child; if you punish them with the rod, they will not die.
					\item[NRSV] Do not withhold discipline from your children; if you beat them with a rod, they will not die.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:14} % *PROVERBS-23-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-14}

			Thou shalt beat him with the rod, and shalt deliver his soul from hell.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַ֭תָּה בַּשֵּׁ֣בֶט תַּכֶּ֑נּוּ וְ֝נַפְשׁ֗וֹ מִשְּׁא֥וֹל תַּצִּֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thou shalt beat him with the rod, And shalt deliver his soul from Sheol.
					\item[NIV] Punish them with the rod and save them from death.
					\item[NRSV] If you beat them with the rod, you will save their lives from Sheol.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:15} % *PROVERBS-23-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-15}

			My son, if thine heart be wise, my heart shall rejoice, even mine.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭נִי אִם־חָכַ֣ם לִבֶּ֑ךָ יִשְׂמַ֖ח לִבִּ֣י גַם־אָֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, if thy heart be wise, My heart will be glad, even mine:
					\item[NIV] My son, if your heart is wise, then my heart will be glad indeed;
					\item[NRSV] My child, if your heart is wise, my heart too will be glad.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:16} % *PROVERBS-23-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-16}

			Yea, my reins shall rejoice, when thy lips speak right things.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְתַעְלֹ֥זְנָה כִלְיוֹתָ֑י בְּדַבֵּ֥ר שְׂ֝פָתֶ֗יךָ מֵישָׁרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, my heart will rejoice, When thy lips speak right things.
					\item[NIV] my inmost being will rejoice when your lips speak what is right.
					\item[NRSV] My soul will rejoice when your lips speak what is right.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:17} % *PROVERBS-23-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-17}

			Let not thine heart envy sinners: but be thou in the fear of the LORD all the day long.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־יְקַנֵּ֣א לִ֭בְּךָ בַּֽחַטָּאִ֑ים כִּ֥י אִם־בְּיִרְאַת־יְ֝הוָ֗ה כָּל־הַיּֽוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let not thy heart envy sinners; But be thou in the fear of Jehovah all the day long:
					\item[NIV] Do not let your heart envy sinners, but always be zealous for the fear of the LORD.
					\item[NRSV] Do not let your heart envy sinners, but always continue in the fear of the LORD.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:18} % *PROVERBS-23-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-18}

			For surely there is an end; and thine expectation shall not be cut off.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֭י אִם־יֵ֣שׁ אַחֲרִ֑ית וְ֝תִקְוָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תִכָּרֵֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For surely there is a reward; And thy hope shall not be cut off.
					\item[NIV] There is surely a future hope for you, and your hope will not be cut off.
					\item[NRSV] Surely there is a future, and your hope will not be cut off.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:19} % *PROVERBS-23-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-19}

			Hear thou, my son, and be wise, and guide thine heart in the way.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁמַע־אַתָּ֣ה בְנִ֣י וַחֲכָ֑ם וְאַשֵּׁ֖ר בַּדֶּ֣רֶךְ לִבֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hear thou, my son, and be wise, And guide thy heart in the way.
					\item[NIV] Listen, my son, and be wise, and set your heart on the right path:
					\item[NRSV] Hear, my child, and be wise, and direct your mind in the way.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:20} % *PROVERBS-23-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-20}

			Be not among winebibbers; among riotous eaters of flesh:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תְּהִ֥י בְסֹֽבְאֵי־יָ֑יִן בְּזֹלֲלֵ֖י בָשָׂ֣ר לָֽמוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not among winebibbers, Among gluttonous eaters of flesh:
					\item[NIV] Do not join those who drink too much wine or gorge themselves on meat,
					\item[NRSV] Do not be among winebibbers, or among gluttonous eaters of meat;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:21} % *PROVERBS-23-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-21}

			For the drunkard and the glutton shall come to poverty: and drowsiness shall clothe a man with rags.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי־סֹבֵ֣א וְ֭זוֹלֵל יִוָּרֵ֑שׁ וּ֝קְרָעִ֗ים תַּלְבִּ֥ישׁ נוּמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the drunkard and the glutton shall come to poverty; And drowsiness will clothe a man with rags.
					\item[NIV] for drunkards and gluttons become poor, and drowsiness clothes them in rags.
					\item[NRSV] for the drunkard and the glutton will come to poverty, and drowsiness will clothe them with rags.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:22} % *PROVERBS-23-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-22}

			Hearken unto thy father that begat thee, and despise not thy mother when she is old.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁמַ֣ע לְ֭אָבִיךָ זֶ֣ה יְלָדֶ֑ךָ וְאַל־תָּ֝ב֗וּז כִּֽי־זָקְנָ֥ה אִמֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hearken unto thy father that begat thee, And despise not thy mother when she is old.
					\item[NIV] Listen to your father, who gave you life, and do not despise your mother when she is old.
					\item[NRSV] Listen to your father who begot you, and do not despise your mother when she is old.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:23} % *PROVERBS-23-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-23}

			Buy the truth, and sell it not; also wisdom, and instruction, and understanding.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱמֶ֣ת קְ֭נֵה וְאַל־תִּמְכֹּ֑ר חָכְמָ֖ה וּמוּסָ֣ר וּבִינָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Buy the truth, and sell it not; Yea, wisdom, and instruction, and understanding.
					\item[NIV] Buy the truth and do not sell it— wisdom, instruction and insight as well.
					\item[NRSV] Buy truth, and do not sell it; buy wisdom, instruction, and understanding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:24} % *PROVERBS-23-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-24}

			The father of the righteous shall greatly rejoice: and he that begetteth a wise child shall have joy of him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גִּ֣יל יָ֭גִיל אֲבִ֣י צַדִּ֑יק וְיוֹלֵ֥ד חָ֝כָ֗ם וישמח־[יִשְׂמַח־] בּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The father of the righteous will greatly rejoice; And he that begetteth a wise child will have joy of him.
					\item[NIV] The father of a righteous child has great joy; a man who fathers a wise son rejoices in him.
					\item[NRSV] The father of the righteous will greatly rejoice; he who begets a wise son will be glad in him.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:25} % *PROVERBS-23-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-25}

			Thy father and thy mother shall be glad, and she that bare thee shall rejoice.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִֽשְׂמַח־אָבִ֥יךָ וְאִמֶּ֑ךָ וְ֝תָגֵ֗ל יֽוֹלַדְתֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thy father and thy mother be glad, And let her that bare thee rejoice.
					\item[NIV] May your father and mother rejoice; may she who gave you birth be joyful!
					\item[NRSV] Let your father and mother be glad; let her who bore you rejoice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:26} % *PROVERBS-23-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-26}

			My son, give me thine heart, and let thine eyes observe my ways.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תְּנָֽה־בְנִ֣י לִבְּךָ֣ לִ֑י וְ֝עֵינֶ֗יךָ דְּרָכַ֥י תִּצֹּֽרְנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, give me thy heart; And let thine eyes delight in my ways.
					\item[NIV] My son, give me your heart and let your eyes delight in my ways,
					\item[NRSV] My child, give me your heart, and let your eyes observe my ways.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:27} % *PROVERBS-23-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-27}

			For a whore is a deep ditch; and a strange woman is a narrow pit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־שׁוּחָ֣ה עֲמֻקָּ֣ה זוֹנָ֑ה וּבְאֵ֥ר צָ֝רָ֗ה נָכְרִיָּֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For a harlot is a deep ditch; And a foreign woman is a narrow pit.
					\item[NIV] for an adulterous woman is a deep pit, and a wayward wife is a narrow well.
					\item[NRSV] For a prostitute is a deep pit; an adulteress is a narrow well.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:28} % *PROVERBS-23-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-28}

			She also lieth in wait as for a prey, and increaseth the transgressors among men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַף־הִ֭יא כְּחֶ֣תֶף תֶּֽאֱרֹ֑ב וּ֝בוֹגְדִ֗ים בְּאָדָ֥ם תּוֹסִֽף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, she lieth in wait as a robber, And increaseth the treacherous among men.
					\item[NIV] Like a bandit she lies in wait and multiplies the unfaithful among men.
					\item[NRSV] She lies in wait like a robber and increases the number of the faithless.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:29} % *PROVERBS-23-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-29}

			Who hath woe? who hath sorrow? who hath contentions? who hath babbling? who hath wounds without cause? who hath redness of eyes?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְמִ֨י א֥וֹי לְמִ֪י אֲב֡וֹי לְמִ֤י מִדְיָנִ֨ים ׀ לְמִ֥י שִׂ֗יחַ לְ֭מִי פְּצָעִ֣ים חִנָּ֑ם לְ֝מִ֗י חַכְלִל֥וּת עֵינָֽיִם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who hath woe? who hath sorrow? who hath contentions? Who hath complaining? who hath wounds without cause? Who hath redness of eyes?
					\item[NIV] Who has woe? Who has sorrow? Who has strife? Who has complaints? Who has needless bruises? Who has bloodshot eyes?
					\item[NRSV] Who has woe? Who has sorrow? Who has strife? Who has complaining? Who has wounds without cause? Who has redness of eyes?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:30} % *PROVERBS-23-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-30}

			They that tarry long at the wine; they that go to seek mixed wine.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לַֽמְאַחֲרִ֥ים עַל־הַיָּ֑יִן לַ֝בָּאִ֗ים לַחְקֹ֥ר מִמְסָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They that tarry long at the wine; They that go to seek out mixed wine.
					\item[NIV] Those who linger over wine, who go to sample bowls of mixed wine.
					\item[NRSV] Those who linger late over wine, those who keep trying mixed wines.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:31} % *PROVERBS-23-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-31}

			Look not thou upon the wine when it is red, when it giveth his colour in the cup, when it moveth itself aright.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֵּ֥רֶא יַיִן֮ כִּ֪י יִתְאַ֫דָּ֥ם כִּֽי־יִתֵּ֣ן בַּכּ֣וֹס עֵינ֑וֹ יִ֝תְהַלֵּ֗ךְ בְּמֵישָׁרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Look not thou upon the wine when it is red, When it sparkleth in the cup, When it goeth down smoothly:
					\item[NIV] Do not gaze at wine when it is red, when it sparkles in the cup, when it goes down smoothly!
					\item[NRSV] Do not look at wine when it is red, when it sparkles in the cup and goes down smoothly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:32} % *PROVERBS-23-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-32}

			At the last it biteth like a serpent, and stingeth like an adder.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַ֭חֲרִיתוֹ כְּנָחָ֣שׁ יִשָּׁ֑ךְ וּֽכְצִפְעֹנִ֥י יַפְרִֽשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] At the last it biteth like a serpent, And stingeth like an adder.
					\item[NIV] In the end it bites like a snake and poisons like a viper.
					\item[NRSV] At the last it bites like a serpent, and stings like an adder.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:33} % *PROVERBS-23-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-33}

			Thine eyes shall behold strange women, and thine heart shall utter perverse things.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֵ֭ינֶיךָ יִרְא֣וּ זָר֑וֹת וְ֝לִבְּךָ֗ יְדַבֵּ֥ר תַּהְפֻּכֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thine eyes shall behold strange things, And thy heart shall utter perverse things.
					\item[NIV] Your eyes will see strange sights, and your mind will imagine confusing things.
					\item[NRSV] Your eyes will see strange things, and your mind utter perverse things.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:34} % *PROVERBS-23-34
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-34}

			Yea, thou shalt be as he that lieth down in the midst of the sea, or as he that lieth upon the top of a mast.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְ֭הָיִיתָ כְּשֹׁכֵ֣ב בְּלֶב־יָ֑ם וּ֝כְשֹׁכֵ֗ב בְּרֹ֣אשׁ חִבֵּֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yea, thou shalt be as he that lieth down in the midst of the sea, Or as he that lieth upon the top of a mast.
					\item[NIV] You will be like one sleeping on the high seas, lying on top of the rigging.
					\item[NRSV] You will be like one who lies down in the midst of the sea, like one who lies on the top of a mast.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 23:35} % *PROVERBS-23-35
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-23-35}

			They have stricken me, shalt thou say, and I was not sick; they have beaten me, and I felt it not: when shall I awake? I will seek it yet again.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הִכּ֥וּנִי בַל־חָלִיתִי֮ הֲלָמ֗וּנִי בַּל־יָ֫דָ֥עְתִּי מָתַ֥י אָקִ֑יץ א֝וֹסִ֗יף אֲבַקְשֶׁ֥נּוּ עֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They have stricken me, shalt thou say, and I was not hurt; They have beaten me, and I felt it not: When shall I awake? I will seek it yet again.
					\item[NIV] “They hit me,” you will say, “but I’m not hurt! They beat me, but I don’t feel it! When will I wake up so I can find another drink?”
					\item[NRSV] “They struck me,” you will say, “but I was not hurt; they beat me, but I did not feel it. When shall I awake? I will seek another drink.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 24} % *PROVERBS-24
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-24}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:1} % *PROVERBS-24-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-1}

			Be not thou envious against evil men, neither desire to be with them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תְּ֭קַנֵּא בְּאַנְשֵׁ֣י רָעָ֑ה תִּ֝תְאָ֗יו לִהְי֥וֹת אִתָּֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not thou envious against evil men; Neither desire to be with them:
					\item[NIV] Do not envy the wicked, do not desire their company;
					\item[NRSV] Do not envy the wicked, nor desire to be with them;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:2} % *PROVERBS-24-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-2}

			For their heart studieth destruction, and their lips talk of mischief.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּי־שֹׁ֭ד יֶהְגֶּ֣ה לִבָּ֑ם וְ֝עָמָ֗ל שִׂפְתֵיהֶ֥ם תְּדַבֵּֽרְנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For their heart studieth oppression, And their lips talk of mischief.
					\item[NIV] for their hearts plot violence, and their lips talk about making trouble.
					\item[NRSV] for their minds devise violence, and their lips talk of mischief.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:3} % *PROVERBS-24-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-3}

			Through wisdom is an house builded; and by understanding it is established:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּ֭חָכְמָה יִבָּ֣נֶה בָּ֑יִת וּ֝בִתְבוּנָ֗ה יִתְכּוֹנָֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Through wisdom is a house builded; And by understanding it is established;
					\item[NIV] By wisdom a house is built, and through understanding it is established;
					\item[NRSV] By wisdom a house is built, and by understanding it is established;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:4} % *PROVERBS-24-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-4}

			And by knowledge shall the chambers be filled with all precious and pleasant riches.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּ֭בְדַעַת חֲדָרִ֣ים יִמָּלְא֑וּ כָּל־ה֖וֹן יָקָ֣ר וְנָעִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And by knowledge are the chambers filled With all precious and pleasant riches.
					\item[NIV] through knowledge its rooms are filled with rare and beautiful treasures.
					\item[NRSV] by knowledge the rooms are filled with all precious and pleasant riches.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:5} % *PROVERBS-24-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-5}

			A wise man is strong; yea, a man of knowledge increaseth strength.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גֶּֽבֶר־חָכָ֥ם בַּע֑וֹז וְאִֽישׁ־דַּ֝֗עַת מְאַמֶּץ־כֹּֽחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A wise man is strong; Yea, a man of knowledge increaseth might.
					\item[NIV] The wise prevail through great power, and those who have knowledge muster their strength.
					\item[NRSV] Wise warriors are mightier than strong ones, and those who have knowledge than those who have strength;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:6} % *PROVERBS-24-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-6}

			For by wise counsel thou shalt make thy war: and in multitude of counsellors there is safety.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֣י בְ֭תַחְבֻּלוֹת תַּעֲשֶׂה־לְּךָ֣ מִלְחָמָ֑ה וּ֝תְשׁוּעָ֗ה בְּרֹ֣ב יוֹעֵֽץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For by wise guidance thou shalt make thy war; And in the multitude of counsellors there is safety.
					\item[NIV] Surely you need guidance to wage war, and victory is won through many advisers.
					\item[NRSV] for by wise guidance you can wage your war, and in abundance of counselors there is victory.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:7} % *PROVERBS-24-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-7}

			Wisdom is too high for a fool: he openeth not his mouth in the gate.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָאמ֣וֹת לֶֽאֱוִ֣יל חָכְמ֑וֹת בַּ֝שַּׁ֗עַר לֹ֣א יִפְתַּח־פִּֽיהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wisdom is too high for a fool: He openeth not his mouth in the gate.
					\item[NIV] Wisdom is too high for fools; in the assembly at the gate they must not open their mouths.
					\item[NRSV] Wisdom is too high for fools; in the gate they do not open their mouths.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:8} % *PROVERBS-24-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-8}

			He that deviseth to do evil shall be called a mischievous person.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְחַשֵּׁ֥ב לְהָרֵ֑עַ ל֝֗וֹ בַּֽעַל־מְזִמּ֥וֹת יִקְרָֽאוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that deviseth to do evil, Men shall call him a mischief-maker.
					\item[NIV] Whoever plots evil will be known as a schemer.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever plans to do evil will be called a mischief-maker.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:9} % *PROVERBS-24-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-9}

			The thought of foolishness is sin: and the scorner is an abomination to men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זִמַּ֣ת אִוֶּ֣לֶת חַטָּ֑את וְתוֹעֲבַ֖ת לְאָדָ֣ם לֵֽץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The thought of foolishness is sin; And the scoffer is an abomination to men.
					\item[NIV] The schemes of folly are sin, and people detest a mocker.
					\item[NRSV] The devising of folly is sin, and the scoffer is an abomination to all.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:10} % *PROVERBS-24-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-10}

			If thou faint in the day of adversity, thy strength is small.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הִ֭תְרַפִּיתָ בְּי֥וֹם צָרָ֗ה צַ֣ר כֹּחֶֽכָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If thou faint in the day of adversity, Thy strength is small.
					\item[NIV] If you falter in a time of trouble, how small is your strength!
					\item[NRSV] If you faint in the day of adversity, your strength being small;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:11} % *PROVERBS-24-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-11}

			If thou forbear to deliver them that are drawn unto death, and those that are ready to be slain;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֭צֵּל לְקֻחִ֣ים לַמָּ֑וֶת וּמָטִ֥ים לַ֝הֶ֗רֶג אִם־תַּחְשֽׂוֹךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Deliver them that are carried away unto death, And those that are ready to be slain see that thou hold back.
					\item[NIV] Rescue those being led away to death; hold back those staggering toward slaughter.
					\item[NRSV] if you hold back from rescuing those taken away to death, those who go staggering to the slaughter;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:12} % *PROVERBS-24-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-12}

			If thou sayest, Behold, we knew it not; doth not he that pondereth the heart consider it? and he that keepeth thy soul, doth not he know it? and shall not he render to every man according to his works?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־תֹאמַ֗ר הֵן֮ לֹא־יָדַ֪עְנ֫וּ זֶ֥ה הֲ‍ֽלֹא־תֹ֘כֵ֤ן לִבּ֨וֹת ׀ הֽוּא־יָבִ֗ין וְנֹצֵ֣ר נַ֭פְשְׁךָ ה֣וּא יֵדָ֑ע וְהֵשִׁ֖יב לְאָדָ֣ם כְּפָעֳלֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If thou sayest, Behold, we knew not this; Doth not he that weigheth the hearts consider it? And he that keepeth thy soul, doth not he know it? And shall not he render to every man according to his work?
					\item[NIV] If you say, “But we knew nothing about this,” does not he who weighs the heart perceive it? Does not he who guards your life know it? Will he not repay everyone according to what they have done?
					\item[NRSV] if you say, “Look, we did not know this”— does not he who weighs the heart perceive it? Does not he who keeps watch over your soul know it? And will he not repay all according to their deeds?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:13} % *PROVERBS-24-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-13}

			My son, eat thou honey, because it is good; and the honeycomb, which is sweet to thy taste:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֱכָל־בְּנִ֣י דְבַ֣שׁ כִּי־ט֑וֹב וְנֹ֥פֶת מָ֝ת֗וֹק עַל־חִכֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, eat thou honey, for it is good; And the droppings of the honeycomb, which are sweet to thy taste:
					\item[NIV] Eat honey, my son, for it is good; honey from the comb is sweet to your taste.
					\item[NRSV] My child, eat honey, for it is good, and the drippings of the honeycomb are sweet to your taste.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:14} % *PROVERBS-24-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-14}

			So shall the knowledge of wisdom be unto thy soul: when thou hast found it, then there shall be a reward, and thy expectation shall not be cut off.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֵּ֤ן ׀ דְּעֶ֥ה חָכְמָ֗ה לְנַ֫פְשֶׁ֥ךָ אִם־מָ֭צָאתָ וְיֵ֣שׁ אַחֲרִ֑ית וְ֝תִקְוָתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תִכָּרֵֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shalt thou know wisdom to be unto thy soul; If thou hast found it, then shall there be a reward, And thy hope shall not be cut off.
					\item[NIV] Know also that wisdom is like honey for you: If you find it, there is a future hope for you, and your hope will not be cut off.
					\item[NRSV] Know that wisdom is such to your soul; if you find it, you will find a future, and your hope will not be cut off.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:15} % *PROVERBS-24-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-15}

			Lay not wait, O wicked man, against the dwelling of the righteous; spoil not his resting place:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֶּאֱרֹ֣ב רָ֭שָׁע לִנְוֵ֣ה צַדִּ֑יק אַֽל־תְּשַׁדֵּ֥ד רִבְצוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lay not wait, O wicked man, against the habitation of the righteous; Destroy not his resting-place:
					\item[NIV] Do not lurk like a thief near the house of the righteous, do not plunder their dwelling place;
					\item[NRSV] Do not lie in wait like an outlaw against the home of the righteous; do no violence to the place where the righteous live;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:16} % *PROVERBS-24-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-16}

			For a just man falleth seven times, and riseth up again: but the wicked shall fall into mischief.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י שֶׁ֨בַע ׀ יִפּ֣וֹל צַדִּ֣יק וָקָ֑ם וּ֝רְשָׁעִ֗ים יִכָּשְׁל֥וּ בְרָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For a righteous man falleth seven times, and riseth up again; But the wicked are overthrown by calamity.
					\item[NIV] for though the righteous fall seven times, they rise again, but the wicked stumble when calamity strikes.
					\item[NRSV] for though they fall seven times, they will rise again; but the wicked are overthrown by calamity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:17} % *PROVERBS-24-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-17}

			Rejoice not when thine enemy falleth, and let not thine heart be glad when he stumbleth:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּנְפֹ֣ל אֽ֭וֹיִבְךָ אַל־תִּשְׂמָ֑ח וּ֝בִכָּשְׁל֗וֹ אַל־יָגֵ֥ל לִבֶּֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Rejoice not when thine enemy falleth, And let not thy heart be glad when he is overthrown;
					\item[NIV] Do not gloat when your enemy falls; when they stumble, do not let your heart rejoice,
					\item[NRSV] Do not rejoice when your enemies fall, and do not let your heart be glad when they stumble,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:18} % *PROVERBS-24-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-18}

			Lest the LORD see it, and it displease him, and he turn away his wrath from him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּן־יִרְאֶ֣ה יְ֭הוָה וְרַ֣ע בְּעֵינָ֑יו וְהֵשִׁ֖יב מֵעָלָ֣יו אַפּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lest Jehovah see it, and it displease him, And he turn away his wrath from him.
					\item[NIV] or the LORD will see and disapprove and turn his wrath away from them.
					\item[NRSV] or else the LORD will see it and be displeased, and turn away his anger from them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:19} % *PROVERBS-24-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-19}

			Fret not thyself because of evil men, neither be thou envious at the wicked;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּתְחַ֥ר בַּמְּרֵעִ֑ים אַל־תְּ֝קַנֵּ֗א בָּרְשָׁעִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Fret not thyself because of evil-doers; Neither be thou envious at the wicked:
					\item[NIV] Do not fret because of evildoers or be envious of the wicked,
					\item[NRSV] Do not fret because of evildoers. Do not envy the wicked;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:20} % *PROVERBS-24-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-20}

			For there shall be no reward to the evil man; the candle of the wicked shall be put out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י ׀ לֹֽא־תִהְיֶ֣ה אַחֲרִ֣ית לָרָ֑ע נֵ֖ר רְשָׁעִ֣ים יִדְעָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For there shall be no reward to the evil man; The lamp of the wicked shall be put out.
					\item[NIV] for the evildoer has no future hope, and the lamp of the wicked will be snuffed out.
					\item[NRSV] for the evil have no future; the lamp of the wicked will go out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:21} % *PROVERBS-24-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-21}

			My son, fear thou the LORD and the king: and meddle not with them that are given to change:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְרָֽא־אֶת־יְהוָ֣ה בְּנִ֣י וָמֶ֑לֶךְ עִם־שׁ֝וֹנִ֗ים אַל־תִּתְעָרָֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, fear thou Jehovah and the king; And company not with them that are given to change:
					\item[NIV] Fear the LORD and the king, my son, and do not join with rebellious officials,
					\item[NRSV] My child, fear the LORD and the king, and do not disobey either of them;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:22} % *PROVERBS-24-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-22}

			For their calamity shall rise suddenly; and who knoweth the ruin of them both?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־פִ֭תְאֹם יָק֣וּם אֵידָ֑ם וּפִ֥יד שְׁ֝נֵיהֶ֗ם מִ֣י יוֹדֵֽעַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For their calamity shall rise suddenly; And the destruction from them both, who knoweth it?
					\item[NIV] for those two will send sudden destruction on them, and who knows what calamities they can bring?
					\item[NRSV] for disaster comes from them suddenly, and who knows the ruin that both can bring?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:23} % *PROVERBS-24-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-23}

			These things also belong to the wise. It is not good to have respect of persons in judgment.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־אֵ֥לֶּה לַֽחֲכָמִ֑ים הַֽכֵּר־פָּנִ֖ים בְּמִשְׁפָּ֣ט בַּל־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] These also are sayings of the wise. To have respect of persons in judgment is not good.
					\item[NIV] These also are sayings of the wise: To show partiality in judging is not good:
					\item[NRSV] These also are sayings of the wise: Partiality in judging is not good.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:24} % *PROVERBS-24-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-24}

			He that saith unto the wicked, Thou art righteous; him shall the people curse, nations shall abhor him:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֹ֤מֵ֨ר ׀ לְרָשָׁע֮ צַדִּ֪יק אָ֥תָּה יִקְּבֻ֥הוּ עַמִּ֑ים יִזְעָמ֥וּהוּ לְאֻמִּֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that saith unto the wicked, Thou art righteous, Peoples shall curse him, nations shall abhor him;
					\item[NIV] Whoever says to the guilty, “You are innocent,” will be cursed by peoples and denounced by nations.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever says to the wicked, “You are innocent,” will be cursed by peoples, abhorred by nations;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:25} % *PROVERBS-24-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-25}

			But to them that rebuke him shall be delight, and a good blessing shall come upon them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְלַמּוֹכִיחִ֥ים יִנְעָ֑ם וַֽ֝עֲלֵיהֶ֗ם תָּב֥וֹא בִרְכַּת־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] But to them that rebuke him shall be delight, And a good blessing shall come upon them.
					\item[NIV] But it will go well with those who convict the guilty, and rich blessing will come on them.
					\item[NRSV] but those who rebuke the wicked will have delight, and a good blessing will come upon them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:26} % *PROVERBS-24-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-26}

			Every man shall kiss his lips that giveth a right answer.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׂפָתַ֥יִם יִשָּׁ֑ק מֵ֝שִׁ֗יב דְּבָרִ֥ים נְכֹחִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He kisseth the lips Who giveth a right answer.
					\item[NIV] An honest answer is like a kiss on the lips.
					\item[NRSV] One who gives an honest answer gives a kiss on the lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:27} % *PROVERBS-24-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-27}

			Prepare thy work without, and make it fit for thyself in the field; and afterwards build thine house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָ֘כֵ֤ן בַּח֨וּץ ׀ מְלַאכְתֶּ֗ךָ וְעַתְּדָ֣הּ בַּשָּׂדֶ֣ה לָ֑ךְ אַ֝חַ֗ר וּבָנִ֥יתָ בֵיתֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Prepare thy work without, And make it ready for thee in the field; And afterwards build thy house.
					\item[NIV] Put your outdoor work in order and get your fields ready; after that, build your house.
					\item[NRSV] Prepare your work outside, get everything ready for you in the field; and after that build your house.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:28} % *PROVERBS-24-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-28}

			Be not a witness against thy neighbour without cause; and deceive not with thy lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תְּהִ֣י עֵד־חִנָּ֣ם בְּרֵעֶ֑ךָ וַ֝הֲפִתִּ֗יתָ בִּשְׂפָתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be not a witness against thy neighbor without cause; And deceive not with thy lips.
					\item[NIV] Do not testify against your neighbor without cause— would you use your lips to mislead?
					\item[NRSV] Do not be a witness against your neighbor without cause, and do not deceive with your lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:29} % *PROVERBS-24-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-29}

			Say not, I will do so to him as he hath done to me: I will render to the man according to his work.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֹּאמַ֗ר כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר עָֽשָׂה־לִ֭י כֵּ֤ן אֶֽעֱשֶׂה־לּ֑וֹ אָשִׁ֖יב לָאִ֣ישׁ כְּפָעֳלֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Say not, I will do so to him as he hath done to me; I will render to the man according to his work.
					\item[NIV] Do not say, “I’ll do to them as they have done to me; I’ll pay them back for what they did.”
					\item[NRSV] Do not say, “I will do to others as they have done to me; I will pay them back for what they have done.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:30} % *PROVERBS-24-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-30}

			I went by the field of the slothful, and by the vineyard of the man void of understanding;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַל־שְׂדֵ֣ה אִישׁ־עָצֵ֣ל עָבַ֑רְתִּי וְעַל־כֶּ֝֗רֶם אָדָ֥ם חֲסַר־לֵֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] I went by the field of the sluggard, And by the vineyard of the man void of understanding;
					\item[NIV] I went past the field of a sluggard, past the vineyard of someone who has no sense;
					\item[NRSV] I passed by the field of one who was lazy, by the vineyard of a stupid person;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:31} % *PROVERBS-24-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-31}

			And, lo, it was all grown over with thorns, and nettles had covered the face thereof, and the stone wall thereof was broken down.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְהִנֵּ֨ה עָ֘לָ֤ה כֻלּ֨וֹ ׀ קִמְּשֹׂנִ֗ים כָּסּ֣וּ פָנָ֣יו חֲרֻלִּ֑ים וְגֶ֖דֶר אֲבָנָ֣יו נֶהֱרָֽסָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And, lo, it was all grown over with thorns, The face thereof was covered with nettles, And the stone wall thereof was broken down.
					\item[NIV] thorns had come up everywhere, the ground was covered with weeds, and the stone wall was in ruins.
					\item[NRSV] and see, it was all overgrown with thorns; the ground was covered with nettles, and its stone wall was broken down.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:32} % *PROVERBS-24-32
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-32}

			Then I saw, and considered it well: I looked upon it, and received instruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וָֽאֶחֱזֶ֣ה אָ֭נֹכִֽי אָשִׁ֣ית לִבִּ֑י רָ֝אִ֗יתִי לָקַ֥חְתִּי מוּסָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Then I beheld, and considered well; I saw, and received instruction:
					\item[NIV] I applied my heart to what I observed and learned a lesson from what I saw:
					\item[NRSV] Then I saw and considered it; I looked and received instruction.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:33} % *PROVERBS-24-33
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-33}

			Yet a little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to sleep:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְעַ֣ט שֵׁ֭נוֹת מְעַ֣ט תְּנוּמ֑וֹת מְעַ֓ט ׀ חִבֻּ֖ק יָדַ֣יִם לִשְׁכָּֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Yet a little sleep, a little slumber, A little folding of the hands to sleep;
					\item[NIV] A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to rest—
					\item[NRSV] A little sleep, a little slumber, a little folding of the hands to rest,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 24:34} % *PROVERBS-24-34
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-24-34}

			So shall thy poverty come as one that travelleth; and thy want as an armed man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וּבָֽא־מִתְהַלֵּ֥ךְ רֵישֶׁ֑ךָ וּ֝מַחְסֹרֶ֗יךָ כְּאִ֣ישׁ מָגֵֽן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So shall thy poverty come as a robber, And thy want as an armed man.
					\item[NIV] and poverty will come on you like a thief and scarcity like an armed man.
					\item[NRSV] and poverty will come upon you like a robber, and want, like an armed warrior.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 25} % *PROVERBS-25
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-25}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:1} % *PROVERBS-25-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-1}

			These are also proverbs of Solomon, which the men of Hezekiah king of Judah copied out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּם־אֵ֭לֶּה מִשְׁלֵ֣י שְׁלֹמֹ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר הֶ֝עְתִּ֗יקוּ אַנְשֵׁ֤י ׀ חִזְקִיָּ֬ה מֶֽלֶךְ־יְהוּדָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] These also are proverbs of Solomon, which the men of Hezekiah king of Judah copied out.
					\item[NIV] These are more proverbs of Solomon, compiled by the men of Hezekiah king of Judah:
					\item[NRSV] These are other proverbs of Solomon that the officials of King Hezekiah of Judah copied.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:2} % *PROVERBS-25-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-2}

			It is the glory of God to conceal a thing: but the honour of kings is to search out a matter.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּבֹ֣ד אֱ֭לֹהִים הַסְתֵּ֣ר דָּבָ֑ר וּכְבֹ֥ד מְ֝לָכִ֗ים חֲקֹ֣ר דָּבָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is the glory of God to conceal a thing; But the glory of kings is to search out a matter.
					\item[NIV] It is the glory of God to conceal a matter; to search out a matter is the glory of kings.
					\item[NRSV] It is the glory of God to conceal things, but the glory of kings is to search things out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:3} % *PROVERBS-25-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-3}

			The heaven for height, and the earth for depth, and the heart of kings is unsearchable.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שָׁמַ֣יִם לָ֭רוּם וָאָ֣רֶץ לָעֹ֑מֶק וְלֵ֥ב מְ֝לָכִ֗ים אֵ֣ין חֵֽקֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As the heavens for height, and the earth for depth, So the heart of kings is unsearchable.
					\item[NIV] As the heavens are high and the earth is deep, so the hearts of kings are unsearchable.
					\item[NRSV] Like the heavens for height, like the earth for depth, so the mind of kings is unsearchable.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:4} % *PROVERBS-25-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-4}

			Take away the dross from the silver, and there shall come forth a vessel for the finer.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָג֣וֹ סִיגִ֣ים מִכָּ֑סֶף וַיֵּצֵ֖א לַצֹּרֵ֣ף כֶּֽלִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Take away the dross from the silver, And there cometh forth a vessel for the refiner:
					\item[NIV] Remove the dross from the silver, and a silversmith can produce a vessel;
					\item[NRSV] Take away the dross from the silver, and the smith has material for a vessel;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:5} % *PROVERBS-25-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-5}

			Take away the wicked from before the king, and his throne shall be established in righteousness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָג֣וֹ רָ֭שָׁע לִפְנֵי־מֶ֑לֶךְ וְיִכּ֖וֹן בַּצֶּ֣דֶק כִּסְאֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Take away the wicked from before the king, And his throne shall be established in righteousness.
					\item[NIV] remove wicked officials from the king’s presence, and his throne will be established through righteousness.
					\item[NRSV] take away the wicked from the presence of the king, and his throne will be established in righteousness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:6} % *PROVERBS-25-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-6}

			Put not forth thyself in the presence of the king, and stand not in the place of great men:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּתְהַדַּ֥ר לִפְנֵי־מֶ֑לֶךְ וּבִמְק֥וֹם גְּ֝דֹלִ֗ים אַֽל־תַּעֲמֹֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Put not thyself forward in the presence of the king, And stand not in the place of great men:
					\item[NIV] Do not exalt yourself in the king’s presence, and do not claim a place among his great men;
					\item[NRSV] Do not put yourself forward in the king’s presence or stand in the place of the great;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:7} % *PROVERBS-25-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-7}

			For better it is that it be said unto thee, Come up hither; than that thou shouldest be put lower in the presence of the prince whom thine eyes have seen.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י ט֥וֹב אֲמָר־לְךָ֗ עֲ‍ֽלֵ֫ה הֵ֥נָּה מֵֽ֭הַשְׁפִּ֣ילְךָ לִפְנֵ֣י נָדִ֑יב אֲשֶׁ֖ר רָא֣וּ עֵינֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For better is it that it be said unto thee, Come up hither, Than that thou shouldest be put lower in the presence of the prince, Whom thine eyes have seen.
					\item[NIV] it is better for him to say to you, “Come up here,” than for him to humiliate you before his nobles. What you have seen with your eyes
					\item[NRSV] for it is better to be told, “Come up here,” than to be put lower in the presence of a noble. What your eyes have seen
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:8} % *PROVERBS-25-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-8}

			Go not forth hastily to strive, lest thou know not what to do in the end thereof, when thy neighbour hath put thee to shame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תֵּצֵ֥א לָרִ֗ב מַ֫הֵ֥ר פֶּ֣ן מַה־תַּ֭עֲשֶׂה בְּאַחֲרִיתָ֑הּ בְּהַכְלִ֖ים אֹתְךָ֣ רֵעֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Go not forth hastily to strive, Lest thou know not what to do in the end thereof, When thy neighbor hath put thee to shame.
					\item[NIV] do not bring hastily to court, for what will you do in the end if your neighbor puts you to shame?
					\item[NRSV] do not hastily bring into court; for what will you do in the end, when your neighbor puts you to shame?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:9} % *PROVERBS-25-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-9}

			Debate thy cause with thy neighbour himself; and discover not a secret to another:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רִֽ֭יבְךָ רִ֣יב אֶת־רֵעֶ֑ךָ וְס֖וֹד אַחֵ֣ר אַל־תְּגָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Debate thy cause with thy neighbor himself, And disclose not the secret of another;
					\item[NIV] If you take your neighbor to court, do not betray another’s confidence,
					\item[NRSV] Argue your case with your neighbor directly, and do not disclose another’s secret;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:10} % *PROVERBS-25-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-10}

			Lest he that heareth it put thee to shame, and thine infamy turn not away.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּֽן־יְחַסֶּדְךָ֥ שֹׁמֵ֑עַ וְ֝דִבָּתְךָ֗ לֹ֣א תָשֽׁוּב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lest he that heareth it revile thee, And thine infamy turn not away.
					\item[NIV] or the one who hears it may shame you and the charge against you will stand.
					\item[NRSV] or else someone who hears you will bring shame upon you, and your ill repute will have no end.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:11} % *PROVERBS-25-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-11}

			A word fitly spoken is like apples of gold in pictures of silver.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תַּפּוּחֵ֣י זָ֭הָב בְּמַשְׂכִּיּ֥וֹת כָּ֑סֶף דָּ֝בָ֗ר דָּבֻ֥ר עַל־אָפְנָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A word fitly spoken Is like apples of gold in network of silver.
					\item[NIV] Like apples of gold in settings of silver is a ruling rightly given.
					\item[NRSV] A word fitly spoken is like apples of gold in a setting of silver.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:12} % *PROVERBS-25-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-12}

			As an earring of gold, and an ornament of fine gold, so is a wise reprover upon an obedient ear.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶ֣זֶם זָ֭הָב וַחֲלִי־כָ֑תֶם מוֹכִ֥יחַ חָ֝כָ֗ם עַל־אֹ֥זֶן שֹׁמָֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As an ear-ring of gold, and an ornament of fine gold, So is a wise reprover upon an obedient ear.
					\item[NIV] Like an earring of gold or an ornament of fine gold is the rebuke of a wise judge to a listening ear.
					\item[NRSV] Like a gold ring or an ornament of gold is a wise rebuke to a listening ear.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:13} % *PROVERBS-25-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-13}

			As the cold of snow in the time of harvest, so is a faithful messenger to them that send him: for he refresheth the soul of his masters.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּצִנַּת־שֶׁ֨לֶג ׀ בְּי֬וֹם קָצִ֗יר צִ֣יר נֶ֭אֱמָן לְשֹׁלְחָ֑יו וְנֶ֖פֶשׁ אֲדֹנָ֣יו יָשִֽׁיב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As the cold of snow in the time of harvest, So is a faithful messenger to them that send him; For he refresheth the soul of his masters.
					\item[NIV] Like a snow-cooled drink at harvest time is a trustworthy messenger to the one who sends him; he refreshes the spirit of his master.
					\item[NRSV] Like the cold of snow in the time of harvest are faithful messengers to those who send them; they refresh the spirit of their masters.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:14} % *PROVERBS-25-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-14}

			Whoso boasteth himself of a false gift is like clouds and wind without rain.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נְשִׂיאִ֣ים וְ֭רוּחַ וְגֶ֣שֶׁם אָ֑יִן אִ֥ישׁ מִ֝תְהַלֵּ֗ל בְּמַתַּת־שָֽׁקֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As clouds and wind without rain, So is he that boasteth himself of his gifts falsely.
					\item[NIV] Like clouds and wind without rain is one who boasts of gifts never given.
					\item[NRSV] Like clouds and wind without rain is one who boasts of a gift never given.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:15} % *PROVERBS-25-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-15}

			By long forbearing is a prince persuaded, and a soft tongue breaketh the bone.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֹ֣רֶךְ אַ֭פַּיִם יְפֻתֶּ֣ה קָצִ֑ין וְלָשׁ֥וֹן רַ֝כָּ֗ה תִּשְׁבָּר־גָּֽרֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] By long forbearing is a ruler persuaded, And a soft tongue breaketh the bone.
					\item[NIV] Through patience a ruler can be persuaded, and a gentle tongue can break a bone.
					\item[NRSV] With patience a ruler may be persuaded, and a soft tongue can break bones.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:16} % *PROVERBS-25-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-16}

			Hast thou found honey? eat so much as is sufficient for thee, lest thou be filled therewith, and vomit it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דְּבַ֣שׁ מָ֭צָאתָ אֱכֹ֣ל דַּיֶּ֑ךָּ פֶּן־תִּ֝שְׂבָּעֶ֗נּוּ וַהֲקֵֽאתֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Hast thou found honey? eat so much as is sufficient for thee, Lest thou be filled therewith, and vomit it.
					\item[NIV] If you find honey, eat just enough— too much of it, and you will vomit.
					\item[NRSV] If you have found honey, eat only enough for you, or else, having too much, you will vomit it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:17} % *PROVERBS-25-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-17}

			Withdraw thy foot from thy neighbour’s house; lest he be weary of thee, and so hate thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הֹקַ֣ר רַ֭גְלְךָ מִבֵּ֣ית רֵעֶ֑ךָ פֶּן־יִ֝שְׂבָּעֲךָ֗ וּשְׂנֵאֶֽךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let thy foot be seldom in thy neighbor's house, Lest he be weary of thee, and hate thee.
					\item[NIV] Seldom set foot in your neighbor’s house— too much of you, and they will hate you.
					\item[NRSV] Let your foot be seldom in your neighbor’s house, otherwise the neighbor will become weary of you and hate you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:18} % *PROVERBS-25-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-18}

			A man that beareth false witness against his neighbour is a maul, and a sword, and a sharp arrow.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵפִ֣יץ וְ֭חֶרֶב וְחֵ֣ץ שָׁנ֑וּן אִ֥ישׁ עֹנֶ֥ה בְ֝רֵעֵ֗הוּ עֵ֣ד שָֽׁקֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man that beareth false witness against his neighbor Is a maul, and a sword, and a sharp arrow.
					\item[NIV] Like a club or a sword or a sharp arrow is one who gives false testimony against a neighbor.
					\item[NRSV] Like a war club, a sword, or a sharp arrow is one who bears false witness against a neighbor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:19} % *PROVERBS-25-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-19}

			Confidence in an unfaithful man in time of trouble is like a broken tooth, and a foot out of joint.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֵׁ֣ן רֹ֭עָה וְרֶ֣גֶל מוּעָ֑דֶת מִבְטָ֥ח בּ֝וֹגֵ֗ד בְּי֣וֹם צָרָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Confidence in an unfaithful man in time of trouble Is like a broken tooth, and a foot out of joint.
					\item[NIV] Like a broken tooth or a lame foot is reliance on the unfaithful in a time of trouble.
					\item[NRSV] Like a bad tooth or a lame foot is trust in a faithless person in time of trouble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:20} % *PROVERBS-25-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-20}

			As he that taketh away a garment in cold weather, and as vinegar upon nitre, so is he that singeth songs to an heavy heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַ֥עֲדֶה בֶּ֨גֶד ׀ בְּי֣וֹם קָ֭רָה חֹ֣מֶץ עַל־נָ֑תֶר וְשָׁ֥ר בַּ֝שִּׁרִ֗ים עַ֣ל לֶב־רָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As one that taketh off a garment in cold weather, and as vinegar upon soda, So is he that singeth songs to a heavy heart.
					\item[NIV] Like one who takes away a garment on a cold day, or like vinegar poured on a wound, is one who sings songs to a heavy heart.
					\item[NRSV] Like vinegar on a wound is one who sings songs to a heavy heart. Like a moth in clothing or a worm in wood, sorrow gnaws at the human heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:21} % *PROVERBS-25-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-21}

			If thine enemy be hungry, give him bread to eat; and if he be thirsty, give him water to drink:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִם־רָעֵ֣ב שֹׂ֭נַאֲךָ הַאֲכִלֵ֣הוּ לָ֑חֶם וְאִם־צָ֝מֵ֗א הַשְׁקֵ֥הוּ מָֽיִם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If thine enemy be hungry, give him bread to eat; And if he be thirsty, give him water to drink:
					\item[NIV] If your enemy is hungry, give him food to eat; if he is thirsty, give him water to drink.
					\item[NRSV] If your enemies are hungry, give them bread to eat; and if they are thirsty, give them water to drink;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:22} % *PROVERBS-25-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-22}

			For thou shalt heap coals of fire upon his head, and the LORD shall reward thee.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י גֶֽחָלִ֗ים אַ֭תָּה חֹתֶ֣ה עַל־רֹאשׁ֑וֹ וַֽ֝יהוָ֗ה יְשַׁלֶּם־לָֽךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For thou wilt heap coals of fire upon his head, And Jehovah will reward thee.
					\item[NIV] In doing this, you will heap burning coals on his head, and the LORD will reward you.
					\item[NRSV] for you will heap coals of fire on their heads, and the LORD will reward you.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:23} % *PROVERBS-25-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-23}

			The north wind driveth away rain: so doth an angry countenance a backbiting tongue.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ר֣וּחַ צָ֭פוֹן תְּח֣וֹלֵֽל גָּ֑שֶׁם וּפָנִ֥ים נִ֝זְעָמִ֗ים לְשׁ֣וֹן סָֽתֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The north wind bringeth forth rain: So doth a backbiting tongue an angry countenance.
					\item[NIV] Like a north wind that brings unexpected rain is a sly tongue—which provokes a horrified look.
					\item[NRSV] The north wind produces rain, and a backbiting tongue, angry looks.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:24} % *PROVERBS-25-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-24}

			It is better to dwell in the corner of the housetop, than with a brawling woman and in a wide house.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֗וֹב שֶׁ֥בֶת עַל־פִּנַּת־גָּ֑ג מֵאֵ֥שֶׁת מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים וּבֵ֥ית חָֽבֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is better to dwell in the corner of the housetop, Than with a contentious woman in a wide house.
					\item[NIV] Better to live on a corner of the roof than share a house with a quarrelsome wife.
					\item[NRSV] It is better to live in a corner of the housetop than in a house shared with a contentious wife.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:25} % *PROVERBS-25-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-25}

			As cold waters to a thirsty soul, so is good news from a far country.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַ֣יִם קָ֭רִים עַל־נֶ֣פֶשׁ עֲיֵפָ֑ה וּשְׁמוּעָ֥ה ט֝וֹבָ֗ה מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מֶרְחָֽק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As cold waters to a thirsty soul, So is good news from a far country.
					\item[NIV] Like cold water to a weary soul is good news from a distant land.
					\item[NRSV] Like cold water to a thirsty soul, so is good news from a far country.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:26} % *PROVERBS-25-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-26}

			A righteous man falling down before the wicked is as a troubled fountain, and a corrupt spring.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַעְיָ֣ן נִ֭רְפָּשׂ וּמָק֣וֹר מָשְׁחָ֑ת צַ֝דִּ֗יק מָ֣ט לִפְנֵֽי־רָשָֽׁע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a troubled fountain, and a corrupted spring, So is a righteous man that giveth way before the wicked.
					\item[NIV] Like a muddied spring or a polluted well are the righteous who give way to the wicked.
					\item[NRSV] Like a muddied spring or a polluted fountain are the righteous who give way before the wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
