					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:27} % *PROVERBS-25-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-27}

			It is not good to eat much honey: so for men to search their own glory is not glory.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֘כֹ֤ל דְּבַ֣שׁ הַרְבּ֣וֹת לֹא־ט֑וֹב וְחֵ֖קֶר כְּבֹדָ֣ם כָּבֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is not good to eat much honey: So for men to search out their own glory is grievous.
					\item[NIV] It is not good to eat too much honey, nor is it honorable to search out matters that are too deep.
					\item[NRSV] It is not good to eat much honey, or to seek honor on top of honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 25:28} % *PROVERBS-25-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-25-28}

			He that hath no rule over his own spirit is like a city that is broken down, and without walls.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עִ֣יר פְּ֭רוּצָה אֵ֣ין חוֹמָ֑ה אִ֝֗ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֤ר אֵ֖ין מַעְצָ֣ר לְרוּחֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He whose spirit is without restraint Is like a city that is broken down and without walls.
					\item[NIV] Like a city whose walls are broken through is a person who lacks self-control.
					\item[NRSV] Like a city breached, without walls, is one who lacks self-control.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 26} % *PROVERBS-26
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-26}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:1} % *PROVERBS-26-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-1}

			As snow in summer, and as rain in harvest, so honour is not seemly for a fool.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּשֶּׁ֤לֶג ׀ בַּקַּ֗יִץ וְכַמָּטָ֥ר בַּקָּצִ֑יר כֵּ֤ן לֹא־נָאוֶ֖ה לִכְסִ֣יל כָּבֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As snow in summer, and as rain in harvest, So honor is not seemly for a fool.
					\item[NIV] Like snow in summer or rain in harvest, honor is not fitting for a fool.
					\item[NRSV] Like snow in summer or rain in harvest, so honor is not fitting for a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:2} % *PROVERBS-26-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-2}

			As the bird by wandering, as the swallow by flying, so the curse causeless shall not come.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּצִּפּ֣וֹר לָ֭נוּד כַּדְּר֣וֹר לָע֑וּף כֵּ֥ן קִֽלְלַ֥ת חִ֝נָּ֗ם ל֣וֹ תָבֹֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As the sparrow in her wandering, as the swallow in her flying, So the curse that is causeless alighteth not.
					\item[NIV] Like a fluttering sparrow or a darting swallow, an undeserved curse does not come to rest.
					\item[NRSV] Like a sparrow in its flitting, like a swallow in its flying, an undeserved curse goes nowhere.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:3} % *PROVERBS-26-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-3}

			A whip for the horse, a bridle for the ass, and a rod for the fool’s back.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שׁ֣וֹט לַ֭סּוּס מֶ֣תֶג לַחֲמ֑וֹר וְ֝שֵׁ֗בֶט לְגֵ֣ו כְּסִילִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A whip for the horse, a bridle for the ass, And a rod for the back of fools.
					\item[NIV] A whip for the horse, a bridle for the donkey, and a rod for the backs of fools!
					\item[NRSV] A whip for the horse, a bridle for the donkey, and a rod for the back of fools.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:4} % *PROVERBS-26-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-4}

			Answer not a fool according to his folly, lest thou also be like unto him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּ֣עַן כְּ֭סִיל כְּאִוַּלְתּ֑וֹ פֶּֽן־תִּשְׁוֶה־לּ֥וֹ גַם־אָֽתָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Answer not a fool according to his folly, Lest thou also be like unto him.
					\item[NIV] Do not answer a fool according to his folly, or you yourself will be just like him.
					\item[NRSV] Do not answer fools according to their folly, or you will be a fool yourself.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:5} % *PROVERBS-26-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-5}

			Answer a fool according to his folly, lest he be wise in his own conceit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֲנֵ֣ה כְ֭סִיל כְּאִוַּלְתּ֑וֹ פֶּן־יִהְיֶ֖ה חָכָ֣ם בְּעֵינָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Answer a fool according to his folly, Lest he be wise in his own conceit.
					\item[NIV] Answer a fool according to his folly, or he will be wise in his own eyes.
					\item[NRSV] Answer fools according to their folly, or they will be wise in their own eyes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:6} % *PROVERBS-26-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-6}

			He that sendeth a message by the hand of a fool cutteth off the feet, and drinketh damage.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְקַצֶּ֣ה רַ֭גְלַיִם חָמָ֣ס שֹׁתֶ֑ה שֹׁלֵ֖חַ דְּבָרִ֣ים בְּיַד־כְּסִֽיל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that sendeth a message by the hand of a fool Cutteth off his own feet, and drinketh in damage.
					\item[NIV] Sending a message by the hands of a fool is like cutting off one’s feet or drinking poison.
					\item[NRSV] It is like cutting off one’s foot and drinking down violence, to send a message by a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:7} % *PROVERBS-26-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-7}

			The legs of the lame are not equal: so is a parable in the mouth of fools.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דַּלְי֣וּ שֹׁ֭קַיִם מִפִּסֵּ֑חַ וּ֝מָשָׁ֗ל בְּפִ֣י כְסִילִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The legs of the lame hang loose; So is a parable in the mouth of fools.
					\item[NIV] Like the useless legs of one who is lame is a proverb in the mouth of a fool.
					\item[NRSV] The legs of a disabled person hang limp; so does a proverb in the mouth of a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:8} % *PROVERBS-26-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-8}

			As he that bindeth a stone in a sling, so is he that giveth honour to a fool.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּצְר֣וֹר אֶ֭בֶן בְּמַרְגֵּמָ֑ה כֵּן־נוֹתֵ֖ן לִכְסִ֣יל כָּבֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As one that bindeth a stone in a sling, So is he that giveth honor to a fool.
					\item[NIV] Like tying a stone in a sling is the giving of honor to a fool.
					\item[NRSV] It is like binding a stone in a sling to give honor to a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:9} % *PROVERBS-26-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-9}

			As a thorn goeth up into the hand of a drunkard, so is a parable in the mouth of fools.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ח֭וֹחַ עָלָ֣ה בְיַד־שִׁכּ֑וֹר וּ֝מָשָׁ֗ל בְּפִ֣י כְסִילִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a thorn that goeth up into the hand of a drunkard, So is a parable in the mouth of fools.
					\item[NIV] Like a thornbush in a drunkard’s hand is a proverb in the mouth of a fool.
					\item[NRSV] Like a thornbush brandished by the hand of a drunkard is a proverb in the mouth of a fool.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:10} % *PROVERBS-26-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-10}

			The great God that formed all things both rewardeth the fool, and rewardeth transgressors.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַ֥ב מְחֽוֹלֵֽל־כֹּ֑ל וְשֹׂכֵ֥ר כְּ֝סִ֗יל וְשֹׂכֵ֥ר עֹבְרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As an archer that woundeth all, So is he that hireth a fool and he that hireth them that pass by.
					\item[NIV] Like an archer who wounds at random is one who hires a fool or any passer-by.
					\item[NRSV] Like an archer who wounds everybody is one who hires a passing fool or drunkard.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:11} % *PROVERBS-26-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-11}

			As a dog returneth to his vomit, so a fool returneth to his folly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּ֭כֶלֶב שָׁ֣ב עַל־קֵא֑וֹ כְּ֝סִ֗יל שׁוֹנֶ֥ה בְאִוַּלְתּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a dog that returneth to his vomit, So is a fool that repeateth his folly.
					\item[NIV] As a dog returns to its vomit, so fools repeat their folly.
					\item[NRSV] Like a dog that returns to its vomit is a fool who reverts to his folly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:12} % *PROVERBS-26-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-12}

			Seest thou a man wise in his own conceit? there is more hope of a fool than of him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָאִ֗יתָ אִ֭ישׁ חָכָ֣ם בְּעֵינָ֑יו תִּקְוָ֖ה לִכְסִ֣יל מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Seest thou a man wise in his own conceit? There is more hope of a fool than of him.
					\item[NIV] Do you see a person wise in their own eyes? There is more hope for a fool than for them.
					\item[NRSV] Do you see persons wise in their own eyes? There is more hope for fools than for them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:13} % *PROVERBS-26-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-13}

			The slothful man saith, There is a lion in the way; a lion is in the streets.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָמַ֣ר עָ֭צֵל שַׁ֣חַל בַּדָּ֑רֶךְ אֲ֝רִ֗י בֵּ֣ין הָרְחֹבֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sluggard saith, There is a lion in the way; A lion is in the streets.
					\item[NIV] A sluggard says, “There’s a lion in the road, a fierce lion roaming the streets!”
					\item[NRSV] The lazy person says, “There is a lion in the road! There is a lion in the streets!”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:14} % *PROVERBS-26-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-14}

			As the door turneth upon his hinges, so doth the slothful upon his bed.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַ֭דֶּלֶת תִּסּ֣וֹב עַל־צִירָ֑הּ וְ֝עָצֵ֗ל עַל־מִטָּתֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As the door turneth upon its hinges, So doth the sluggard upon his bed.
					\item[NIV] As a door turns on its hinges, so a sluggard turns on his bed.
					\item[NRSV] As a door turns on its hinges, so does a lazy person in bed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:15} % *PROVERBS-26-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-15}

			The slothful hideth his hand in his bosom; it grieveth him to bring it again to his mouth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טָ֘מַ֤ן עָצֵ֣ל יָ֭דוֹ בַּצַּלָּ֑חַת נִ֝לְאָ֗ה לַֽהֲשִׁיבָ֥הּ אֶל־פִּֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sluggard burieth his hand in the dish; It wearieth him to bring it again to his mouth.
					\item[NIV] A sluggard buries his hand in the dish; he is too lazy to bring it back to his mouth.
					\item[NRSV] The lazy person buries a hand in the dish, and is too tired to bring it back to the mouth.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:16} % *PROVERBS-26-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-16}

			The sluggard is wiser in his own conceit than seven men that can render a reason.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָכָ֣ם עָצֵ֣ל בְּעֵינָ֑יו מִ֝שִּׁבְעָ֗ה מְשִׁ֣יבֵי טָֽעַם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The sluggard is wiser in his own conceit Than seven men that can render a reason.
					\item[NIV] A sluggard is wiser in his own eyes than seven people who answer discreetly.
					\item[NRSV] The lazy person is wiser in self-esteem than seven who can answer discreetly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:17} % *PROVERBS-26-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-17}

			He that passeth by, and meddleth with strife belonging not to him, is like one that taketh a dog by the ears.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַחֲזִ֥יק בְּאָזְנֵי־כָ֑לֶב עֹבֵ֥ר מִ֝תְעַבֵּ֗ר עַל־רִ֥יב לֹּֽא־לֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that passeth by, and vexeth himself with strife belonging not to him, Is like one that taketh a dog by the ears.
					\item[NIV] Like one who grabs a stray dog by the ears is someone who rushes into a quarrel not their own.
					\item[NRSV] Like somebody who takes a passing dog by the ears is one who meddles in the quarrel of another.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:18} % *PROVERBS-26-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-18}

			As a mad man who casteth firebrands, arrows, and death,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּֽ֭מִתְלַהְלֵהַּ הַיֹּרֶ֥ה זִקִּ֗ים חִצִּ֥ים וָמָֽוֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a madman who casteth firebrands, Arrows, and death,
					\item[NIV] Like a maniac shooting flaming arrows of death
					\item[NRSV] Like a maniac who shoots deadly firebrands and arrows,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:19} % *PROVERBS-26-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-19}

			So is the man that deceiveth his neighbour, and saith, Am not I in sport?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֵּֽן־אִ֭ישׁ רִמָּ֣ה אֶת־רֵעֵ֑הוּ וְ֝אָמַ֗ר הֲ‍ֽלֹא־מְשַׂחֵ֥ק אָֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] So is the man that deceiveth his neighbor, And saith, Am not I in sport?
					\item[NIV] is one who deceives their neighbor and says, “I was only joking!”
					\item[NRSV] so is one who deceives a neighbor and says, “I am only joking!”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:20} % *PROVERBS-26-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-20}

			Where no wood is, there the fire goeth out: so where there is no talebearer, the strife ceaseth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֶ֣פֶס עֵ֭צִים תִּכְבֶּה־אֵ֑שׁ וּבְאֵ֥ין נִ֝רְגָּ֗ן יִשְׁתֹּ֥ק מָדֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For lack of wood the fire goeth out; And where there is no whisperer, contention ceaseth.
					\item[NIV] Without wood a fire goes out; without a gossip a quarrel dies down.
					\item[NRSV] For lack of wood the fire goes out, and where there is no whisperer, quarreling ceases.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:21} % *PROVERBS-26-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-21}

			As coals are to burning coals, and wood to fire; so is a contentious man to kindle strife.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּחָ֣ם לְ֭גֶחָלִים וְעֵצִ֣ים לְאֵ֑שׁ וְאִ֥ישׁ מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים לְחַרְחַר־רִֽיב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As coals are to hot embers, and wood to fire, So is a contentious man to inflame strife.
					\item[NIV] As charcoal to embers and as wood to fire, so is a quarrelsome person for kindling strife.
					\item[NRSV] As charcoal is to hot embers and wood to fire, so is a quarrelsome person for kindling strife.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:22} % *PROVERBS-26-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-22}

			The words of a talebearer are as wounds, and they go down into the innermost parts of the belly.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּבְרֵ֣י נִ֭רְגָּן כְּמִֽתְלַהֲמִ֑ים וְ֝הֵ֗ם יָרְד֥וּ חַדְרֵי־בָֽטֶן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of a whisperer are as dainty morsels, And they go down into the innermost parts.
					\item[NIV] The words of a gossip are like choice morsels; they go down to the inmost parts.
					\item[NRSV] The words of a whisperer are like delicious morsels; they go down into the inner parts of the body.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:23} % *PROVERBS-26-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-23}

			Burning lips and a wicked heart are like a potsherd covered with silver dross.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֶּ֣סֶף סִ֭יגִים מְצֻפֶּ֣ה עַל־חָ֑רֶשׂ שְׂפָתַ֖יִם דֹּלְקִ֣ים וְלֶב־רָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Fervent lips and a wicked heart Are like an earthen vessel overlaid with silver dross.
					\item[NIV] Like a coating of silver dross on earthenware are fervent lips with an evil heart.
					\item[NRSV] Like the glaze covering an earthen vessel are smooth lips with an evil heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:24} % *PROVERBS-26-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-24}

			He that hateth dissembleth with his lips, and layeth up deceit within him;

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּ֭שְׂפָתָיו יִנָּכֵ֣ר שׂוֹנֵ֑א וּ֝בְקִרְבּ֗וֹ יָשִׁ֥ית מִרְמָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that hateth dissembleth with his lips; But he layeth up deceit within him:
					\item[NIV] Enemies disguise themselves with their lips, but in their hearts they harbor deceit.
					\item[NRSV] An enemy dissembles in speaking while harboring deceit within;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:25} % *PROVERBS-26-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-25}

			When he speaketh fair, believe him not: for there are seven abominations in his heart.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּֽי־יְחַנֵּ֣ן ק֭וֹלוֹ אַל־תַּֽאֲמֶן־בּ֑וֹ כִּ֤י שֶׁ֖בַע תּוֹעֵב֣וֹת בְּלִבּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When he speaketh fair, believe him not; For there are seven abominations in his heart:
					\item[NIV] Though their speech is charming, do not believe them, for seven abominations fill their hearts.
					\item[NRSV] when an enemy speaks graciously, do not believe it, for there are seven abominations concealed within;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:26} % *PROVERBS-26-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-26}

			Whose hatred is covered by deceit, his wickedness shall be shewed before the whole congregation.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תִּכַּסֶּ֣ה שִׂ֭נְאָה בְּמַשָּׁא֑וֹן תִּגָּלֶ֖ה רָעָת֣וֹ בְקָהָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Though his hatred cover itself with guile, His wickedness shall be openly showed before the assembly.
					\item[NIV] Their malice may be concealed by deception, but their wickedness will be exposed in the assembly.
					\item[NRSV] though hatred is covered with guile, the enemy’s wickedness will be exposed in the assembly.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:27} % *PROVERBS-26-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-27}

			Whoso diggeth a pit shall fall therein: and he that rolleth a stone, it will return upon him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֹּֽרֶה־שַּׁ֭חַת בָּ֣הּ יִפֹּ֑ל וְגֹ֥לֵ֥ל אֶ֝בֶן אֵלָ֥יו תָּשֽׁוּב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso diggeth a pit shall fall therein; And he that rolleth a stone, it shall return upon him.
					\item[NIV] Whoever digs a pit will fall into it; if someone rolls a stone, it will roll back on them.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever digs a pit will fall into it, and a stone will come back on the one who starts it rolling.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 26:28} % *PROVERBS-26-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-26-28}

			A lying tongue hateth those that are afflicted by it; and a flattering mouth worketh ruin.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לְֽשׁוֹן־שֶׁ֭קֶר יִשְׂנָ֣א דַכָּ֑יו וּפֶ֥ה חָ֝לָ֗ק יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה מִדְחֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A lying tongue hateth those whom it hath wounded; And a flattering mouth worketh ruin.
					\item[NIV] A lying tongue hates those it hurts, and a flattering mouth works ruin.
					\item[NRSV] A lying tongue hates its victims, and a flattering mouth works ruin.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 27} % *PROVERBS-27
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-27}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:1} % *PROVERBS-27-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-1}

			Boast not thyself of to morrow; for thou knowest not what a day may bring forth.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַֽל־תִּ֭תְהַלֵּל בְּי֣וֹם מָחָ֑ר כִּ֤י לֹא־תֵ֝דַ֗ע מַה־יֵּ֥לֶד יֽוֹם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Boast not thyself of to-morrow; For thou knowest not what a day may bring forth.
					\item[NIV] Do not boast about tomorrow, for you do not know what a day may bring.
					\item[NRSV] Do not boast about tomorrow, for you do not know what a day may bring.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:2} % *PROVERBS-27-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-2}

			Let another man praise thee, and not thine own mouth; a stranger, and not thine own lips.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יְהַלֶּלְךָ֣ זָ֣ר וְלֹא־פִ֑יךָ נָ֝כְרִ֗י וְאַל־שְׂפָתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let another man praise thee, and not thine own mouth; A stranger, and not thine own lips.
					\item[NIV] Let someone else praise you, and not your own mouth; an outsider, and not your own lips.
					\item[NRSV] Let another praise you, and not your own mouth— a stranger, and not your own lips.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:3} % *PROVERBS-27-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-3}

			A stone is heavy, and the sand weighty; but a fool’s wrath is heavier than them both.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כֹּֽבֶד־אֶ֭בֶן וְנֵ֣טֶל הַח֑וֹל וְכַ֥עַס אֱ֝וִ֗יל כָּבֵ֥ד מִשְּׁנֵיהֶֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A stone is heavy, and the sand weighty; But a fool's vexation is heavier than they both.
					\item[NIV] Stone is heavy and sand a burden, but a fool’s provocation is heavier than both.
					\item[NRSV] A stone is heavy, and sand is weighty, but a fool’s provocation is heavier than both.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:4} % *PROVERBS-27-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-4}

			Wrath is cruel, and anger is outrageous; but who is able to stand before envy?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַכְזְרִיּ֣וּת חֵ֭מָה וְשֶׁ֣טֶף אָ֑ף וּמִ֥י יַ֝עֲמֹד לִפְנֵ֥י קִנְאָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Wrath is cruel, and anger is overwhelming; But who is able to stand before jealousy?
					\item[NIV] Anger is cruel and fury overwhelming, but who can stand before jealousy?
					\item[NRSV] Wrath is cruel, anger is overwhelming, but who is able to stand before jealousy?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:5} % *PROVERBS-27-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-5}

			Open rebuke is better than secret love.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{ט֭וֹבָה תּוֹכַ֣חַת מְגֻלָּ֑ה מֵֽאַהֲבָ֥ה מְסֻתָּֽרֶת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is open rebuke Than love that is hidden.
					\item[NIV] Better is open rebuke than hidden love.
					\item[NRSV] Better is open rebuke than hidden love.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:6} % *PROVERBS-27-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-6}

			Faithful are the wounds of a friend; but the kisses of an enemy are deceitful.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶ֭אֱמָנִים פִּצְעֵ֣י אוֹהֵ֑ב וְ֝נַעְתָּר֗וֹת נְשִׁיק֥וֹת שׂוֹנֵֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Faithful are the wounds of a friend; But the kisses of an enemy are profuse.
					\item[NIV] Wounds from a friend can be trusted, but an enemy multiplies kisses.
					\item[NRSV] Well meant are the wounds a friend inflicts, but profuse are the kisses of an enemy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:7} % *PROVERBS-27-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-7}

			The full soul loatheth an honeycomb; but to the hungry soul every bitter thing is sweet.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֶ֣פֶשׁ שְׂ֭בֵעָה תָּב֣וּס נֹ֑פֶת וְנֶ֥פֶשׁ רְ֝עֵבָ֗ה כָּל־מַ֥ר מָתֽוֹק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The full soul loatheth a honeycomb; But to the hungry soul every bitter thing is sweet.
					\item[NIV] One who is full loathes honey from the comb, but to the hungry even what is bitter tastes sweet.
					\item[NRSV] The sated appetite spurns honey, but to a ravenous appetite even the bitter is sweet.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:8} % *PROVERBS-27-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-8}

			As a bird that wandereth from her nest, so is a man that wandereth from his place.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּ֭צִפּוֹר נוֹדֶ֣דֶת מִן־קִנָּ֑הּ כֵּֽן־אִ֝֗ישׁ נוֹדֵ֥ד מִמְּקוֹמֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a bird that wandereth from her nest, So is a man that wandereth from his place.
					\item[NIV] Like a bird that flees its nest is anyone who flees from home.
					\item[NRSV] Like a bird that strays from its nest is one who strays from home.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:9} % *PROVERBS-27-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-9}

			Ointment and perfume rejoice the heart: so doth the sweetness of a man’s friend by hearty counsel.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֶׁ֣מֶן וּ֭קְטֹרֶת יְשַׂמַּֽח־לֵ֑ב וּמֶ֥תֶק רֵ֝עֵ֗הוּ מֵֽעֲצַת־נָֽפֶשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Oil and perfume rejoice the heart; So doth the sweetness of a man's friend that cometh of hearty counsel.
					\item[NIV] Perfume and incense bring joy to the heart, and the pleasantness of a friend springs from their heartfelt advice.
					\item[NRSV] Perfume and incense make the heart glad, but the soul is torn by trouble.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:10} % *PROVERBS-27-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-10}

			Thine own friend, and thy father’s friend, forsake not; neither go into thy brother’s house in the day of thy calamity: for better is a neighbour that is near than a brother far off.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רֵֽעֲךָ֨ וְרֵ֪עַ אָבִ֡יךָ אַֽל־תַּעֲזֹ֗ב וּבֵ֥ית אָחִ֗יךָ אַל־תָּ֭בוֹא בְּי֣וֹם אֵידֶ֑ךָ ט֥וֹב שָׁכֵ֥ן קָ֝ר֗וֹב מֵאָ֥ח רָחֽוֹק׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Thine own friend, and thy father's friend, forsake not; And go not to thy brother's house in the day of thy calamity: Better is a neighbor that is near than a brother far off.
					\item[NIV] Do not forsake your friend or a friend of your family, and do not go to your relative’s house when disaster strikes you— better a neighbor nearby than a relative far away.
					\item[NRSV] Do not forsake your friend or the friend of your parent; do not go to the house of your kindred in the day of your calamity. Better is a neighbor who is nearby than kindred who are far away.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:11} % *PROVERBS-27-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-11}

			My son, be wise, and make my heart glad, that I may answer him that reproacheth me.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֲכַ֣ם בְּ֭נִי וְשַׂמַּ֣ח לִבִּ֑י וְאָשִׁ֖יבָה חֹרְפִ֣י דָבָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] My son, be wise, and make my heart glad, That I may answer him that reproacheth me.
					\item[NIV] Be wise, my son, and bring joy to my heart; then I can answer anyone who treats me with contempt.
					\item[NRSV] Be wise, my child, and make my heart glad, so that I may answer whoever reproaches me.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:12} % *PROVERBS-27-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-12}

			A prudent man foreseeth the evil, and hideth himself; but the simple pass on, and are punished.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עָר֤וּם רָאָ֣ה רָעָ֣ה נִסְתָּ֑ר פְּ֝תָאיִ֗ם עָבְר֥וּ נֶעֱנָֽשׁוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A prudent man seeth the evil, and hideth himself; But the simple pass on, and suffer for it.
					\item[NIV] The prudent see danger and take refuge, but the simple keep going and pay the penalty.
					\item[NRSV] The clever see danger and hide; but the simple go on, and suffer for it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:13} % *PROVERBS-27-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-13}

			Take his garment that is surety for a stranger, and take a pledge of him for a strange woman.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קַח־בִּ֭גְדוֹ כִּי־עָ֣רַב זָ֑ר וּבְעַ֖ד נָכְרִיָּ֣ה חַבְלֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Take his garment that is surety for a stranger; And hold him in pledge that is surety for a foreign woman.
					\item[NIV] Take the garment of one who puts up security for a stranger; hold it in pledge if it is done for an outsider.
					\item[NRSV] Take the garment of one who has given surety for a stranger; seize the pledge given as surety for foreigners.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:14} % *PROVERBS-27-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-14}

			He that blesseth his friend with a loud voice, rising early in the morning, it shall be counted a curse to him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְבָ֘רֵ֤ךְ רֵעֵ֨הוּ ׀ בְּק֣וֹל גָּ֭דוֹל בַּבֹּ֣קֶר הַשְׁכֵּ֑ים קְ֝לָלָ֗ה תֵּחָ֥שֶׁב לֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that blesseth his friend with a loud voice, rising early in the morning, It shall be counted a curse to him.
					\item[NIV] If anyone loudly blesses their neighbor early in the morning, it will be taken as a curse.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever blesses a neighbor with a loud voice, rising early in the morning, will be counted as cursing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:15} % *PROVERBS-27-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-15}

			A continual dropping in a very rainy day and a contentious woman are alike.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דֶּ֣לֶף ט֭וֹרֵד בְּי֣וֹם סַגְרִ֑יר וְאֵ֥שֶׁת מִ֝דְיָנִ֗ים נִשְׁתָּוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A continual dropping in a very rainy day And a contentious woman are alike:
					\item[NIV] A quarrelsome wife is like the dripping of a leaky roof in a rainstorm;
					\item[NRSV] A continual dripping on a rainy day and a contentious wife are alike;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:16} % *PROVERBS-27-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-16}

			Whosoever hideth her hideth the wind, and the ointment of his right hand, which bewrayeth itself.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צֹפְנֶ֥יהָ צָֽפַן־ר֑וּחַ וְשֶׁ֖מֶן יְמִינ֣וֹ יִקְרָֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that would restrain her restraineth the wind; And his right hand encountereth oil.
					\item[NIV] restraining her is like restraining the wind or grasping oil with the hand.
					\item[NRSV] to restrain her is to restrain the wind or to grasp oil in the right hand.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:17} % *PROVERBS-27-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-17}

			Iron sharpeneth iron; so a man sharpeneth the countenance of his friend.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּרְזֶ֣ל בְּבַרְזֶ֣ל יָ֑חַד וְ֝אִ֗ישׁ יַ֣חַד פְּנֵֽי־רֵעֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Iron sharpeneth iron; So a man sharpeneth the countenance of his friend.
					\item[NIV] As iron sharpens iron, so one person sharpens another.
					\item[NRSV] Iron sharpens iron, and one person sharpens the wits of another.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:18} % *PROVERBS-27-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-18}

			Whoso keepeth the fig tree shall eat the fruit thereof: so he that waiteth on his master shall be honoured.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נֹצֵ֣ר תְּ֭אֵנָה יֹאכַ֣ל פִּרְיָ֑הּ וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣יו יְכֻבָּֽד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso keepeth the fig-tree shall eat the fruit thereof; And he that regardeth his master shall be honored.
					\item[NIV] The one who guards a fig tree will eat its fruit, and whoever protects their master will be honored.
					\item[NRSV] Anyone who tends a fig tree will eat its fruit, and anyone who takes care of a master will be honored.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:19} % *PROVERBS-27-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-19}

			As in water face answereth to face, so the heart of man to man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּ֭מַּיִם הַפָּנִ֣ים לַפָּנִ֑ים כֵּ֤ן לֵֽב־הָ֝אָדָ֗ם לָאָדָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As in water face answereth to face, So the heart of man to man.
					\item[NIV] As water reflects the face, so one’s life reflects the heart.
					\item[NRSV] Just as water reflects the face, so one human heart reflects another.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:20} % *PROVERBS-27-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-20}

			Hell and destruction are never full; so the eyes of man are never satisfied.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁא֣וֹל וַ֭אֲבַדּוֹ לֹ֣א תִשְׂבַּ֑עְנָה וְעֵינֵ֥י הָ֝אָדָ֗ם לֹ֣א תִשְׂבַּֽעְנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Sheol and Abaddon are never satisfied; And the eyes of man are never satisfied.
					\item[NIV] Death and Destruction are never satisfied, and neither are human eyes.
					\item[NRSV] Sheol and Abaddon are never satisfied, and human eyes are never satisfied.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:21} % *PROVERBS-27-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-21}

			As the fining pot for silver, and the furnace for gold; so is a man to his praise.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַצְרֵ֣ף לַ֭כֶּסֶף וְכ֣וּר לַזָּהָ֑ב וְ֝אִ֗ישׁ לְפִ֣י מַהֲלָלֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The refining pot is for silver, and the furnace for gold; And a man is tried by his praise.
					\item[NIV] The crucible for silver and the furnace for gold, but people are tested by their praise.
					\item[NRSV] The crucible is for silver, and the furnace is for gold, so a person is tested by being praised.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:22} % *PROVERBS-27-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-22}

			Though thou shouldest bray a fool in a mortar among wheat with a pestle, yet will not his foolishness depart from him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֥ם תִּכְתּֽוֹשׁ־אֶת־הָאֱוִ֨יל ׀ בַּֽמַּכְתֵּ֡שׁ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָ֭רִיפוֹת בַּֽעֱלִ֑י לֹא־תָס֥וּר מֵ֝עָלָ֗יו אִוַּלְתּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Though thou shouldest bray a fool in a mortar with a pestle along with bruised grain, Yet will not his foolishness depart from him.
					\item[NIV] Though you grind a fool in a mortar, grinding them like grain with a pestle, you will not remove their folly from them.
					\item[NRSV] Crush a fool in a mortar with a pestle along with crushed grain, but the folly will not be driven out.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:23} % *PROVERBS-27-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-23}

			Be thou diligent to know the state of thy flocks, and look well to thy herds.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָדֹ֣עַ תֵּ֭דַע פְּנֵ֣י צֹאנֶ֑ךָ שִׁ֥ית לִ֝בְּךָ֗ לַעֲדָרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Be thou diligent to know the state of thy flocks, And look well to thy herds:
					\item[NIV] Be sure you know the condition of your flocks, give careful attention to your herds;
					\item[NRSV] Know well the condition of your flocks, and give attention to your herds;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:24} % *PROVERBS-27-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-24}

			For riches are not for ever: and doth the crown endure to every generation?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י לֹ֣א לְעוֹלָ֣ם חֹ֑סֶן וְאִם־נֵ֝֗זֶר לְד֣וֹר וָדֽוֹר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For riches are not for ever: And doth the crown endure unto all generations?
					\item[NIV] for riches do not endure forever, and a crown is not secure for all generations.
					\item[NRSV] for riches do not last forever, nor a crown for all generations.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:25} % *PROVERBS-27-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-25}

			The hay appeareth, and the tender grass sheweth itself, and herbs of the mountains are gathered.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גָּלָ֣ה חָ֭צִיר וְנִרְאָה־דֶ֑שֶׁא וְ֝נֶאֶסְפ֗וּ עִשְּׂב֥וֹת הָרִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The hay is carried, and the tender grass showeth itself, And the herbs of the mountains are gathered in.
					\item[NIV] When the hay is removed and new growth appears and the grass from the hills is gathered in,
					\item[NRSV] When the grass is gone, and new growth appears, and the herbage of the mountains is gathered,
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:26} % *PROVERBS-27-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-26}

			The lambs are for thy clothing, and the goats are the price of the field.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כְּבָשִׂ֥ים לִלְבוּשֶׁ֑ךָ וּמְחִ֥יר שָׂ֝דֶ֗ה עַתּוּדִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The lambs are for thy clothing, And the goats are the price of the field;
					\item[NIV] the lambs will provide you with clothing, and the goats with the price of a field.
					\item[NRSV] the lambs will provide your clothing, and the goats the price of a field;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 27:27} % *PROVERBS-27-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-27-27}

			And thou shalt have goats’ milk enough for thy food, for the food of thy household, and for the maintenance for thy maidens.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְדֵ֤י ׀ חֲלֵ֬ב עִזִּ֗ים לְֽ֭לַחְמְךָ לְלֶ֣חֶם בֵּיתֶ֑ךָ וְ֝חַיִּ֗ים לְנַעֲרוֹתֶֽיךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] And there will be goats' milk enough for thy food, for the food of thy household, And maintenance for thy maidens.
					\item[NIV] You will have plenty of goats’ milk to feed your family and to nourish your female servants.
					\item[NRSV] there will be enough goats’ milk for your food, for the food of your household and nourishment for your servant-girls.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 28} % *PROVERBS-28
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-28}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:1} % *PROVERBS-28-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-1}

			The wicked flee when no man pursueth: but the righteous are bold as a lion.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נָ֣סוּ וְאֵין־רֹדֵ֣ף רָשָׁ֑ע וְ֝צַדִּיקִ֗ים כִּכְפִ֥יר יִבְטָֽח׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The wicked flee when no man pursueth; But the righteous are bold as a lion.
					\item[NIV] The wicked flee though no one pursues, but the righteous are as bold as a lion.
					\item[NRSV] The wicked flee when no one pursues, but the righteous are as bold as a lion.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:2} % *PROVERBS-28-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-2}

			For the transgression of a land many are the princes thereof: but by a man of understanding and knowledge the state thereof shall be prolonged.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּפֶ֣שַֽׁע אֶ֭רֶץ רַבִּ֣ים שָׂרֶ֑יהָ וּבְאָדָ֥ם מֵבִ֥ין יֹ֝דֵ֗עַ כֵּ֣ן יַאֲרִֽיךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] For the transgression of a land many are the princes thereof; But by men of understanding and knowledge the state thereof shall be prolonged.
					\item[NIV] When a country is rebellious, it has many rulers, but a ruler with discernment and knowledge maintains order.
					\item[NRSV] When a land rebels it has many rulers; but with an intelligent ruler there is lasting order.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:3} % *PROVERBS-28-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-3}

			A poor man that oppresseth the poor is like a sweeping rain which leaveth no food.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גֶּ֣בֶר רָ֭שׁ וְעֹשֵׁ֣ק דַּלִּ֑ים מָטָ֥ר סֹ֝חֵ֗ף וְאֵ֣ין לָֽחֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A needy man that oppresseth the poor Is like a sweeping rain which leaveth no food.
					\item[NIV] A ruler who oppresses the poor is like a driving rain that leaves no crops.
					\item[NRSV] A ruler who oppresses the poor is a beating rain that leaves no food.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:4} % *PROVERBS-28-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-4}

			They that forsake the law praise the wicked: but such as keep the law contend with them.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹזְבֵ֣י ת֭וֹרָה יְהַֽלְל֣וּ רָשָׁ֑ע וְשֹׁמְרֵ֥י ת֝וֹרָ֗ה יִתְגָּ֥רוּ בָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] They that forsake the law praise the wicked; But such as keep the law contend with them.
					\item[NIV] Those who forsake instruction praise the wicked, but those who heed it resist them.
					\item[NRSV] Those who forsake the law praise the wicked, but those who keep the law struggle against them.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:5} % *PROVERBS-28-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-5}

			Evil men understand not judgment: but they that seek the LORD understand all things.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַנְשֵׁי־רָ֭ע לֹא־יָבִ֣ינוּ מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּמְבַקְשֵׁ֥י יְ֝הוָ֗ה יָבִ֥ינוּ כֹֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Evil men understand not justice; But they that seek Jehovah understand all things.
					\item[NIV] Evildoers do not understand what is right, but those who seek the LORD understand it fully.
					\item[NRSV] The evil do not understand justice, but those who seek the LORD understand it completely.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:6} % *PROVERBS-28-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-6}

			Better is the poor that walketh in his uprightness, than he that is perverse in his ways, though he be rich.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טֽוֹב־רָ֭שׁ הוֹלֵ֣ךְ בְּתֻמּ֑וֹ מֵעִקֵּ֥שׁ דְּ֝רָכַ֗יִם וְה֣וּא עָשִֽׁיר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Better is the poor that walketh in his integrity, Than he that is perverse in his ways, though he be rich.
					\item[NIV] Better the poor whose walk is blameless than the rich whose ways are perverse.
					\item[NRSV] Better to be poor and walk in integrity than to be crooked in one’s ways even though rich.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:7} % *PROVERBS-28-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-7}

			Whoso keepeth the law is a wise son: but he that is a companion of riotous men shameth his father.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נוֹצֵ֣ר תּ֭וֹרָה בֵּ֣ן מֵבִ֑ין וְרֹעֶה זֽ֝וֹלְלִ֗ים יַכְלִ֥ים אָבִֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso keepeth the law is a wise son; But he that is a companion of gluttons shameth his father.
					\item[NIV] A discerning son heeds instruction, but a companion of gluttons disgraces his father.
					\item[NRSV] Those who keep the law are wise children, but companions of gluttons shame their parents.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:8} % *PROVERBS-28-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-8}

			He that by usury and unjust gain increaseth his substance, he shall gather it for him that will pity the poor.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַרְבֶּ֣ה ה֭וֹנוֹ בְּנֶ֣שֶׁךְ וְתַרְבִּ֑ית לְחוֹנֵ֖ן דַּלִּ֣ים יִקְבְּצֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that augmenteth his substance by interest and increase, Gathereth it for him that hath pity on the poor.
					\item[NIV] Whoever increases wealth by taking interest or profit from the poor amasses it for another, who will be kind to the poor.
					\item[NRSV] One who augments wealth by exorbitant interest gathers it for another who is kind to the poor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:9} % *PROVERBS-28-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-9}

			He that turneth away his ear from hearing the law, even his prayer shall be abomination.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֵסִ֣יר אָ֭זְנוֹ מִשְּׁמֹ֣עַ תּוֹרָ֑ה גַּֽם־תְּ֝פִלָּת֗וֹ תּוֹעֵבָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that turneth away his ear from hearing the law, Even his prayer is an abomination.
					\item[NIV] If anyone turns a deaf ear to my instruction, even their prayers are detestable.
					\item[NRSV] When one will not listen to the law, even one’s prayers are an abomination.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:10} % *PROVERBS-28-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-10}

			Whoso causeth the righteous to go astray in an evil way, he shall fall himself into his own pit: but the upright shall have good things in possession.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַשְׁגֶּ֤ה יְשָׁרִ֨ים ׀ בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ רָ֗ע בִּשְׁחוּת֥וֹ הֽוּא־יִפּ֑וֹל וּ֝תְמִימִ֗ים יִנְחֲלוּ־טֽוֹב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso causeth the upright to go astray in an evil way, He shall fall himself into his own pit; But the perfect shall inherit good.
					\item[NIV] Whoever leads the upright along an evil path will fall into their own trap, but the blameless will receive a good inheritance.
					\item[NRSV] Those who mislead the upright into evil ways will fall into pits of their own making, but the blameless will have a goodly inheritance.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:11} % *PROVERBS-28-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-11}

			The rich man is wise in his own conceit; but the poor that hath understanding searcheth him out.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָכָ֣ם בְּ֭עֵינָיו אִ֣ישׁ עָשִׁ֑יר וְדַ֖ל מֵבִ֣ין יַחְקְרֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The rich man is wise in his own conceit; But the poor that hath understanding searcheth him out.
					\item[NIV] The rich are wise in their own eyes; one who is poor and discerning sees how deluded they are.
					\item[NRSV] The rich is wise in self-esteem, but an intelligent poor person sees through the pose.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:12} % *PROVERBS-28-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-12}

			When righteous men do rejoice, there is great glory: but when the wicked rise, a man is hidden.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בַּעֲלֹ֣ץ צַ֭דִּיקִים רַבָּ֣ה תִפְאָ֑רֶת וּבְק֥וּם רְ֝שָׁעִ֗ים יְחֻפַּ֥שׂ אָדָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the righteous triumph, there is great glory; But when the wicked rise, men hide themselves.
					\item[NIV] When the righteous triumph, there is great elation; but when the wicked rise to power, people go into hiding.
					\item[NRSV] When the righteous triumph, there is great glory, but when the wicked prevail, people go into hiding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:13} % *PROVERBS-28-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-13}

			He that covereth his sins shall not prosper: but whoso confesseth and forsaketh them shall have mercy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְכַסֶּ֣ה פְ֭שָׁעָיו לֹ֣א יַצְלִ֑יחַ וּמוֹדֶ֖ה וְעֹזֵ֣ב יְרֻחָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that covereth his transgressions shall not prosper: But whoso confesseth and forsaketh them shall obtain mercy.
					\item[NIV] Whoever conceals their sins does not prosper, but the one who confesses and renounces them finds mercy.
					\item[NRSV] No one who conceals transgressions will prosper, but one who confesses and forsakes them will obtain mercy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:14} % *PROVERBS-28-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-14}

			Happy is the man that feareth alway: but he that hardeneth his heart shall fall into mischief.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַשְׁרֵ֣י אָ֭דָם מְפַחֵ֣ד תָּמִ֑יד וּמַקְשֶׁ֥ה לִ֝בּ֗וֹ יִפּ֥וֹל בְּרָעָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Happy is the man that feareth alway; But he that hardeneth his heart shall fall into mischief.
					\item[NIV] Blessed is the one who always trembles before God, but whoever hardens their heart falls into trouble.
					\item[NRSV] Happy is the one who is never without fear, but one who is hard-hearted will fall into calamity.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:15} % *PROVERBS-28-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-15}

			As a roaring lion, and a ranging bear; so is a wicked ruler over the poor people.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֲרִי־נֹ֭הֵם וְדֹ֣ב שׁוֹקֵ֑ק מֹשֵׁ֥ל רָ֝שָׁ֗ע עַ֣ל עַם־דָּֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] As a roaring lion, and a ranging bear, So is a wicked ruler over a poor people.
					\item[NIV] Like a roaring lion or a charging bear is a wicked ruler over a helpless people.
					\item[NRSV] Like a roaring lion or a charging bear is a wicked ruler over a poor people.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:16} % *PROVERBS-28-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-16}

			The prince that wanteth understanding is also a great oppressor: but he that hateth covetousness shall prolong his days.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נָגִ֗יד חֲסַ֣ר תְּ֭בוּנוֹת וְרַ֥ב מַעֲשַׁקּ֑וֹת שֹׂ֥נֵא בֶ֝֗צַע יַאֲרִ֥יךְ יָמִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The prince that lacketh understanding is also a great oppressor; But he that hateth covetousness shall prolong his days.
					\item[NIV] A tyrannical ruler practices extortion, but one who hates ill-gotten gain will enjoy a long reign.
					\item[NRSV] A ruler who lacks understanding is a cruel oppressor; but one who hates unjust gain will enjoy a long life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:17} % *PROVERBS-28-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-17}

			A man that doeth violence to the blood of any person shall flee to the pit; let no man stay him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אָ֭דָם עָשֻׁ֣ק בְּדַם־נָפֶשׁ עַד־בּ֥וֹר יָ֝נ֗וּס אַל־יִתְמְכוּ־בֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man that is laden with the blood of any person Shall flee unto the pit; let no man stay him.
					\item[NIV] Anyone tormented by the guilt of murder will seek refuge in the grave; let no one hold them back.
					\item[NRSV] If someone is burdened with the blood of another, let that killer be a fugitive until death; let no one offer assistance.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:18} % *PROVERBS-28-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-18}

			Whoso walketh uprightly shall be saved: but he that is perverse in his ways shall fall at once.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הוֹלֵ֣ךְ תָּ֭מִים יִוָּשֵׁ֑עַ וְנֶעְקַ֥שׁ דְּ֝רָכַ֗יִם יִפּ֥וֹל בְּאֶחָֽת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso walketh uprightly shall be delivered; But he that is perverse in his ways shall fall at once.
					\item[NIV] The one whose walk is blameless is kept safe, but the one whose ways are perverse will fall into the pit.
					\item[NRSV] One who walks in integrity will be safe, but whoever follows crooked ways will fall into the Pit.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:19} % *PROVERBS-28-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-19}

			He that tilleth his land shall have plenty of bread: but he that followeth after vain persons shall have poverty enough.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹבֵ֣ד אַ֭דְמָתוֹ יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־לָ֑חֶם וּמְרַדֵּ֥ף רֵ֝קִ֗ים יִֽשְׂבַּֽע־רִֽישׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that tilleth his land shall have plenty of bread; But he that followeth after vain persons shall have poverty enough.
					\item[NIV] Those who work their land will have abundant food, but those who chase fantasies will have their fill of poverty.
					\item[NRSV] Anyone who tills the land will have plenty of bread, but one who follows worthless pursuits will have plenty of poverty.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:20} % *PROVERBS-28-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-20}

			A faithful man shall abound with blessings: but he that maketh haste to be rich shall not be innocent.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ אֱ֭מוּנוֹת רַב־בְּרָכ֑וֹת וְאָ֥ץ לְ֝הַעֲשִׁ֗יר לֹ֣א יִנָּקֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A faithful man shall abound with blessings; But he that maketh haste to be rich shall not be unpunished.
					\item[NIV] A faithful person will be richly blessed, but one eager to get rich will not go unpunished.
					\item[NRSV] The faithful will abound with blessings, but one who is in a hurry to be rich will not go unpunished.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:21} % *PROVERBS-28-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-21}

			To have respect of persons is not good: for for a piece of bread that man will transgress.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הַֽכֵּר־פָּנִ֥ים לֹא־ט֑וֹב וְעַל־פַּת־לֶ֝֗חֶם יִפְשַׁע־גָּֽבֶר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] To have respect of persons is not good; Neither that a man should transgress for a piece of bread.
					\item[NIV] To show partiality is not good— yet a person will do wrong for a piece of bread.
					\item[NRSV] To show partiality is not good— yet for a piece of bread a person may do wrong.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:22} % *PROVERBS-28-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-22}

			He that hasteth to be rich hath an evil eye, and considereth not that poverty shall come upon him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נִֽבֳהָ֥ל לַה֗וֹן אִ֭ישׁ רַ֣ע עָ֑יִן וְלֹֽא־יֵ֝דַע כִּי־חֶ֥סֶר יְבֹאֶֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that hath an evil eye hasteth after riches, And knoweth not that want shall come upon him.
					\item[NIV] The stingy are eager to get rich and are unaware that poverty awaits them.
					\item[NRSV] The miser is in a hurry to get rich and does not know that loss is sure to come.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:23} % *PROVERBS-28-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-23}

			He that rebuketh a man afterwards shall find more favour than he that flattereth with the tongue.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מ֘וֹכִ֤יחַ אָדָ֣ם אַ֭חֲרַי חֵ֣ן יִמְצָ֑א מִֽמַּחֲלִ֥יק לָשֽׁוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that rebuketh a man shall afterward find more favor Than he that flattereth with the tongue.
					\item[NIV] Whoever rebukes a person will in the end gain favor rather than one who has a flattering tongue.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever rebukes a person will afterward find more favor than one who flatters with the tongue.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:24} % *PROVERBS-28-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-24}

			Whoso robbeth his father or his mother, and saith, It is no transgression; the same is the companion of a destroyer.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גּוֹזֵ֤ל ׀ אָ֘בִ֤יו וְאִמּ֗וֹ וְאֹמֵ֥ר אֵֽין־פָּ֑שַׁע חָבֵ֥ר ה֝֗וּא לְאִ֣ישׁ מַשְׁחִֽית׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso robbeth his father or his mother, and saith, It is no transgression, The same is the companion of a destroyer.
					\item[NIV] Whoever robs their father or mother and says, “It’s not wrong,” is partner to one who destroys.
					\item[NRSV] Anyone who robs father or mother and says, “That is no crime,” is partner to a thug.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:25} % *PROVERBS-28-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-25}

			He that is of a proud heart stirreth up strife: but he that putteth his trust in the LORD shall be made fat.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רְחַב־נֶ֭פֶשׁ יְגָרֶ֣ה מָד֑וֹן וּבוֹטֵ֖חַ עַל־יְהוָ֣ה יְדֻשָּֽׁן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that is of a greedy spirit stirreth up strife; But he that putteth his trust in Jehovah shall be made fat.
					\item[NIV] The greedy stir up conflict, but those who trust in the LORD will prosper.
					\item[NRSV] The greedy person stirs up strife, but whoever trusts in the LORD will be enriched.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:26} % *PROVERBS-28-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-26}

			He that trusteth in his own heart is a fool: but whoso walketh wisely, he shall be delivered.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בּוֹטֵ֣חַ בְּ֭לִבּוֹ ה֣וּא כְסִ֑יל וְהוֹלֵ֥ךְ בְּ֝חָכְמָ֗ה ה֣וּא יִמָּלֵֽט׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that trusteth in his own heart is a fool; But whoso walketh wisely, he shall be delivered.
					\item[NIV] Those who trust in themselves are fools, but those who walk in wisdom are kept safe.
					\item[NRSV] Those who trust in their own wits are fools; but those who walk in wisdom come through safely.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:27} % *PROVERBS-28-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-27}

			He that giveth unto the poor shall not lack: but he that hideth his eyes shall have many a curse.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נוֹתֵ֣ן לָ֭רָשׁ אֵ֣ין מַחְס֑וֹר וּמַעְלִ֥ים עֵ֝ינָ֗יו רַב־מְאֵרֽוֹת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that giveth unto the poor shall not lack; But he that hideth his eyes shall have many a curse.
					\item[NIV] Those who give to the poor will lack nothing, but those who close their eyes to them receive many curses.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever gives to the poor will lack nothing, but one who turns a blind eye will get many a curse.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 28:28} % *PROVERBS-28-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-28-28}

			When the wicked rise, men hide themselves: but when they perish, the righteous increase.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּק֣וּם רְ֭שָׁעִים יִסָּתֵ֣ר אָדָ֑ם וּ֝בְאָבְדָ֗ם יִרְבּ֥וּ צַדִּיקִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the wicked rise, men hide themselves; But when they perish, the righteous increase.
					\item[NIV] When the wicked rise to power, people go into hiding; but when the wicked perish, the righteous thrive.
					\item[NRSV] When the wicked prevail, people go into hiding; but when they perish, the righteous increase.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 29} % *PROVERBS-29
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-29}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:1} % *PROVERBS-29-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-1}

			He, that being often reproved hardeneth his neck, shall suddenly be destroyed, and that without remedy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִ֣ישׁ תּ֭וֹכָחוֹת מַקְשֶׁה־עֹ֑רֶף פֶּ֥תַע יִ֝שָּׁבֵ֗ר וְאֵ֣ין מַרְפֵּֽא׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that being often reproved hardeneth his neck Shall suddenly be destroyed, and that without remedy
					\item[NIV] Whoever remains stiff-necked after many rebukes will suddenly be destroyed—without remedy.
					\item[NRSV] One who is often reproved, yet remains stubborn, will suddenly be broken beyond healing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:2} % *PROVERBS-29-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-2}

			When the righteous are in authority, the people rejoice: but when the wicked beareth rule, the people mourn.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּרְב֣וֹת צַ֭דִּיקִים יִשְׂמַ֣ח הָעָ֑ם וּבִמְשֹׁ֥ל רָ֝שָׁ֗ע יֵאָ֥נַֽח עָֽם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the righteous are increased, the people rejoice; But when a wicked man beareth rule, the people sigh.
					\item[NIV] When the righteous thrive, the people rejoice; when the wicked rule, the people groan.
					\item[NRSV] When the righteous are in authority, the people rejoice; but when the wicked rule, the people groan.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:3} % *PROVERBS-29-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-3}

			Whoso loveth wisdom rejoiceth his father: but he that keepeth company with harlots spendeth his substance.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִֽישׁ־אֹהֵ֣ב חָ֭כְמָה יְשַׂמַּ֣ח אָבִ֑יו וְרֹעֶ֥ה ז֝וֹנ֗וֹת יְאַבֶּד־הֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso loveth wisdom rejoiceth his father; But he that keepeth company with harlots wasteth his substance.
