					\item[NIV] A man who loves wisdom brings joy to his father, but a companion of prostitutes squanders his wealth.
					\item[NRSV] A child who loves wisdom makes a parent glad, but to keep company with prostitutes is to squander one’s substance.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:4} % *PROVERBS-29-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-4}

			The king by judgment establisheth the land: but he that receiveth gifts overthroweth it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֶ֗לֶךְ בְּ֭מִשְׁפָּט יַעֲמִ֣יד אָ֑רֶץ וְאִ֖ישׁ תְּרוּמ֣וֹת יֶֽהֶרְסֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The king by justice establisheth the land; But he that exacteth gifts overthroweth it.
					\item[NIV] By justice a king gives a country stability, but those who are greedy for bribes tear it down.
					\item[NRSV] By justice a king gives stability to the land, but one who makes heavy exactions ruins it.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:5} % *PROVERBS-29-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-5}

			A man that flattereth his neighbour spreadeth a net for his feet.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גֶּ֭בֶר מַחֲלִ֣יק עַל־רֵעֵ֑הוּ רֶ֝֗שֶׁת פּוֹרֵ֥שׂ עַל־פְּעָמָֽיו׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man that flattereth his neighbor Spreadeth a net for his steps.
					\item[NIV] Those who flatter their neighbors are spreading nets for their feet.
					\item[NRSV] Whoever flatters a neighbor is spreading a net for the neighbor’s feet.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:6} % *PROVERBS-29-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-6}

			In the transgression of an evil man there is a snare: but the righteous doth sing and rejoice.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּפֶ֤שַֽׁע אִ֣ישׁ רָ֣ע מוֹקֵ֑שׁ וְ֝צַדִּ֗יק יָר֥וּן וְשָׂמֵֽחַ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] In the transgression of an evil man there is a snare; But the righteous doth sing and rejoice.
					\item[NIV] Evildoers are snared by their own sin, but the righteous shout for joy and are glad.
					\item[NRSV] In the transgression of the evil there is a snare, but the righteous sing and rejoice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:7} % *PROVERBS-29-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-7}

			The righteous considereth the cause of the poor: but the wicked regardeth not to know it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יֹדֵ֣עַ צַ֭דִּיק דִּ֣ין דַּלִּ֑ים רָ֝שָׁ֗ע לֹא־יָבִ֥ין דָּֽעַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The righteous taketh knowledge of the cause of the poor; The wicked hath not understanding to know it.
					\item[NIV] The righteous care about justice for the poor, but the wicked have no such concern.
					\item[NRSV] The righteous know the rights of the poor; the wicked have no such understanding.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:8} % *PROVERBS-29-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-8}

			Scornful men bring a city into a snare: but wise men turn away wrath.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַנְשֵׁ֣י לָ֭צוֹן יָפִ֣יחוּ קִרְיָ֑ה וַ֝חֲכָמִ֗ים יָשִׁ֥יבוּ אָֽף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Scoffers set a city in a flame; But wise men turn away wrath.
					\item[NIV] Mockers stir up a city, but the wise turn away anger.
					\item[NRSV] Scoffers set a city aflame, but the wise turn away wrath.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:9} % *PROVERBS-29-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-9}

			If a wise man contendeth with a foolish man, whether he rage or laugh, there is no rest.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִֽישׁ־חָכָ֗ם נִ֭שְׁפָּט אֶת־אִ֣ישׁ אֱוִ֑יל וְרָגַ֥ז וְ֝שָׂחַ֗ק וְאֵ֣ין נָֽחַת׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If a wise man hath a controversy with a foolish man, Whether he be angry or laugh, there will be no rest.
					\item[NIV] If a wise person goes to court with a fool, the fool rages and scoffs, and there is no peace.
					\item[NRSV] If the wise go to law with fools, there is ranting and ridicule without relief.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:10} % *PROVERBS-29-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-10}

			The bloodthirsty hate the upright: but the just seek his soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַנְשֵׁ֣י דָ֭מִים יִשְׂנְאוּ־תָ֑ם וִֽ֝ישָׁרִ֗ים יְבַקְשׁ֥וּ נַפְשֽׁוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The bloodthirsty hate him that is perfect; And as for the upright, they seek his life.
					\item[NIV] The bloodthirsty hate a person of integrity and seek to kill the upright.
					\item[NRSV] The bloodthirsty hate the blameless, and they seek the life of the upright.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:11} % *PROVERBS-29-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-11}

			A fool uttereth all his mind: but a wise man keepeth it in till afterwards.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־ר֭וּחוֹ יוֹצִ֣יא כְסִ֑יל וְ֝חָכָ֗ם בְּאָח֥וֹר יְשַׁבְּחֶֽנָּה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A fool uttereth all his anger; But a wise man keepeth it back and stilleth it.
					\item[NIV] Fools give full vent to their rage, but the wise bring calm in the end.
					\item[NRSV] A fool gives full vent to anger, but the wise quietly holds it back.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:12} % *PROVERBS-29-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-12}

			If a ruler hearken to lies, all his servants are wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֹ֭שֵׁל מַקְשִׁ֣יב עַל־דְּבַר־שָׁ֑קֶר כָּֽל־מְשָׁרְתָ֥יו רְשָׁעִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] If a ruler hearkeneth to falsehood, All his servants are wicked.
					\item[NIV] If a ruler listens to lies, all his officials become wicked.
					\item[NRSV] If a ruler listens to falsehood, all his officials will be wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:13} % *PROVERBS-29-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-13}

			The poor and the deceitful man meet together: the LORD lighteneth both their eyes.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רָ֤שׁ וְאִ֣ישׁ תְּכָכִ֣ים נִפְגָּ֑שׁוּ מֵ֤אִיר־עֵינֵ֖י שְׁנֵיהֶ֣ם יְהוָֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The poor man and the oppressor meet together; Jehovah lighteneth the eyes of them both.
					\item[NIV] The poor and the oppressor have this in common: The LORD gives sight to the eyes of both.
					\item[NRSV] The poor and the oppressor have this in common: the LORD gives light to the eyes of both.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:14} % *PROVERBS-29-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-14}

			The king that faithfully judgeth the poor, his throne shall be established for ever.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מֶ֤לֶךְ שׁוֹפֵ֣ט בֶּֽאֱמֶ֣ת דַּלִּ֑ים כִּ֝סְא֗וֹ לָעַ֥ד יִכּֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The king that faithfully judgeth the poor, His throne shall be established for ever.
					\item[NIV] If a king judges the poor with fairness, his throne will be established forever.
					\item[NRSV] If a king judges the poor with equity, his throne will be established forever.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:15} % *PROVERBS-29-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-15}

			The rod and reproof give wisdom: but a child left to himself bringeth his mother to shame.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֵׁ֣בֶט וְ֭תוֹכַחַת יִתֵּ֣ן חָכְמָ֑ה וְנַ֥עַר מְ֝שֻׁלָּ֗ח מֵבִ֥ישׁ אִמּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The rod and reproof give wisdom; But a child left to himself causeth shame to his mother.
					\item[NIV] A rod and a reprimand impart wisdom, but a child left undisciplined disgraces its mother.
					\item[NRSV] The rod and reproof give wisdom, but a mother is disgraced by a neglected child.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:16} % *PROVERBS-29-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-16}

			When the wicked are multiplied, transgression increaseth: but the righteous shall see their fall.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּרְב֣וֹת רְ֭שָׁעִים יִרְבֶּה־פָּ֑שַׁע וְ֝צַדִּיקִ֗ים בְּֽמַפַּלְתָּ֥ם יִרְאֽוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] When the wicked are increased, transgression increaseth; But the righteous shall look upon their fall.
					\item[NIV] When the wicked thrive, so does sin, but the righteous will see their downfall.
					\item[NRSV] When the wicked are in authority, transgression increases, but the righteous will look upon their downfall.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:17} % *PROVERBS-29-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-17}

			Correct thy son, and he shall give thee rest; yea, he shall give delight unto thy soul.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יַסֵּ֣ר בִּ֭נְךָ וִֽינִיחֶ֑ךָ וְיִתֵּ֖ן מַעֲדַנִּ֣ים לְנַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Correct thy son, and he will give thee rest; Yea, he will give delight unto thy soul.
					\item[NIV] Discipline your children, and they will give you peace; they will bring you the delights you desire.
					\item[NRSV] Discipline your children, and they will give you rest; they will give delight to your heart.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:18} % *PROVERBS-29-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-18}

			Where there is no vision, the people perish: but he that keepeth the law, happy is he.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בְּאֵ֣ין חָ֭זוֹן יִפָּ֣רַֽע עָ֑ם וְשֹׁמֵ֖ר תּוֹרָ֣ה אַשְׁרֵֽהוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Where there is no vision, the people cast off restraint; But he that keepeth the law, happy is he.
					\item[NIV] Where there is no revelation, people cast off restraint; but blessed is the one who heeds wisdom’s instruction.
					\item[NRSV] Where there is no prophecy, the people cast off restraint, but happy are those who keep the law.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:19} % *PROVERBS-29-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-19}

			A servant will not be corrected by words: for though he understand he will not answer.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בִּ֭דְבָרִים לֹא־יִוָּ֣סֶר עָ֑בֶד כִּֽי־יָ֝בִ֗ין וְאֵ֣ין מַעֲנֶֽה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A servant will not be corrected by words; For though he understand, he will not give heed.
					\item[NIV] Servants cannot be corrected by mere words; though they understand, they will not respond.
					\item[NRSV] By mere words servants are not disciplined, for though they understand, they will not give heed.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:20} % *PROVERBS-29-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-20}

			Seest thou a man that is hasty in his words? there is more hope of a fool than of him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָזִ֗יתָ אִ֭ישׁ אָ֣ץ בִּדְבָרָ֑יו תִּקְוָ֖ה לִכְסִ֣יל מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Seest thou a man that is hasty in his words? There is more hope of a fool than of him.
					\item[NIV] Do you see someone who speaks in haste? There is more hope for a fool than for them.
					\item[NRSV] Do you see someone who is hasty in speech? There is more hope for a fool than for anyone like that.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:21} % *PROVERBS-29-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-21}

			He that delicately bringeth up his servant from a child shall have him become his son at the length.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מְפַנֵּ֣ק מִנֹּ֣עַר עַבְדּ֑וֹ וְ֝אַחֲרִית֗וֹ יִהְיֶ֥ה מָנֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] He that delicately bringeth up his servant from a child Shall have him become a son at the last.
					\item[NIV] A servant pampered from youth will turn out to be insolent.
					\item[NRSV] A slave pampered from childhood will come to a bad end.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:22} % *PROVERBS-29-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-22}

			An angry man stirreth up strife, and a furious man aboundeth in transgression.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אִֽישׁ־אַ֭ף יְגָרֶ֣ה מָד֑וֹן וּבַ֖עַל חֵמָ֣ה רַב־פָּֽשַׁע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] An angry man stirreth up strife, And a wrathful man aboundeth in transgression.
					\item[NIV] An angry person stirs up conflict, and a hot-tempered person commits many sins.
					\item[NRSV] One given to anger stirs up strife, and the hothead causes much transgression.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:23} % *PROVERBS-29-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-23}

			A man’s pride shall bring him low: but honour shall uphold the humble in spirit.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גַּאֲוַ֣ת אָ֭דָם תַּשְׁפִּילֶ֑נּוּ וּשְׁפַל־ר֝֗וּחַ יִתְמֹ֥ךְ כָּבֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A man's pride shall bring him low; But he that is of a lowly spirit shall obtain honor.
					\item[NIV] Pride brings a person low, but the lowly in spirit gain honor.
					\item[NRSV] A person’s pride will bring humiliation, but one who is lowly in spirit will obtain honor.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:24} % *PROVERBS-29-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-24}

			Whoso is partner with a thief hateth his own soul: he heareth cursing, and bewrayeth it not.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חוֹלֵ֣ק עִם־גַּ֭נָּב שׂוֹנֵ֣א נַפְשׁ֑וֹ אָלָ֥ה יִ֝שְׁמַ֗ע וְלֹ֣א יַגִּֽיד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Whoso is partner with a thief hateth his own soul; He heareth the adjuration and uttereth nothing.
					\item[NIV] The accomplices of thieves are their own enemies; they are put under oath and dare not testify.
					\item[NRSV] To be a partner of a thief is to hate one’s own life; one hears the victim’s curse, but discloses nothing.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:25} % *PROVERBS-29-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-25}

			The fear of man bringeth a snare: but whoso putteth his trust in the LORD shall be safe.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חֶרְדַּ֣ת אָ֭דָם יִתֵּ֣ן מוֹקֵ֑שׁ וּבוֹטֵ֖חַ בַּיהוָ֣ה יְשֻׂגָּֽב׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The fear of man bringeth a snare; But whoso putteth his trust in Jehovah shall be safe.
					\item[NIV] Fear of man will prove to be a snare, but whoever trusts in the LORD is kept safe.
					\item[NRSV] The fear of others lays a snare, but one who trusts in the LORD is secure.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:26} % *PROVERBS-29-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-26}

			Many seek the ruler’s favour; but every man’s judgment cometh from the LORD.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַ֭בִּים מְבַקְשִׁ֣ים פְּנֵי־מוֹשֵׁ֑ל וּ֝מֵיְהוָ֗ה מִשְׁפַּט־אִֽישׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Many seek the ruler's favor; But a man's judgment cometh from Jehovah.
					\item[NIV] Many seek an audience with a ruler, but it is from the LORD that one gets justice.
					\item[NRSV] Many seek the favor of a ruler, but it is from the LORD that one gets justice.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 29:27} % *PROVERBS-29-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-29-27}

			An unjust man is an abomination to the just: and he that is upright in the way is abomination to the wicked.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תּוֹעֲבַ֣ת צַ֭דִּיקִים אִ֣ישׁ עָ֑וֶל וְתוֹעֲבַ֖ת רָשָׁ֣ע יְשַׁר־דָּֽרֶךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] An unjust man is an abomination to the righteous; And he that is upright in the way is an abomination to the wicked.
					\item[NIV] The righteous detest the dishonest; the wicked detest the upright.
					\item[NRSV] The unjust are an abomination to the righteous, but the upright are an abomination to the wicked.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
	% -----
	\subsubsection{Proverbs 30} % *PROVERBS-30
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-30}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:1} % *PROVERBS-30-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-1}

			The words of Agur the son of Jakeh, even the prophecy: the man spake unto Ithiel, even unto Ithiel and Ucal,

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּבְרֵ֤י ׀ אָג֥וּר בִּן־יָקֶ֗ה הַמַּ֫שָּׂ֥א נְאֻ֣ם הַ֭גֶּבֶר לְאִֽיתִיאֵ֑ל לְאִ֖יתִיאֵ֣ל וְאֻכָֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of Agur the son of Jakeh; the oracle. The man saith unto Ithiel, unto Ithiel and Ucal:
					\item[NIV] The sayings of Agur son of Jakeh—an inspired utterance. This man’s utterance to Ithiel: “I am weary, God, but I can prevail.
					\item[NRSV] The words of Agur son of Jakeh. An oracle. Thus says the man: I am weary, O God, I am weary, O God. How can I prevail?
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:2} % *PROVERBS-30-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-2}

			Surely I am more brutish than any man, and have not the understanding of a man.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י בַ֣עַר אָנֹכִ֣י מֵאִ֑ישׁ וְלֹֽא־בִינַ֖ת אָדָ֣ם לִֽי׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Surely I am more brutish than any man, And have not the understanding of a man;
					\item[NIV] Surely I am only a brute, not a man; I do not have human understanding.
					\item[NRSV] Surely I am too stupid to be human; I do not have human understanding.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-3}

			I neither learned wisdom, nor have the knowledge of the holy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וְלֹֽא־לָמַ֥דְתִּי חָכְמָ֑ה וְדַ֖עַת קְדֹשִׁ֣ים אֵדָֽע׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]

					\item[NIV] I have not learned wisdom, nor have I attained to the knowledge of the Holy One.
					\item[NRSV] I have not learned wisdom, nor have I knowledge of the holy ones.

		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:4} % *PROVERBS-30-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-4}

			Who hath ascended up into heaven, or descended? who hath gathered the wind in his fists? who hath bound the waters in a garment? who hath established all the ends of the earth? what is his name, and what is his son’s name, if thou canst tell?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מִ֤י עָלָֽה־שָׁמַ֨יִם ׀ וַיֵּרַ֡ד מִ֤י אָֽסַף־ר֨וּחַ ׀ בְּחָפְנָ֡יו מִ֤י צָֽרַר־מַ֨יִם ׀ בַּשִּׂמְלָ֗ה מִ֭י הֵקִ֣ים כָּל־אַפְסֵי־אָ֑רֶץ מַה־שְּׁמ֥וֹ וּמַֽה־שֶּׁם־בְּ֝נ֗וֹ כִּ֣י תֵדָֽע׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Who hath ascended up into heaven, and descended? Who hath gathered the wind in his fists? Who hath bound the waters in his garment? Who hath established all the ends of the earth? What is his name, and what is his son's name, if thou knowest?
					\item[NIV] Who has gone up to heaven and come down? Whose hands have gathered up the wind? Who has wrapped up the waters in a cloak? Who has established all the ends of the earth? What is his name, and what is the name of his son? Surely you know!
					\item[NRSV] Who has ascended to heaven and come down? Who has gathered the wind in the hollow of the hand? Who has wrapped up the waters in a garment? Who has established all the ends of the earth? What is the person’s name? And what is the name of the person’s child? Surely you know!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-5}

			Every word of God is pure: he is a shield unto them that put their trust in him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כָּל־אִמְרַ֣ת אֱל֣וֹהַּ צְרוּפָ֑ה מָגֵ֥ן ה֝֗וּא לַֽחֹסִ֥ים בּֽוֹ׃}

					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Every word of God is tried: He is a shield unto them that take refuge in him.
					\item[NIV] “Every word of God is flawless; he is a shield to those who take refuge in him.
					\item[NRSV] Every word of God proves true; he is a shield to those who take refuge in him.

			Add thou not unto his words, lest he reprove thee, and thou be found a liar.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תּ֥וֹסְףְּ עַל־דְּבָרָ֑יו פֶּן־יוֹכִ֖יחַ בְּךָ֣ וְנִכְזָֽבְתָּ׃}

					\item[NIV] Do not add to his words, or he will rebuke you and prove you a liar.
					\item[NRSV] Do not add to his words, or else he will rebuke you, and you will be found a liar.

			Two things have I required of thee; deny me them not before I die:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁ֭תַּיִם שָׁאַ֣לְתִּי מֵאִתָּ֑ךְ אַל־תִּמְנַ֥ע מִ֝מֶּ֗נִּי בְּטֶ֣רֶם אָמֽוּת׃}

					\item[NIV] “Two things I ask of you, LORD; do not refuse me before I die:
					\item[NRSV] Two things I ask of you; do not deny them to me before I die:

		% % ---

			Remove far from me vanity and lies: give me neither poverty nor riches; feed me with food convenient for me:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שָׁ֤וְא ׀ וּֽדְבַר־כָּזָ֡ב הַרְחֵ֬ק מִמֶּ֗נִּי רֵ֣אשׁ וָ֭עֹשֶׁר אַל־תִּֽתֶּן־לִ֑י הַ֝טְרִיפֵ֗נִי לֶ֣חֶם חֻקִּֽי׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NRSV] Remove far from me falsehood and lying; give me neither poverty nor riches; feed me with the food that I need,

		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:9} % *PROVERBS-30-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-9}

			Lest I be full, and deny thee, and say, Who is the LORD? or lest I be poor, and steal, and take the name of my God in vain.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּ֥ן אֶשְׂבַּ֨ע ׀ וְכִחַשְׁתִּי֮ וְאָמַ֗רְתִּי מִ֥י יְה֫וָ֥ה וּפֶֽן־אִוָּרֵ֥שׁ וְגָנַ֑בְתִּי וְ֝תָפַ֗שְׂתִּי שֵׁ֣ם אֱלֹהָֽי׃}

					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[NIV] Otherwise, I may have too much and disown you and say, ‘Who is the LORD?’ Or I may become poor and steal, and so dishonor the name of my God.
					\item[NRSV] or I shall be full, and deny you, and say, “Who is the LORD?” or I shall be poor, and steal, and profane the name of my God.

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-10}

			Accuse not a servant unto his master, lest he curse thee, and thou be found guilty.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תַּלְשֵׁ֣ן עֶ֭בֶד אֲדֹנָ֑יו פֶּֽן־יְקַלֶּלְךָ֥ וְאָשָֽׁמְתָּ׃}

			There is a generation that curseth their father, and doth not bless their mother.

		% % ---

			There is a generation, O how lofty are their eyes! and their eyelids are lifted up.

					\item[BHS] \he{דּ֭וֹר מָה־רָמ֣וּ עֵינָ֑יו וְ֝עַפְעַפָּ֗יו יִנָּשֵֽׂאוּ׃}

					\item[NIV] those whose eyes are ever so haughty, whose glances are so disdainful;
					\item[NRSV] There are those—how lofty are their eyes, how high their eyelids lift!—

		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:14} % *PROVERBS-30-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-14}

			There is a generation, whose teeth are as swords, and their jaw teeth as knives, to devour the poor from off the earth, and the needy from among men.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דּ֤וֹר ׀ חֲרָב֣וֹת שִׁנָּיו֮ וּֽמַאֲכָל֪וֹת מְֽתַלְּעֹ֫תָ֥יו לֶאֱכֹ֣ל עֲנִיִּ֣ים מֵאֶ֑רֶץ וְ֝אֶבְיוֹנִ֗ים מֵאָדָֽם׃}

					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] There is a generation whose teeth are as swords, and their jaw teeth as knives, To devour the poor from off the earth, and the needy from among men.

					\item[NRSV] there are those whose teeth are swords, whose teeth are knives, to devour the poor from off the earth, the needy from among mortals.

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:15} % *PROVERBS-30-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-15}

			The horseleach hath two daughters, crying, Give, give. There are three things that are never satisfied, yea, four things say not, It is enough:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לַֽעֲלוּקָ֨ה ׀ שְׁתֵּ֥י בָנוֹת֮ הַ֤ב ׀ הַ֥ב שָׁל֣וֹשׁ הֵ֭נָּה לֹ֣א תִשְׂבַּ֑עְנָה אַ֝רְבַּ֗ע לֹא־אָ֥מְרוּ הֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]

			The grave; and the barren womb; the earth that is not filled with water; and the fire that saith not, It is enough.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שְׁאוֹל֮ וְעֹ֪צֶ֫ר רָ֥חַם אֶ֭רֶץ לֹא־שָׂ֣בְעָה מַּ֑יִם וְ֝אֵ֗שׁ לֹא־אָ֥מְרָה הֽוֹן׃}

		\paragraph{Proverbs 30:17} % *PROVERBS-30-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-17}

			The eye that mocketh at his father, and despiseth to obey his mother, the ravens of the valley shall pick it out, and the young eagles shall eat it.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עַ֤יִן ׀ תִּֽלְעַ֣ג לְאָב֮ וְתָב֪וּז לִֽיקֲּהַ֫ת־אֵ֥ם יִקְּר֥וּהָ עֹרְבֵי־נַ֑חַל וְֽיֹאכְל֥וּהָ בְנֵי־נָֽשֶׁר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The eye that mocketh at his father, And despiseth to obey his mother, The ravens of the valley shall pick it out, And the young eagles shall eat it.
					\item[NIV] “The eye that mocks a father, that scorns an aged mother, will be pecked out by the ravens of the valley, will be eaten by the vultures.
					\item[NRSV] The eye that mocks a father and scorns to obey a mother will be pecked out by the ravens of the valley and eaten by the vultures.
					% % \item[OTX*]

					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-30-19}

			The way of an eagle in the air; the way of a serpent upon a rock; the way of a ship in the midst of the sea; and the way of a man with a maid.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דֶּ֤רֶךְ הַנֶּ֨שֶׁר ׀ בַּשָּׁמַיִם֮ דֶּ֥רֶךְ נָחָ֗שׁ עֲלֵ֫י צ֥וּר דֶּֽרֶךְ־אֳנִיָּ֥ה בְלֶב־יָ֑ם וְדֶ֖רֶךְ גֶּ֣בֶר בְּעַלְמָֽה׃}

					\item[NRSV] the way of an eagle in the sky, the way of a snake on a rock, the way of a ship on the high seas, and the way of a man with a girl.

			\label{PROVERBS-30-33}

			Surely the churning of milk bringeth forth butter, and the wringing of the nose bringeth forth blood: so the forcing of wrath bringeth forth strife.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כִּ֤י מִ֪יץ חָלָ֡ב י֘וֹצִ֤יא חֶמְאָ֗ה וּֽמִיץ־אַ֭ף י֣וֹצִיא דָ֑ם וּמִ֥יץ אַ֝פַּ֗יִם י֣וֹצִיא רִֽיב׃}

					% % \item[AB ]

					% % \item[OTR1]

					\item[ASV] For the churning of milk bringeth forth butter, And the wringing of the nose bringeth forth blood; So the forcing of wrath bringeth forth strife.
					\item[NIV] For as churning cream produces butter, and as twisting the nose produces blood, so stirring up anger produces strife.”
					\item[NRSV] For as pressing milk produces curds, and pressing the nose produces blood, so pressing anger produces strife.

	\subsubsection{Proverbs 31} % *PROVERBS-31
	% -----
		\label{PROVERBS-31}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:1} % *PROVERBS-31-1
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-1}

			The words of king Lemuel, the prophecy that his mother taught him.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דִּ֭בְרֵי לְמוּאֵ֣ל מֶ֑לֶךְ מַ֝שָּׂ֗א אֲ‍ֽשֶׁר־יִסְּרַ֥תּוּ אִמּֽוֹ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The words of king Lemuel; the oracle which his mother taught him.
					\item[NIV] The sayings of King Lemuel—an inspired utterance his mother taught him.
					\item[NRSV] The words of King Lemuel. An oracle that his mother taught him:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:2} % *PROVERBS-31-2
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-2}

			What, my son? and what, the son of my womb? and what, the son of my vows?

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַה־בְּ֭רִי וּמַֽה־בַּר־בִּטְנִ֑י וּ֝מֶה בַּר־נְדָרָֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] What, my son? and what, O son of my womb? And what, O son of my vows?
					\item[NIV] Listen, my son! Listen, son of my womb! Listen, my son, the answer to my prayers!
					\item[NRSV] No, my son! No, son of my womb! No, son of my vows!
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:3} % *PROVERBS-31-3
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-3}

			Give not thy strength unto women, nor thy ways to that which destroyeth kings.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַל־תִּתֵּ֣ן לַנָּשִׁ֣ים חֵילֶ֑ךָ וּ֝דְרָכֶ֗יךָ לַֽמְח֥וֹת מְלָכִֽין׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Give not thy strength unto women, Nor thy ways to that which destroyeth kings.
					\item[NIV] Do not spend your strength on women, your vigor on those who ruin kings.
					\item[NRSV] Do not give your strength to women, your ways to those who destroy kings.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:4} % *PROVERBS-31-4
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-4}

			It is not for kings, O Lemuel, it is not for kings to drink wine; nor for princes strong drink:

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אַ֤ל לַֽמְלָכִ֨ים ׀ לְֽמוֹאֵ֗ל אַ֣ל לַֽמְלָכִ֣ים שְׁתוֹ־יָ֑יִן וּ֝לְרוֹזְנִ֗ים אֵ֣י שֵׁכָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] It is not for kings, O Lemuel, it is not for kings to drink wine; Nor for princes to say, Where is strong drink?
					\item[NIV] It is not for kings, Lemuel— it is not for kings to drink wine, not for rulers to crave beer,
					\item[NRSV] It is not for kings, O Lemuel, it is not for kings to drink wine, or for rulers to desire strong drink;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:5} % *PROVERBS-31-5
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-5}

			Lest they drink, and forget the law, and pervert the judgment of any of the afflicted.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פֶּן־יִ֭שְׁתֶּה וְיִשְׁכַּ֣ח מְחֻקָּ֑ק וִֽ֝ישַׁנֶּה דִּ֣ין כָּל־בְּנֵי־עֹֽנִי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Lest they drink, and forget the law, And pervert the justice due to any that is afflicted.
					\item[NIV] lest they drink and forget what has been decreed, and deprive all the oppressed of their rights.
					\item[NRSV] or else they will drink and forget what has been decreed, and will pervert the rights of all the afflicted.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:6} % *PROVERBS-31-6
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-6}

			Give strong drink unto him that is ready to perish, and wine unto those that be of heavy hearts.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תְּנוּ־שֵׁכָ֣ר לְאוֹבֵ֑ד וְ֝יַיִן לְמָ֣רֵי נָֽפֶשׁ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Give strong drink unto him that is ready to perish, And wine unto the bitter in soul:
					\item[NIV] Let beer be for those who are perishing, wine for those who are in anguish!
					\item[NRSV] Give strong drink to one who is perishing, and wine to those in bitter distress;
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:7} % *PROVERBS-31-7
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-7}

			Let him drink, and forget his poverty, and remember his misery no more.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יִ֭שְׁתֶּה וְיִשְׁכַּ֣ח רִישׁ֑וֹ וַ֝עֲמָל֗וֹ לֹ֣א יִזְכָּר־עֽוֹד׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Let him drink, and forget his poverty, And remember his misery no more.
					\item[NIV] Let them drink and forget their poverty and remember their misery no more.
					\item[NRSV] let them drink and forget their poverty, and remember their misery no more.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:8} % *PROVERBS-31-8
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-8}

			Open thy mouth for the dumb in the cause of all such as are appointed to destruction.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פְּתַח־פִּ֥יךָ לְאִלֵּ֑ם אֶל־דִּ֝֗ין כָּל־בְּנֵ֥י חֲלֽוֹף׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Open thy mouth for the dumb, In the cause of all such as are left desolate.
					\item[NIV] Speak up for those who cannot speak for themselves, for the rights of all who are destitute.
					\item[NRSV] Speak out for those who cannot speak, for the rights of all the destitute.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:9} % *PROVERBS-31-9
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-9}

			Open thy mouth, judge righteously, and plead the cause of the poor and needy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פְּתַח־פִּ֥יךָ שְׁפָט־צֶ֑דֶק וְ֝דִ֗ין עָנִ֥י וְאֶבְיֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Open thy mouth, judge righteously, And minister justice to the poor and needy.
					\item[NIV] Speak up and judge fairly; defend the rights of the poor and needy.
					\item[NRSV] Speak out, judge righteously, defend the rights of the poor and needy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:10} % *PROVERBS-31-10
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-10}

			Who can find a virtuous woman? for her price is far above rubies.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{אֵֽשֶׁת־חַ֭יִל מִ֣י יִמְצָ֑א וְרָחֹ֖ק מִפְּנִינִ֣ים מִכְרָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] A worthy woman who can find? For her price is far above rubies.
					\item[NIV] A wife of noble character who can find? She is worth far more than rubies.
					\item[NRSV] A capable wife who can find? She is far more precious than jewels.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:11} % *PROVERBS-31-11
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-11}

			The heart of her husband doth safely trust in her, so that he shall have no need of spoil.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{בָּ֣טַח בָּ֭הּ לֵ֣ב בַּעְלָ֑הּ וְ֝שָׁלָ֗ל לֹ֣א יֶחְסָֽר׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] The heart of her husband trusteth in her, And he shall have no lack of gain.
					\item[NIV] Her husband has full confidence in her and lacks nothing of value.
					\item[NRSV] The heart of her husband trusts in her, and he will have no lack of gain.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:12} % *PROVERBS-31-12
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-12}

			She will do him good and not evil all the days of her life.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{גְּמָלַ֣תְהוּ ט֣וֹב וְלֹא־רָ֑ע כֹּ֝֗ל יְמֵ֣י חַיֶּֽיה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She doeth him good and not evil All the days of her life.
					\item[NIV] She brings him good, not harm, all the days of her life.
					\item[NRSV] She does him good, and not harm, all the days of her life.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:13} % *PROVERBS-31-13
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-13}

			She seeketh wool, and flax, and worketh willingly with her hands.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{דָּ֭רְשָׁה צֶ֣מֶר וּפִשְׁתִּ֑ים וַ֝תַּ֗עַשׂ בְּחֵ֣פֶץ כַּפֶּֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She seeketh wool and flax, And worketh willingly with her hands.
					\item[NIV] She selects wool and flax and works with eager hands.
					\item[NRSV] She seeks wool and flax, and works with willing hands.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:14} % *PROVERBS-31-14
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-14}

			She is like the merchants’ ships; she bringeth her food from afar.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{הָ֭יְתָה כָּאֳנִיּ֣וֹת סוֹחֵ֑ר מִ֝מֶּרְחָ֗ק תָּבִ֥יא לַחְמָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She is like the merchant-ships; She bringeth her bread from afar.
					\item[NIV] She is like the merchant ships, bringing her food from afar.
					\item[NRSV] She is like the ships of the merchant, she brings her food from far away.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:15} % *PROVERBS-31-15
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-15}

			She riseth also while it is yet night, and giveth meat to her household, and a portion to her maidens.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{וַתָּ֤קָם ׀ בְּע֬וֹד לַ֗יְלָה וַתִּתֵּ֣ן טֶ֣רֶף לְבֵיתָ֑הּ וְ֝חֹ֗ק לְנַעֲרֹתֶֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She riseth also while it is yet night, And giveth food to her household, And their task to her maidens.
					\item[NIV] She gets up while it is still night; she provides food for her family and portions for her female servants.
					\item[NRSV] She rises while it is still night and provides food for her household and tasks for her servant-girls.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:16} % *PROVERBS-31-16
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-16}

			She considereth a field, and buyeth it: with the fruit of her hands she planteth a vineyard.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{זָמְמָ֣ה שָׂ֭דֶה וַתִּקָּחֵ֑הוּ מִפְּרִ֥י כַ֝פֶּ֗יהָ נָ֣טְעָה כָּֽרֶם׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She considereth a field, and buyeth it; With the fruit of her hands she planteth a vineyard.
					\item[NIV] She considers a field and buys it; out of her earnings she plants a vineyard.
					\item[NRSV] She considers a field and buys it; with the fruit of her hands she plants a vineyard.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:17} % *PROVERBS-31-17
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-17}

			She girdeth her loins with strength, and strengtheneth her arms.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{חָֽגְרָ֣ה בְע֣וֹז מָתְנֶ֑יהָ וַ֝תְּאַמֵּ֗ץ זְרֹעוֹתֶֽיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She girdeth her loins with strength, And maketh strong her arms.
					\item[NIV] She sets about her work vigorously; her arms are strong for her tasks.
					\item[NRSV] She girds herself with strength, and makes her arms strong.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:18} % *PROVERBS-31-18
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-18}

			She perceiveth that her merchandise is good: her candle goeth not out by night.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{טָ֭עֲמָה כִּי־ט֣וֹב סַחְרָ֑הּ לֹֽא־יִכְבֶּ֖ה בַלַּ֣יְלָה נֵרָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She perceiveth that her merchandise is profitable; Her lamp goeth not out by night.
					\item[NIV] She sees that her trading is profitable, and her lamp does not go out at night.
					\item[NRSV] She perceives that her merchandise is profitable. Her lamp does not go out at night.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:19} % *PROVERBS-31-19
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-19}

			She layeth her hands to the spindle, and her hands hold the distaff.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{יָ֭דֶיהָ שִׁלְּחָ֣ה בַכִּישׁ֑וֹר וְ֝כַפֶּ֗יהָ תָּ֣מְכוּ פָֽלֶךְ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She layeth her hands to the distaff, And her hands hold the spindle.
					\item[NIV] In her hand she holds the distaff and grasps the spindle with her fingers.
					\item[NRSV] She puts her hands to the distaff, and her hands hold the spindle.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:20} % *PROVERBS-31-20
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-20}

			She stretcheth out her hand to the poor; yea, she reacheth forth her hands to the needy.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{כַּ֭פָּהּ פָּרְשָׂ֣ה לֶעָנִ֑י וְ֝יָדֶ֗יהָ שִׁלְּחָ֥ה לָֽאֶבְיֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She stretcheth out her hand to the poor; Yea, she reacheth forth her hands to the needy.
					\item[NIV] She opens her arms to the poor and extends her hands to the needy.
					\item[NRSV] She opens her hand to the poor, and reaches out her hands to the needy.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:21} % *PROVERBS-31-21
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-21}

			She is not afraid of the snow for her household: for all her household are clothed with scarlet.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{לֹא־תִירָ֣א לְבֵיתָ֣הּ מִשָּׁ֑לֶג כִּ֥י כָל־בֵּ֝יתָ֗הּ לָבֻ֥שׁ שָׁנִֽים׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She is not afraid of the snow for her household; For all her household are clothed with scarlet.
					\item[NIV] When it snows, she has no fear for her household; for all of them are clothed in scarlet.
					\item[NRSV] She is not afraid for her household when it snows, for all her household are clothed in crimson.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:22} % *PROVERBS-31-22
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-22}

			She maketh herself coverings of tapestry; her clothing is silk and purple.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{מַרְבַדִּ֥ים עָֽשְׂתָה־לָּ֑הּ שֵׁ֖שׁ וְאַרְגָּמָ֣ן לְבוּשָֽׁהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She maketh for herself carpets of tapestry; Her clothing is fine linen and purple.
					\item[NIV] She makes coverings for her bed; she is clothed in fine linen and purple.
					\item[NRSV] She makes herself coverings; her clothing is fine linen and purple.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:23} % *PROVERBS-31-23
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-23}

			Her husband is known in the gates, when he sitteth among the elders of the land.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{נוֹדָ֣ע בַּשְּׁעָרִ֣ים בַּעְלָ֑הּ בְּ֝שִׁבְתּ֗וֹ עִם־זִקְנֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Her husband is known in the gates, When he sitteth among the elders of the land.
					\item[NIV] Her husband is respected at the city gate, where he takes his seat among the elders of the land.
					\item[NRSV] Her husband is known in the city gates, taking his seat among the elders of the land.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:24} % *PROVERBS-31-24
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-24}

			She maketh fine linen, and selleth it; and delivereth girdles unto the merchant.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{סָדִ֣ין עָ֭שְׂתָה וַתִּמְכֹּ֑ר וַ֝חֲג֗וֹר נָתְנָ֥ה לַֽכְּנַעֲנִֽי׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She maketh linen garments and selleth them, And delivereth girdles unto the merchant.
					\item[NIV] She makes linen garments and sells them, and supplies the merchants with sashes.
					\item[NRSV] She makes linen garments and sells them; she supplies the merchant with sashes.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:25} % *PROVERBS-31-25
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-25}

			Strength and honour are her clothing; and she shall rejoice in time to come.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{עֹז־וְהָדָ֥ר לְבוּשָׁ֑הּ וַ֝תִּשְׂחַ֗ק לְי֣וֹם אַחֲרֽוֹן׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Strength and dignity are her clothing; And she laugheth at the time to come.
					\item[NIV] She is clothed with strength and dignity; she can laugh at the days to come.
					\item[NRSV] Strength and dignity are her clothing, and she laughs at the time to come.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:26} % *PROVERBS-31-26
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-26}

			She openeth her mouth with wisdom; and in her tongue is the law of kindness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{פִּ֭יהָ פָּתְחָ֣ה בְחָכְמָ֑ה וְתֽוֹרַת־חֶ֝֗סֶד עַל־לְשׁוֹנָֽהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She openeth her mouth with wisdom; And the law of kindness is on her tongue.
					\item[NIV] She speaks with wisdom, and faithful instruction is on her tongue.
					\item[NRSV] She opens her mouth with wisdom, and the teaching of kindness is on her tongue.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:27} % *PROVERBS-31-27
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-27}

			She looketh well to the ways of her household, and eateth not the bread of idleness.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{צ֭וֹפִיָּה הֲלִיכ֣וֹת בֵּיתָ֑הּ וְלֶ֥חֶם עַ֝צְל֗וּת לֹ֣א תֹאכֵֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] She looketh well to the ways of her household, And eateth not the bread of idleness.
					\item[NIV] She watches over the affairs of her household and does not eat the bread of idleness.
					\item[NRSV] She looks well to the ways of her household, and does not eat the bread of idleness.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:28} % *PROVERBS-31-28
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-28}

			Her children arise up, and call her blessed; her husband also, and he praiseth her.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{קָ֣מוּ בָ֭נֶיהָ וַֽיְאַשְּׁר֑וּהָ בַּ֝עְלָ֗הּ וַֽיְהַֽלְלָהּ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Her children rise up, and call her blessed; Her husband also, and he praiseth her, saying:
					\item[NIV] Her children arise and call her blessed; her husband also, and he praises her:
					\item[NRSV] Her children rise up and call her happy; her husband too, and he praises her:
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:29} % *PROVERBS-31-29
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-29}

			Many daughters have done virtuously, but thou excellest them all.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{רַבּ֣וֹת בָּ֭נוֹת עָ֣שׂוּ חָ֑יִל וְ֝אַ֗תְּ עָלִ֥ית עַל־כֻּלָּֽנָה׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Many daughters have done worthily, But thou excellest them all.
					\item[NIV] “Many women do noble things, but you surpass them all.”
					\item[NRSV] “Many women have done excellently, but you surpass them all.”
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:30} % *PROVERBS-31-30
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-30}

			Favour is deceitful, and beauty is vain: but a woman that feareth the LORD, she shall be praised.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{שֶׁ֣קֶר הַ֭חֵן וְהֶ֣בֶל הַיֹּ֑פִי אִשָּׁ֥ה יִרְאַת־יְ֝הוָ֗ה הִ֣יא תִתְהַלָּֽל׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Grace is deceitful, and beauty is vain; But a woman that feareth Jehovah, she shall be praised.
					\item[NIV] Charm is deceptive, and beauty is fleeting; but a woman who fears the LORD is to be praised.
					\item[NRSV] Charm is deceitful, and beauty is vain, but a woman who fears the LORD is to be praised.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}

		% % ---
		\paragraph{Proverbs 31:31} % *PROVERBS-31-31
		% % ---
			\label{PROVERBS-31-31}

			Give her of the fruit of her hands; and let her own works praise her in the gates.

			\begin{versebox}
					\item[BHS] \he{תְּנוּ־לָ֭הּ מִפְּרִ֣י יָדֶ֑יהָ וִֽיהַלְל֖וּהָ בַשְּׁעָרִ֣ים מַעֲשֶֽׂיהָ׃}
					% % \item[LXX] \gr{}
					% % \item[BSV]
					% % \item[AB ]
					% % \item[NETS]
					% % \item[OTR1]
					% % \item[OTR2]
					\item[ASV] Give her of the fruit of her hands; And let her works praise her in the gates.
					\item[NIV] Honor her for all that her hands have done, and let her works bring her praise at the city gate.
					\item[NRSV] Give her a share in the fruit of her hands, and let her works praise her in the city gates.
					% % \item[OTX*]
					% % \item[OTXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXX*]
					% % \item[OTXXXXXX*]
			\end{versebox}
